




jinavaṃsadīpaṃ 

||namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

1.

 mahādayau yau hadayau’dayau’dayau

hitāya dukkhānubhavai bhavai bhavai,

akāsi sambaudhipadaṃ padaṃ padaṃ

tamābhivandāmi jinaṃ jinaṃ jinaṃ| (yamakabandhanaṃ)

2. 

 pahāya yatthā’bhiratiṃ ratiṃ ratiṃ

ramanti dhammaiva munī munī munī,

vimuttidaṃ sabbabhavā bhavā’bhavā

tamābhivandai mahitaṃ hitaṃ hitaṃ| (yamakabandhanaṃ)

3. 

 nipītasaddhammarasā rasā’rasā

supuñ‍ñakhaittau’rasataṃ sataṃ sataṃ,

gatā vidhūtā vinayaina yaina yai

tamābhivandai’sigaṇaṅgaṇa’ṅgaṇaṃ| (yamakabandhanaṃ)

4. 

 jinā’natambhauruha haṃsarājinī

jinaurasānaṃ mukhapañ‍jarā’li nī,

sadatthasāraṃ sarasaṃ visūda nī

upaitu mai mānasamaiva vāṇi nī|

5.

 kammāvasaisā vicitau’pajātyā

ganthā’hisaṅkhāravibandhakā mai,

paṇāma puñ‍ñātisayaina’naina

mā pākadānā’vasarā bhavantu|

6.

 suvaṇṇavaṇṇassa jinassa vaṇṇaṃ

vaṇṇaiyya kappampi kajitau suvaṇṇau,

kappassi’vausāna manattatāya

na pāpuṇai buddhaguṇāna mantaṃ|

7. 

 niddhanta cāmīkara cāru rūpaṃ

sarassatī bhūsaṇa bhāsanaṃca,

anañ‍ña sādhāraṇa ñāṇamassa

avāviyā’cintiya mapakpamaiyyaṃ|

8.

 kuhiṃ asādhāraṇa rūpa līlā

kuhiṃ asādhāraṇa vāṇi līlā,

kuhiṃ asādhāraṇañāṇa līlā

kuhiṃ nu mai mandamatissa līlā|

9. 

 vibhāvimānī paravamhinau yai

issā’bhimānaina vibhañ‍ñamānā,

gavaisayantī’dha parassa randhaṃ

taisaṃ pasaṃsāgarahāhi kimmai|

10.

 pasattha satthāgama pāradassī

yai sādhavau sādhu guṇappasantā,

ganthassa nimmāṇaparissamaṃ nau

jānanti taiyaiva idhappamāṇā|

11.

 ādic‍cavaṃsappabhavassa tassa

jinassa satthāgamakauvidaihi,

vuttaupi pubbācariyaihi yaisu

ganthaisu saṅkhaipavasaina vaṃsau|

12.

 na taihi sak‍kā sugatassa vaṃsaṃ

kiñ‍cāpi viñ‍ñātu masaisayitvā,

sampuṇṇavaṃsassa vibhāvanāya

tasmā samussāhita mānasaina|

13. 

 abhippasan‍nau ratanattayamhi

pasatthavaṃsappabhavau pabhunaṃ,

vibhusaṇau vissutakittighausau

yau bhāti laṅkāya muḷārabhāgyau|

14.

 amandacāgā’bhiratassa

punandu nāmassa dayādhanassa,

buddhai pasādātisayassa tassa

ajjhaisanañ‍cāpi paṭiggahaitvā|

15.

 nassāya pubbācariyau’padaisaṃ

sautūna matthāya mayā hitāya,

niruttiyā māgadhikāya sammā

vidhīyatai’daṃ jinavaṃsadīpaṃ|

16. 

 saddhāsinaihānugatāya pañ‍ñā-

dasāya sautūhi manauvimānai,

padīpitau’yaṃ jinavaṃsadīpa-

dīpauharaipāpatamappabandhaṃ|

17.

 puraṅgapuṇṇā sirijambudīpai

sampattibhāraina divā’vatiṇṇā,

yā daivarājassa’marāvatī’vā-

marāvatīnāma purī purai’sī|

18.

 vij‍jādharānañ‍ca vihaṅgamānaṃ

vibandha vaihāsagatiṃ bahāsa,

yasmiṃ purasmiṃ jitavairi cak‍kaṃ

pākāracak‍kaṃ viya cak‍kavāḷaṃ|

19. 

 sañ‍cumbitamhauja rajau pabandha-

supiñ‍jarāpā parikhāhirāmā,

puritthi pākāra nitambabhāgai

samubbahī kañ‍cana maikhalā’bhaṃ|

20.

 rattindivā rattamaṇi’ndanīla-

maṇippabhārañ‍jita rājadhāni,

babandha yā’mandasurindacāpa-

samuj‍jalākāsatalabbilāsaṃ|

21.

 yahiṃvadhūnaṃ vadanambujaihi

katāvamānaṃ hariṇaṅkabimbaṃ,

pabhāhi nīlaupalatauraṇātaṃ

saukābhībhūtaṃca vivaṇṇamāpa|

22. 

 sarauruha’ntī maṇimandirābhā-

sañ‍cumbitaṃ puṇṇasasaṅkabimbaṃ|

saṅkāya rāmājanatā’bhirāmā

karai pasāraisi puramhi yasmiṃ|

23.

 yattha’ṅganānaṃ paṭibimbitāni

ādāsabhittīsu mukhambujāni,

āsuṃ vighātāya madhubbatānaṃ

vilaucanālīna manuggahāya|

24.

 sammatta mātaṅga dharādharaihi

yasmiṃ abhissanda madassavanti,

turaṅga raṅgaihī taraṅga mālā

samākulaivā’si vidhūta dhūlī|

25. 

 nikkhittavīṇā maṇinupurānaṃ

vilāsinīnaṃ mudupāṇi pādai,

mattālimālā kalanādinī kiṃ

nālaṅkaruṃ yattha katāvakāsā|

26.

 dhavatthinīnaṃ kucasārasaihi

naittālibhārā’nananīrajaihi,

yā hāsavīcīhi purī rajanyā

rarāja samaphul‍lasaraujinī’va|

27.

 candappabhā cumbita candakanta

pāsāṇadhārā maṇicandikāsū,

candānanānaṃ yahi maṅganāhaṃ

parissamassau’pasamāya’haisuṃ|

28. 

 yasmiṃ pūrai uddhamadhau vinaddha-

jutippabandhau maṇimandirānaṃ,

samubbahī gairuka paṅka diddha-

vitāna pac‍cattharaṇabbilāsaṃ

29.

 suvaṇṇa muttā maṇi vaṃsavaṇṇā-

pavāḷa rūpī vajiraihiñ‍cā’pi,

yā sattadhañ‍ñaihi dhanaihi phītā

ahū puri dhañ‍ñavatī’va nārī| (silaisabandhanaṃ)

30.

 pasāritā’naikadisāmukhaisu

vicittavatthā’bharaṇādipūrā,

yatthā’paṇā nij‍jitakapparukkhā

kariṃsu laukābhimatatthasiddhiṃ|

31. 

 parāgarattā madhupātimattā

samhin‍navailā ghananīlavālā,

haṃsāsayā pañ‍casarābhirāmā

yasmiṃ taḷākā viya kāmabhaugī| (silaisabandhanaṃ)

32.

 purantarasmiṃ ratanagghikānaṃ

raṃsippabandhaihi hatandhakārai,

kundāravindabbhudayainayasmiṃ

rattindivābhaida mavaidi laukau|

33. 

 mātaṅgajīmūtaghaṭāya ghaṇṭā-

ṭaṅkāragambhīraravāya yasmiṃ,

palambhītā mattasikhaṇḍimālā

akā vikālaipi akhaṇḍakīḷaṃ|

34.

 puramhi yasmiṃ caraṇambujaihi

vadhūjatau bandhitanūpuraihi,

vikāsa kaukāsana sīsa baddha

mattāli saisa’mbujinī ajaisi|

35.

 rasātalaṃ nāgaphaṇāvanaddhaṃ

nabhautalaṃ vij‍julatāvanaddhaṃ,

yā chāditā rūpiyajātarūpa-

dhajāvalīhā’jini rājadhānī|

36. 

 nānatthasāraṃ mitadhātuvaṇṇaṃ

chandārahaṃ pāṇagaṇā’bhirāmaṃ,

kavippasatthaṃ sarasaṃ silaisā-

laṅkārapaj‍jaṃ’va puraṃ yamāsi| (silaisabandhanaṃ)

37.

 puramhi tasmiṃ karuṇānidhānau

buddhaṅkurau brāhmaṇasāravaṃsai,

asaṅkhakappāna mitau catun‍naṃ

lakkhādikānaṃ udapādi pubbai|

38.

 bhauvādivaṃsai’kadivākarassa

puñ‍ñānubhāvau’dayamaṅgalaihi,

jātassa khau sampati jambudīpau

vilumpayī maṅgalavāsalīlaṃ|

39. 

 jātakkhaṇai tassa sarīrajaina

gandhaina vaṇṇaina sakai nikaitai,

hatappabhā candanatailadīpā

saṇṭhānamattaihi vijāniyāsuṃ|

40.

 vimuttadausāhi sukhaidhitāhi

dhātīhi kumbhaurupayaudharāhi,

bhatau kumārau sukumārakāyau

khaipaisi sau kānici vāsarāni|

41.

 mahāmahaicā’tha pavattamānai

savaida vaidaṅga vidū vidūhi,

kārāpayuṃ tai pitarau’rasassa

nāmaṃ sumaidhau’ti padatthasāraṃ|

42. 

 uḷārabhāgyaina samaṃ kumārai

saṃvaddhamānai jananī na tittiṃ,

pāyāsi nīlāmakalalaucanāliṃ

mukhambujaṃ tassa’bhicumbamānā|

43.

 sukhaidhita’ṅgāvayavau kumārau

vimānabhumyā maṇinimmitāya,

paraudi mātāpitarau’bhiyācaṃ

bimbaṃ kanijaṃ jānuyugaina gacchaṃ|

44.

 suvaṇṇabimbau’pamacārurūpau

samācaraṃ dhātibhujā’valambaṃ,

viñ‍ñāsapāda’ṅgulimañ‍jarīhi

salīlamāvāsamalaṅkarittha|

45. 

 nijaina taijaina ca jivalaukaṃ

yasaina’pubbācarimaṃ phusantau,

tiraukaritvā ravicandasaubhaṃ

saṃvaḍḍhi dhīrau ubhatau sujatau|

46.

 sau sattamā yāva pitāmahassa

yugā sagabbhāsayasuddhikau’si,

nihīnajac‍cau’ti na jātivādā

khittau’pakuṭṭhau bhavi vippasaiṭṭhau|

47.

 vaidantayaṃ sau sanighaṇṭu satthaṃ

sakaiṭubhaṃ sākkharabhaida satthaṃ,

sādhabbatabbaidi’tihāsa satthaṃ

avaidi vaidaṅgayutaṃ pa satthaṃ|

48.

 ajjhāyakau mantadharau pavīṇau

kalāsu laukāyatalakkhaṇaisu,

papūrakāri padakau kavīnaṃ

taitā’si vaiyyākaraṇau gaṇisau|

49. 

 kandappadappā’naladhumarāji-

līlāvalambi nijamassurāji,

na kaivalaṃ kaumalagaṇḍabhāgaṃ

manampi thīnaṃ malinīkarittha|

50.

 tandaibhavaṇṇāyatana’ṇṇavamhi

narūpataṇhātaraṇi narānaṃ,

pāyāsi cakkhāyatanappiyāhi

tīrantaraṃ cittaniyāmakaṭṭhā|

51.

 dvijau sumaidhau suvisuddhamaidhau

mātāpitun‍naṃ nidhanāvasānai,

puñ‍ñānubhāvappabhavaṃ agāra-

majjhāvasaṃ kāmasukhaṃ’nubhuñ‍jī|

52. 

 nisaj‍ja pāsādatalai’kadā sau

pal‍laṅkamādhāya rahaugatauva,

punabbhavuppatti sarīrabhaidau

dukkhau’ti cintaisi sabhāvacintī|

53.

 jātau sa’haṃ jātijarārujādi-

dhammau’mhi tasmā bhavadukkhasuñ‍ñaṃ,

nic‍caṃ ajātiṃ ajaraṃ araugaṃ

gavaisituṃ vaṭṭati nibbuti’nti|

54.

 yathāpidukkhai sati ca’tthisātaṃ

tadañ‍ñamuṇhai sati sītamatthi,

bhavamhi santai vibhavau’pi aivaṃ

nibbāṇamatthī tividhaggisantai|

55. 

 sāvaj‍jadhammai ihavij‍jamānai

saṃvij‍jatai bhau niravaj‍jadhammau,

ajāti hauti sati jātiyā’ti

aivaṃ vicintaisi sadatthavintī|

56.

 disvā yathā guthagatau taḷākaṃ

na tassa dausau na tamautaraiyya,

kilaisadhauvai amatamhi santai

tathā na saivaitha na tassa dausau|

57.

 pāpāriruddhau sati khaimamaggai

na tassa dausau na sukhaṃ vajaiyya,

pāpāriruddhau sati khaimamaggai

tathā nagacchaiyya na tassa dausau| (yamakabandhanaṃ)

58. 

 yathāpi vaij‍jai sati ghauraraugī

na tassa dausau ta labhai tikicchaṃ,

rāgādiraugī sati buddhavaij‍jai

dhammausadhaṃ nai’cchati kassa dausau|

59.

 yau kaṇṭhabaddhaṃ kuṇapaṃ pahāya

yathāsukhaṃ gacchati sairicārī,

tathaivi’maṃ kucchita pūtikāyaṃ

yaṃnūna gacchaiyyamahaṃ jahitvā|

60.

 uc‍cāraṭhānamhi janā’napaikkhā

katvā karīsāni kayathā vajanti,

tathā sarīraṃ kuṇapaihi pūraṃ

yaṃnūna gacchaiyyamahaṃ jahitvā|

61. 

 nāvaṃ yathā jaj‍jaramāpagāhiṃ

vajaiyya naitā atapaikkhakauva,

tathā navadvārasavaṃ sarīraṃ

yaṃnūna gacchaiyyamahaṃ jahitvā|

62.

 cauraihi gacchaṃ avahārabhītyā

khaimaṃ sumaidhau puramautaraiyya,

tathā sarīraṃ kusalāvahāraṃ

yaṃnūna gacchaiyyamahaṃ jahitvā|

63.

 naikkhamma saṅkappa parau’pamāhi

anussaritvai’vamuḷāravīrau,

hatau’rapārai tibhavai asārai

vihāsi uk‍kaṇṭhitamānasau sau|

64. 

 suvaṇṇa muttā maṇi rūpiyādi-

dhanaihi dhañ‍ñaihi ca pūritāni,

avāpuritvāna,tha kausakauṭṭhā-

gārāni taṃ dassayi rāsivaḍḍhau|

65.

 pitāmahānaṃ pakapitāmahānaṃ

mātāpitun‍naṃ vibhavā panaittha,

anappakāthāvarajaṅgamātai

saṃdissarai dhīra sumaidhavippa|

66.

 sau sattamā yāva pavaiṇivaṭṭā

vibhāvayitvā vibhavassarāsiṃ,

dhanāgamassāpi dhanabbayassa

pamāṇa’mācīkkhipamāṇadassiṃ|

67.

 kuṭumbamaitaṃ paṭipaj‍jamānau

kāmaisu daivauviya indriyāni,

icchānurūpaṃ paricārayassu

ic‍caiva māraucayi rāsivaḍḍhau|

68. 

 amuṃ mahantaṃ dhanadhañ‍ñarāsiṃ

samāvinitvai’ka kahāpaṇampi,

nā’dāya mātāpitaraupya’hau tau

gatā yathākamma mitau parattha|

69.

 tabbatthusāraggahaṇātisūrau

vaussaggasantau atha sattasārau,

rañ‍ñau samārauciya aitamatthaṃ

bhairiṃ carāpaisi sakai puramhi|

70.

 santappayi bhairivirāvagandha-

māghāya sampattajātā’lijātaṃ,

bhauvādi nānāratanādibhauga-

madhūhi sattāha’manāthanāthau|

71. 

 tadagga yañ‍ñālaya vārivāha-

dhārānipātaddhanavuṭṭhihaitu,

mahā janassā’dhikavatthutaṇhā-

taṭāni bhin‍nāni manaudahaisu|

72.

 sukhaidhitau kāmasukhaṃ pahāya

gharā’bhīnikkhamma tatau sumaidhau,

ajjhaugahaitvā himavanta’māpa

dhammaisakau dhammakapakabbata’ntaṃ|

73.

 vitak‍kamañ‍ñāya’tha daivarañ‍ñā

vyāpāritau māpayi vissakammau,

tahiṃ vivaikakkhamaka massamañ‍ca

manauramaṃ caṅkamabhumibhāgaṃ|

74. 

 tamassamaṃ pabbajitaihi suñ‍ñaṃ

upaic‍ca sauñ‍cāramavāpuritvā,

ñatvā tadantaulikhita’kkharāni

khāriṃparikkhārabharaṃavaikkhi|

75.

 nivatthavatthaṃnavavadausupaitaṃ

vivaj‍jiyāvaj‍jiyavaj‍jadassi,

dhāraisitaṃbārasadhānisaṃsa-

manaujapupphattharavākacīraṃ|

76.

 pun‍nāgapupphattharakā’bhirāmaṃ

aṃsai vidhāyā’jinacammakhaṇḍaṃ,

katvā jaṭāmaṇḍala mittamaṅgai

tivaṅka mādāya’tha khārikājaṃ|

77.

 bhujaṅgabhaugau’rubhujaina dhīrau

ādāya cālambanadaṇḍakauṭiṃ,

samaggahī tāpasavaisamaivaṃ

virattacittauka vibhavaiva bhavai’pi|

78. 

 sau caṅkamī caṅkamamautaritvā

silātalasmiñ‍ca divā nisaj‍ji,

sāyaṃ paviṭṭhau vasi paṇṇasālaṃ

nipaj‍ji kaṭṭhattharasaisamañ‍cai|

79.

 pacacūsakālamhi pabujjhitau sau

āvaj‍jayitvā’gamanappavattiṃ,

vivaikakāmassa mamai’ttha vāsau

kāmaṃ gharāvāsasamau siyā’ti|

80.

 aduñhi paṇṇacchadanaṃ kapauta-

pādāruṇaṃ bailuvapak‍kavaṇṇā,

bhūmīpi bhittī rajatāvadātā

mañ‍cau’pi cittattharavārurūpau|

81. 

 subhāka manāpā mama pakaṇṇasālā

sādīnavā dupparibhāriyā’yaṃ,

paṇītabhikkhā pariyaiṭṭhi mūla-

dukkhassa natthi’ti pamāṇa mantau|

82.

 agārasañ‍ñāya paṭikkhapitvā

tañ‍ca’ṭṭhadausā kulapaṇṇasālaṃ,

dasaṅga sādhāraṇa rukkhamūlaṃ

phalāphakhalāhāra mupaic‍ca bhaujī|

83.

 sumaidhasau sau divasāni satta

mahāpadhānaṃ padahaṃ sumaidhau,

pattau abhiñ‍ñāsu vasisu pāraṃ

sabbaṃka samāpattisukhaṃ avindi|

84. 

 tasmiṃkhaṇai kānana daivatāhi

sādhū’ti nigghausitapītighausau,

abbhuggatau tassa yasaina saddhiṃ

visuddhavij‍jācaraṇu’bbhavaina|

85.

 vij‍jādharā tagguṇadīpakāni

mutiṅgavīṇādhanibandhavāni,

gāyiṃsu gītāni’va nac‍camānau

himācalau sampati sampavaidhi|

86.

 muddhaṅkuraṃ bhudharakuṭabāhu-

sataihi tan‍nijjhara cāmaraihi,

vidhūyamānaihi vidhūtapāpaṃ

kataupahāraiva mahāsarā’pī|

87. 

 akālamaighaddhani bhairirāva-

vyāpāritā mattasikhaṇḍisaṇḍā|

ajjhāvasantaṃ vanasaṇḍamajjhaṃ

mahiṃsucā’khaṇḍanataṇḍavaina|

88.

 mandā’nilā’mandabhujā’valamba-

sunīlasākhāmaṇivijanīhi,

lataṅganā’liṅgitasālasāmī

saṃvijayuṃ vitadarampi dhīraṃ|

90. 

 kapītanā’sauka tamāla nīpā

kapītanā’sauka tamāla nīpā, (samattapādabhyāsa mahā yamakaṃ)

kapītanā’sauka tamāla nīpā

kapītanā’sauka tamāla nīpā|

91.

 na vailalitā kiṃ pasavakā’vataṃsā

latāvitānā madhupālisālī,

latāvitānā madhupā’lisālī

na vail‍litā kiṃ pakasavā’vataṃsā|(samuggabhaida yamakaṃ)

92. 

 pupphāvalī kandala pāṭalaggā

kalāpinī sā vanarājinīlā,

pupphākulī kandana pāṭalakkhī

kalāpanīlā vara rājinīva| (addhagaumuttikā bandhanaṃ)

93. 

 natāsirau mañ‍jarikāsuramhā

natāsirau pañ‍jalikāva rammai,

vanai nibaddhaṃ ramitau vibhāsi

vinaiyya bandhūracītau pahārau| (pādagaumuttikā bandhanaṃ)

94.

 rajaukirantā’vanatā latāsuṃ

lājaukirantā vanitā natāva,

dvijauarañ‍ñaṃ vasitā pitāghau

gajautarantauva latā vitānaṃ| (silaukagaumuttikā bandhanaṃ ākulajālamitipi)

95. 

 mataṅgajindā na masak‍kariṃsu

pādāni natvāna padipadhāmaṃ,

pañ‍ñādhavaṃ pīna tapaṃ phalaihi

himaddipādai parisuttamañhi| (kabbanāma gabbha cak‍kaṃ)

96. 

 maittāya chattaṃ’va phaṇaṃ phaṇindau

dhāraisi sīsai vasinau cacāra,

nathāmavā’kāva’balaisu kiñ‍ci

maidhāya nandau thiravāci khaimai| (kavināma gabbha cak‍kaṃ)

97. 

 nau’sitaihi’ssa santāsa’nū’na tausa vatau dau,

dāyatau vasatau na’nusantāsassa hitaisinau| (gāthaddhavisaya paṭilauma yamakaṃ)

98.

 yaukā’sā’vāsa kāyau kāma’kāma’makāma’kā,

sakāyanā’nāya’kāsa vāma nā ga ganā’mavā| (sabbatau bhadda bandhanaṃ)

99. 

 dayāya vasitau dāyai yāpajāsiva māsadā,

yajārahaṃ rañ‍jamānau vasihaṃsau ciraṃvasi| (addhabbhama bandhanaṃ)

100.

 madhumada madhukara virutai virutai

malayaja surahīta pavanai pavanai

himavati vikasita padumai padumai

adhisukha manubhavi savasi savakasi| (pādanta yamakaṃ)

iti maidhānandābhidhānaina yatinā viracitai sakalakavijana hadayānandadānanidānai jinavaṃsadīpai dūrainidānai sumaidhabrāhmaṇāpadānaparidīpau|

paṭhamau saggau|

1. 

 (mandā’k‍kantā) marapurasiriṃ sabbasampattisāraṃ

jambuddīpā’sama sarasijai kaṇṇikā san‍nikāsaṃ,

rammaṃ rammavhaya puravaraṃ pāramīpāradassī

buddhau dīpaṅkara dasabalau sabbalaukaikadī| ()

tasmiṃ kālai vipulakaruṇā nārisañ‍caudita’ttau

nānā khīṇāsava parivutau cārikaṃ sañ‍carantau,

saṃvattaintau sunipuṇatayaṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ kamaina

patvā tasmiṃ paṭivasati saudassanavhai vihārai| ()

3. 

 sutvā dipaṅkara bhagavatau nāgarā kittisaddaṃ

sambuddhau sau itipi arahaṃ tyādinā’bbhuggataṃ taṃ,

gāhāpaitvā tuvaṭatuvaṭaṃ vatthabhaisaj‍ja pānaṃ

tan‍nittā’suṃ pamuditamatā gandhamakālādihatthā| ()

4.

 patvā dīpaṅkaratarihariṃ gandhamālādikaihi

pūjaitvāta’ñajalimukulikā aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā,

dhammaṃ sutvā savaṇasubhagaṃ buddhapāmaukkhasaṅghaṃ

saṃyācitvā muditahadayā svāttayā’pagañ‍juṃ| ()

5. 

 saj‍jaitvā tai dutiyadivasai saj‍janā dānasālaṃ

ussāpattā dhajakadaliyau puṇṇakumbhai ṭhapain‍nā,

kubbantā’pi dhavalasuḷinu’kkhaipa lājaupahāraṃ

aivaṃ tassā’gamana mayanaṃ laṅkarauntā vihāsuṃ| ()

6.

 abbhuggantvā atha himavatā sau sumaidhau tapassi

gacchaṃ taisaṃ upari nabhasā vākacīraṃ dhunan‍nau,

disvā pītippamuditajatai añ‍jasaṃ saudhayantai

sañjhāmaighau riva parilasaṃ dhataritthai’kamantaṃ| ()

7.

 saṃsaudhaintā kalalavisamaṭṭhāna saṅkāradhānaṃ

kasmā tumhai paṭipathamimaṃ’laṅkarauthā’ti pucchi,

bhantai dīpaṅkaratarahari’dāni nissāya rammaṃ

buddhau hutvā viharati mahādhammasaṅkhaṃ dhamantau| ()

8. 

 sau sambuddhau parivutamahābhikkhusaṅghau yatau nau

gāmakkhaittaṃ pavisati tatau’laṅkaraumā’bruviṃsu,

buddhūppādau kimuta sutaraṃ dul‍labhau buddhasaddau

ic‍caivaṃsau sumariya alaṅkattukāmau’si maggaṃ| ()

9.

 jhānā’bhiñ‍ñā ratatakavacu’j‍jautamāna’ttabhāvau

saddhāyai’sau acalasadisau iddhimā tāpasau’ti,

sal‍lakkhaitvā kalalavisamaṃ duggamaggappadaisaṃ

saj‍jaituṃ tai sapadi muditā sādhavau tassa’daṃsu| ()

10. 

 nānāpupphaṃ jalajathalajaṃ aucinitvā vanamhā

taitvā daivāsurabhavanatau kauviḷārādipupphai,

ānaitvā’haṃ bhujagabhavatā phul‍lakaṇḍuppalāni

chaikausmī’ti vithariya pathaṃ iddhiyā saṃvidhātuṃ| ()

11.

 katvai’vaṃ mai hadayamakuḷaṃ tauvikāsaiyya tasmā

vaiyyāvac‍caṃ visadamatinau kāyikaṃ saṃvidhāya,

aj‍jaivā’haṃ vipulakusalaṃ sañ‍cinissa’ntī dhīrau

saṃsaudhaituṃ kalalakalusaṃ añ‍jasaṃ ārabhittha| ()

12.

 passantānaṃ vimalanayanau’bhāsa jimūtagabbhai

buddhaubuddhau’tya’bhihitavacau vij‍jurājīva cārī,

tasmiṃ paṅkai nijakaratala’maibhājapacchihi dhīmā

paṃsuṃ datvā rajatadhavalaṃ vālukaṃ vaukirantau| ()

13. 

 tasmi ṭhānai kal‍lalulitai suṭṭhu nā’laṅkataiva

saddhiṃ dīpaṅkara’nadhivarau’naikakhīṇāsavaihi,

pattau brahmā’maranaraphaṇisiddhavij‍jādharānaṃ

saṃvattantai suvipulamahai pāṭihīrai uḷārai| ()

14.

 haima’mbhaujau’pamasuvadanaṃ maṇḍitaṃ lakkhaṇhau-

sītyā’nubyañ‍janavilasitaṃ kaitumālāvilāsaṃ,

satthāraṃ taṃ disidisi pabhāniccharanta’ñ‍jasambhi

āgacchantaṃ viya maṇitalai mattamātaṅgarājā| ()

15. 

 aulaukaitvā vimalanayanañ‍candaniluppalāni

ummilaitvā ratanaphalakaṃ ak‍kamantauva piṭṭhiṃ,

nānākhīṇāsavaparicutau kaddamaṃ nā’k‍kamitvā

sambuddhauyaṃ vajatu iti mai dīgharattaṃ hitāya| ()

16.

 sal‍lakkhaitvā khara’jinajaṭāvākacīrāni kaisai

aumuñ‍citvā visamakalalai pattharitvā’ttabhāvaṃ,

saituṃ katvā paramapaṇidhī kauminī caudita’ttau

pañ‍cā’bhiñ‍ñāratanamaṇimā svā’cakuj‍jau nipaj‍ji| ()

17.

 sutvā gāthāpadampi na mai bhāriyaṃ saṃkilaisai

viddhaṃsaitvā varasivuraṃ pattumicchai sacā’haṃ,

saṃvij‍jantai tibhavabhavanai dukkhitā’nantasattai

sau’bhaṃ aikau kathamadhigamai dhamma mañ‍ñātavaisau|()

18. 

 yan‍nūnā’haṃ parahitaratau sammadañ‍ñāya baudhiṃ

āraupaitvā nikhilajanataṃ’nuttaraṃ dhammanāvaṃ,

uttāraitvā varasivapuraṃ vaṭṭadukkhaudadhimhā

pacchā dīpaṅkaramuni yathā nibbutiṃ pāpuṇissaṃ| ()

19.

 ic‍caivaṃ sau pumariya samaudhānayitvā’ṭṭhadhammai

saṃsāramhā’vataraṇamahāsaiturūpau pajānaṃ,

muddhābaddha’ñ‍jalipuṭajaṭau paṅkapiṭṭhai nipan‍nau

sambaudhatthaṃ paṇidhimakari tāva tappādamūlai| ()

20. 

 ussisaṭhau sapadi bhagavā pañ‍cavaṇṇappasādaṃ

ummīlaitvā nayanayugalaṃ phul‍lanīluppalābhaṃ,

disvā nīlaupalamaṇimayaṃ vātapānañ‍cayaṃ’va

ugghāṭaintau isivaramhāpaṅkajaṃ paṅkapiṭṭhai| ()

21.

 aitassi’jajhissati iti ayaṃ patthanā’nāgataṃsa-

ñāṇaṃ sammā patiniya itau kappalakkhādhikānaṃ,

āvaj‍jaintau upari caturāsaṅkhiyānantya’vaidi

patvā baudhiṃ ahamiva siyā gautamau nāma buddhau| ()

22.

 tumhai sampassatha iti imaṃ tāpasaṃ saṅghamajjhai

vatvai’vaṃ sau padamasadisaṃ dhammarājā dadantau,

samhinditthā’dharakisalayā’lattakaṃ nāgataṃya-

pañ‍ñāmuddā’ṅkitapadasataṃ vattasandaisagabbhaṃ| ()

23. 

 vāsaṭṭhānaṃ kapilanagaraṃ nāma māsāmahaisi

mātā suddhaudananarapati tai pitā’dic‍cavaṃsai,

bimbā bimbā dharavati piyā haima bimbā bhirāmā

tasmiṃkālai tanujaratanaṃ rāhulau haissatai tai| ()

24.

 haissantai tai paṭhamadutiyassāvakā sāriputta-

mauggal‍lānā dvijakulabhavā bhuripañ‍ñiddhimantau,

ānandākhyau yati pati rupaṭṭhāyakausāvikānaṃ

khaimāthairi parama yugalaṃ uppalabbaṇṇathairi| ()

25. 

 assatthau tai vijayaviṭapī tvañ‍ca khau gautamavhau

chabbassānī padahiya gharā nikkhamitvā sakamhā,

pāyāsaggaṃ parivisiya bhau tvaṃ sujātāya din‍naṃ

baudhiṃ bujjhissasi iti dhuvaṃ baudhimūlai nisaj‍ja| ()

26.

 satthā sañjhāghanapaṭalatau muttavij‍jul‍latai,va

sandassaitvā nijabhujalataṃ cīvarabbhantaramhā,

pakhyākāsi jaladhararavā’kāragambhīraghauyaṃ

nicchāraitvā suradhanurivau’bhāsa chabbaṇṇaraṃsi| ()

27.

 amhai dīpaṅkarabhagavatau sāsanai nā’vabuddhā

lacchāmā’ti tava parimukhai’vā’yatiṃ maukkhadhammaṃ,

tasmiṃ pattā’khīla suranarāpatthayuṃ taṅkhaṇaivaṃ

pūjaitvā’tañ‍jalisarasijai pādapīṭhamhi tassa| ()

28. 

 buddhau brahmāmaranarasirau cumbitaṅghī saraujau

sampūjaitvā’ṭṭhahi jaṭilakaṃ pupphamuṭṭhīhi tamhā,

pak‍kāmi sau kanakasikharīhāri kiñ‍jakkhabhārai

ubbhūta’mhauruhavanasirai appayantau padāni| ()

29.

 rammaṃ rammaṃ mahīya jaṭilaṃ pupphamuṭṭhīhi katvā

khīṇā khīṇāsavavasigaṇā dakkhiṇaṃ pak‍kamiṃsu,

daivā’daivā pavuramakaruṃ vandanāmānapūjaṃ

dīpaṃ dīpaṅkaradasabalañ‍cā’nugantvā nivattā| ()

30. 

 tamhā ṭhānā gatasati janai san‍nisin‍nassa tassa

pal‍laṅkainā’maranara paric‍canta pupphāsanamhi,

jātikkhaittā tahimupagatā daivatā aitamatthaṃ

āraucaisuṃ mahitavaraṇā añ‍jalimañ‍jarīhiṃ| ()

31.

 pubbai pupphāsanupari samārūḷhabuddhaṅkurānaṃ

addhānai’vai’tarahi bhavataucā’sanārauhaṇamhi,

aikālaukā dasahi guṇitā laukadhātu sahassī

saṃvattantai tvamanavarataṃ haissasai taina buddhau| ()

32.

 tāsaṃ vācaṃ savaṇamadhuraṃ daivatānaṃ nisamma

bhiyyau cittappabhavavīriyau pītivipphāritattau,

pubbai sattuttamaparicitā baudhisambhāradhammā

āvaj‍jaisi kati iti sudhī dhammadhātuṃ sahaituṃ| ()

33. 

 aukuj‍jitvā dharaṇiṭhapitau puṇṇa kumbhau sumaidha

vissandaitvā salilamakhilaṃ kintupac‍cāharaitha,

aivaṃ datvā dhanasutakalatta’ṅgapac‍caṅgajīvai

nibbin‍nau mā bhavi’ti paṭhamaṃ pāramiṃ’dhiṭṭhahi sau| ()

34.

 nā’paikkhitvā yathariva nijaṃ jīvitaṃ jīvitaṃ’va

rakkhantau sañ‍carati camari cāmara candikābhaṃ,

aivaṃ sīlaṃ varasivapuradvāramārakkha dhīra

ajjhiṭṭhāsi iti sadutiyaṃ pāramiṃ suddhasīlau| ()

35. 

 saṃviggau yau ciraparivasaṃ ghaurakārāgharamhi

muttīṃ tamhā’gamayati yathā hauhi naikkhammanittau

nibbin‍nau tvaṃ tathariva bhavai bandhanāgārarūpai

ajjhiṭṭhāsi tatiyampi sau pāramintyai’kacārī| ()

36.

 hīnuk‍kaṭṭhaṃ kulamanugharaṃ bhikkhakau bhikkhu bhikkhaṃ

aṇvāhiṇḍaṃ labhati naciraṃ saṃvaraṭṭhau yathai’vaṃ,

sambaudhattha bhaja paṭibalai paṇḍitai puṭṭhapañhau

ajjhiṭṭhāsi tvamiti matimā pāramiṃ sau catutthiṃ| ()

37.

 nic‍cussāhau vicarati yathā kaisarī sairicārī

aivaṃ ṭhānai gamanasayanaicā’sanai tvaṃ sumaidha,

ussauḷhī tyāsithilavīriyau hauti sambaudhanatthaṃ

ajjhīṭṭhāsi thiravīriyavā pañ‍camiṃ pāramiṃ sau| ()

38. 

 iṭṭhāniṭṭhaṃ pathaviriva bhau sabbamānāvamānaṃ

nāpaj‍jitvā manasiviktiṃ tvaṃ sahantau khamantau,

sambaudhatthaṃ paravadhakhamau hauhi’tī khantivādī

ajjhiṭṭhāsi parahitaratau chaṭṭhamiṃ pāramiṃ sau| ()

39.

 vīthiṃ nātik‍kamati niyamaṃ ausadhītārakā’yaṃ

aivaṃ santuttama paricitaṃ sac‍cavācaṃ sumaidha,

tvaṃ māvitik‍kami karahaci bauddhukāmau subaudhiṃ

ajjhiṭṭhāsi’tya’vitathakathi sattamiṃ pāramiṃ sau| ()

40. 

 tamhāṭhānā balavapavanai vāyamānai’pi thaukaṃ

kappaṭṭhāsi tacalati yathā pabbatau suppataṭṭhau,

tvaṃ tiṭṭhāhi tathariva adhiṭṭhānadhammaisu daḷhaṃ

ajjhiṭṭhāsī’tyavalasadisau ca’ṭṭhamiṃ pāramiṃ sau| ()

41.

 autiṇṇaisu udakarahadau bhau nihīnuttamaisu

sītattaṃ sampharati hi samaṃ vārinā bhāvayaini,

maittāyaivaṃ tibhavabhavanai sabbasattaisu tulyaṃ

ajjhiṭṭhāsi samuti navamiṃ pāramiṃ maitta cittau| ()

42.

 iṭṭhāniṭṭhai sati paṭihatai vatthujātai yathāhi

majjhattā’yaṃ vasumativadhū hauti dukkhai sukhaika vā,

aivaṃ bhau tvaṃ bhava samatulāsantibhau’paikkha kau’ti

ajjhiṭṭhāsi savasi dasamiṃ pāramiṃ bhurimaidhau| ()

43. 

 ālaulaintau tidasapamitaṃ pāramisāgaraṃ sau

sattādhisau nisitamatimā ñāṇamatthā’calaina,

āvaj‍jaisi vasumatavadhu sādhukāraṃ’va dainti

saṃkampi sampati satimatau dhammataijaina taina| ()

44.

 bhīrūcchamhī ghaṇapathaviyā kampamātāyi’māya

patvā dīpaṅkarabhagavatau rammavāsī samīpaṃ,

sampucchiṃsu vasumati bhusaṃ kampi taṃkissahaitu

āvaj‍jaitvā samuti muninau tampavattiṃ kathaisi| ()

45.

 nik‍kaṅkhā tai punapi nagarā nāgarā taṃ upaic‍ca

sampūjaisuṃ caraṇayugalaṃ gandhamālādikaihi,

katvā taina’ñ‍jalisarasijai yaina dīpaṅkarai’ṇau

uṭṭhāsi sau purisatisahau san‍nīsin‍nāsanamhā| ()

46. 

 mā tai raugau bhavi paṭibhayaṃ mā bhavi chambhitattaṃ

saṅkappau tai paramapaṇidhi sijjhataṃ khippamaiva,

itthañ‍cā’sithutipadasataṃ jātikhaittā gatā taṃ

pupphādīhi mahīya jaṭilaṃ nij‍jarā byāhariṃsu| ()

47.

 abbhuggantvā pavanapadaviṃ daivatānaṃ manāni

baudhātvau himavati sakaṃ assamaṃ tāpasau sau,

pattau atthācalamupagamī taṅkhaṇai raṃsimālī

saṅkaucaitvā sarasijavanaṃ saṃharitvā’ṃsujālaṃ| ()

48. 

 rammaṃ dīpaṅkarabhagavatau rammavatyā’bhidhānaṃ

vāsaṭṭhānaṃ janakajananī dvai sudaivassumaidhā,

nic‍caupaṭṭhāyakayativarau sāgataumaṅgalauca

tissaucā’suṃ paṭhamadutiyassāvakā thairanāgā| ()

49.

 nānākhīṇāsavaparivutau cā’si nandā sunandā

tassā’haisuṃ paṭhamadutiyassāvikā aggabhūtā,

kāyau’sitiratanapamitau pipphalināmabaudhi

aṭṭhāsi sau pacurajanataṃ tārayaṃ vassalakkhaṃ| ()

50.

 satthā dīpaṅkaravhau suranarasaraṇaudīpadīpaucirassaṃ

dīpaivau dhammadīpaṃ tibhuvanabhavanai vīta’vij‍jandhakāraṃ

aggikkhandhau’vabhāsaṃ vihariya parinibbāyi khīṇāsavā’pi

khīṇasnaihāpadīpāyathariva ariyā sāvakā nibbutā’suṃ| ()

iti maidhānandābhidhānaina yatinā viracitai sakalakavijana hadayānandadānanidānai jitavaṃsadīpai dūrainidānai sumaidha tāpasassa mūlapaṇiṭhānaṭṭhapanapavatti paridīpau dutiyau saggau|

1. 

 laukaṃ (’vasantatilakau) kumudākaraṃ mā

kauṇḍañ‍ñanāmabhagavā’tha pabaudhayattau,

jātau tadā varamatī vijitāvi rājā

sampan‍na cak‍karatanau’bhavi cak‍kamatti| ()

2.

 saṅghassa buddhapamukhassa uḷāradānaṃ

datvā vidhāya paṇidhiṃ varabaudhiyā sau,

raj‍jaṃ pahāya jinasāsanamautaritvā

jhānānya’lattha paṭilaṅvarappadānau| ()

3. 

 tassā’si rammavatināma puraṃ sunandau

rājā ahausi janakau jananī sujātā,

bhaddassubhaddasamaṇā varasāvakā’suṃ

tissau’patissa’samaṇi varasāvikāyau| ()

4.

 lakkhāyukau vijayabaudhi visālasāla-

kalyāṇi nāma tadupaṭṭhahi cā’nuruddhau,

tassā’ṭṭha sītiratanappamitaṃ sarīraṃ

āsuṃ tayau ariyasāvakasan‍nipātā| ()

5.

 tassā’paraina samayaini’ha’naṅgabhaṅgau

uppaj‍ji maṅgalajinau janamaṅgalāya,

buddhaṅkurau’tirucirau surucī samañ‍ñau

āsi tadā’vatisurau dvijavaṃsakaitu| ()

6. 

 datvā sasāvakajitassa dināni satta

patthaisi baudhimasamaṃ gavapānadānaṃ,

pabyākatau bhagavatā bhavanā’hīgantvā

pabbaj‍jitau sukhamavindi samādhijaṃ sau| ()

7.

 tassu’ntaraṃ puravaraṃ pitarau’ttara’vhā

āsuṃ sudaivasamaṇau vasi dhammasainau,

tassā’ggasāvakayugaṃ sakasāvikānaṃ

bhaddaṃyugaṃ abhavi sivalicā’pya’saukā| ()

8. 

 taṃ pālitau jinamupaṭṭhahi aṭṭha’sīti

hatthau’si tassa vajirūpamarūpakāyau,

baudhī’pi nāgataru sāvakasantipātā

āsuṃ tayau navutivassasahassamāyu| ()

9.

 tassā’paraina sumanau karuṇānidhānau

nāthau manaujamathanau udapādi laukai,

buddhaṅkurau’bhavi tadā’tulanāgarājā

taija’ggijālajalitau atuliddhimā sau| ()

10.

 nāgau’pi nāgabhavanamhi sasāvakassa

buddhassa dibbaturiyaihi katupahārau,

datvāna dānamatulaṃ paṇidhiṃ akāsi

buddhau bhavissasi tuvanti ahāsi buddhau| ()

11. 

 khaimavhayaṃ puramahū janakau sudantau

rājā janaitti sirimā nijasāvakānaṃ|

aggā bhaviṃsu saraṇau vasi bhāvitattau

sauṇā tadaggasamaṇi’si tathu’pasauṇā| ()

12.

 tassā’si nāgataru baudhi udainatairau-

paṭṭhāyakau navutivassasahassamāyu,

ubbaidhatau navutihatthamitaṃ sarīraṃ

āsuṃ tayau ariyasāvakasan‍nipātā| ()

13.

 tassā’paraina udapādi’ha raivatākhyau

daivādivanditapadau bhuvi daivadaivau,

sattuttamau bhavi tadā atidaivanāmau

bhauvādivaṃsatilakau catuvaidavaidī| ()

14. 

 baddhañ‍jalī sirasi dhammakathaṃ nisamma

gantvāna taṃ saraṇamuttaramuttariyaṃ|

datvā’hipatthayi sukhaudhimathau mahaisi

buddhau bhavissasi tuvantī visākarittha| ()

15.

 tassā’si dhañ‍ñavatināma puraṃ jinassa

mātā mahaisi vipulā vipulau pitā’si,

sabrahmadaivavaruṇau bhavi saṅghamajjhai

bhaddā ca bhaddayugalaṃ duvidhaṃ subhaddā| ()

16.

 taṃ sambhavau vasi upaṭṭhahi nāgabaudhi

rukkhaupya’sitiratanaṃ bhavi attabhāvau,

āyuppamāṇampi saṭṭhisahassavassaṃ

āsuṃ tayau ariyasāvakasan‍nipātāta|

17. 

 tassā’paramhi samayai janapārijātau

uppaj‍ji saubhitajinau jitapañ‍camārau,

ajjhāyakau sakalavaida muḷārabhaugī

buddhaṅkurau bhavi tadā’jitanāmavappau|

18.

 dhammaṃ nisamma saraṇaisu patiṭṭhahitvā

saṅghassa buddhapamukhassa uḷāradānaṃ,

datvā padhānapaṇidhāna makāsi dhīrau

tvaṃ lacchasi’ti varabaudhi mahāsi satthā| ()

19.

 rammaṃ sudhammamahu tassa puraṃ sudhammau

rājā ahausi janakau janikā sudhammā

tassā’ggasāvakayugaṃ asamau sunaittau

tassāvikā’ggayugalaṃ nakulā sujātā| ()

20. 

 nāgassa nāgataru baudhi sarīramaṭṭha-

paṇṇāsahatthapamitaṃ tamataumathairau,

saupaṭṭhahī navutivassasahassamāyu

āsuṃ tayau ariya sāvaka san‍nipātā| ()

21.

 uppaj‍ji tassa aparaina anaumadassi

buddhau pabuddhakamalāmalanīlanaittau,

buddhaṅkurau jitasurāri tadāni yakkha-

saināpatī bhavi mahiddhimahānubhāvau| ()

22.

 sambaudhi maggapurisau paṇidhānayaṃ sau

saṅghassa buddhapamukhassa uḷāradānaṃ,

pādāsi tisu saraṇaisu patiṭṭhahitvā

buddhau bhavissasi tuvanti jinau’bruvitaṃ| ()

23. 

 ṭhānañhi candavatināma yasaudharākhyā

mātā mahaisi yasavā janakau janindau,

tassa’ggasāvakayugaṃ nisabhau ataumau

dvai sundarī ca sumanā carasāvikā’suṃ| ()

24.

 baudhī’pi tassa kakudhau munidaihamaṭṭha-

paṇṇāsahatthapamitaṃ varuṇābhidhānau,

thairau upaṭṭhahi ca lakkhapamāṇamāyu

āsuṃ tayau ariyasāvakasan‍nipātā| ()

25.

 tassā’paraina padumau dipadānamindau

jātau pabujjhitamanaupadumau pajānaṃ,

dhīrau babhūva varavāraṇakumbhabhaidī

sīhau tadā rucirakaisarabhāragīvau| ()

26. 

 buddhaṃ niraudhasukhavaidiyanaṃ vatamhi

sattāhamakkhipadūmaihi tamac‍cayitvā,

cittaṃ pasādiya punā’gatasāvakaisu

sīhau vibhāsi paṭiladdhavarappadānau| ()

27.

 tassā’si campakapuraṃ padumābhidhānau

rājā ahausi janakau asamā janaittī,

sālaupasālayatayau varasāvakā’suṃ

rāmā’pi tassa paramāsamaṇi surāmā| ()

28.

 nāmainu’paṭṭhahi vasi varuṇau tamaṭṭha-

paṇṇāsahatthamita massa sarīramā’si,

baudhi’pi sauṇataru lakkhapamāṇamāyu

āsuṃ tayau ariyasāvakasan‍nipātā| ()

29. 

 tassā’paraina varadau muni nāradavhau

pāpandhakāranikaraṃ bhīdurau’dapādi,

buddhaṅkurau bhavi tadā’khilajhātabhiñ‍ñā-

lābhī pavattaphalabhauji tapaudhanīsau| ()

30.

 katvānu’ḷārapaṇidhāna muḷāravirau

datvā sasāvakajinassa uḷāradānaṃ,

pūjaisi taṃ surabhinā haricandanaina

satthāpi sampati viyākaraṇaṃ adāsi| ()

31.

 tassā’si dhañ‍ñavatināma puraṃ sumaidhau

rājā ahausi janakau jananī anaumā,

dvai bhaddasālajitamittavasi vasina-

maggau’ntarā samaṇi phagguṇi bhikkhunītaṃ| ()

32. 

 vāsaiṭṭhabhikkhu tadupaṭṭhahi rūpakāyau

tassā’ṭṭhasitiratanaṃ mahasauṇasākhī,

baudhiddumau navutivassasahassamāyu

āsuṃ tayau ariyasāvakasantipātā| ()

33.

 tassā’paraina padumuttara dhammarājā

jātau tilaukapadumau padumappitaṅghī,

aḍḍhau uḷāravibhavau maharaṭṭhiyau sau

buddhaṅkurau bhavi tadā jaṭilābhidhānau|()

34.

 sambaudhiyā’dhigama pac‍cayapatthanaṃ sau

virauvidhāya padumuttarapādamūlai,

saṅghassa buddhapamukhassa ticivarāni

pādāsi tīsuratanaisu abhippasan‍nau| ()

35. 

 tassā’si haṃsavatināma puraṃ jinassa

ānandabhupati pitā janikā sujātā,

dvai tassa daivalasujātavasi vasinaṃ

aggā bhaviṃsu samaṇisvāmitāsamā’ggā| ()

36.

 lakkhāyukau sajayabaudhika visālasāla

rukkhau upaṭṭhahi muniṃ sumanābhidhānau,

tassa’ṭṭha’sitiratanappamitaṃ sarīraṃ

āsuṃ tayau bhagavatau gaṇasan‍nipātā| ()

37.

 tassā’paraina samayaina sumaidhanāmau

laukamhi pātubhavi laukahitāya satthā,

buddhaṅkurau kira tadānyu’bhatau sujātau

svā’sitikauṭivibhavau’ttara māṇavau’si| ()

38. 

 vissaj‍jiyāna vibhavaṃ tiṃsatikauṭiṃ

datvāna dānamasmaṃ sugatai sasaṅghai,

pabbaj‍jitau paramabaudhi mapatthayittha

byākāsi saumuni ta’mijjhanabhāva’maddhā| ()

39.

 rammaṃ sudassanamahū nagaraṃ sudantau

tassā’si bhūpati pitā jananī sudattā,

saṅghaisu’hausu saraṇau vasi sabbakāmau

rāmā yamāni paramānya’bhavuṃka surāmā| ()

40. 

 baudhī’pi nīpataru sāgaranāmathairau

’paṭṭhāsi taṃ navutivassasahassamāyu,

tassā’ḍhasitiratanu’ggatamāsi gattaṃ

āsuṃ tayau satimatau gaṇasantipātā| ()

41.

 tassā’paraina samayaina janappadīpau

jātau sujātabhagavā jitapañ‍camārau,

sampan‍nasattaratatau varacak‍kavatti

rājā babhūvi’ha mahāpurisau tadāsau| ()

42.

 dhammā’mataina muditau ratanadvayassa

datvā sasattaratanaṃ catudīparaj‍jaṃ,

pabbaj‍ji baudhipaṇidhiṃ paṇidhāya dhīmā

ñatvā mahāmuni tamijjhanabhāvamāha|

43. 

 rammaṃ sumaṅgalamahū puramuggatākhyau

rājā pitābhavi pabhāvatināma mātā,

aggābhaviṃsu ca sudassanadaivathairā

nāgā gaṇassadasi nāgasamālathairi| ()

44.

 taṃ nāradaumuniru’paṭṭhahi ca’ttabhāvau

paṇṇāsahatthapamitau bhavivaiṇubaudhi,

tassā’bhavī navutivassasahassamāyu

āsuṃ tayau dhītimatau gaṇasan‍nipātā| ()

45.

 tassā’paraini’ha nirūpamarūpasārau

jātaubabhūva piyadassisamantadassi,

dhīrau tadanya’bhavi kassapamāṇavau sau

vaidaisu tīsu kusalau kusalaṃ gavaisi| ()

46. 

 sau kauṭilakkhaparimāṇadhatabbayaina

saṅgassa buddhapamukhassa mahāvihāraṃ,

katvā padāsi abhipatthitabuddhabhāvau

buddhau’pi tappaṇidhisiddhi siyā’tya’bhāsi| ()

47.

 candāmahaisi jananī janakau pudin‍nau

rājā babhūva puramassa anaumanāmaṃ,

āsuṃ tadaggayugalāni sujātadhamma-

din‍nā gaṇassadasi pakālitasabbadassi| ()

48.

 taṃ sautavhasamaṇau samupaṭṭhahittha

baudhī piyaṅgu bhagavā’si asitihatthau,

aṭṭhāsika sau navutivassasahassama’ssa

āsuṃ tayau matimatau gaṇasan‍nipātā| ()

49. 

 tassā’paraina samayainu’dapādi laukai

laukatthasādhanaratau munira’tthadassī,

sattuntamau’pi niratik‍kamadhammasimau

taijiddhimā isi tadā’si susimanāmau| ()

50.

 ānīya dibbabhavanā kusumāni tassa

mandāravāni supatiṭṭhitapādapīṭhai,

sampūjiyāna paṇidhānamakāsi satthā

tvaṃ mādisau’bruvi bhavissasi cā’yatinti| ()

51.

 tassā’si saubhitapuraṃ bhavisāgaravhau

rājā pitā janatidaivi sudassanākhyā,

santaupasantasamaṇā varasāvakā’suṃ

dhammā tadaggasamaṇipya’bhavuṃ sudhammā| ()

52. 

 tañ‍cā’bhayau munirūpaṭṭhahi saupya’sīti-

hatthuggatau satasahassapamāṇamāyu,

campaiyyasākhi bhavi baudhi subaudhihaitu

āsuṃ tayau ariyasavakasan‍nitā| ()

53.

 tassā’paraina udapādi’ha dhammadassī

nissīmadhī’nadhivarau bhavapāradassi,

sau tāvatiṃsabhavatamhi mahānubhāvau

buddhaṅkurau bhavi tadā kira daivarājā|()

54.

 dibbāni gandhakusumāni kathāgatassa

cak‍kaṅkitaurucaraṇamburuhāsanamhi,

pūjaisi dibbaturiyaihi ca buddhabhāvaṃ

sau patthayaṃ munitamijjha nabhāvamāha| ()

55. 

 ṭhāniyamāsi saraṇaṃ sugatassa tassa

rājā pitā’si saraṇau janani sunandā,

aggābhaviṃsu padumauvasi phussadaivau

khaimā ca bhikkhusamaṇisva’pi sabbanāmā| ()

56.

 thairau sunaittavisutau tadupaṭṭhahi sau

lakkhāyukau’si jayabaudhi ñ‍ca bimbijālau,

tassā’pya’sitiratanappamitaṃ sarīraṃ

āsuṃ tayau ariyasāvakasan‍nipātā| ()

57.

 tassā’paraina samayaini’ga siddhabaudhi

siddhatthanāmaviditau udapādi satthā,

buddhaṅkurau bhavi tadā’khīlaṅajhānalābhī

bhauji pavattitaphalaṃ vasi maṅgalākhyau| ()

58. 

 sampan‍nagandharasikaṃ paripak‍kamaikaṃ

ānīya sau vipakulajambubhalaṃ vanamhā,

pādāsi tassa paṇidhīkatabuddhabhāvau

tañ‍cānubhuya bhagavāpi viyākarittha| ()

59.

 vaihāramāsi nagaraṃ jayasainanāmau

rājā ahausi janakau jananī suphassā

bhikkhūsu tassa vasi sambahulau sumittau

dvai sīvali ñ‍ca samaṇīsu varā surāmā| ()

60.

 taṃ raivataumuni munindamuṭṭhahittha

baudhī’pi tassa kaṇikāramabhīruhau’si,

lakkhāyukau sa’narasārathi saṭṭhihatthau

āsuṃ tayau ariyasāvakasan‍nipātā| ()

61. 

 tassā’paraini’ha samubbhavi nissanāmau

satthā pasatthacaraṇau caturau’ghatiṇṇau,

buddhaṅkurau bhavi tadāni sujātarājā

rājañ‍ñamauḷimaṇilaṅkatapādapīṭhau| ()

62.

 hitvā sa’raj‍jamisivaiyadharau sudhīrau

dibbaihi’naikakusumaihi jinaṃ vajantaṃ,

pūjaisi muddhani tamāpavitānasaubhaṃ

satthā’pi tappaṇidhisiddhi siyā’tya’bhāsi| ()

63.

 khaimaṃ purañhi janakau janasandhanāmau

rājā janaitti padumā nijasaṅghamajjhai,

dvai brahmadaivudaya vissutathairanāgā

phussā ca aggayugalānya’bhavuṃ sudattā| ()

64. 

 taṃ sambhavauvasi vasindamupaṭṭhahittha

tassā’sanavhataru baudhi sa’saṭṭhitatthau,

aṭṭhāsi vassagaṇanāya mahaisi lakkhaṃ

āsuṃ tayau ariyasāvakasan‍nipātā| ()

65.

 tassā’paraina bhavasāgarapādassi

phussau mahāmuniri’hakabbhudapādi laukai,

dhīrau tadāni vijitāvi jitārivaggau

rājā babhūva surarājanibhau’rutaijau| ()

66.

 sambaudhi maggapurisau paṇīdhāya phītaṃ

raj‍jaṃ vivaj‍jiya sa’pabbajitau janassa,

añ‍ñāya tīṇipiṭakāni kathaisi dhammaṃ

vyākāsi phussabhagavā’pi’va pubbabuddhā| ()

67. 

 tassā’si kāsinagaraṃ jayasainanāmau

rājā pitā’si janati sirimā mahaisi,

saṅghaisu’bhausu’pi surakkhitadhammasaitā

cālā tadaggayugalāni tathū’pañ‍cālā| ()

68.

 baudhiddumā’malakasākhi sarīramaṭṭha-

paṇṇāsahatthapamitaṃ sabhiyābhidhānau,

saupaṭṭhahī navutivassasahassamāyu

āsuṃ tayau bhakavatau gaṇasan‍nipātā| ()

69.

 tassā’paraina sanarāmarasattasārau

satthā vipassi’ha samubbhavi sabbadassī,

kammaina kainaci mahiddhimahānubhāvau

buddhaṅkurau’bhavi tadā’tulanāgarājā| ()

70. 

 aṅgīrasassa ghanakañ‍canahaddapīṭhaṃ

pādāsi tassa khavitaṃ ratanaihi nānā,

sau buddhabhāvamahipatthiya baudhisattau

vyākāsi tatthasunisaj‍ja jinau vipassi| ()

71.

 tassā’si bandhumatināmapuraṃ tadaiva-

nāmau pitā janani bandhumatī mahaisi,

dvai khaṇḍatissavasinau varasāvakā’suṃ

candā ca bhaddayugalaṃ bhavi candamittā| ()

72.

 daihaṃ asitiratataṃ tamasaukathairau-

paṭṭhāsi baudhiviṭapī bhavi kaṇhavaṇṭā,

vāsaṃakā munira’sītisahassavassaṃ

āsuṃ tayau ariyasāvakasan‍nipātā| ()

73. 

 tassā’paraina adhisīlasamādhipañ‍ñau

satthā samubbhavī sikhī janakappasākhī,

dhīrau tadā’bhavi arindamanāmarājā

saddhau pahūtaratatau ratanattayamhi| ()

74.

 bhikkhañ‍ca sattaratanābharaṇābhirāmaṃ

ñatvāna hatthiratanaṃ sugatai sasaṅghai,

sau buddhabhāvamahipatthayi sattasārau

vyākāsi lacchasi sukhaudhipadanti satthā| ()

75.

 buddhassa cāriṇavatī nagaraṃ ahausi

mātā pabhāvati pitā aruṇavha rājā,

saṅghaisu’bhausu abhibhuvasi sambhavau ca

aggābhaviṃsu makhilāpadumābhidhānā| ()

76. 

 khaimaṅkarau jinamupaṭṭhahi sattatiṃsa-

hatthucchitau vijayabaudhi ca puṇḍarikau,

sau sattatiṃsatisahassa mitāyukau’si

āsuṃ tayau tadiyasāvakasan‍nipātā| ()

77.

 tassā’paraini’ha samubbhavi kaitumālā-

byāmappabhāparilasaṃ munivaissabhū’ti,

buddhaṅkurau kira tadāni sudassanavha-

rājā babhūva pararājagajindasīhau| ()

78.

 saṅghassa buddhapamukhassa sacīvaraṃ sau

datvāna dānamatulaṃ jinasāsanamhi,

sabbañ‍ñubaudhimabhipatthiya pabbajittha

buddhau bhavissasi dhuvanti tamāhasatthā| ()

79. 

 tassā’pya’naupamapuraṃ bhavi suppatītau

rājā pitā yasavatī janikā mahaisī,

sauṇuttarā ca nijasāvakasāvikānaṃ

dāmā matiddhiparamā paramā samālā| ()

80.

 baudhī’pi tassa bhavi sālamahīrūhau’pa-

sampan‍nabhikkhu tadupaṭṭhahi saṭṭhihatthau,

satthā vihāsi samasaṭṭhisahassavassaṃ

āsuṃ tayau tadiyasāvakasattipātā| ()

81.

 tassā’paraini’kaha samubbhavi sac‍casandau

vainaiyyabandhu bhagavā kakusandhanāmau,

buddhaṅkurau bhuvi tadā’bhavi khaimarājā

dānappabandhajalasaikasudhautahatthau| ()

82. 

 sau pattacīvarapabhūtikaman‍napānaṃ

datvā sasāvakajinassa gharā’bhigantvā,

pabbaj‍ji baudhipaṇidhiṃ paṇidhāya rājā

satthāsayā’tya’vaca tappaṇidhānasiddhi| ()

83.

 khaimavahayaṃ nagaramassa pitā’ggidattau

vippau vibhāvi abhavi janikā visākhā,

sañ‍jivathairadutiyau vidhurau ca thairau

sāmā tadaggayugalaṃ bhavi campakākhyā| ()

84. 

 taṃ buddhijau jinamupaṭṭhahi tassa gattaṃ

tāḷisahatthamitamāsi sirisabaudhi,

tāḷisahāyanasahassapamāṇamāyu

aikau’va tassa bhavi sāvakasan‍nipātau| ()

85.

 tassā’paramhi samayai karuṇānidhānau

laukābhibhū kanakabhudharahārirūpau,

uppaj‍ji kauṇagamanaumuni pabbatākhyau

bhumissarau bhavi mahāpurisau tadāni| ()

86.

 saṅghassa buddhapamukhassa uḷāradānaṃ

datvā mahagghavaracivarasāṭakai ca,

sau pakabbajittha abhipatthita buddhabhāvau

buddhau bhavissasi tuvanti tamāhasatthā| ()

87. 

 nāmaina saubhavati tampuramuttarākhyā

mātā pitā’vanisurau bhavi yañ‍ñadattau,

hīyausauttaravasī samaṇi samuddā

tassau’ttarā ca paramā parisāsu’bhausu| ()

88.

 taṃ sautthijau jinamupaṭṭhahi tiṃsahatthau

aṅgīrasau bhavi udumbarasākhi baudhi,

sau tiṃsahāyanasahassamitāyukau’si

aikau’va tassa bhavi sāvakasantipātau| ()

89.

 tassā’paraini’ha mahāmuni kassapākhyau

laukamhi pātubhavi khaggavisāṇakappau,

buddhaṅkurau bhuvi tadā’bhavi jautipālau

vaidaisu tīsu sakalāsu kalāsu chaikau| ()

90. 

 kalyāṇamittadutiyau sugataṃ upaic‍ca

sutvāna dhammamathasāsana mautaritvā,

sabbañ‍ñubhāvamhipatthayi māṇavau sau

vyākāsi kassapamunī’pi munī’ca pubbā| ()

91.

 bārāṇasi nagaramāsi pitā ca mātā

dvai brahmadattadhanavatya’bhidhānavantau,

bhikkhusu tassa samaṇisva’pi tissabhāra-

dvājā ca bhaddayugalātya’nulauruvailā| ()

92.

 taṃ sabbamittasamaṇau samupaṭṭhahittha

nigraudhasākhī jayabaudhi sa’vīsahatthau,

tassā’si visatisahassa pamāṇamāyu

aikau’va’hū ariyasāvakasan‍nīpātau| ()

iti maidhānandābhidhānaina yatitā viracitai sakalakavijana hadayānanda dānanidānai jinavaṃsadīpai dūrainidānai baudhisattassa saisapaṇidhātaṭṭhapana pavatti paridīpau tatiyau saggau|

1. 

 dīpaṅkarādicatuvīsati buddhapāda-

mūlaisu laṅapaṇidhāna mahānidhānau,

lakkhādhikaṃ caturasaṅkhiyakappasaṅkhaṃ

puñ‍ñābhisandamabhisaṅkhari baudhisattau| ()

2.

 aitthantarai vividhabāhiravatthujātaṃ

sisa’kkhimaṃsarudhirāni ca puttadārai,

sau jivitampi kapaṇaḍikayācakānaṃ

vīrau paric‍caji pajāya hitatthamaiva| ()

3. 

 dānādhimuttipariyaisana vippavaisau

patvā tapauvanamakitti tapaudhanassa,

tihaṃ alattha sakaṭāhamalauṇaḍākaṃ

yassā’bhibhūtajaṭharassa jighacchitattā| () (akitticariyaṃ)

4.

 kantāramagga paṭipan‍na manātapattaṃ

pac‍caika buddhamahipassiya saṅakkhavippau,

phuṭṭhaṃ sakic‍capasutau sūriyātapaina

yau chattupāhaṇamadāsi sudhautapāṇi| () (saṅkhacariyaṃ)

5.

 dubbhikkhapīḷanabhayaina kaliṅgaraṭṭhā

saṃyācataṃ yadupagamma dhanañ‍jayavhau,

sauṇḍāya gayha nijamañ‍jana nāgarājaṃ

yau dakkhiṇaudaka nisaikamakāsi rājā| () (kurudhammacariyaṃ)

6. 

 hutvāna yau mahasudassana cak‍kavattī

rājā kusāvatipuramhi divā ca rattau,

vatthattapānamupataisi carāpayitvā

bhairiṃka asaisakapaṇḍikayācakānaṃ| () (mahāsudassanacariyaṃ)

7.

 yau sattabhumibhujasāsi purauhitau’pi

rājūhi laddhamakhilaṃ dhanadhañ‍ñarāsiṃ,

sampattayācakajanassa paricchajitvā

puñ‍ñappabandhamabhisañ‍cini baudhihaitu| () (mahāgauvindacariyaṃ)

8.

 dhammānusāsi nimināma mahībhujau’pi

sālaṃ vidhāya mithilāya catummukhaṃ yau,

acchin‍namatthijanapakkhicatuppadānaṃ

dānaṃ pavattayī purā dadataṃ variṭṭhau| () (nimirājacariyaṃ)

9. 

 yau aikarājasutacandakumārabhūtau

muddhābhisaika karaṇāya janaihi gacchaṃ,

saṃvaijitau savibhavai tibhavai’pi yañ‍ñā-

vāṭaṃ vidhāya’bhipavattayi dānavaṭṭaṃ| () (candakumāracariyaṃ)

10.

 yau vatthusāragahaṇaina atittarūpau

bhumissarau’pi sivināma surādhipassa,

jac‍candhavaisagahitassa vilaucanāni

uppāṭayitva padadaṃ labhi dibbacakkhū| () (sivirājacariyaṃ|)

11.

 dānādhimuttiparamau sasapaṇḍitau yau

mittainu sāsiya adhiṭṭhitupausathaṅgau,

aṅgāramuddhani papāta sajīvitāsaṃ

hitvā dvijassa tanumaṃsapadātukāmau| () (sasapaṇḍitacariyaṃ| itidānapāramīṃ)

12. 

 yau mātupausakakari bhisamuddharattau

andhāya hatthidamakaina karaiṇukāya,

sauṇḍāya suṭṭhugahitau’pya’vikaṇḍitassa

sīlassa khaṇḍanabhayā najanaisi kaupaṃ| () (mātupausakacariyaṃ)

13.

 yau bhuridattabhujagau’parivammikaṭṭhau,

sīlabbataṃ visadharau samadhiṭṭhahitvā,

paiḷāya khittabhujagai ahiguṇṭhikamhi

sīlassa kuppanabhayaina jahāsi kaupaṃ| () (bhuridattacariyaṃ|)

14.

 sīlabbatādivibhavau jalitiddhimā yau

campaiyyanāmabhujagau ahiguṇṭhikamhi,

icchānurūpavicarau camarī’va vālaṃ

sīlaṃ jugaupa napi tabbadhakai cukaupa| () (campaiyyacariyaṃ|)

15. 

 yau cūlabaudhivisutau samadiṭṭhahatvā

sīlabbataṃ vanamupaic‍ca vasaṃ piyāya,

tāyaṃ pasayha gahitāya’pi kāsirañ‍ñā

sīlabbisaudhanaparau pajahittha rausaṃ| () (cūlabaudhicariyaṃ)

16.

 yau bhiṃsarūpi mahisau’pi valimukhassa

āguṃ titikkhamakhīlaṃ parisuddhasilau,

rukkhaṭṭhayakkhavacanāni paṭikkhipitvā

taṃ sīlabhaṅgabhayatau bhayatau mumauca| ()

17.

 yau vuyhamānamapanīya nadīpavāhā

mittadduhiṃ putasajīvitadānahaitu,

rañ‍ñā mumauca vadhiyaṃ avikaupanaina

sīlassa rūruhariṇau’pi harissavaṇṇau, () (rūrumigarājacariyaṃ|)

18. 

 yau dantakaṭṭhasakalaihi jaṭākulaihi

kuddhaina kuṭajaṭilaina katāhisāpau,

mātaṅganāmamuni sīladhanaṃ jugaupa

sampātasāparipumiddhibalaina rakkhaṃ| () (mātaṅgacariyaṃ|)

19.

 maggāvatiṇṇamadhamaṃ kalhābhīlāsā-

saṅghaṭṭitaubhayarathaṅgamadhammayakkhaṃ,

kaupagginā naparijhāpayamiddhimā yau

sīlaṃ rarakkha khalu dhammikayakkharājā| () (dhammādhamma daivaputtacariyaṃ|)

20.

 yau paurisādavasagassa jayaddisassa

rañ‍ñau paṭiñ‍ñamadhikic‍ca vijivitāsau,

khīttāyudhau tadupagamma alīnasattau

yakkhaṃ damaisi nanu sīlavataṃ nidānā| () (alīnasattacariyaṃ|)

21. 

 yau saṅkhapālabhujagau nijabhaugapūra-

vyābhaṅgibhārataravāhiti bhaujaputtai,

kāruñ‍ñamāpa abhigantumapādatāya

sīlassa bhaṅgabhayatau’pi hutāsataijau| () (saṅkhapālacariyaṃ| iti sīlapāramiṃ|)

22.

 saṅkhāradhammakhaṇabhaṅgasabhāvadassi

ussāvabinduvilayaṃ’va yudhañ‍jayau yau,

rājā janassa rudatau pavihāya raj‍jaṃ

naikkhammapāramimapurayi pabbajitvā| () (yudhañ‍jaya cariyaṃ|)

23.

 yau saumanassavisutau kururājaputtau

dussīlakuṭajaṭilabbacanaṃ paṭic‍ca,

rañ‍ñā niyaujitavadhau vadhakāvakāsaṃ

laddhānusāsiya’bhinikkhami cattaraj‍jau| () (saumanassa cariyaṃ|)

24. 

 yau kāsirājatanujau’pi ayaugharākhyau

īhaṃ bhatau ciramayaugharavāsahīrū,

raj‍jaṃ pahāya paramaṃ pitarā padattaṃ

naikkhammapāramiparau vanamautarittha| () (ayaugharacariyaṃ|)

25.

 yau pañ‍cakāmaguṇadīpanatau’padiṭṭha-

sambhattamittavacanampi paṭikkhipitvā,

niddhantakañ‍cananibhacchavi kañ‍canākhyau

patvā tapauvanamapabbaji bandhavaihi| () (bhisacariyaṃ|)

26. 

 pakkhittadaddulanahārurivā’nalamhi

saṅkhāradhammavisayai paṭivaṭṭitattau,

yau sauṇabhusurasudhī vibhavaṃ pahāya

pabbaj‍jituṃ saparisau pavanaṃ jagāma| () (sauṇapaṇḍitacariyaṃ| iti naikkhamma pārami|)

27.

 yau saiṇakau sudhi pasibbakagabbhasāyiṃ

vippassi mauhakalusikatamānasassa,

sappaṃ sughauramupadassiya dīghadassī

pañ‍ñāsupāramimapūrayi bhurimaidhau| () (saiṇakapaṇḍita cariyaṃ|)

28.

 yau yaṃ mahausadhasamākhyasudhī sudhīsau

ummaggasaṃvutanisaggavatisamau’pi,

ummaggatau’va sabalaṃ mithilādhināthaṃ

pañ‍ñāpajāpatipati riputau mumauca| () (mahausadhacariyaṃ iti pañ‍ñā pāramī|)

29. 

 vālainu’ḷāravīriyau vīriyaina ghauraṃ

saṃsāradukkhamiva yau kisakālakau’pi,

gambhīrasāgarajalaṃ sapajānukampī

ussiñ‍cituṃ satatamārahi sattasārau| () (kālaka cariyaṃ)

30.

 rājāmahādijanakau janakundacandau

gambhīrabhurisalilaṃ salilākaraṃ yau,

sūrau’rubāhuvīriyau vīriyaṃ tatāra

saṃsārasindhutaraṇai taraṇīsarūpau| () (mahājanakacariyaṃ| iti vīriyapārami|)

31.

 yau khantitintahadayau yatikhantivādī

chaidāpitai’pi sakalaṃ sakalattabhāvai,

samapūtakhantijalamaivabhūsaṃ sisaica

vyāpādapāvakapadittakalāburājai| () (khantivādī cariyaṃ)

32. 

 yau dhammapālanaparau susu dhammapālau

kārāpitai’pi vadhakaiha’simālakammaṃ,

āsan‍natāpanirayamhi patāparājai

khantiṃ pavattayi manappitakhantimaittau| () (dhammapāla cariyaṃ| iti khantipāramī|)

33.

 yau antarīpagabhayaṃkarasuṃsumāra-

muddhāsamappitapadau kapirājabhūtau,

din‍naṃ paṭiñ‍ñamanukubbamanañ‍ñalabbhaṃ

naj‍jā papāta paratīramasac‍cabhīrū| () (kapirāja cariyaṃ)

34. 

 yau sac‍capāramiparau vasi pac‍canāmau

sac‍caina sac‍camahīsandhiya sac‍cadassī,

pauriṃ samaggakaraṇiṃ sirijambudīpai

sampālayaṃ sakalalaukamavauca vācaṃ| () (sac‍casavbhaya cariyaṃ|)

35.

 yau vaṭṭapautakadijau avirūḷhapakkha-

pādau’tikhuddakakulāvakagabbhasāyī,

sac‍caina sauḷasakarisamitappadaisai

dāvagginibbutimakā thiramāyugantaṃ| () (vaṭṭapautaka cariyaṃ|)

36.

 yau gijjhakākabakabāṇakabhakkhabhuta-

bandhu nidāgharavitāpaparikkhayā’pai,

rittai saramhi parimaucayi maccharājā

sac‍caina’kālajaladāgamapac‍cayaina| () (maccharāja cariyaṃ|)

37. 

 duṭṭhāhidaṭṭhavisavaigavimucchitaṃ yau

maṇḍabbatāpasavarau’rasayañ‍ñadattaṃ,

katvāna sac‍cakiriyaṃ karuṇāya kaṇha-

dīpāyanau muni mumauca tamāpadamhā| () (kaṇhādīpāyana cariyaṃ|)

38.

 yau paurisādavasagau sutasaumarājā

raj‍jai tiyaujanasatai sakajīvitai’pi,

sac‍caṃ rarakkha nanu sac‍caparau nirāsau

din‍naṃ paṭissavamubhin‍namapānukubbaṃ| () (sutasaumacariyaṃ| iti sac‍capāramī|)

39.

 nibbin‍naraj‍javibhavau bhavabhīrūtāya

yau mūgapakkhabadhirākati mūgapakkhau,

nītai nikhātumapaki sīvathikāvakāsai

daḷhaṃ adhiṭṭhitavataṃ vata naujahāsi| () (taimiyacariyaṃ| iti adhiṭṭhāna pāramī|)

40. 

 maittavihāraparamau piturandhayaṭṭhi

yau saumasaummayadayau’pi suvaṇṇasāmau,

vāḷaihi samparivutau pavanai vihāri

maittākhyapāramimapūrayi pāpabhīrū| () (suvaṇṇasāmacariyaṃ|)

41.

 yau aikarājavisutau bhuvi kāsirājā

maittākalattadutiyau sakajīvitai’pi,

avachin‍naraj‍javibhāvai paṭipakkharājai

maittāya samphari samaṃ paribhāvitāya| () (aikarājacariyaṃ| iti maittā pāramī|)

42. 

 yā laumaihaṃsavisutau’pi tulāsarikkhau

mānāvamānanakaraisu sukhai ca dukkha,

vāsaṃ cavaṭṭhiparikiṇṇasusānamajjhai

vairaṃ avairamanupaic‍ca rarakkhu’paikkhaṃ| () (laumahaṃsacariyaṃ| iti upaikkhāka pakāramiṃ)

43.

 sau baudhiyā’bhiniyatau paripak‍kañāṇau

buddhaṅkurau’pacitapāramitābalaina,

nijjhāmataṇha’sitakañ‍jakkhuppipāsa-

laukantaraurunirayaisu na jātu jātau| ()

44.

 nālattha paṇḍakanapuṃsakamūgapakkha-

chac‍candhajātibadhiritthijaḷattabhāvaṃ,

sau makkhikāmakasakutthakipil‍likādi-

jātyā napātubhavi kīṭapaṭaṅgajātyā| ()

45. 

 nālattha gandhagajatau puthulatta bhāvaṃ

nālattha vaṭṭasusukā sukhumanta bhāvaṃ,

nālattha umdanamammanakatta bhāvaṃ

nālattha aivamatihīnataratta bhāvaṃ|

46.

 nāhausi mātuvadhakau pitughātakau vā

nāhausi saṅghabhidurau arahantaghātau,

nāhausi duṭṭhahadayaina tathāgatassa

nāhausi saṃjananakau rudhirassa kāyai| ()

47.

 kammaṃ phakhalaṃ tadubhayaṃ paṭibāhinau yai

ucchaidadiṭṭhigatikā vihariṃsu taisaṃ,

laddhiṃ kadāci naparāmasi saddahānau

kammaṃ phalaṃ niyatabaudhiparāyaṇau sau| ()

48. 

 yasmiṃ bhavai bhavati nāmacatuk‍kamattaṃ

tatrā’pi puñ‍ñakaraṇattha masañ‍ñasattai,

aṭṭhānatau napaṭisandhimagaṇhi suddhā-

vāsaisu pañ‍casu kadāci papañ‍cabhīrū| ()

49.

 buddhaṅkurau niyatabaudhipadau kadāci

dīghāyukaisu’pibhavaisu sukhānapaikhau,

katvā’dhimuttivacanaṃ idha jīvalaukai

nibbatti sau tidasapāramipūraṇatthaṃ| ()

50.

 ic‍caivaṃ sau purisanisabhau duppavaisassa baudhi-

pāsādassā’vataraṇasamattiṃsatissaiṇirūpaṃ,

nipphādaintau parahitaratau pāramīdhammajātaṃ

saṃsārai saṃsari cirataraṃ ghauradukkhaṃ titikkhaṃ| ()

iti maidhānandābhidhānaina yatinā viracitai sakalakavijana hadayānanda dānanidānai jinavaṃsadīpai durainidānai pāramidhammābhisaṅkharaṇa pavatti paridīpau catutthau saggau|

1. 

 atthavattapadaṃ nānāvaṇṇamaṇṇavajānvitaṃ,

patthyāvattamivāhausi jaituttarapuraṃ purai| () (silaisa bandhanaṃ|)

2.

 parikhāmaikhalādāma baddhapākārasauṇinī,

rarāja rājadhānī sā vadhūva patimaṇḍitā| ()

3. 

 maṇisiṅgaṃsumālābhi bālaṃsumālivibbhamaṃ,

sasaṅkamaṇḍalaṃ tasmiṃ palamhaisā’bhīsārikā| ()

4.

 indirāmandirā’mandamaṇimandirasālinī,

haimaddhajāvali tasmiṃ kiḷāpayi kalāpinau| ()

5.

 rarāja nāgarājānaṃ kappitābharaṇaihi ca,

dāṭhāhi dānadhārāhi maighavacchantā’va sā purī| ()

6.

 turaṅganikarauṇḍutadhulidhūsaritambaraṃ,

nivāritātapaṃraṅgavitānassirimāhari| ()

7. 

 nīlasaivāladhammil‍lā samphul‍lakamalā’nanā,

tahimuppalalaula’kkhī haṃsapīnapayaudharā| ()

8.

 kiñ‍jakkharājirasānāruddharaudhanitambinī,

bhiṅgālimaṇimañ‍jirā nārī’vā’suṃ saraujinī| () (silaisa bandhanaṃ|)

9.

 kaivakalaṃ kapparukkhaihi vinā sā rājadhānya’hu,

visāṇārājadhānī’va sabbasampattisālinī| ()

10.

 kadāci purisājañ‍ñau rājā’hausi purai tahiṃ,

vaissantarau’tināmaina vissutau bhuvanattayai| ()

11. 

 kumārau’va samānau sau dānakīḷāparāyaṇau,

kāyūpagāni dhātīnaṃ ratanābharaṇāni’pi| ()

12.

 khaṇḍākhaṇḍaṃ karitvāna navakkhantuṃ kaparic‍caji,

aivaṃ bāhiravatthūnidadantau aṭṭhavassikau| ()

13.

 pāsādamabhirīhitvā saunisaj‍jā’bhiyācanaṃ,

dassāmī’ti vicintaisi sisakkhimaṃsalauhitaṃ| ()

14.

 sukhaidhitau mahāsattau suk‍kapakkhai’va candimā,

pālaisi dasadhammaina patvā raj‍jasiriṃ pajaṃ| ()

15.

 nisaj‍jau paripāsādai sau rājā aikadā rahau,

kāmānaṃ saṅkilaisañ‍ca vaukārādīnavaṃ sari| ()

16. 

 pabbaj‍jāhiratau rājā nibbintau vibhavaibhavai,

sampattisāramādāya hitvā raj‍jasiriṃ varaṃ| ()

17.

 mattamātaṅgarājā’va aggipaj‍jalakānanā,

rudatau ñātisaṅghassa agārasmā’bhi nikkhamī| ()

18.

 campakāsaukavakulatarusaṇḍasumaṇḍitaṃ,

sikhaṇḍimaṇḍalākhaṇḍakīḷaṃ kaukilakūjanaṃ| ()

19.

 anaikamigapakkhīnamāsayaṃ salilāsayaṃ,

vīkāsakusumāmaudappavāsitasamīraṇaṃ| ()

20.

 madhumattā’liṅdhaṅkāranibbhara’mburuhākaraṃ,

sampātanijjharā’rāvagambhīrabhuribhūdharaṃ| ()

21. 

 pavavaikakkhamaṃ vaṅkapabbhāraṃ girigabbharaṃ,

duppavaisapakathaṃ vaṅkagirināmatapauvanaṃ| ()

22.

 patvā laddhai’sipabbaj‍jā kavilāsau sau mahībhujau,

carantau brahmacariyaṃ cirassaṃ vītināmayi| ()

23.

 tassarañ‍ñau mahaisipi maddīnāma sukhaidhitā,

puttadhītūbhisaddhiṃ taṃ tapauvanamupāvisi| ()

24.

 mahicchau pūjakauvippau tadā bīgacchadassanau,

yaina vaissantarausattasārau tainupasaṅkami| ()

25. 

 atthau kammakaraihī’ti jarājaj‍jaritassa mai,

puttañ‍cadhītaraṃ yācī dhīraṃ patvā dayāparaṃ| ()

26.

 ubhau kaṇhājinaṃ jāliṃ sasainahabhārabhājanaṃ,

sammāsambaudhikāmau sau taṇhādāsabyamuttiyā| ()

27.

 dakkhiṇaudakasamputajūjaka’ñ‍jalibhājanai,

samappayittha bandhitvā agamā’dāya niddayau| ()

28.

 dānādhimuttīvīmaṃsī vippākappainu’pāgatau,

saṃyāci daivarājā’tha maddidaiviṃ patibbataṃ| ()

29.

 dakkhiṇaudakaniddhautahatthau sau dakkhiṇaudakaṃ,

katvā daivaisavippassa daiviṃ daivau paric‍caji| ()

30. 

 sattakkhattuṃ pakampittha tassa pāramitaijasā,

sādhusādhūti pattānumaudantī’va mahīvadhū| ()

31.

 ic‍caivaṃ purisārañ‍ñau paripācinapāramī,

maṇiraṃsismuj‍jauta pāsādasatalaṅkatai| ()

32.

 mandamandānilauṇḍūta pañ‍cavaṇṇaddhajālīnaṃ,

maṇikiṃkiṇijālānurāvasautarasāyanai| ()

33.

 dibbaihi nac‍cagītaihi vāditaihi manauramai,

kandappamaṇḍapākāra raṅgamaṇḍapamaṇḍitai| ()

34.

 dibbantadibbarājūnaṃ indacāpasataihi’va,

cūḷāmaṇīmarīcihī sambādhīkaḷita’mbarai| ()

35. 

 accharāhi kucañ‍candanamitaṅgīhi dūratau,

vidhūtacandikārāji cārucāmara mārutai| ()

36.

 suttappabuddhapausau’va tusitai tidasālayai,

tatau cavitvā nibbatti hutvā santusita’vhayau| ()

37.

 dibbaisu pañ‍cakāmaisu vasantau tusitālayai,

pañ‍cindriyāni laukaikalaucanau paricārayi| ()

38.

 tadā dasasahassaisu cak‍kavāḷaisu daivatā,

aikattha san‍nipatitā sutvā buddhahaḷāhaḷaṃ| ()

39.

 tainau’pasaṅkamitvāna yainā’si purisuttamau,

katvā tabbadanambhaujaṃ nayanālikulālayaṃ| ()

40. 

 cūḷāmaṇimayukhambutiddhautacaraṇāsanai,

baddhañ‍jalipuṭambhaujamakulāti samappayuṃ| ()

41.

 cak‍kavattipadaṃ sak‍kamārabrahmapadatayā,

nakhau mārisa patthaitvā pāramī paripācitā| ()

42.

 vainaiyyabandhubhutaina sammāsambaudhimicchatā,

tayā mārisa kicchaina pūritā dasapāramī| ()

43.

 sadaivakassa laukassa hitāya mātukucchiyaṃ,

uppaj‍jatūti yāciṃsu taṃdhīraṃ karuṇāparaṃ| ()

44.

 satavassāyuhaiṭṭhāpi uddhaṃ sahasahassatau,

yasmā akālau buddhānaṃ tasmā kālaṃ vipassi sau| ()

45. 

 yasmā añ‍ñaisu dīpaisu sambuddhā naupapaj‍jarai,

jāyanti jambudīpasmiṃ tasmā dīpaṃ vipassi sau| ()

46.

 yasmā milakkhadaisaisu nūppaj‍janti tathāgatā,

jāyarai majjhimai daisai tasmādaisaṃ vipassi sau| ()

47.

 yasmā najāyarai vaissasuddatvayaisu jāyarai,

khantiyai brāhmaṇai buddhā kulaṃ tasmā vipassi sau| ()

48.

 yasmā anañ‍ñavisayā kucchi sambuddhamātuyā,

tasmā āyuparicchaidavasaina passi mātaraṃ| ()

49.

 laukaikalaucanau aivaṃ katvā pañ‍cāvalaukanaṃ,

tāsaṃ paṭiñ‍ñaṃ pādāsi karuṇāpuṇṇamānasau| ()

50. 

 tappādatāmarasacumbita mauḷimālā

sampattadaivaparisā’ntaradhāyi tāva,

augayha nandanavanaṃ tusitādhirājā

tamhā cavī matidayādayināsabhāyau| ()

iti maidhānandābhidhānainayatinā viracitai sakalakavijana hadayānandadānatidānai jinavaṃsadīpai durainidānai vaissantaracariyappabhutidaivārādhanā pavatti paridīpau pañ‍camau saggau|

jinavaṃsadīpai dūrainidāna bhāgau paṭhamau|

1. 

 yā sattasārapabhavā sirijambudīpai

phītā’marāvatipurī’va purīvataṃsā,

ādic‍cavaṃsikanarindapavaiṇi bhūmi

lakkhyālayā kapilavatthupurī purai’si| ()

2.

 vaṇṇaihi kaṇṇasukhasaddarasaihi jāta-

rūpaihi atthavisaraihi yatīhi yuttā,

yā rājadhāni puthupāṇagaṇappadaihi

āsī (vasantatilakā) racanā yathaiva| () (silaisa bandhanaṃ|)

3. 

 pācīdisābhujalatāya mahīyuvatyā

san‍naddhasaṅkhavalayassirimāvahantaṃ,

yassā sudhāsuparikammakataṃ rarāja

pākāracak‍kamacalaṃ makuṭānukāraṃ| ()

4.

 yassā rarāja parikhā nagarindirāya

kiñ‍jakkhiñ‍jaritatīradasābhirāmā,

saṃsibbitālivittīmaṇitantupantī

pākārasauṇibharatau galitambaraṃ’va| ()

5.

 kandappadappamadirāmadamattadhuttā

mandānilairitasunīlalatāvitānaṃ,

yasmiṃ vilaulanayanañ‍jalipiyamānaṃ

āpānabhuminibhamaupavanaṃbhajiṃsu| ()

6. 

 mattaṅganāya navayaubbanagabbitāya

raṅgālayaggamaṇidappaṇabimbitaihi,

yā rājadhāni ghanapīnapayaudharaihi

saummānanaihi bhaji mānasavāpisaubhaṃ| ()

7.

 saṅkappitābharaṇaraṃsi satairatālī

dāṭhābalākavisarā madavuṭṭhidhārā,

yasmiṃ purai gharamayūrakulaṃ akālai

kīḷāpayiṃsu varavāraṇavārivāhā| ()

8.

 pākāracak‍kabahiniggata muggarāgaṃ

yasmiṃ turaṅganikaruddhaṭadhūlijālaṃ,

racchāvatārajanatāya khaṇaṃ janaisi

bhītiṃ padittapalayānalajālasaubhaṃ| ()

9. 

 yasmiṃ vimānamaṇisiṅgajutippabandha-

sañ‍cumbitaṃ jitaraviṃ hariṇḍakabimbaṃ,

nārījanānanasaraujakatāvamānaṃ

kaudhābhibhutamiva vaṇṇavikāramāpa| ()

10.

 yatthissaraihi samadhiggahitāni tuṅga-

kailāsakuṭadhavalāti manauharāti,

nic‍caṃ sudhāsuparikammakatāni cā’suṃ

lakkhīnikaitananibhāni nikaitanāni| ()

11.

 yatthindanilamaṇimāpitamaṇḍapaggai

haimaddhajāvaliparibbhamaṇaṃka puramhi,

jīmūtakūṭapamukhai satavij‍jumālā-

līlāvahaṃ virahinīnamaghaṃ janaisi| ()

12. 

 sañjhānurāgamaṇitauraṇadīdhitīhi

bhin‍nandhakāranikarā’khilanāgarānaṃ,

yā rājadhāni janayantipi tuṅgatuṭṭhiṃ

pītyā navāsi rajanīsma bhisārikānaṃ| ()

13.

 saivālakaisarasamākulatīrabhāgā

samphul‍larattapadumuppalakal‍lahārā,

haṃsālisārasasamausaraṇābhirāmā

yasmiṃ sunimmalajalā kamalākarāsuṃ| ()

14.

 yasmiṃpurai kulavadhūvadanambujānaṃ

laddhuṃ nirūpamasiriṃ bhusamussahantā,

yāvaj‍ja saitasarasiruhasītaraṃsī

añ‍ñauñ‍ñabaddhapaṭighā’va vajanti nāsaṃ| ()

15. 

 yasmiṃ suvaṇṇamakaṇirūpiyavaṃsavaṇṇa-

muttāpavāḷacajiraihi mahārahaihi,

nānāpaṇā sukhumakāsikasāṇahaṅga-

kausaiyyakhaumavasanaihi’bhavuṃ papuṇṇā| ()

16.

 cak‍kāsisattidhanukuntagadādibhatthā

san‍naddhahaimakavacā vijitārivaggā,

saṅgāmasāgarasamuttaraṇātisurā

yaudhā yahiṃ puravarai akariṃsu rakkhaṃ| ()

17.

 phūṭṭhā ka vāṭanikaṭai maṇimandirānaṃ

kappāsapaṭṭadhavalā saradabbharājī,

yasmiṃ khaṇaṃ javanikāsirimādadhānā

thīnaṃ jugaupa madhupaihi mukhambujāni| ()

18. 

 nibbiddhavīthivisaraihi susaj‍jitaihi

nic‍cussavāya puramussitataurasaihi,

bhaugindabhauganikaraihi rasātalaṃ’va

yaṃ sampasāritaphaṇaihi babhuva rammaṃ| ()

19.

 yasmiṃpurai vivaṭamandirajālakānaṃ

uddhūtadhulimalinīkaḷitālakānaṃ,

nārinamindurucīrānana dappaṇaisu

laukassa laucanamaṇi paṭibimbitā’suṃ| ()

20.

 gambhīrasaṅkhapaṭabhaddhanibhūrighausaṃ

kailāsakūṭadhavalālayaphaiṇapiṇḍaṃ,

yaṃ puṇṇasattaratanaṃ purakhīrasindhuṃ

lakkhī alaṅkari turaṅgataraṅgavaigaṃ| ()

21. 

 raiṇuppabandhamalinaṃ kavanarājinīlaṃ

madhavātimattamadhupaṃ padumābhirāmaṃ,

yaṃ rājahaṃsabhajitaṃ avatiṇṇalaukaṃ

āsi puraṃ vikacakañ‍javanaṃ yathaiva| () (silaisabandhanaṃ|)

22.

 yasmiṃpuramhi ratanuj‍jalanīlakaṇṭhā

rāgāvamadditadharā’vivaṭadvijāli,

āsuṃpayaudharabharā’viraḷappadaisā

sampattavuṭṭhidivasāviyamātugāmā| () (silaisabandhanaṃ|)

23. 

 dhammānudhammapaṭipatti parāyaṇassa

saṃsārabhīrukajanassa tapauvanābhā,

yā rājadhāni pacurandhaputhuj‍janānaṃ

āpānabhumiva babhūvu’bhayappakārā| ()

24.

 buddhaṅkurassa ravivaṃsapabhaṅkarassa

sammā sukhānubhāvanāya subhumibhutā,

bhau yādisi kapilavatthupuri purai’si

dhammassabhāvamadhunā paridīpayai sā| ()

25.

 tasmiṃ babhūva nagarai nagarādhirājai

rājā sunīticaturau catusaṅgahaihi,

dhammaina sabbajanarañ‍janakau kadāci

suddhaudanavhavisutau ravivaṃsakaitu| ()

26. 

 disvā’vatārakalusikatamattabhāvaṃ

uk‍kaṇṭhitai’va kamalā kamalāpatissa,

bhūpālipālibhajitaṃ caraṇāravindaṃ

saṃsaivi yassa ravivaṃsadhajassa rañ‍ñau| ()

27.

 yassā’vanīsakavinau kavikaṇṭhabhusā

vāṇivadhū madhurakaumakalakattavāṇī,

patvā catummukhamukhambujakānanamhā

haṃsīva mānasataḷākamalaṅkarittha| ()

28.

 sabbāridappamathanaupari aikadhatvā

ajjhattikāriparājiyamappasayhaṃ,

saṃsuddhacittanikasai nisitaina tāva

pañ‍ñāyudhaina avadhittha mahībhujau yau| ()

29. 

 yasmā mahīpatimahīdharatau upaikkhā-

vaiḷātalāvadhi dayāsalilaina puṇṇā,

maittāsavanti pabhavā muditumimālā

ajjhauttharittha bhuvanattayagaṃjanaughaṃ| ()

30.

 sampan‍nadānasadanambudharaihi yassa

dānādhimuttiparamassa manaudahaisu,

taṇhātaṭāni kapaṇaddhikayācakānaṃ

hin‍nāni sattaratanambunipātanaina| ()

31.

 yassindanīlanayanaṃ rajatāvadāta-

dantaṃ suvaṇṇavadanañ‍ca pavāḷasīsaṃ,

muttāmayaṅgavayavaṃ ratanaihi nānā

daihaṃ sumāpitamivāsi pitāmahaina| () (sīlaisabandhanaṃ)

32. 

 yassātipaṇḍarayasauvisarau’sadhīsau

saṅaik‍kācitānanasarau’rinarādhipānaṃ,

saukandhakārabhidurau ripurājinīnaṃ

āsāgarantapathaviṃ paridhikarittha| ()

33.

 rājañ‍ñachappadakulaṃ sakalaṃ padaisa-

raj‍jādhipac‍camakarandarasābhilāsaṃ,

yassattabhāvakamalākaraphul‍litāni

saṃsaivi cārucaraṇambūruhāni bhatyā| ()

34. 

 saitātapattamiva vissutakittipuñ‍jaṃ

katvā’sipattamiva pāvakabhīmataijaṃ,

yasmiṃ saraj‍jamanusāsati saisabhūpā

chattāsibhūsitakarā sakakiṅkarā’va| ()

35.

 dvārāni’naikakapaṇḍikayācakānaṃ

ugghāṭitaupya’virataṃ ratanālayaisu,

saddhādisattadhanarakkhaṇatapparau saṃ

dvārattayaṃ pidahi yau kapilādhināthau| ()

36.

 yassussitaddhanuaiṇau pabalārivaggaṃ

vissaṭṭhābāṇavisarabbisamubbahantau,

bhasmikari karikarāyatapīnabāhu-

sappau suphauṭhitajiyāpariphandajivhau| ()

37. 

 lakkhīnidhānanagaraṇṇavapātubhūtau

mantindakūṭasikharīvalayāvutau yau,

vālaggamattampi rājasiṇairurājā

kaudhānilaina ripurañ‍ñamakampiyau’si| ()

38.

 bhasmikatākhīlavipakkhanarindakaṭṭhau

kaudhānalau sarasamīraṇabhāvitaupi,

nibbāyi paggharitabappajalaihi yassa

laulabbilaucanaghaṭaihi vipakkhathīnaṃ| ()

39.

 san‍nītimaggajalituggamatippadīpau

kittippabandhadhavalīkatajivalaukau,

rājindamauḷimaṇilaṅkatapādapīṭhau

dhammaina raj‍jamanusāsi ciraṃ sarājā| ()

40. 

 tassatipīvarapayaudharabhaddakumbha-

dvandātibhāraviraḷīkatamajjhabhāgā,

niddausabālaravimaṇḍalacārugaṇḍā

dibbaccharājitavirājitarūpasaubhā| ()

41.

 samphul‍lanīlakamalāmalanīlanaittā

aulambamānamaṇikuṇḍalalambakaṇṇā,

muttāvalivadasanāvali haṃsadhainu-

hailāpahāsagamanā muducāruvāṇī| ()

42.

 bimbādharā jaladharāyatakaisapāsā

sauvaṇṇadappaṇanibhānanacandabimbā,

san‍nīrapupphamakuḷaupamacārujaṅghā

kandappa maṅgalasilātalasauṇibhāgā| ()

43. 

 nābhālavāḷaruhanīlatamālaval‍lī-

līlāvinaddhanavakaumalaraumarājī,

lāvaṇṇavāridhitaraṅgabhujā’bhinīla-

subbhulatāmakarakaitanacāparūpā| ()

44.

 bhupālavaṃsakamalākararājabhaṃsi

māyāvadhu iva sujampatinau sujātā,

candassakaumudi’va vij‍juriva’mbudassa

rañ‍ñau’ticārucaritāsi piyā mahaisi| ()

45. 

 tasmiṃ nagaupamagharai nagarai tadāsi

āsāḷhimaṅgalamahau divasānisatta,

milāsugandhaparamaṃ vigatāsavaṃ taṃ

nakkhattakīḷamakarittha mahaisi māyā| ()

46.

 vuṭṭhāya sattamadināgatapuṇṇamāya

pātau sugandhaparivāsitavārinā sā,

katvā sinānamatulaṃ kapaṇḍikānaṃ

dānaṃ adāsi catulakkhadhanabbayaina| ()

47.

 vatthāhataihi sunivatthasupārutā sā

bhutvā’ggabhaujanamadhiṭṭhitupausatha’ṅgā,

niddāturā supinamauvarakaṃ pavissa

kalyāṇamaddasa sirīsayanai nipan‍nā| ()

48. 

 naitvā nipan‍nasayanaṃ himavantapassai

haiṭṭhā visālatarasāḷamahīruhassa,

naṃ saṭṭhiyaujanakacārumanausilāyaṃ

āraupayiṃsu caturau kira daivarājā| ()

49.

 naitvā manussamalasaṃharaṇāya tamhā’

nautattanāmarahadaṃ sunahāpayitvā,

daivitthiyau sapadi dibbamayaihi naisaṃ

vatthaihi gandhakusumaihi alaṅkaritvā| ()

50.

 tatthubbhavau lasati rūpiyapakabbatau yau

tassaudarai’tirucirai kanakabbimānai,

pācīnasīsavati dibbamayamhi sammā

pañ‍ñāpitaggasayanamhi sayāpayiṃsu| ()

51.

 auruyha saitavaravāraṇarājavaisau

buddhaṅkurau rucirakañ‍canapabbatamhā,

āruyha sajjhudharaṇidharamuttarāya

sauṇḍāya saitasarasiruhamubbahantau| ()

52. 

 patvā vimānavathakuñ‍canadaṃ naditvā

katvā padakkhiṇamalaṅkatamātusaiyyaṃ,

bhaitvāna tāyapana dakkhiṇapassamantau

kucchiṃ paviṭṭhasadisau supinaina diṭṭhau| ()

53.

 māyāya rājavadhuyā rucirānanāya

āsāḷhipuṇṇamiyamuttara’sāḷhabhaina,

buddhaṅkurassa paṭhamaina mahāvipāka-

cittaina sampati ahū paṭisandhigabbhai| ()

54.

 buddhaṅkurassa paṭisandhigatassa gabbhai

māyāya cārucaritāya ca khaggahatthā,

nissaisupaddavanirākaraṇāya rakkhaṃ

gaṇhiṃsu tāva caturau surarājaputtā| ()

55. 

 māyāya bhattuparamāya tatauppabhuti

nūppaj‍ji kiñ‍ci purisaisu sarāgacittaṃ,

sā pañ‍cakāmasukhinī akilantakāyā

lābhainuḷārayasasāpyabhivaḍḍhitāsi| ()

56.

 pañ‍ñāyi dhautaratanai janikāya antau

kucchiṃ gatau yatharivāvutapaṇḍusuttaṃ,

taṃ kucchinā pariharī dasamāsamattaṃ

pattaina tailamiva rājini appamattā| ()

57.

 pātau’va pāṭipadagai divasai pabuddhā

rañ‍ñau kathaisi supinaṃ atha sau narindau,

vaidaṅgavaidacaturai catusaṭṭhamattai

pak‍kausayī dvijavarai dvijavaṃsakaitū| ()

58. 

 lājuttarāya paribhaṇḍakatāya bhumyā

pañ‍ñāpitaisu sukhumattharaṇatthataisu,

bhaddāsanaisu bhavanamhinisin‍nakānaṃ

naimittikānamavanīpati bhusurānaṃ| ()

59.

 pakkhittasappimadhusakkhirakhīramissa-

pāyāsapuṇṇaharirūpiyabhājanaihi,

vatthāhatāni dhanadhañ‍ñacayañ‍ca dhainū

datvāna diṭṭhasupinassa phakhalaṃ apucchi| ()

60.

 mācintayittha tava rājiniyā janinda

kucchimhi tampati patiṭṭhahi puttagabbhau,

ajjhāvasissati sañ‍caipana cak‍kavatti

rājā bhavissati agāramasaṃsayaṃ sau| ()

61. 

 hitvā sasattaratanaṃ catudīparaj‍jaṃ

sau pabbajissati sacai bhavanā’bhigantvā,

buddhau bhavissati dhuvaṃ catusac‍cabuddhau

ic‍cabruviṃsu supinatthavidū vidū tai| ()

62.

 sā gabbhabhāravaṭharikatamajjhabhāgā

gantuṃ sakaṃ kulagharaṃ kulakañ‍jahaṃsi,

icchāmahanti paṭivaidayi daivi rañ‍ñau

sau sampaṭicchi vacanaṃ karavīkavāṇyā| ()

63.

 tamhā mahānagaratau nagaraṅgapuṇṇaṃ

sau yāva daivadahanāmikarājadhānī,

muttā’vadātapuḷinattharaṇaihi rājā

lājaupahāravidhinā kamaluppalaihi| ()

64. 

 santīrapupphakalasaihi samappitaihi

mandātilairitapaṭāka dhajāvalīhi,

kārāpayī kanakarūpiyatauraṇaihi

addhānamaggasamalaṅkaraṇaṃ’takhippaṃ| ()

65.

 vandī’bhigītathutimaṅgalagītikāhi

pañ‍caṅgikaihi turiyaihi katupahāraṃ,

tasmiṃ sumaṇḍitapasādhitamañ‍jasamhi

dibbavcarāsiriviḍambanarūpasaubhaṃ| ()

66.

 daiviṃ suvaṇṇasivikāya susaj‍jitāya

āraupayitva khacitāya maṇīhi nānā,

paisaisi bhupati purakkhatañātisaṅghaṃ

saddhiṃ sahassasacivaihi sukhaidhitaṃ sau| ()

67. 

 samthul‍lapupphaphalapal‍lavavattabhāra-

rukkhākulaṃ ghanasunīlalatāvitānaṃ,

hintālatālanaḷakīcakanāḷikaira-

san‍nīrapūgatiṇapādapapantisāliṃ| ()

68.

 saivālanīlasalilānilasītalaihi

autiṇṇakahaṃsavisaraihi samul‍lasattaṃ,

jhaṅkārarāvamukharālikulākarāla-

kiñ‍jakkhajālabharitamburuhākaraihi| ()

69.

 pupphābhigandhasurabhīkatagandhavāhaṃ

addakkhisabbajanalaucanapīyamānaṃ,

nindantanandanavanaṃ vanajāyatakkhī

sā lumbinīvanamanaṅgavimānabhūtiṃ| ()

70. 

 sā rājinī navadalaṅgulipantīcāru-

sākhābhujaupahitamañ‍jaricāmaraihi,

san‍naddhakaumalalatāvanitānamaggai

attupahārakaraṇāya katāvakāsā| ()

71.

 saināya cārucaraṇamburuhauddhaṭaihi

raiṇūhi dhūsaritamaggamanak‍kamanti,

saddhiṃ sakāya parisāya tatautaritvā

taṃ lumbinīvanamupāvisi rāmaṇaiyyaṃ|()

72.

 taṃ rājiniṃ vanavadhu jitahaṃsagāmiṃ

āmaudamandamalayānilahatthagaihi,

sambhāvayittha mukharālikulābhikiṇṇa-

raiṇuppabandhaharisaṅkhasataihi maggai| ()

73. 

 gacchantiyā caraṇanūpuranādapāsa-

baddhānamun‍natasiraumigapautakānaṃ,

ummīlitāyatavilaucanapantipakkhai

dassaisi nīlanalinīvanarājisaubhaṃ| ()

74.

 uddhaṃ samaggasikharaihi katāvakāsa-

maggantaraihi kalikākusumākulaihi,

nānālatākulamahiruhatauraṇaihi

uyyānabhumi upahāraratai’va bhuya| ()

75.

 ukkhittapiñchabharamantasikhaṇḍimālā-

kīḷāhi kaukilakulaṅanikāhaḷaihi,

uyyānabhumi makaraṅajaraṅgabhumi-

līlaṃ bhajittha bhamaraddhanival‍lakīhi| ()

76. 

 nic‍caṃ vasantasamayassirimubbahantaṃ

taṃkhauvanaṃ vanavadhūhadayānutāpī,

pattau nidāghasamayaupi janaisi tuṭṭhiṃ

tassā sirisakusumālikulāvataṃsau| ()

77.

 tasmiṃ nidāghasūriyātapatāpitāmbhaṃ

rittālavāḷamivakālamakālamaighau,

cintāturaṃ hadayamattasakhījanaupi

pīṇaisi gabbhaparipākabharaṃ vāhantyā| ()

78.

 kaṭṭhāvasiṭṭhataravau parihīnapattā

tassādharakkhidasanaj‍jutisaṅgamaina,

āsuṃ navaṅkurapalāsavikāsapuppha-

saṃvail‍litā’va ramaṇīyavanappa daisai| ()

79. 

 gimhābhītāpaparipīḷitaṅdhal‍likānaṃ

gambhīrarāvamukharīkatadāyarāji,

dukkhāturabbirahinīpamadājanassa

āsi vilāpabadhirīkaḷitai’va sāḷā| ()

80.

 tasmiṃ vikāsakalikāvalihārihārā

kiñ‍cāpi pak‍kaphakhalaval‍larikaṇṭhabhusā,

nāsakkhi pūgatarupanti sumaṇḍitāya

māyāya tāya sirimābharituṃ ghaṭantī| ()

81.

 uyyānamubbhamitamattamadhubbataihi

campaiyyapupphamakulaihi samākulaṃtaṃ,

uddhutadhumapaṭalaihi manaubhavassa

dīpaihi vāsabhavakanaṃ’va lasantamāsi| ()

82. 

 gabbhūpagaṃ bhamarakaisakalāpabhārā

buddhaṅkuraṃ pariṇataṅkuralaumahaṃsā,

vandantiyau viya tahiṃ thabakañ‍jalīhi

mandānilairitalatāvanitā kanatā’suṃ| ()

83.

 gabbhūpagassa paripak‍kaphalaihi nānā

puñ‍ñānubhāvapabhavautusamubbhavaihi,

māyāya gabbhabalikammani tapparai’va

uyyānabhumi janataṃ bhavi tappayanti| ()

84.

 gabbhūpagassa hi mahāpurisassa gabbha-

vuṭṭhānamaṅgalamahussavavāsaramhi,

uyyānabhumi sakalautusamubbhavaihi

āsi vikāsakusumaihi samābhikiṇṇā| ()

85. 

 sālumbinīvanasiriṃ kalahaṃsaghausaṃ

samaphul‍lapupphasurabhiṃphalasambhavaujaṃ,

pañ‍cindriyaihi girinijjharasitavātaṃ

pac‍cakkhapañ‍cavidhakāmarasaṃavindi| ()

86.

 niyyāsasārasurahiṃphakhalapal‍lavaihi

jhaṃkāritālikulakujitakaukilaihi,

samphul‍lapupphanikaraihi samābhikiṇṇa

maddakkhisāyuvatimaṅgalasāḷasālaṃ| ()

87.

 samphul‍lasāḷakalikaṃtayatālimālā

sañ‍cumbitaṃkuvalayāmalalaucanāya,

sākhaṃsukaumalakaraṅgulipal‍lavaihi

māyāmahaisisamalaṅkari vitamāyā| ()

88. 

 bhāraunatā’va ruciraṅgulipal‍lavānaṃ

jhaṅkārarāvamukharālikulābhirāmā,

sākhā vikāsakusumaihisamākulā sā

aulambayaṭṭhi bhavi gabbhabharāturāya| ()

89.

 tassā calittha pavanau calalaucanāya

kammubbhavau varatiraukaraṇaihi tāva,

daiviṃ nirūpamasiriṃ suparikkhipitvā

tamhā paṭik‍kami janau kaḷitāvakāsau| ()

90.

 brahmāmarāsuranarauragapūjanīyaṃ

battiṃsalakkhaṇasamuj‍jalarūpa sāraṃ

niddhautajātimaṇisan‍nibhasuddhagattaṃ

sattuttamaṃ sapadi daivi ṭhitā vijāyi| ()

91. 

 duggandhamuttamalasauṇitamakkhitaṅgā

jāyantya’saisamanujā manujaisunai’vaṃ,

caṅgauṭakamhi jinadhāturivādhivāsau

thūpamhi sauṇṇapaṭimāriva mātugabbhaṃ| ()

92.

 nissaiṇitauva purisau ratanāsanamhā

thairauva dhammakathikau ṭhitakau’tarantau,

sammā pasāriya ubhau mudupāṇipādai

sau nikkhamittha kuṇapaihi amakkhitaṅgau| ()

93. 

 tatraupagamma caturau caturānanā taṃ

jālaina kañ‍canamayaina visuddhacittā,

ādāya mātupuratau tanayaṃ ṭhapaitvā

candānanai bhavatu nandamanā’tya’vaucuṃ| ()

94.

 ādic‍cavaṃsakamalākarabhākarassa

buddhaṅkurassa subhasītalavāridhārā,

nikkhamma tāva nabhasā nijamātuyā ca

gāhāpayuṃ utumubhausu kalaibaraisu| ()

95.

 taisaṃ karaihi caturau surarājaputtā

gaṇhiṃsu saṇhasukhumāya’jiṇappavaiṇyā,

taisañhi pāṇitalatau paṇipātapubbaṃ

gaṇhiṃsu taṃ dukulacumbaṭakaina’mac‍cā| ()

96. 

 taisaṃ karaihi pathavitalamautaritvā

ṭhatvā puratthimadisaṃ asamau vipassi,

uddhaṃ adhau catudisānudisā ca aivaṃ

aikaṅganaṃ bhavitadā’khilalaukadhātu| ()

97.

 tumhaihi uttaritarau bhuvasai tīsu

natthīti matthakajaṭāmakuṭappitaihi,

katvānijañ‍jalipuṭaihi nipac‍cakāraṃ

brahmāmarāsuranarā tamabhitthaviṃsu| ()

98.

 sauca’ttanā samamadisva disāsu tāsū

tappādavītiharaṇaina padānisatta,

gantvāna uttaradisā’bhimukhau avanyā

abbhuggatamburuhamuddhani tiṭṭhamānau| ()

99. 

 aggau’hamasmi ahamasmi janassa jaiṭṭhau

saiṭṭhau’hamasmi ayamantima’jāti mayhaṃ,

dhīrau mamaitarahi natthi punabbhavau’ti

nicchāritāsabhivacau nadi sīhakanādaṃ| ()

100.

 vaisākhaimāsai suhakujadinai puṇṇamāyaṃ visākhai

nakkhattaiyaugai suragurugatai sau kuḷīravharāsiṃ,

sañ‍jātau nāthau paramakaruṇābhāvanābhāvatattau

māyākucchimhā kusumitalatāvailalituyyānabhumyā| ()

iti maidhānandābhidhānaina yatinā viracitai sakala kavijana hadayānandadānanidānai jinavaṃsadīpai avidurainidānai pacchimabhavika mahābaudhi sattuppatti pavattiparidīpau chaṭṭhausaggau|

1. 

 atharammatarā’si jātikhaitta-

pariyāpatta’vakāsalaukadhātu,

kamaluppala (mālabhārinī) hi

tadupaṭṭhānagatāhi daivatāhi| ()

2.

 kamalāsanadaivadānavānaṃ

bhuvanai’kattha samāgamau tadā’si,

jinacak‍kapaṭiggahassa ṭhānaṃ

avivādaina sadaivamānusānaṃ| ()

3.

 paṭilābhanimittamādisantī

vata sabbañ‍ñutañāṇasampadāya,

dasasaṅkhasahassilaukadhātu

abhikampī pahaṭai’va kaṃsapātī| ()

4. 

 jananussavavāsaramhi tasmiṃ

nijadaihaj‍jutipiñ‍jarau’dapādi,

dasasaṅkhasahassacak‍kavāḷa-

kuharālaukakarau mahāvabhāsau| ()

5.

 apatāḷitacammanaddhabhairī-

vikatīnaṃ sayamaiva vaj‍janampi,

tadanuttaradhammadaisanāya

bhavi ṭhānaṃ anusāvaṇassa laukai| ()

6.

 ghaṇakāhaḷavaṃsasaṅkhavīṇā-

bharaṇānaṃ sayamaiva vaj‍janampī,

anupubbavihārabhāvanānaṃ

paṭilābhāya nibandhanaṃ babhūva| ()

7. 

 parimuttivarattapāsakārā-

ghara,yausaṅkhalikādibandhanaihi,

migapakkhinarānamasmimāna-

vigamassā’si nidānamādibhutaṃ| ()

8.

 bhuvanaisu mahājanassa raugā-

pagamainā’disanaṃ ahausukhanti,

caturāriyasac‍cadassanaina

bhavi ṭhānaṃ catusac‍cadaisanāya| ()

9.

 vividhabbhutarūpagaucarānaṃ

bhuvi jac‍candhajanassalaucanānaṃ,

pabhavau pabhavau’si dibbacakkhu

paṭilābhāya tilaukalaucanassa| ()

10. 

 thutagītisudhārasassa pānaṃ

badhirānaṃ savaṇañ‍jalīpuṭaihi,

atimānusadibbasautadhātu-

paṭivaidhāya nidānamāsi tassa| ()

11.

 bhuvi jātijaḷādipuggalānaṃ

tadahai’nussatiyā supātubhāvau,

bhavi pubbamupaṭṭhitassatissa

satipaṭṭhānanibaudhanāya ṭhānaṃ| ()

12.

 visikhācaraṇaṃ saraujacāru-

padaviñ‍ñāsavasaina paṅgulānaṃ,

purimaṃ caturiddhipādavaiga-

paṭilābhāya nimittamāsi laukai| ()

13.

 madhuraina saraina jātimūgā

thutigītānya’vadiṃsu vandinau’va,

bhuvi khuj‍jajanau’jugattalābhau

kuṭilattā’pagamāya ṭhānamāsi| ()

14. 

 saraṇaṃ purisāsabhau siyānau

bhavatau duggatitau vimuttiyā’ti,

karinau’pi kariṃsu kuñ‍canādaṃ

turagā haisamakaṃsu pītiyai’va| ()

15.

 visadā paṭisambhidā catassau

paṭivijjhissati cā’yatiṃ sacā’yaṃ,

sakapaṭṭanamaiva tan‍nidānā

taraṇī sīghamupāgamuṃ vidaisā| ()

16.

 sayamaiva viraucanaṃ tadāni

ratanānaṃ bhuvanākarubbhavānaṃ,

ravivaṃsaravissa dhammaraṃsī

visarassu’j‍jalanāya ṭhānamāsī| ()

17. 

 turiyāni sakaṃsakaṃ ninādaṃ

akaruṃ tagguṇadīpakānivā’tra,

vivaṭā vidisādisā sakitti-

visaraukāsakatai’va’hippasattā| ()

18.

 sakalassa kilaisapāvakassa

parinibbānasabhāvadīpanaina,

nirayaisu hutāsajālamālā

tadahai nibbutimāpa jātikhaittai| ()

19.

 parisāsu visāradassa tassa

catuvaisāradañāṇalābhahaitu,

bhuvanaisu tadāmahānadīnaṃ

anabhissandanamāsi kun‍nadīnaṃ| ()

20.

 udapādi pabhā nirākaritvā-

bilalaukantariyaisu andhakāraṃ,

hatamauhatama’gga maggañāṇa-

j‍jutilābhāya nibandhanaṃ tamāsi| ()

21. 

 suvimuttirasau siyā’va tassa

caturāsitasahassadhammakhandhau,

madhuraṃ caturaudadhīnamāsi

salilaṃ santataraṃ taraṅgarittaṃ| ()

22.

 vidisāsu catuddisāsu caṇḍa-

pavanassā’pi avāyanaṃ tadāni,

bhavi pubbanimittamattanau’pi

bhaṭadiṭṭhyābhavadaṭṭhibhaidanāya| ()

23.

 navapal‍lavapattasaikharānaṃ

viṭapīnaṃ kusumāhikiṇṇabhāvau,

bhavi pubbanibandhanaṃ vimutti

kusumaihā’tumadaihabhūsaṇāya| ()

24. 

 kumudākarabaudhakassa canda-

kiraṇassā’tiviraucanaṃ tadāsi,

satibuddhasudhākarau’dayamhi

janasandauhamanaupasādahaitu| ()

25.

 vimalattamanuṇhatā nidāgha-

sūriyassū’pasamau nimittamaggaṃ,

bhavi caitasikassa kāyikassa

paṭilābhāya sukhassa tamhijātai| ()

26.

 gaganā’ganagāditau’taritvā

pathavisaṅkamaṇaṃ tadā khagānaṃ,

saraṇāgamanassa ṭhānamāsi

jinadhammaṃ sunisamma saj‍janānaṃ| ()

27.

 nabhasā’bhipavassanaṃ tadāni

catudīpaisu akālavāridānaṃ,

parisāsu akhaṇḍadhammavuṭṭhi-

patanassā’si nibandhanaṃ jinamhā| ()

28. 

 chaṇamaṅgalakīḷaṇaṃ tadāni

tidasānampi sakaisakai vimānai,

upagamma tahiṃtahiṃ udāna

samudānassanidānamā’si baudhiṃ| ()

29.

 vivaṭā sayamaiva mandirānaṃ

pihitañ‍cārakavāṭavātapānā,

bhavadukkhaniraudhagāmimagga-

paṭilābhāya nimittamāhariṃsu| ()

30.

 tadahai madhurāmisassa paittī-

visayaisvāharaṇaṃ khudāturānaṃ,

bhavi kāyagatāsatāmatassa

paṭilābhāya nimittamattanau’pi| ()

31. 

 divasai jananussavai pipāsā-

vigamau dīnajanassa paitalaukai,

sukhitattassa upaic‍cabuddhabhāvaṃ| ()

32.

 paṭipakkhajanassa maittilābhau

tadahai vāyasavāyasārinampi,

bhaviṭhānamanantasattalauka-

visayabrahmavihārabhāvanāya| ()

33.

 satimaggaphalubbhavai yathaiva

bhavabhītyāpagamau tathāgatānaṃ,

sati jātamahāmahai bhayaṃ vā

natiracchānagatānamāsi tāsau| ()

34.

 piyabhāvupasaṅkamau pajānaṃ

hadayānandakarāya khau girāya,

jinadhammakathāya sāvakānaṃ

viya sāmaggirasassa pātubhāvau| ()

35. 

 sitakittilatāya raupitāya

bhavatau’smiṃ bhuvanālavāḷagabbhai,

viya nimmitayantavāridhārā

jaladhārā dharaṇītaluṭṭhahiṃsu| ()

36.

 bhamarāvalibhārapañ‍cavaṇṇa-

kamalacchan‍namahītalaṃ rarāja,

jalajaṃ thalajaṃ parāgahāraṃ

bhuvi sabbattha apupphi pupphajātaṃ, ()

37.

 viṭapīsu latāsu khandhasākhā-

satapattāni tadā supupphitāni,

naravīra’bhirūpadassanāya

bhūsamummīlitalaucanāni’vāsuṃ| ()

38. 

 uparūpari sattasatta hutvā

satapattāni sīlātalubbhavāni,

tava abbhudayau sudul‍labhauti

kathayantivi’ha kapātubhāvatau nau| ()

39.

 nijapāramitālatāya katti-

latayā’laṅkatapupphahāsarūpā,

samalaṅkari yāvatā bhavaggaṃ

dhajamālā jananussavai tilaukaṃ| ()

40.

 avakuj‍jasarauruhābhirāmaṃ

nabhavambhaujavanassiriṃ babandha,

bhuvi paukkharavassamīdisanti

vadamānaṃva pavassi dhammavassaṃ| ()

41.

 ramaṇī ramaṇīyarūpasaubhā

acaruṃ dhammamanaṅgaraṅgabhūmi,

madhupā madhupānamandirāni

tadahai nāvasariṃsu dhammakāmā| () (abyapaita paṭhamadutiya pādādiyamaikaṃ)

42. 

 kamalā kamalālayā vivaisa

bhuvanaṃ bhūrinavāvatārahārī,

dharaṇī dharaṇīdharāvataṃsā

upahārātibharāturaiva kampi| () (abyapaita paṭhamadutiya pādādiyamakaṃ)

43.

 ruciraṃ ruciraṅganā tadāni

akaruṃ kīḷamanaikacandikāsu,

suvīraṃ suciraṃsikiṇṇatārā-

nikarau’bhāsatarau’si bhākarau’va| () (abyapaita paṭhamadutiya pādādiyamakaṃ)

44.

 pavanau’pavanau’pavāyamānau

pavinaudaisi parissamaṃ janassa,

vanadā vanadāhavupasantiṃ

akaruṃ sabbadhi sassasampadañ‍ca| () (abyapaita paṭhamadutiya pādādiyamakaṃ)

45. 

 visadā visadā sakittirāmā

mukharaṅgālaya māpakauvidānaṃ,

sujanā’sujanā bhajiṃsu tassa

caraṇānyaṅkitacak‍kalakkhaṇāni| () (abyapaita paṭhamadutiya pādādiyamakaṃ)

46.

 vasudhaṃ vasudhampatī samaggā

dasadhammaina’nusāsayuṃ tadāni,

hadayaṃ yadayaṅgamāya vāṇyā

asataṃ mittaduhī vidhānayiṃsu| () (abyapaita paṭhamadutiya pādādiyamakaṃ)

47.

 timadā’timadā gajādhipāpi

migarājūhi tadā samācariṃsu,

pabalā’pabalā migā tadañ‍ñai

paṭisatthāramakaṃsu añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ| () (abyapaita paṭhamadutiya pādādiyamakaṃ)

48. 

 bhuvanai bhuvanaikalaucanassa

jananasmiṃ divasai samuj‍jalānī,

nihanitā’nihitāyudhāti bhitiṃ

janayuṃ pāpimatauva naitaraisaṃ| () (abyapaita paṭhamadutiya pādādiyamakaṃ)

49.

 parivāditadibbabhairivīṇā-

turiyaṃ dassitadibbanac‍cabhaidaṃ,

gaganaṃ suraraṅgamaṇḍalābhaṃ

tidasānaṃ upahārasāramāsi| ()

50. 

 sauvaṇṇavaṇṇavadhuyā garugabbhagasmiṃ

taṃnandanabbanasamānavanaṅgatasmiṃ,

bhutabbhutanvitamahai nayanañ‍janasmiṃ

jātamhi tamhi tanujai bhavi bhaddabhatti| () (mālābandhanaṃ)

iti maidhānandābhidhānaina yatinā viracitai sakalakavijana hadayānandananidānai jinavaṃsadīpai avidūrainidānai vividhapubbanimittapātubhāvappavatti paridipau| sattamausaggau|

1. 

 visuddha (vaṃsaṭṭha) masaisabandhavau

kumāramādāya tilaukalaucanaṃ,

agaṃsu tamhā kapilavhayaṃpuraṃ

purīvataṃsaṃ samalaṅkatañ‍jasaṃ| ()

2.

 tadā abhiñ‍ñāsu vasisu pāragau

samādhivikkhambhitasabbakibbisau,

vihāsi suddhaudanabhumibhattunau

kulūpagau daivalanāmatāpasau| ()

3. 

 tapaudhanau sau pavivaikakāmavā

divāvihāratthamupāgatau divaṃ,

tamatthamañ‍ñātumapucchi daivatā-

pavattitaṃ passiyamaṅgalussavaṃ| ()

4.

 tavaivupaṭṭhāyakabhumibhattunau

varaurasau māriya māravāhiniṃ,

parājayaṃ lacchati baudhimāyatiṃ

mahussavau tampati vattatai, bravuṃ| ()

5.

 imāya vuttantakathāya cauditau

tapaudhanau iddhibalaina iddhimā,

surālayai antaradhāna’nantaraṃ

nikaitanai pāturahausi rājinau| ()

6. 

 kumāranijjhānamanaurathauka vasi

tahiṃ supañ‍ñattamahārahakāsanai,

nisaj‍ja suddhaudanarājinau’bruvi

tavatrajaṃ daṭṭhumidhāgatautya’haṃ| ()

7.

 narindacūḷāmaṇicumbitānya’tha

padāni vandāpayitu tapassinau,

vibhusaṇālaṅkatamattasambhavaṃ

narādhipau rājakumāramāhari| ()

8.

 tadattabhāvaina’hivādanāraha-

ssa’bhāvatau tassa rarāja sūnunau,

pavaṭṭayitvā jaṭilassa sampati

jaṭāsu cak‍kaṅkitapādapaṅkajaṃ| ()

9.

 kumāramādāya samappitañ‍jaliṃ

vidhāya pādaisvanisammakārinau,

sacai ṭhapaiyyuṃ jaṭilassa sattadhā

phalaiyya muddhā jaṭitaujaṭāya’pi| ()

10.

 sakāsanuṭṭhāya athai’sibhūmiyā

nihac‍ca sau dakkhiṇajānumaṇḍalaṃ,

akā mahākāruṇikassa gāravaṃ

sirauvirūḷhañ‍jali pupphamañ‍jarī| ()

11.

 udikkhamānau vasinā samappitaṃ

tamañ‍jaliṃ bhattibharaina bhūpati,

athau’namaitvā tanumīsakaṃ sakaṃ

pavandi pādamburuhāni sununau| ()

12.

 siyā’va buddhau purisāsabhau ayaṃ

nadassanaṃ tassa siyā mamantī sau,

nirūpamaṃ rūpasiriṃ samaikkhiya

payāsi niṭṭhaṃ upadhārayaṃ vasi| ()

13. 

 vavatthayitvaivamuḷārabuddhimā

vitiṇṇakaṅkhau hasamīsakaṃ rudaṃ,

narindamauraudhapurakkhataṃ vasī

tadavasīdāpayi saṃsayaṇṇavai| ()

14.
