 tamāharitvāna pakavattimabbhutaṃ

purakkhatau’raudhajanassa rājinau,

yatissarau saṃsayasal‍lamuddharaṃ

sabandhavai’nussari kāladaivalau|()

15.

 sakaikulai nāḷakanāmadārakau

sayambhunau lacchati dassanaṃ iti,

tamatthamañ‍ñā samupaic‍ca taṃkulaṃ

tvamāha pabbaj‍jiti bhāginaiyyakaṃ| ()

16. 

 nirāmayaṃ pañ‍camavāsaramhi sau

varaurasaṃ vāsitagandhavārinā,

pavattamānai bhavanai mahāmahai

nahāpayi bhūpati bandhumajjhagau| ()

17.

 pasatthamanvatthabhidhāna mattanau

kumāramāraupayituṃ parausataṃ,

savaidavaidaṅgapabhaidakauvidai

dvijai nimantāpayi sau narādhipau| ()

18.

 surindarūpau suramandiraupamaṃ

tamindirādhāranarindamandiraṃ,

janindasīhau’pagatā’vanīsurai

amandapūjāvidhinā’bhirādhayi| ()

19.

 anainasiddhā samatiṃsapāramī-

sadatthasampattiparatthakārinā,

tathā’ttha saṅkhātadhanaṃ nidhānagaṃ

imamhi siddhaṃ sahajātiyā yatau| (5)

20. 

 dvijaihi tantibbacanadvayārahaṃ

samāsasaṃkhittapadatthasaṃhitaṃ,

sutassa siddhattha’bhidhānamattanau

tatau’bhivauhārasukhāya kārayi| ()

21.

 suyāmarāmaddhajamantilakkhaṇa-

subhaujakauṇḍañ‍ñasudattasañ‍ñinau,

imai dvijā rājasupūjitā tadā

vicakkhaṇālakkhaṇapāṭhakā’bhavaṃ| ()

22.

 subhāsubhaṃ passiya daihalakkhaṇaṃ

vibhāvayavhau iti taisamabruvi,

samukkhipitvā’ṅgulipal‍lavadvayaṃ

janādhipaṃ sattajanā’bravuṃ dvidhā| ()

23. 

 sacai mahārāja agāramāvasai

varaurasau tai paricāritindriyau,

samaṅgibhutau ratanaihi sattahi

bhavaiyya rājā vatacakkavatya’yaṃ| ()

24.

 mahādayau’yaṃ karuṇāya cauditau

gharā’bhigantvā yadi pabbajissati,

bhavaiyya buddhau’khilañaiyyamaṇḍalaṃ

sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya nahai’tthasaṃsayau| ()

25.

 kaṇiṭṭhabhūtau vayasā tadantarai

pasatthasatthā’vagamaina jaiṭṭhakau,

samukkhipitvā’ṅgulimaikamabruvi

itīha kauṇḍañ‍ñasamañ‍ñabhusurau| ()

26. 

 imassa vainaiyyajanākatañ‍jalī

samaṃ phusantāni vasundharātalaṃ,

subhāni bhaupādatalāti sabbadā

bhajanti bhatyā kamalānivālinau| ()

27.

 imassa khaimantamasaisapāṇinau

tipaṭṭakantārapathāvatāraṇai,

susaj‍jitaṃ cak‍kayugaṃva pāramī

rathamhi pādaṅkitacak‍kalakkhaṇaṃ| ()

28.

 imassa bhau kambalabhaiṇḍukaupamaṃ

sumaṭṭavaṭṭāyatapaṇhimaṇḍalaṃ,

sadā padambhaujanivāsalakkhiyā

tanauti pīnatthanapiṇḍavibbhamaṃ| ()

29. 

 imassa dīghaṅgulipanti vaṭṭita-

manausilālattakavattikaupamā,

vibhāti bhau bāhulatāya pāramī-

latāya vā nūtanapattapantiva| ()

30.

 imassa pāraivaṭapāda pāṭalā

sapāṇipādā taḷuṇātikaumalā,

uḷārapūjāvidhisampaṭicchanai

pavāḷapāti’va samul‍lasanti bhau| ()

31.

 imassarūpassirimandiraudarai

samappamāṇābhinavaṅgulīhi bhau,

padissarai jālakavāṭasantibhā

sapāṇipādā tatajālalakkhaṇā ()

32. 

 imassa ussaṅkhapadassa gaupphakā

padambujānaṃ caturaṅgulaupari,

patiṭṭhitā puṇṇaghaṭaisukandharā-

vilāsamāliṅgiya rājabhāsarai| ()

33.

 imassa daihaj‍jutivāripūrita-

sarīrakaidārabhavā phalatthinau,

surattasālyaudarasan‍nibhā subhā

duvaiṇijaṅghā abhipīṇayantī bhau| ()

34.

 imassa bhau jānuyugaṃ parāmasaṃ

anaunamantau ṭhitakau mahābhujau,

sahattanā sambhavabaudhisākhinau

visālasākhāya vilāsamādisai| ()

35.

 imassa kausauhitavatthaguyhakau

abhin‍nakaukāsakakausakausagau,

anañ‍ñasādhāraṇatādinā’yatiṃ

anaṅgasaṅgāmaparammukhau siyā| ()

36. 

 imassa bhau gautamagauttakaitunau

kalaivarai kañ‍canasattibhattacau,

suvaṇṇavaṇṇau jinacīvarassapi

tanauti saubhaṃ ghanabuddharaṃsinau| ()

37.

 imassa jambaunadīpiñ‍jarāya bhau

sirisapupphassukumāracāriyā

rajaunumattaṃ chaviyā nalimpatai

saraujapattairiva vāribindavau| ()

38.

 imassa laumāni kalaivarai varai

visuṃ visuṃ kūpagatāni sūnunau,

vilumparai rūpavilāsalakkhiyā

manauramaṃ bhau kamaṇikañ‍cukassiriṃ| ()

39. 

 imassa uddhaggamukhaṃ mukhassiriṃ

padakkhiṇāvatta mapaikkhinau yathā,

tiraukarai raumavitāna mindirā-

nikaitanindīvarakānanassiriṃ| ()

40.

 imassa bhaurāja saraujayauninau

yathaujugattaṃ ujugattamāyatiṃ,

anañ‍ñasāmañ‍ñaguṇāvakāsatau

pajābhipūjāvidhibhājanaṃ siyā| ()

41.

 imassa sattussadalakkhaṇaṃ subhaṃ

samaṃsamaddiṭṭhasirāvaliṃ sadā,

dadhāti bhau pāramidhammasipakpinau

sudhantacāmīkarapiṇḍavibbhamaṃ| ()

42. 

 imassa puṇṇaubhayakāyabhāgimaṃ

migindapubbaddhasarīralakkhaṇaṃ,

kudiṭṭhivādībhasirauvidāraṇai

narindasāmatthiyamubbahai nakiṃ| ()

43.

 imassu’paitau ghanamaṃsavaṭṭiyā

citantaraṃsau muducārupiṭṭhiyaṃ,

bhavaṇṇavā kañ‍canapac‍carisiraṃ

tanauti sattuttaraṇai narādhipa| ()

44.

 imassa nigraudhamahīruhassiva

samappamāṇau parimaṇḍalaupya’yaṃ,

kadāci dukkhātapakhin‍nadaihinaṃ

parissamaṃ bhau pajahai bhavañ‍jasai| ()

45. 

 imassa rājittayarañ‍jitau’ttariṃ

karīyamānu’ttamadhammanissanau,

sumaṭṭavaṭṭau samavattakhandhakau

mutiṅgakhandhauriva rāja rājatai| () (silaisa bandhanaṃ)

46.

 imassa bhau sattasahassasammitā

yathā’matajjhauharaṇābhilāsinau,

rasaggasā santi rasādanummukhā

rasaggasaggī’tya’bhidhīyatai tatau| ()

47.

 imassa’nubyañ‍janatārakākulai

anantarūpāyatanambarau darai,

viraucataibārasamīsasiriva

narindasīhassahanūpamāhanū| ()

48. 

 imassa tāḷisatidantapanti bhau

pahūtajivhārathikaṃ carantiyā,

manuñ‍ñavāṇivanitāya tatvatai

pasatthamuttāvalilīlamāyatiṃ| ()

49.

 imassa javhāvarakaṇṇikāvahai

mukhambujai sac‍casugandhavāsitai,

samappamāṇā dasanāvalī subhā

vibhāti kiñ‍jakkhatatīva bhūpatī| ()

50.

 imassa bhau khaṇḍitasaṅkhapaṇḍarā

dvijāvalī nibbivarantarāyatiṃ,

samubbhavāyuttilatāya tāyati

mukhālavāḷai mukulāvalissiriṃ| ()

51. 

 imassa pīṇānanacandacandikā

susuk‍kadāṭhāvali sac‍cavādinau,

padissatai dhammataḷākakīḷanai

katābhilāsārivahaṃsamālinī| ()

52.

 imassa cānuttaradhammadaisanā-

taraṇyamālaulalakārarūpinī,

pahūtajivhā bhavasāgarā’yatiṃ

narinda pāraṃ janatā’vatārayai| ()

53.

 imassa bhau brahmasaraupamau sadā

sahassadhā’yaṃ karavīkarāvatau,

manauharaṭṭhaṅgasamaṅgisussarau

sasautakānaṃ maṇikuṇḍalāyatai| ()

54. 

 imassa nīlaṃ nayanuppalañ‍cayaṃ

nirūpamai rūpavilāsamandirai,

niraupitaṃ bhau maṇisīhapañ‍jara-

dvayaṃva bhāsai kusalaina kainaci| ()

55.

 imassa pāṭhīnayugaṃ’va dissatai

visiṭṭharūpāyatanāpagāsayaṃ,

subhaṃ gavacchāpavilaucanaupamaṃ

maṇippabhaṃ gaupakhumadvayaṃ sadā| ()

56.

 imassā uṇṇā bhamukantarubbhavā

ḷāṭamajjhaupagatā viraucati,

yadatthi sañajhāghanarājimajjhagaṃ

sasaṅkahīnaṃ sasimaṇḍalaṃ tathā ()

57. 

 imassa uṇhīsakasīsalakkhaṇaṃ

sadhammaraj‍jissariyaṃ anāgatai,

kariyamānassa hi cak‍kavattinau

dadhāti uṇhīsakasisavibbhamaṃ| ()

58.

 imassa bhumissara supakpatiṭṭhita-

padaṅkitai cak‍kayugamhi dissarai,

arāsahassāni ca naiminābhiyau

tivaṭṭaraikhā sirivacchakādayau| ()

59.

 imaihi battiṃsatilakkhaṇaihi bhau

asītyanubyañ‍janalakkhaṇaihi’pi,

samuj‍jalantau purisāsabhautyayaṃ

bhavaiyya buddhau bhavabandhanacchidau| ()

60. 

 sasautamāpāthagatāya tāvadai

dvijassa vitthārakathāya cauditau,

apucchi rājā kimayaṃ samaikkhiya

anāgatai brāhmaṇa pabbajissati| ()

61.

 kadāci uyyānagatau mahāpathai

jarārujāmac‍cuvirūpadassanaṃ,

vidhāya nibbintamanau bhavattayai

tapaudhanaṃ passiya pabbajissati| ()

62.

 itiha vatvāna sakaṃsakaṃ gharaṃ

tatau’pagantvā’ddhanimittapāṭhakā,

mahal‍lakā’dāni mayanti sunavau

tamānupabbaj‍jitumauvadiṃsu tai| ()

63. 

 dvijaisu vuddhaisu mataisu sattasu

ayaṃhi kauṇḍañ‍ñasamavhayau sudhī,

mahāpadhānaṃ purisāsabhau’dhunā

karauti sutvā karuṇāya cauditau| ()

64.

 samānaladdhihi kulaisu taisu hi

catuhi vippaihi saha’ntapañ‍camau,

athau’ruvailaṃ upagamma pabbaji

bhaviṃsu taipañ‍ci’dha pañ‍cavaggiyā| ()

65.

 kadāci laddhā pariyantasāgaraṃ

imaṃ catuddīpikaraj‍jamatrajaṃ,

jitārivaggaṃ vicarantamambarai

karaumi pac‍cakkhamahanti cintiya| ()

66.

 nimittarūpakkhipathappavaisanaṃ

nivāraṇatthaṃ tanijarājasūnunā,

narādhipau sau purisaihi sabbathā

disāsu rakkhāvaraṇaṃ akārayi| ()

67. 

 ayaṃ kumārau yadi cak‍kavattivā

bhavaiyya sambaudhipadaṃ labhaiyyavā,

sakaikulai khattiyabandhavaihi sau

purakkhatauyaiva carissataṃ iti| ()

68.

 kumāranāmaṭṭhapanamhi vāsarai

sahassamattaisu kulaisva’sitiyā,

adāsi pac‍caikajanau paṭissavaṃ

padātukāmauva visuṃvisuṃ sutai| ()

69.

 asaisadausāpagatā sukhaidhi tā

suvaṇṇakumbhaurupayaudharau na tā,

anaikadhātī varavaṇṇagabbi tā

sapac‍cupaṭṭhāpayi taṅkhaṇaipi tā| ()

70. 

 taḷākatīramhi taraṅgabhāsurai

yathaiva haṃsyā kalahaṃsapautakaṃ,

mahaisiyā’ṅkai sayanai sitattharai

suvāsarai bhupati puttamaddasa| ()

71.

 adiṭṭhaputtānanapaṅkajā ciraṃ

lahuṃ parikkhīṇavayauguṇā itau,

cutā’va māyājanani nirāmayā

upāvisi sattamavāsarai divaṃ| ()

72.

 mahaisimāyābhaginī tadā mahā-

pajāpatigautamināmarājinī,

nijaṃ kumāraṃ bharaṇāya dhātinaṃ

vidhāya bhāraṃ paṭijaggi taṃ sayaṃ| ()

73. 

 tadā’bhavuṃ dīpasikhā jagantayai

kalaibarau’bhāsalavaina sununau,

vinaṭṭhataijāriva raṅgadīpikā

vimānadipaisu kathāvakā’ttanau| ()

74.

 vicittabhummattharaṇai abhikkhaṇaṃ

sajan‍nukaihā’cari mandiraudarai,

mahāvanasmiṃ maṇivālukātalai

vijambhamānauriva sihapautakau| ()

75.

 sutassa kīḷāpasutassa mandi rai

bhamantabimbaṃ maṇidappaṇauda rai

nibaddhamaddakkhi carantamamba rai

yathaiva cak‍kaṃ ratanaṃ mahībhu jau| ()

76. 

 tadaṅghiviñ‍ñāsavasaina bhumiyā

vajantamaṅkau’panidhāya bhumipau,

tadā’bhinicchārita māsabhiṃ giraṃ

idāni maṃ sāvaya puttamabruvi| ()

77.

 nibaddhamantaumaṇivaidikātalai

mukhaindubimbuddharaṇai payaujayaṃ,

sayaṃ palambhaisi amac‍casūnavau

vayaina mandau’pi amandabuddhimā| ()

78.

 uḷārasaukaṃ pitucittasambhavaṃ

tilaukadīpau nijapuñ‍ñataijasā,

tamaupabandhaṃ bhuvanau’darubbhavaṃ

nirākari bālaravī’va raṃsinā| ()

79.

 vikiṇṇalājākusumākulañ‍jasai

vitānaraṅgaddhajanibbharambarai,

purai tahiṃ maṅgalakic‍casammataṃ

kadāci rañ‍ñau bhavi vappamaṅgalaṃ| ()

80. 

 sugandhamālābharaṇādimaṇḍita-

pasādhitā kāpilavatthavā narā,

sakiṅkarā kammakarā’pi kappitā

tatau tatau sattipatiṃsu taṃ kulaṃ| ()

81.

 mahac‍casaināyapurakkhatauhisau

uḷārarājiddhisamuj‍jalaṃtatau

payāsikammantapadaisamatrajaṃ

kumāramādāyapurindadaupamau| ()

82.

 chaṇamhi tasmiṃ manuvaṃsakaitunau

manauramaṃ maṅgalanaṅgalādikaṃ,

suvaṇṇapaṭṭaihi parikkhaṭaṃka mahā-

janassa’pī rūpiyapaṭṭachāditaṃ| ()

83. 

 visālasākhākulajambusākhinau

vidhāya haiṭṭhā sayanai mahārahai,

nijaṃ kumāraṃ sajanau janādhipau

samārahī sampati vappamaṅgalaṃ| ()

84.

 kumārarakkhāvaraṇāyu’paṭṭhitā

tamussavaṃ dhātijanā vipassituṃ,

apak‍kamitvā bahi sāṇitau khaṇaṃ

pamattarūpā vicariṃsvi’taucitau| ()

85.

 pariggahaitvā’namapāna māsanai

nisaj‍ja pal‍laṅka malattha bandhiya,

jinaṅkurau nīvaraṇaihi nissaṭaṃ

vivaikajaṃ jhānamagādhabuddhimā| ()

86. 

 vipassa puttassu’pavaisanaṃ tahiṃ

dumassa chāyāya nivattataṃ tathā,

pavandi rājā paṭihārikākathā-

pacauditauputtamupaic‍ca taṅkhaṇai| ()

87.

 kalāsu vuj‍jāsu ca puttamattanau

vinaitukāmau vinayakkhamaṃ pitā|

pasatthasatthantarapāradassinaṃ

kadāci vippācariyaṃ kirā’nayi| ()

88.

 samappitaṃ taṃ gurunau karambujai

sadaivalaukassa guruṃ sagāravaṃ,

mahīsurau sau jalabindunā yathā

suduttarāgādhamahaudadhīrasaṃ| ()

89. 

 savaṇṇabhaidaṃ sanighaṇṭukaiṭubhaṃ

athabbabaidaini’nihāsapañ‍camaṃ,

tivaidamuddaisapadaina duddasaṃ

tathā kalāsippataṃ nibaudhayī| ()

90.

 anañ‍ñasādhāraṇapuñ‍ñavāsanā-

vidhūtasammauhavisuddhabuddhinau,

samattavij‍jā sakalākalā dhiyā

kalampi nālaṃ bahubhāsanaina kiṃ| ()

91.

 na kaivalaṃ tassa kalaibaraṃ bahi

vibhāti battiṃsatilakkhaṇaihi bhau,

bhusaṃ tadabbhantaravatthu dippatai

subuddhasatthantaralakkhaṇaihī’pi| ()

92. 

 tilaucanassā’pi tilaukacakkhunau

ayaṃ visaisau nayanaihi dissatai,

lalāṭanaittau purimau nasaubhati

parau’va abbhattarañāṇalaucanau| ()

93.

 anubbajantau navayaubbanassiriṃ

yasaupabandhaina sakai nikaitanai,

pavaḍḍhi dhīrau sakalaṃ kalāntaraṃ

kalānidhī raṃsicayaini’va’mbarai| ()

94.

 upaḍḍhagaṇḍāhitadāṭhikāya sau

yasaudhanau sauḷasavassikau yadā,

kapaulaphuṭṭhañ‍janadānarājiyā

kari yathā bāladasaṃ vyatik‍kami ()

95. 

 tadā narindau suramandirau’pamaṃ

ututtayānucchavikaṃ manauramaṃ,

payaujayitvāna paviṇasippikai

sutāya kārāpayi mandirattayaṃ| ()

96.

 nisitasambādhatalaṃ nivārita-

saraunilaṃ phassitasihapañ‍jaraṃ,

mahivataṃsaṃ navabhumikaṃ gharaṃ

babhuva rammaṃ bhuvi rammanāmikaṃ| ()

97.

 sasikara’mbhaudhararāvanibbhara-

vitāna mugghāṭakavāṭabandhanaṃ,

surammanāmaṃ hataghammamindirā-

nivāsarammaṃ bhavi pañ‍cabhūmikaṃ| ()

98.

 ahiṇhasituṇbhaguṇaihi pāvusai

sukhānulaumaṃ samasattabhumikaṃ,

suphassitā’phassitasihapañ‍jaraṃ

subhaṃ subhaṃ nāma nikaitanaṃ bhavi| ()

99. 

 vayaunupattassa narindasununau

uḷārarājiddhivilāsadassanai,

katā’bhilāsau janakau janādhipau

padātukāmau nijaraj‍jasampadaṃ| ()

100.

 vasanti cai yaubbanahāridārikā

narindasandaisaharaihi paisayi

sa sākiyānaṃ sacivaihi sāsanai| ()

101.

 nivaidayuṃ yaubbanagabbitassa tai

nakiñ‍cisikappāyatana’ntadassinau,

sutassa dārābharaṇāya dhītarau

kathan‍nu dassāma mayanti khattiyā| ()

102. 

 sutaina taṃ rājasutaina cauditau

pitā carāpaisi puramhi bhairiyau,

mama’trajau kāhati sippadīpanaṃ

itauparaṃ sattamavāsarai iti| ()

103.

 varau kumārau hi kumāravik‍kamau

kalāpasan‍naddha kalaibarau tadā,

vipassataṃ bandhujanānamausari

anappadappau raṇakaiḷimaṇḍalaṃ| ()

104.

 dhanuddharau sau paṭhamaṃ sakai bhujai

sahassathāmaṃ sasaraṃ sarāsanaṃ,

vidhāya pauṭhaisi jiyaṃ vasundharā-

vidāraṇākāramahāravaṃ ravi| ()

105. 

 catuddisā’dharadhanuddharā mamaṃ

karauntu lakkhaṃ nijakhāṇapattiyā,

itīha vatvā saravāraṇaina sau

abhutapubbaṃ sarasippamāhari| ()

106.

 catuddisāyaṃ caturau dhanuddharai

mamai’kabāṇaina haṇāma’haṃ iti,

akāsi tadadīpayamañ‍ñathā’bbhutaṃ

sa cak‍kavaidhavhayasippadīpanaṃ| ()

107.

 saraihi vaiṇyā’yatayaṭṭhiraj‍jukaṃ

saraihi cā’rauhaṇamaṇḍapālayaṃ,

saraihi pākārataḷākapaṅkajaṃ

saraihi vassaṃ itisippamāhari| ()

108. 

 mahāsattau laukappabhavamasamaṃ sippajātaṃ janānaṃ

tadā saṃdassaisi muditahadayā sākiyā dārikāyau,

upaṭṭhāpaisuṃ tā suratiratisaṅgāmacaturā

sahassānaṃ tāḷisatiparimitā nāṭikā’suṃ gharaisū| ()

iti maidhānandābhidhānaina yatinā viracitai sakalakavijana hadayānanda dānanidānai jinavaṃsadīpai avidūrainidānai lakkhaṇapaṭiggahaṇa kumārasambharaṇādi pavatti paridipau aṭṭhamau saggau|

1. 

 iti vihitai sati sippadīpanasmiṃ

tadabhimukhai’tarakhattiyā’tisūrā,

apagatamānamadābhaviṃsumañ‍ñau

(taruṇamigindamukhai)va mattadanti| ()

2.

 vijaṭita saṃsayabandhanau padātuṃ

sumariya daivadahamhi supakpabuddhau,

narapavarau nijadhītaraṃ kumāriṃ

suvimala kauliyavaṃsakañ‍jahaṃsiṃ| ()

3.

 tahimathamantivaraihi mantayitvā

nikhilapavattinivaidanāya dūtai,

pahiṇi kavivāhamahai vidhiyatanti

tava tanujaina mamañhi dhītukañ‍ñā| ()

4. 

 valayitatāramupaṭṭhitau’dayaṃ tai

himakarabimbamivau’paviṭṭhapīṭhaṃ,

parivutamantigaṇaṃ paṇamma rājaṃ

kapilapuraupagatā tamatthamāhu|

5.

 atha paṭiladdhapaṭissavainu’daggā

padayugamañ‍jalipupphamañ‍jarīhi,

sumahiya taṃ paṭivaidayiṃsu rañ‍ñau

sakavisayaṃ samupaic‍ca rājadūtā| ()

6.

 ubhayakulamhi mahībhujā’ñ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ

punarapi mantivaraihi mantayitvā,

visadamatīhi nimittapāṭhakaihi

niyamitamaṅgalavāsaramhitamhā| ()

7.

 kanakavitānavinaddhahārihāraṃ

kusumasamākulahaimapuṇṇakumbhaṃ,

tidivavimānasamānamul‍lasan‍na-

ratanavicittavivāhamaṇḍapaggaṃ| ()

8. 

 gahitavitānasitātapattakaitu-

ddhajamaṇivijanicarucāmaraihi,

pacurajanaihi katupahāramaggai

suparivutaṃ catuṅgiranidhajinyā| (6)

9.

 vividhavibhūsaṇabhusitattabhāvaṃ

abhinavapīnapayaudharābhirāmaṃ,

harisivikāya yasaudharaṃ kumāriṃ

maṇikhacitāya vidhāya cā’nayiṃsu| ()

10.

 valayitamālatidāmahaimamālā

parimalabhāvitakuntalappavaiṇi,

viraḷabakāvalimappavij‍jurājiṃ

jaladharamālamajaisi kaumalāya ()

11. 

 niravadhirūpanabhautalamhi tassā

janamanakundavikāsanaṃ babhāsa,

kuṭilatarālakakālamaigharāji-

jaṭitalalāṭataladdhacandabimbaṃ| ()

12.

 migamadakuṅkumagandhapaṅkalittau

kulavadhuyā tilakau lalāṭamajjhai,

makaradhajaina niraupitauri’vā’si

tibhuvanabhutajayāya pupphakaitu| ()

13.

 jananayanañ‍janarūpasampadāya

kulamapadāya rarāja nimmadāya,

paramasiriṃ sarusīruhābhirāmaṃ

vadanamanaṅgasuvaṇṇadappaṇābhaṃ| ()

14. 

 maṇigaṇamaṇḍitakuṇḍalaihi tassā

savaṇyugaṃ ghaṭitāvagaṇḍabhāgaṃ,

manasijasākuṇikaina khittapāsa-

yugalamivakkhivibhaṅgamānamāsi| ()

15.

 suvimalakantipabandhasandanatthaṃ

nayananadīnamubhin‍namantarāḷai,

kanakapaṇāḷisamappitai’va tāya

varavadanāya rarāja tuṅganāsā| ()

16.

 nirupamarūpavilāsamandirasmiṃ

sajavanikāni’va sīhapañ‍jarāni,

ruciravilāsiniyā lasiṃsu pambhā-

valisahitāni sunīlalaucanāni| ()

17. 

 kanakakapālanibhaṃ manaubhavassa

vimalakapaulayugaṃ siniddhakan‍niṃ,

navasasimaṇḍalapuṇḍarīkasaṇḍa-

sasirimavarundhimanauharādharāya| (6)

18.

 sucaritapāramitālatāya tāya

pariṇatarāgalatāya bhūlatāya,

adharayugaṃ taruṇaṅkuradvayaṃ vā

kimiti vitak‍kahatau’yamāsi laukau| ()

19.

 kulavadhuyā vadanā’lavāḷagabbhai

navakalikāvaliphul‍litai’vakiñ‍ci,

sumadhuravāṇilatāya mandahāsa-

j‍jutidhavali dasanavali rarāja| ()

20. 

 kuvalayanīlavilaulalaucanāya

mukhakamalā’likulānukārinībhu,

nayanamayūkhaguṇaiha’pāṅgabhaṅga-

nisitsaraihi anaṅgacāparūpā| ()

21.

 kalaravamañ‍jugirā tivaṭṭarāji

ghanakucabhaddaghaṭāya kambugīvā,

madhuragabhīravirāva raṅgalaikhaṃ

ajini suvaṇṇamutiṅgabhairisaṅkhaṃ| ()

22.

 abhinavapinapayaudharau’padhānaṃ

sukhumataracchavikaujavābhirāmaṃ,

urasayanaṃ samalaṅkataṃ viyā’si

nijapatisaṅgamamaṅgalāya tāya| ()

23. 

 kucakanakā’calasambhavāya nābhi-

kuharataṭābhimukhāya kantinaj‍jā,

chaṭhāravalittayamindiraupamāya

avahari tuṅgataraṅgapantikantiṃ| ()

24.

 maṇirasanāguṇamantharāya tassā

ghanakucabhārakisau kisaudarāya,

harisirivacchasuhaj‍jamajjhabhāgau

madadhanumuṭṭhivilāsamāharittha| ()

25.

 sarasijatantupavaisanāvakāsa

mavahari pīnapayaudharantarāḷaṃ,

nijagaḷabhāsurahāranijjharaihi

kanakadarimukhavibbhamaṃ yuvatyā| ()

26. 

 avikalarūpavilāsasindhuvailā

viralavilagginiyāka visālasauṇi,

parihari rājakumārikāya tāya

kusumasarābhavabhumibhāgasaubhaṃ| ()

27.

 kulavadhuyā kamalāmalānanāya

kuvalayakaumalanilaraumarāji,

bhusamabhicumbi gabhiranābhigabbhaṃ|

kamalavivāyatamattabhiṅgarāji ()

28.

 ruciratarauruyugaṃ suvaṇṇa rambhā-

karikarapīvaramindiraupamāya,

bhaji makaraddhajaraṅgamandirasmiṃ

harimayathambhayugassiriṃ ramāya| ()

29. 

 madarayarūparasadvayaṃ tulāya

suparimitāya catummukhaina tulyaṃ,

nijamihajānuyugaṃ pavāḷapāti-

yugalavivāsi avammukhaupanītaṃ| ()

30.

 visayavitak‍katamākulaṃ yuvatyā

madanupasaṅkamaṇai manauvimānaṃ,

jitamadamattamayūrakaṇṭhabhūti

jalitapadīpasikhai’va cārujaṅghā| ()

31.

 maṇimayanūpurabhāsuraihi tassā

caraṇatalaihi parājitāni thīnaṃ,

mukhapadumāniva saṅkucanti mañ‍ñai

bhamarabharamburuhāni kañ‍janīnaṃ| ()

32.

 karacaraṇaṅgulipal‍la’vaggasālī-

jalalavapantinibhā’tikaumalāya,

abhinavatambanakhāvalī babhūva

makaradhajassa katai’va pupphapūjā| ()

33. 

 saparijanau vanitāya tāya saddhiṃ

maṇigaṇamaṇḍitamaṇḍapappadaisai,

dinakaravaṃsadhajassa rājaputta-

ssupagamanaṃ apalaukayaṃ nisīdi| ()

34.

 parivutabandhujanaihi rājaputtau

yathariva daivagaṇaihi daivarājā,

sapadi turaṅgarathaṃ samāhirūḷhau

tadabhimukhau yasasā jalaṃ payāsi| ()

35.

 tahimupagamma ṭhitassa maṇḍapasmiṃ

paridahitu’ttarasāṭakaina tassa,

harimaṇimaṇḍanamaṇḍita’ttabhāvau

himapaṭalaina himācalau rivāsi| ()

36. 

 maṇimakuṭaina nivatthakāsikaina

narapatisunu sumaṇḍitau rarāja,

surabhavanaina ca khīrasāgaraina

kanakasiṇairugirī’va nic‍calaṭṭhau| ()

37.

 nabhasi samākulatārakāvalī’va

urasi virājitatārahārapantī,

narapavarau pivi tāya rūpasāraṃ

amatamivā’yatalaucana’ñ‍jalīhi| ()

38.

 tadahani rājakumārapubbasaila-

ppabhavavarānanacandamaṇḍalaina,

mukuḷitalaucananīlanīrajāya

abhavimanaukumudākarappabaudhau| ()

39. 

 yuvatayuvānamapaikkhataṃ janānaṃ

animisalaucananīlakantigaṅgā,

ruciravadhūhi vidhūtacāmaraihi

anilavilaulataraṅgasālinīva| ()

40.

 gaganatalaupari tārakākulamhi

yuvayuvatīnavacandacandikaiva,

nicitasuvaṇṇakahāpaṇai varaijuṃ

athamaṇimaṇḍapavaidikātalamhi| ()

41.

 sakalakalākusalau’pagammavippā-

cariyagaṇau jayamaṅgalāya taisaṃ,

suparisamāpayi sabbapubbakic‍caṃ

sapadi pavassi akhaṇḍalāvujaṭṭhi| ()

42. 

 karatalatāmarasaisu kuṇḍikāya

maṇikhavitāya purauhitau ubhin‍naṃ,

subhamabhisaikajalaṃ nipātayaṃ tai

punahipayaujayi pāṇipīḷaṇasmiṃ| ()

43.

 suradhanuvij‍julatai’va vārivāhaṃ

rathamabhiruyha gahīramandaghausaṃ,

parivutakhattiyabandhavaihi tamhā

kapilapurā’bhimukhābhavuṃ ubhauta tai| ()

44.

 atha samalaṅkatavīthimajjhigānaṃ

vivaṭanikaitanasīhapañ‍jaraṭṭhā,

animisalaucanapaṅkajaupahāraṃ

bhūsamakaruṃ kapilaṅganā pasan‍nā| ()

45. 

 gamanavilāsamudikkhataṃ janānaṃ

rucirasiraupahita’ñ‍jalīhi bhattyā,

maṇikalasappitapupphamañ‍jarīhi

racitamivaubhayavīthipassamāsi| ()

46.

 tikhiṇavilaucanabāṇalakkhabhāvaṃ

nirupamarūpini kāminīhi nītai,

patita’nurāgasaraihiyaiva tāsaṃ

hadayavidāraṇamāsi tapphalaṃ’va| ()

47.

 kathamapi kāpilavatthavā ahaisuṃ

tadahani nic‍calalaucanuppalaihi,

kapilapuraṃ tidasālayāvatiṇṇā

tidasagaṇā’va vipassanāyu’bhin‍naṃ| ()

48. 

 tidivapurā nijavaijayantanāma-

surabhavanaṃ’va sujampatī sujātā,

kapilapurā punarāgamiṃsu tamhā

patipatinī nijarājamandiraṃ dvai| ()

49.

 dharaṇipatisutau pattaraj‍jābhisaikau

kapilapuravarai taisu tisvālayaisu,

aparimitasukhaṃ tāya bimbāya saddhiṃ

suciramanubhavi candabimbānanāya| ()

50.

 vikacakamala (nandīmukhi) mañ‍jubhāṇī

tividhavayasi dibbaccharārūpasaubhā,

agami khayavayaṃ sā’pi bimbāmahaisi

saratha saratha saṅkhāradhammassabhāvaṃ| ()

iti mbaidhānandābhidhānaina yatinā viracitai sakalakavijana hadayānandadānanidānai jinavaṃsadīpai avidūrainidānai pāṇiggahamaṅgalussavapavattiparidīpau| navamau saggau|

1. 

 guṇamaṇimaṇimā sau daivarājā’va rājā

sukhamanubhavamānau vājiyānaina yaina,

bhamarabharitamālā maṅgaluyyānabhūmi

tadavasari kadāci sālinī (mālinī) hi| ()

2.

 upagatasamayau’ti bujjhanatthāya baudhiṃ

sumariya suraputtā baudhisattai vajantai,

bhavaviratisamatthaṃ dassayuṃ māpayitvā

jarasakaṭasarūpaṃ jiṇṇarūpaṃka virūpaṃ| ()

3. 

 kimidamitihapuṭṭhau jiṇṇarūpaṃ vipassa

vimatiparavasau sau sārathiṃ rūpasārau,

nijahadayanidhānai daivatācauditassa

nidahi dhanamivagghaṃ tassadhammaupadaisaṃ| ()

4.

 haritanaḷakalāpaṃ maṅgaluyyānamaggai

yathariva’himukhaṭṭhā kuñ‍jari bhañ‍jamānā,

tathariva’bhibhavantī sabbayaubbañ‍ñadappaṃ

kharatarajaratā sā attapac‍cakkhabhūtā| ()

5.

 mukhakukavalayagabbhā bhaṭṭhakiñ‍jakkhasaubhā

bhavi kaṭhinajarārikhaṇḍadantaṭṭhipanti,

kuṭilapalitajātaṃ tañ‍jarāyā’bhisaikai

sirasi racitasaitacchattasaubhaṃ babandha| ()

6. 

 aviraḷavaliyau tā jiṇṇarūpassa cammai

pabhavabalavataijaudhātuvaiguṭṭhitā’suṃ,

taralasalilapiṭṭhai saiyyathāpau’dadhissa

taralasalilapiṭṭhai saiyyathāpau’dadhissa

palayapavanavaigu’ttuṅgakal‍laulamālā ()

7.

 ṭhitamupavanapanthai vaṅkagaupāṇasi’va

anujukamujubhutaṃ daṇḍamaulubbha bhumyā,

nijataruṇavilāsaṃ lakkhamāpādayantaṃ

dhanumiva saguṇaṃ taṃ rūpamaddakkhi dhīrau| ()

8. 

 jiniajagarabhaugai jiṇṇanimmaukabhāraṃ

tilakavibatacammaṃ tantiraujattabhāvai,

apagataghanamaṃsau phāsukaṭṭhippandhau

ajinitiravalaipaṃ kuḍḍadāruppabandhaṃ| ()

9.

 vigatabalamadādiṃ vissavantaṃ sarīrai

navhi vaṇamukhaihi guthamuttādya’sūviṃ,

valivisamakapaulaṃ kampamānuttamaṅgaṃ

suvisadamatinau taṃ passatau jiṇṇarūpaṃ| ()

10.

 bhuvanamanavasaisaṃ tassu’paṭṭhāsi sampa-

j‍jalitamatha jarāyā’dittagaihattayaṃ’va,

ahamapaki anatītau dhammamaitanti vatvā

bhavanavanamagañchi jātiyu’k‍kaṇṭhitattau| ()

11. 

 punarupavanamaggaṃ augahantassa rañ‍ñau

sukhamanubhavanatthaṃ daivatāmāpayitvā,

parapariharanīyaṃ ghauraraugāvatiṇṇaṃ

aparamapi virūpaṃ dassayuṃ vyādhirūpaṃ| ()

12.

 visadamati vimatyā sakyavaṃsaikakaitu

kimimivapalipan‍naṃ vac‍capassāvapaṅkai,

pabhavabalavadukkhaṃ taṃ parādhīnavuttiṃ

kimidamiti padisvā pājitāraṃ apucchi| ()

13.

 varamati varadhammaṃ taina sūtaina vuttaṃ

amatamiva pibantau sautadhātvañ‍jalīhi,

agamapi anatitau byādhidhammanti tamhā

nijabhavanavanaṃ saupā’ga saṃviggarūpau| ()

14. 

 tadupavanavimānaṃ vājiyānaina nāthai

vajati sati kadāci māpayuṃ daivatāyau,

suṇakhakulalagijjhādīhīvā khaj‍jamānaṃ

narakuṇapasarīraṃ uddhumātaṃ paṭhamhi| ()

15.

 pabhavakimikulānaṃ cālayaṃ nīlavaṇṇaṃ

vaṇavivaramukhaihi lauhitaṃ paggharantaṃ,

sakuṇagaṇvitacchiṃ makkhikāmakkhitaṅgaṃ

sumati matasarīraṃ addasā sārahīnaṃ| ()

16.

 abhigami gamayantau bhāratiṃ sārathissa

nijasavaṇayugasmiṃ haimatāḍaṅkasaubhaṃ,

ahampi maraṇaṃ bhau nātivattauti vatvā

vanamiva migarājā sakyarājā vimānaṃ| ()

17. 

 punapi saparisau sau yānamāruyha bhadraṃ

katipayadivasānaṃ ac‍cayainā’dhirājā,

kapilapuravaramhā’naṅgaraṅgālayābhaṃ

tadupavanamagañchi pañ‍cabāṇābhirūpau| ()

18.

 guṇamaṇi maṇivaṇṇaṃ pattamādāya patthai

panasasarasarāgaṃ vīvaraṃpārupitvā,

ṭhitamavikalacakkhuṃ nimmitaṃ daivatāhi

sumati samaṇarūpaṃ buddharūpaṃ’va passi| ()

19.

 tadahani vibudhā’dhiṭṭhānatau bhāvanīyau

yatipatiriva sūtau atthadhammānusāsi,

samaṇaguṇamanaikādīnavaṃ pañ‍cakāmai

tamavadi gamanasmiṃ ānisaṃsañ‍ca gaihā| ()

20. 

 kuhanalapanamicchājīvamauhāya kucchi-

paraharaṇasamatthaṃ mattikāpattamassa,

kara kamakalagataṃ sau pāramīcauditattau

visadamati padisvā sampasādaṃ janaisi| ()

21.

 vikasitakalikālaṅkāramuddālasāla-

miva samaṇavilāsaṃ cīvaraubhāsamaggai,

nayanamadhukarānaṃ bhāramādhāya dhīrau

nijamanasi janaisi cīvarai sampasādaṃ| ()

22.

 khaṇikamaraṇadukkhāvaṭṭamābādharāsi-

makaranikarabhimaṃ taṃ jarāvīcivaigaṃ,

bhavajaladhimagādhaṃ jātivailāvadhiṃ sau

ayamiva paṭipan‍nā nittaraiyyuntya’vaidi| ()

23. 

 samaṇavadanapīnac‍candabimbaudayaina

vikasitamanakundau nandanuyyānasaubhaṃ,

upavanamabhīgantuṃ sandanaṃ cārayai’ti

tadahani manujindau sandanācārimāha| ()

24.

 valayitanaḷatāḷītālahintālapantiṃ

malayajatarujāyāsītalaṃ nimmalāpaṃ,

upavanamavatiṇṇau nandanaṃ vāsavau’va

akari divasabhāgaṃ sādhukīḷaṃ sarājā| ()

25.

 dasasatakiraṇasmiṃ yassa kittippabandha-

saradajaladarājichāditasmiṃ nabhasmiṃ,

uparisirasi saitacchattasaubhaṃ bhajantai

tadiyacaraṇalakkhyā’laṅkari vāpitīraṃ| ()

26. 

 sarasijavadanaihi haṃsapīnatthanaihi

kuvalayanayanaihī nīlikākuntalaihi,

madhumadamuditālīnūpuraibhā’varaudha-

janamiva ramaṇīyaṃ autari sau taḷākaṃ| () (silaisabandhanaṃ)

27.

 varayuvatijanānaṃ kumbhagambhīranābhi-

vivaragahitavāri vāpi sā rittarūpā,

ghanathanajaghanānaṃ saṅgamainai’tarāsaṃ

punarabhavi kapapuṇṇā pītivipphārinīva| ()

28.

 abhinavaramaṇinaṃ jātulaj‍jāturānaṃ

vimukhanayanamīnai mīnakaitupamaina,

upavadhitumīvaggai khittajālabbilāsaṃ

khaṇamavahari rañ‍ñā viddhahatthambudhārā| ()

29. 

 navajalakaṇikālaṅkāravattāravindā

ghanakucakalahaṃsā kaisasaivālanīlā,

patimathitataraṅgākiṇṇasussauṇivailā

sarasi sarasi’vā’si tatra kīḷāturai’kā| ()

30.

 sasirūciramukhīnaṃ khaumavatthantarīyai

sanikamapanayantai vicīhatthaihi rañ‍ñā,

racitanayanakantī byanta gambhīranābhī-

sarasijapariyantā nāḷapantīri’vā’si| ()

29. 

 navajalakaṇikālaṅkāravattāravindā

ghanakucakalahaṃsā kaisasaivālanīlā,

patimathitataraṅgākiṇṇasussauṇivailā

sarasi sarasi’vā’si tatra kīḷāturai’kā| ()

30.

 sasiruciramukhīnaṃ khaumavatthantarīyai

sanikamapanayantai vivibhaṇthaihi rañ‍ñā,

racitanayanakantī byanta gamhīranāhī

sarasijapariyantā nāḷapantiri’vā’si| ()

31. 

 punarapi kucakumbhañ‍candahārainivā’pai

nijagaḷaparimāṇai san‍nimuggaṅganānaṃ,

malinakamalinī sā laucanaihā’nanaihi

abhavi bhamarabhāra’mbhaujasaṇḍā’kulai’ca| ()

32.

 madhumadamadhupaihi gīyamānaihi vici-

bhujasatapahaṭāhi sauṇibhairīhi thinaṃ,

lalitakamalasīsai nac‍camānindirāya

ajini jalajinī sā dibbasaṅgītasālaṃ| ()

33.

 tuhinakaramukhīnaṃ tantaḷāka’ntalikkhai

sulalitabhujaval‍lī vij‍julaikhābhirāmā,

kuvalayavanarāji nīlajīmutarāji

patinayanamayūrai kīḷayantī rarāja| ()

34. 

 phuṭakuvalayahatthaṃ rājabhaṃsaihi khittaṃ

vividhamadhupabhuttaṃ dhammavailātivattaṃ,

yathariva gaṇikaṃ taṃ kañ‍janiṃ sau janindau

tadahani paribhutvā’mandamānanvdamāpa| () (silaisabandhanaṃ)

35.

 caraṇa’nuvajamānabbīcisaṅkhaubhatīraṃ

jahati sati taḷākaṃ sāvaraudhai narindai,

sarasivirahinīsaṃruddhanissāsarūpau

mudusurabhisamīrau mandamandaṃ pavāyi| ()

36. 

 paribhavi ravibimbaṃ taṅkhaṇatthācalaṭṭhaṃ

tahimupalatalaṭṭhau bhānuvaṃsaikabhānu,

atha sarasivadhūnaṃ kiñ‍cisaṅkauvitāni

sarasijavadanāti saukadīnānivā’suṃ| ()

37.

 asitanabhasi sañjhāmaighamālāvilāsaṃ

abhibhaviya nisin‍nai tatra sakyādhināthai,

tuvaṭamupagatā taṃ kappakā’naikavatthā-

bharaṇavikatihatthā bhūpatiṃ bhūsaṇatthaṃ| ()

38.

 ravikularavinau khau dhammataijābhibhūtau

surapati suraputtaṃ vissakammābhidhānaṃ,

sapadi pahiṇi sammā dibbavatthādinā bhau

tibhuvanasaraṇaṃ taṃ bhūsayassū’ti vatvā| ()

39. 

 gahitamanujavaisau sau’pasaṅkamma sīsai

sukhumapaṭasataihī vaiṭhanañ‍cā’pi datvā,

maṇikanakamayaihi bhūsayi bhūsaṇaihi

tadahani bhavi sak‍kau daivarājā’va rājā| ()

40.

 timirahamarabhāra’k‍kantapācīnapassaṃ

mukulitasatasañjhāmaighapattāvalīnaṃ,

gaganatalataḷākādhāramandāranāḷaṃ

kamalamakulasaubhaṃ bhānubimbaṃ babandha| ()

41.

 pahiṇi pitunarindau sāsanaṃ tāva tassa

nijatanujakumāru’ppattimāraucayitvā,

pamuditabhadayau sau laikhaṇālaukanaina

avaditi mama jātaṃ bandhanaṃ rāhujātau| ()

42. 

 tadahanipiturañ‍ñā vuttavākyānurūpaṃ

tahimakhilapadatthaṃ saddasatthak‍kamaina,

karahacī manujindau ayyakau saṅgahaitvā

avaditi mamatattā rāhulaunāmahautaṃ| ()

43.

 vanasuravanitānaṃ laucanindīvaraihi

mahitasirisarirau bhadramāruyha yānaṃ,

sabhavanamabhigantuṃ ausari nāgarānaṃ

suvimalanayanālītauraṇākiṇṇavīthiṃ| ()

44.

 vivaṭamaṇikavāṭau’pantikaṭṭhā vimānai

jitasuravanitā’si yā pitucchāya dhītā,

nayanakarapuṭaihi rūpasāraṃ nipīya

samitaratipipāsā sā kisāgautamī thī| ()

45. 

 jitamanasijarūpaṃ īdisaṃ yaisamatthi

tanujaratanamaddhā nibbutā sā’pi mātā,

pitujagatipatī sau nibbutau sītibhūtau

nijapiyabhariyā’pi nibbutā’tyai’vamāha| ()

46.

 hadayagatakilaisai nibbutai vūpasantai

yatipatiriva diṭṭhau nibbutau sau’hamasmi,

iti varamati sutvā tāya gāthaṃ sugītaṃ

vividhanayavibhattaṃ tappadatthaṃ avaidi| ()

47.

 ahamitipadamassā nibbutiṃ sāvitau’smi

sumariya garubhatyā tāya lakkhagghamaggaṃ,

dhavalakiraṇabhāraṃ bhāsuraṃ hārihāraṃ

pahiṇiya bhavanaṃ sau pāvisi sāvaraudhau| ()

48. 

 mayamiva varabaudhiṃ bujjhamānassa jātu

manasi vupasamai’ti tuyhamaikādasaggi,

upagamumupasantiṃ vyākarauntī’va tāva

aparadisi vinaddhā’naikasañjhāghanālī| ()

49.

 atuladhavajachattaṃ dhautamuttāvalīhi

valayitamiva rañ‍ñau tassa sihāsanasmiṃ,

udayasikharisīsai tāvatārāvalīhi

parivutamatisaubhaṃ candabimbaṃ bahāsa| ()

50.

 ghanataratimiraihā’vattharattaihi laukai

masimalinavilāsaṃ taṅkhaṇai dassayanti,

rajanikarakaraihi vipphurattaihi phītā

katanavaparikammai’vā’si sā rājadhāni| ()

51. 

 himakarakarabhārak‍kattarattandhakāra-

galitatimiralaikhākāramāvī karaunti,

phuṭakumuda vanaisu cāsikundāṭavīsu

sumadhura madhumattā bhiṅgamālā pamattā| ()

52.

 jitasurapativaisau dhammacintāparau sau

jalita maṇipadīpālaukabhin‍nandhakārai,

nijasiribhavanasmiṃ haimasīhāsanasmiṃ

nacira mabhinisaj‍jī pañ‍cakāmai virattau| ()

53.

 sapadi turiyahatthā nīlajimutakaisā

kuvalayadalanaittā candalaikhālalāṭā,

vikacakamalavattā maikhalābhārasauṇī

kucaharaviraḷaṅgī cāruvāmaurujaṅghā| ()

54. 

 kumudamudakapaulā kuṇḍalaulambakaṇṇā

avivaradasanālimālatīdāmalilā,

kanakaratanamālābhāragīvā, bhirāmā,

abhinavavanitāyau nac‍cagītaisu chaikā| ()

55.

 rahadamivapasan‍naṃ nic‍calāsinaminaṃ

sumatimupanisin‍naṃ saṃvutadvārupaitaṃ,

tamabhiratinirāsaṃ buddhabhāvābhilāsaṃ

abhiramayitukāmā autaruṃ raṅgabhūmiṃ| ()

56. 

 maṇimayavasumatyā pādasaṅghaṭṭanaina

kanakavakalayaghausaṃ kāci nicchārayantī,

calakisalayalīlā aṅguli cālayanti

anulayamabhinac‍cuṃ haimaval‍līvilāsā| ()

57.

 narapatimukhabimbaṃka lakkhamāpādayantī

nayanakharasarānaṃ raṅgasaṅgāmabhūmyā,

jitakalaravavāṇī kāci rāmā bhirāmā

savaṇasubhagagītaṃ gāyamānā vibhāsuṃ| ()

58.

 jitasuralalanāyaukāvi pañ‍caṅgikāni

tadahani turiyāni vādayuṃ laulapāṅgā,

savaṇamadhuravīṇā bhairinādaihi tāsaṃ

gaganatala mivā,si pāvusai raṅgabhūmi| ()

59. 

 varamati ramaṇīnaṃ taṃ mahābhūtarūpa-

ppabhavamivavikāraṃ nac‍camaddakkhi tāsaṃ,

visamabhavakuṭīrai rājaraugāturānaṃ

asunituriyarāvaṃ gītamaṭṭassaraṃ,va| ()

60.

 bhusamanahiratau sau nac‍cagītaisu tāsaṃ

sirisayanavarasmiṃ sīhasaiyyaṃ akāsi,

itigahitavihaisā laddhaniddāvakāsā

sapadi madanapāsā tā nipaj‍jiṃsunārī| ()

61.

 sahakumudiniyā sau suttamattappabuddhau

nijasirisayanasmiṃ san‍nisin‍nau rajanyā,

gahitaturiyabhaṇḍai tatthatatthau, ttharitvā

yuvatijanamapassi daḷhaniddābhibhūtaṃ| ()

62. 

 anilacalakapau lā kāci lālaṃ gilantai

galitabahaḷakhai ḷā kāci khādanti dantai,

bhagamapagatacau ḷā kāci saṃdassaya n‍nai

khalitavacanamā lā kāci yaṃyaṃ lapantai| ()

63.

 khipitamapi karauntī kāci kāsantikāci

iti pacuravikāraṃ nissirikaṃ asāraṃ,

bhavanamanavasaisaṃ tassu, paṭṭhāsi daḷhaṃ

narakuṇapavikiṇṇaṃ āmakāḷāhaṇaṃva| ()

64.

 tadahani tibhavaṃ cā,dittagaihattayaṃ,va

sumariya vatabhau, passaṭṭhamaupaddutaṃ bhau,

iti paramamudānaṃ kavattayaṃ tabbimutyā

manasi purisasūrau sūrabhāvaṃ janaisi| ()

65. 

 suratacaturarāmārakkhasivāsabhūtai

siribhavanavanasmiṃ mauhayantamhi bālai,

alamiti mama vāsau handa nikkhamma tamhā

tibhavabhayavimuttiṃ aisayissāmahaṃ,ti| ()

66.

 upakamiya vimānadvāramummārupantai

sayanupari nipan‍naṃ chan‍namuṭṭhāpayitvā,

tamavadi abhigantuṃ kappayitvaṃ, nayaiti

pabalajavabalaggaṃ vājirājaṃ sarājā, ()

67.

 gatasati hayasāḷaṃ taṅkhaṇai chan‍namac‍cai

sakapatigamanatthaṃ aisamaṃ kappanatthau,

agamiti sahajātau katthakau vājirājā

akari vipulahaisārāvamānandabhārau| ()

68. 

 pavisiya sirigabbhaṃ tailadīpuj‍jalantaṃ

ratanakhacitamañ‍cai gandhapupphābhikiṇṇai,

dharaṇipatinipan‍naṃ haimabimbaupamānaṃ

nijatanujakumāraṃ passibimbāyasaddhiṃ| ()

69.

 yadi ahamapanaitvā daiviyā hatthapāsaṃ

mama tanujakumāraṃ aṅkamāraupayāmi,

vadanajitasaraujā rājinī vuṭṭhahitvā

vanamahīgamanaṃ mai vārayai dun‍nivāraṃ| ()

70.

 tanujamukhasaraujaṃ buddhabhūtau samānau

nayanamadhukarānaṃ jātu kāhāmibhāraṃ,

sumariya caraṇaṃ sau uddharantau,va mairuṃ

avatari bhavanamhā ukkhipitvā pavīrau| ()

71. 

 kuvalayadalanaittañ‍candamamhaujavattaṃ

madanaratharathaṅgākārasussauṇibhāraṃ,

kathamavatari bimbānāmadaiviṃ pahāya

narapati bhavanamhā haimabimbābhirāmaṃ| ()

72.

 marakatapaṭimābhaṃ sambhavaṃ sakyavaṃsai

samupacitasupuñ‍ñaṃ lakkhaṇākiṇṇagattaṃ,

pajahiya sukumāraṃ rāhulākhyaṃ kumāraṃ

kathamavatari pādamandamukkhippa dhīrau| ()

73.

 ripugajamigarājaṃ jambudīpaggarājaṃ

tadahani piturājaṃ puttasaukaṇṇavamhi,

kathamamitadayau sau niddayau pakkhipitvā

avatari bhavanamhāka uddharitvāna pādai| ()

74. 

 sakalapathavicak‍kaṃ cak‍kavāḷāvadhiṃ sau

abhivijiya asatthau sattamai vāsaramhi,

narahari katapuñ‍ñau cak‍kavatti ahutvā

kathamavatari tamhā ukkhipitvāna pādai| ()

75.

 avatariya vimānā aj‍ja maṃ tārayatvaṃ

tvamapitibhavatau)haṃ uttaraiyya’nti vatvā,

tamabhiruhi janindau vājirājinda’maṭṭhā-

rasaratanapamāṇaṃ dhautasaṅkhāvadātaṃ| ()

76.

 pavanaturitavaigau kanthakau vājirājā

yadahani padasaddaṃ cā’pi haisaṃ karaiyya,

nanu sakalapuraṃ sau yāti ajjhauttharitvā

tadahani katasaddaṃ vārayuṃ daivatāyau| ()

77. 

 karakamalatalaisu daivatānimmitaisu

panihitapadavāraṃ assa māruyha dhīrau,

lahumupagami chan‍naṃ vāladhiṃ gāhayitvā

thirapihitakavāṭadvārapākārupantaṃ| ()

78.

 yadi pihitakavāṭugghāṭanaṃ nā’bhavissā

hayavaramapi chan‍nāmac‍camādāya sauhaṃ,

asari purisasihau uppataiyyanti aṭṭhā-

rasaratatapamāṇu’ttuṅgapākāracak‍kaṃ| ()

79.

 tathariva hayarājā chan‍nanāmau ca mantī

vīriyabalasamaṅgī cīntayuṃ tāvadaiva,

vivari tadadhivatthā daivatā cauditattā

purisadasasatainu’gghāṭiyaṃ dvārabāhaṃ| ()

80. 

 mama visayamasaisaṃ aisasiddhatthanāmau

abhibhaviya subaudhiṃ jātu bujjhissatīti,

atha sumariya mārau pāpimā’tīvakuddhau

paṭipathamupagañchi nikkhamitvā vimānā| ()

81.

 turitamahivajantai māravairimhi mārau

asitanabhasi ṭhatvā itthamāraucayittha,

varapurisa itau khau sattamai vāsaramhi

tvamahivijiya laukaṃ haissasai cak‍kavattī| ()

82.

 sukhamanubhavamānau cak‍kavattī bhavitvā

gharamadhivasa cak‍kaṃ vattayaṃ yāvajīvaṃ,

amitamati tuvaṃ mā nikkhamassū’ti mārau

abhigamananisaidhaṃ kātumic‍cānusāsi| ()

83. 

 namuvilapitavācaṃ sautadhātvañ‍jalīhi

savisamiva pibantau taṃ tuvaṃ kau’si pucchi,

pavanapathaṭhitau’haṃ issarau daivatānaṃ

naravara vasavattī pāpimā’tyai’va māha| ()

84.

 suranarasaraṇau sau nibbhayau dibbacadda-

ratanajananamaddhā mārajānāmaha’nti,

paṭivacanamadāsi mādisau duppasayhau

bhavati dasasahassaihā’pi tumhādisaihi| ()

85.

 yadi manasi siyā tai kāmadausabbihiṃsā-

pabhutiparivitak‍kau tāvajānāma’hanti,

paṭighaparavasau sau kiñ‍ciautārapaikhau

anupadamanubandhi tassa chāyāyathaiva| ()

86. 

 punarabhivajatauvā’sāḷhiyā puṇṇamāya

kapilapuravibhūtiṃ daṭṭhukāmamhi jātai,

vasumati parivattī dassayi assarañ‍ñau

puravarabhimukhaṭṭhau caitiyaṭṭhānabhumiṃ| ()

87.

 sapadi dasasahassicak‍kavāḷaisu daivā

tibhuvanasaraṇassā’rakkhaṇai vyāvaṭāsuṃ,

maṇikanakamayaihi daṇḍadīpādikaihi

animisatanayai’kai maggamālaukayiṃsu| ()

88.

 surabhikusumadāmaulambamānabbitāna-

kanakakalasasaitacchattakaituddhajaihi,

tadahigamanamaggaṃ daivatā’laṅkariṃsu

bhuvanakuharamāsi pupphapūjābhirāmaṃ| ()

89. 

 gaganamasanighausacchantamaivaṭṭhasaṭṭhi-

turiyasatasahassāravavipphāramāsi,

mahitasurabhipupphākiṇṇamaggāvatiṇṇau

aturitamabhigantvā yaujanantiṃsamattaṃ| ()

90.

 vimalasalilapuṇṇaṃ phaiṇamālābhikiṇṇaṃ

vikacakamalarājiṃ tuṅgakal‍laularājiṃ,

sasiratarasamīraṃ vāḷukākiṇṇatiraṃ

samupagami anaumānāmagaṅgaṃ savīrau| ()

91.

 asitamaṇitalābhā chan‍na kā nāmikā’yaṃ

iti varamati pucchi sau anaumānadīti,

tamavadi yadi tīrai aittha’haṃ pabbajaiyyaṃ

ativiya saphakhalā mai sā anaumāsiyā’ti| ()

92. 

 ravikulatilakau sau paṇhiyā vājirājaṃ

sajavamadadi sañ‍ñaṃ tāya aṭṭhausabhāya,

suvimalasalilāyā’naumagaṅgāya tīrai

taraṇiriva ṭhitau’si uppatitvā turaṅgau| ()

93.

 sitapulinatalaṭṭhau tiṇhadhārā’sihatthau

visadamati samauḷiṃ cūḷamādāya daḷhaṃ,

aluni sirasi saisā dvaṅgulā nīlakaisā

na tadupari parūḷhā dakkhiṇāvattayiṃsu| ()

94.

 abhavi tadanurūpaṃ dāṭhikā massucā’pi

ayamupari sacā’haṃ ṭhātu buddhau bhavaiyyaṃ,

nabhasi khipi sikhaṃ taṃ ic‍cadhiṭṭhāya dhīrau

paribhaviya ṭhitā sā maighamālāvilāsaṃ| ()

95. 

 sapadi surapuramhā daivarājābhi’gantvā

tamabhihariya cūḷaṃ cāricaṅgauṭakaina,

ratanamayamuḷāraṃ caitiyaṃ māpayittha

suragaṇamahanīyaṃ tatra cūḷāmaṇinti| ()

96.

 vidhirabhavi sahāyau yau ghaṭikāranāmau

adadi samaṇakappaṃka sau kaparikkhāramassa,

gahitasamaṇavaisau pubbabuddhā’va nāthau

nabhasi khipi nivatthaṃ sāṭakaṃ saṃharitvā| ()

97.

 tamabhihariya dussaṃ pakkhipitvā samuggai

ratanamayamatulyaṃ yaujanadvādasuc‍caṃ,

akari parahitatthaṃ brahmalaukaika vidhātā

makuṭamaṇimaricīcumbiyaṃ caitiyaṃsau| ()

98. 

 pitunarapatinau tvaṃ bhusaṇādīni datvā

iti mama vacanainā’raugyamāraucayassu,

sacivamanupalabbhā’ dāni pabbaj‍jituṃ tai

pahiṇi hayasahāyaṃ auvaditvāna chan‍naṃ| ()

99.

 paṭipathamavatiṇṇau gantukāmau saraṭṭhaṃ

dharaṇipativiyaugā saukanibbiddhagattau,

turagapati cavitvā kanthakau daivaputtau

bhavi kanakavimānai taṅkhaṇaitāvatiṃsai| ()

100.

 sakalavanasurānaṃ añ‍jalimañ‍jarihi

mahitañ‍caraṇapīṭhau yaina pabbaj‍jitau sau,

tadavasarianaumānāmanaj‍jā samīpai

vanamanupiyanāmaṃ ambarukkhābhirāmaṃ| ()

101. 

 sau nikkhamma abhin‍nakhattiyakulā naikkhamma dhammālayau

bhaugakkhandhamuḷāracak‍karatanaṃ uc‍cārabhāraṃ viya,

auhāyā’nupiyambanāmavipinai sattāhamajjhāvasaṃ

pabbaj‍jāpaṭilābhasambhavasukhaṃ vaidaisi buddhaṅkurau| ()

iti maidhānandābhidhānaina yatinā viracitai sakala kavijana hadayānanda dānanidānai jinavaṃsadīpai avidūrai nidānai mahābhinikkhamana pavattiparidīpau dasamau saggau|

1. 

 mandānilairitatarusaṇḍamaṇḍitai

tasmiṃ tapauvanagahaṇai tapaudhanau,

bhutvā (pabhāvati) vanadaivatā yathā

dibbaṃ sukhaṃ sukhamanagāriyaṃ tatau| ()

2.

 yau sainiyau narapati māgadhau tadā

yasmiṃ purai vasati puraṅgabhāsurai,

rājaggahaṃ tamahipavaisanatthikau

addhānamausari samatiṃsayaujanaṃ| ()

3.

 cak‍kaṅkitassiricāraṇau susañ‍ñamau

dīghañ‍jasaṃ vasi tadahaina khaipayaṃ,

rājaggahaṃ kapuravaramindirālayaṃ

sampāvisi jitagajarājagāmi sau| ()

4. 

 indāsudhāvalivalayikatau mahā-

maggamhi jaṅgammaṇipabbatauriva,

khattiṃsalakkhaṇasamalaṅkatau mahā-

vīrau tapaunidhi yugamattadassanau| ()

5.

 baudhāpayaṃ budhajanamānasambujai

avhāpayaṃ pathikajana’kkhipakkhinau,

vimhāpayaṃ nijasiriyā sadaivakai

tasmiṃ purai cari sapadānacārikaṃ| ()

6.

 piṇḍāya gacchatisati rūpadassana-

pabyāvaṭā’khilajanatāya gautamai,

saṅghaubhitā’suramisaraihi sā puri

pattai yathaiva’surapuri purindadai| ()

7. 

 khattiṃsalakkhaṇasuracāpabhāsurai

rūpambarai varapurisassa gacchatau,

laukassa laucanasakuṇāvalitadā

antaṃ napāpuṇi parisaṅkamantīpi| ()

8.

 mañ‍jīrapiñ‍jarakara kandharāmaṇi-

kaiyūrabhāsuracaraṇā sirimatau,

sīmantinī paramasiriṃ vipassituṃ

dhāviṃsu nij‍jitakalahaṃsakāminī| ()

9.

 nārijanā mahitumivā’bhigacchatau

rūpindirāya’ nimisalaucanuppalai,

dhammil‍lavail‍litabhujacampakāvalī

dhāviṃsu pīvarakucahārapīḷitā| ()

10. 

 nissāsinī samajalabinducumbita-

vattambujā sithilitakāsikambarā,

kācitthiyau samaṇamudikkhituṃ pathai

dhāvantiyau kimupatisaṅkiyā’bhavuṃ| ()

11.

 ugghāṭitā’sitamaṇisihapañ‍jarā

rāmāpasāritavadanambujāka vasiṃ,

daṭṭhuṃ pabhujanabhavanaisu taṅkhaṇai

mandākinisarasivilāsamāharuṃ| ()

12.

 ucchaṅgataupatitasutā’bhirūpinau

rūpappalamhitahadayāka pūrīvadhū,

passantiyau pathikajanassahatthagai

nākaṃsu kiṃ adhikaraṇā’dhiraupaṇaṃ| ()

13. 

 aikai janā yatipatirūpadassana-

kautuhaḷā sakapaṭibhānamabravuṃ,

sīmantinī manakumudāni baudhayaṃ

pattau’tya’yaṃ pakatinisāpatīnukhau| ()

14.

 sutvāna taṃ sakalakalāntaraupagaṃ

bimbaṃ tadaṅkitahariṇaṅkamambarai,

tumhai napassatha himaraṃsinau iti

gabbābhijappitavacanā’paraijanā| ()

15.

 acchairapaṅkajavisarāni cintiya

sañ‍cumbituṃ puralalanānana’mbujai|

vairaucanau idhupagamā virūpimā

ic‍cabravuṃ punaranisammakārinau| ()

16.

 sutvāna taṃ gaganatalaṅganai’dhunā

vairaucanassa’bhicaratau napassatha,

caṇḍātapaṃ thiraparivaisamambuja-

pāṇinti gaj‍jitavacanā’paraijanā| ()

17. 

 uttuṅgamandiramaṇicandikātalai

dibbaccharānibharamaṇihi vañ‍citau,

aiyā’marāvatinagariti cintiya

sak‍kau carai nanu sakavādamapakpayuṃ| ()

18.

 sak‍kassa dānavavijayā’bhilāsinau

pāṇimhi dissati vajirāyudhaṃ kharaṃ,

saṃvij‍jarai dasasatalaucanāni’pi

nautādisau ayamiti tabbipakkhinau| ()

19.

 yā sālavatya’dhivacanā’bhirūpinī

sañ‍cauditau jitagirijāya tāya hau,

kailāsapabbatanibhapaṇḍavācala-

ñattaṃ vajaṃ ayamitiissarau’bravuṃ| ()

20. 

 tumhai napassatha paramissarassa kiṃ

naggattanaṃ pasusayanau’pavaisanaṃ,

pāṇiṃ kapālakamadhikakkhimaṇḍalaṃ

itthaṃ pavaiditavacanā’paraijanā| ()

21.

 aisā’valambitapurakhīranīradhiṃ

lakkhiṃ samaikkhiya nijalakkhisaṃsayau,

pītambaraṃ paridahiya’ñ‍jasai caraṃ

nārāyanau iti matimappayuṃ sakaṃ| ()

22.

 nārāyanau kuvalayanīlaviggahau

kaupantarau’ragasayanindirādhanau,

cak‍kāyudhau’l‍lasitakarau’tivāmanau

tabbādamaddanacaturai’tarai janā| ()

23. 

 vaidattayaṃ vibudhajanānamānanai

sva’jjhāyataṃ vasatinukhau sarassatī,

sañ‍jātasaṃsaya jaṭitau pitāmahau

tassāgavaisanapasutau’tyu’dīrayuṃ| ()

24.

 sutvāna taṃ sarasijayauninau sadā

pāṇimhi vij‍jati varamattapautthakaṃ,

cattāricānanapadumāni dissarai

gacchaṃ ayaṃpana purisau natādisau| ()

25.

 suddhaudanavhayavasudhādhipa’trajau

buddhau bhavissati iti vaidakauvidā,

kauṇḍañ‍ñabhusurapamukhā dvijā tadā

pabyākaruṃ tanu bahubhāsaṇaina kiṃ| ()

26. 

 uk‍kaṇṭhitau sakabhavanā mahāmatī

nikkhamma sattamadivasauti vissutau,

āpāthagaṃ nijasavaṇañ‍jalihi bhau

taṃ byappathaṃ napivatha kiṃ yathāmataṃ| ()

27.

 vaidāgataṃ varapurisaṅgalakkhaṇaṃ

daihamhi vij‍jati samaṇassa gacchatau,

andhā’va bhau apagatarūpadassanā

tumhai’pi kiṃ talahatha rūpadassanaṃ| ()

28.

 nikkhamma chaḍḍitavibhavau mahākulā

suddhāsayau sugahiya pattacīvaraṃ,

pabbaj‍jiyā’nahiramitau bhavattayai

atthācaraṃ anugharamañ‍jasai’dhunā| ()

29. 

 suddhaudanāvanipatinau varaurasau

aisau samuj‍jalasatapuñ‍ñalakkhaṇau,

vyāpāritau kusalabalaina baudhiyā

hautai’va gautamasamaṇau nasaṃsayau| ()

30.

 disvā tapaudhanamahiyantamañ‍jasai

yai mānavā sakasakavādamappayuṃ,

tabbādabandhanavinivaiṭhanā paraṃ

ic‍cāhu paṇḍitapurisā yathāvatau| ()

31.

 dutā tadā surasi samappitañ‍jalī

rañ‍ñau tamacchariyapavattimāharuṃ,

addakkhi bhupati caramānamañ‍jasai

piṇḍāya’thabbivariya sihapañ‍jaraṃ| ()

32.

 nā gau siyā paṭhaminimuj‍janaṃ ka rai

yakkhau siyā sahayamadassanaṃ ka rai,

dai vau siyā gaganatalaṅgaṇañ‍ca rai

pausau siyā yadi paṭiladdhamāha rai| ()

33. 

 dūtainu’sāsiya magadhādhipau iti

vīmaṃsituṃ pakatimanaṅgahaṅginau,

pāhaisi tai padamanugammu’pāgamuṃ

saddhiṃ mahāsamaṇavaraina paṇḍavaṃ| ()

34.

 vikkhālayaṃ mukhakamalaṃ kulai kulai

bhikkhāṭanaina’ bhihaṭamissabhaujanaṃ,

laddhā jigucchiya vasi paṇḍavācala-

cchāyāya mārabhi sunisaj‍ja bhuñ‍jituṃ| ()

35.

 bhattamhī kuk‍kuravamathūpamai muhuṃ

antaudaraṃ pavisati dhīmatau sati,

antāni bāhirakaraṇāni’vā’bhavuṃ

taṃkhau sahī vasisanisampajañ‍ñavā| ()

36. 

 pabbaj‍jitaṃ sugahitapattacīvaraṃ

disvā ratiṃ pajahiya rājabhaujanai,

siddhattha nau tvamabhigamittha attanā

attānamauvadiya pabhuñ‍ji bhaujanaṃ| ()

37.

 dūtaihi cauditahadayau dayādhanau

sau māgadhau narapati taina pāvisi,

yainā’si paṇḍavagiri bhadravāhanaṃ

āruyha dassanarasaghaidhalaucanau| ()

38.

 añ‍ñāya sākiyakulasambhavaṃ vasiṃ

rājā pasidiya ariyai’riyāpathai,

mā kāhasai sakha iti duk‍karaṃ kharaṃ

raj‍jaina taṃnarapavaraṃ pavārayi| ()

39. 

 raj‍jaina kiṃ tava caturaṇṇavāvadhiṃ

raj‍jaṃ nijaṃ pajahiya āgatassa mai,

baudhiṃ pabujjhiya paṭhamaṃ tathāsati

āgacchataṃ mama vijitantya’vauca naṃ| ()

40.

 datvā paṭissavamatha bhumibhattunau

laukassa laucanamaṇitauraṇākulai,

dīghañ‍jasai vasi ṭhapitaṅghipaṅkajau

āḷārākaṃ isipavaraṃ upāvisi| ()

41.

 patvāna kiṃ kusalagavaisi sau vasi

āḷārakaṃ virajamuḷārajhāyinaṃ,

icchāmahanti’si tava santikai’dhunā

dhammaṃ samācaritumidhāgatau bruvi| ()

42. 

 sutvāna taṃ tatiyamarūpikaṃ vasi

jhānaṃ viyākari paṭiladdhamattanā,

khippaṃ tapaunidhipaguṇaṃ akāsi taṃ

dhammaṃ sakācariyanayā’valambiya| ()

43.

 nā’yaṃ vasi tanutarasaddhayā mamaṃ

dhammaṃ sayaṃ samadhigataṃ viyākarai,

gausāmikau yathariva pañ‍cagaurasaṃ

addhāphalaṃ anubhavatīti tintiya| ()

44.

 kālāma dvai adhigatajhānasambhavaṃ

tvaṃ yāvatāsukha manubhausi maṃ vada,

puṭṭhassa tassi’ti nacakiñ‍ci bhāviyaṃ

ākiñ‍canaṃ avaca akiñ‍canālayau| ()

45. 

 saṃvij‍jarai mamapaki imassi’vai’sinau

saddhāsatīvīriyasamādhibuddhiyau,

aivaṃ vitak‍kiya naciraṃ katussahau

jhānaṃ labhī tatīyamarūpikaṃ vasī|

46.

 yaṃ khau tuvaṃ viharasi jhātamapakpitau

sampaj‍ja sampati viharāmahanti taṃ,

āḷāri’si varapurisaina sāvitau

lābhā’vusautyavaca suladdhamāvusau|

47.

 jānāmi pāvacanamahaṃ yathā tuvaṃ

jānāsi pāvacanamahaṃ yathā tuvaṃ,

tvaṃ tādisau ahamapi yādisau bhavai

tvaṃ yādisau ahamapaki tādisau bhavai|

48. 

 aihā’vusau samaṇa mayaṃ ubhau janā

kāhāmi’tau pariharaṇaṃ gaṇassi’daṃ,

vatvāna ācariyasamānakau sakaṃ

sissaṃ akā tamasamamattanā samaṃ| ()

49.

 dhammaupya’yaṃ nabhavati nibbidāya vā

baudhāyavā navupasamāya kaivalaṃ,

āruppabhumiyamupapattiyā siyā

ic‍cānalaṅkariya tatau apak‍kami| ()

50.

 kālamatau uparivisaisamuddakau

jañ‍ñā’tya’yaṃ sumariya rāmaputtakau,

patvā’ssamaṃ samadhigataṃ tvayā’pa’haṃ

dhammaṃ samācaritumidhāgatau’bruvi| ()

51. 

 ñatvā sakācariyamatañhi buddhimā

dhammañ‍carai mama samayau ca tādisau,

vatvai’vamuddakavasi khippamattanau

sikkhaisi pāvacanapathai tapaudhanaṃ| ()

52.

 saddhāya maṃ sakasamayā’nusāsakau

addhā samādhijaphalamāharai’tya’yaṃ,

cintāparau varapurisau arūpikaṃ

jhānaṃ valañ‍jasi katamantya’pucchi naṃ| ()

53.

 sutvā tamuddakavasi santamānasau

santaṃhi’daṃ paramamidanti bhāviyaṃ,

sāmaṃ valañ‍janakamarūpabhumikaṃ

jhānaṃ catutthakamavikampamāharī| ()

54. 

 saṃvij‍jarai mamapi manaunikaitanai

saddhādisagguṇaratanāni’massi’va,

aivaṃsaraṃ naciramarūpikaṃ vasi

jhānaṃ labhī vīriyabalainavā’ntamaṃ| ()

55.

 laddhaṃ tayā yamadhigatanti tammayā

āraucitai samaṇa varai’sipuṅgavau,

amhai gaṇaṃ supariharāmu’bhau’timaṃ

vatvā tamācariyadhuraina mānayi| ()

56.

 nā’yaṃ pathau bhavaparimuttiyā siyā

addhābhavai mamapaki bhavaggapattiyā,

aivaṃ vavatthitahadayau mahādayau

nibbij‍jasau tadapagatau’nalaṃiti| ()

57. 

 maukkhaisakau jitavaravāraṇak‍kamau

aikaucaraṃ vasi magadhaisu cārikaṃ,

sainānivissun‍nigamau yahiṃsiyā

taṃ tāpasālayamuruvailamausari| ()

58.

 addakkhi sau hariṇavihaṅgamākulaṃ

mandānilairitatarusaṇḍamaṇḍitaṃ,

nairañ‍jarāsalilapavāhasitalaṃ

pāsādikaṃ paramatapauvanaṃ tahiṃ| ()

59.

 antaujaṭaṃ jaṭilajaṭā’livumbita-

pādambujau vijaṭayituṃ ghaṭaṃ vasī,

attāhitāpanapaṭipattiyā tahiṃ

vij‍jādharai jaṭilavarai pasādayī| ()

60.  sañ‍cāritau janapadacārikaṃ tadā

patvā tapauvanamatha pañ‍cavaggiyā,

bhikkhu mahāpurisamupaṭṭhahiṃsu taṃ

āraddhaduk‍karakiriyaṃ yathābalaṃ| ()

61. dhīrau’tiduk‍karapaṭipattipūrakau

dantāni vīsatidasanaihi vīsati,

tāluṃ nirumhiya rasanāya caitasā

cittaṃ nipīḷayi paritāpayi tahiṃ| ()

62. paggayha muddhani balavā’tidubbalaṃ

nipphīḷayai yathariva dhīmatau tathā,

attāhitāpanapasutassa paggharuṃ

kacchādinā’dhīkatarasaidabindavau| ()

63.  maggaubhavatya’yamiti baudhisiddhiyā

appāṇakaṃ paṭipada mācaraṃ ciraṃ,

vāsaṃ akā vasi mukhatau ca nāsatau

assāsamappaṭipaṭimau’parundhiya| ()

64.

 ruddhaisu taisva’ pihitasautarandhatau

vātau’bhinikkhami adhimattanissanau,

kammāragaggarimukhatau ravau bhusaṃ

niggacchatai abhidhamanaina saiyyathā| ()

65.

 yāvaidanā kharasikharaina jāyarai

sīsassa vijjhanasamayai sukhatthinau,

aivaṃ tadā kaṭhinasiraurujā’bhavuṃ

ruddhānilassa hi mukhakaṇṇanāsatau| ()

66. 

 vātābhighātanasamayai sudhimatau

sīsai’bhavuṃ punarapisisavaidanā,

daḷhaina yau sirasi varattakaina yaṃ

daḷhaṃ dadai yathariva sisavaiṭhanaṃ| ()

67.

 sammā nirumhitamukhakaṇṇanāsatau

dhīrau samīraṇamuparundhicu’ttariṃ,

gabbhantaraṃ kharataravaidanā’turaṃ

vātā’bhimanthiya parikantayuṃ tatau| ()

68.

 gaughātakau caturatarau vikattayai

kucchiṃ gavaṃ tikhiṇavikantanaina cai,

ruddhātilaiha’nariyamaggagāminau

jātā tathā kharatara kucchivaidanā| ()

69. 

 appānakaṃ punarapi jhānamācaraṃ

vīrau samīraṇa muparundhi sabbathā,

cīntubbhavaṃ sakamukhakaṇṇanāsagaṃ

tainā’si kāyikadarathau dhitīmatau| ()

70.

 daḷhaṃ ubhau carapurisā mahabbalā

bāhāsu gaṇhiya purisaṃ’tidubbalaṃ,

aṅgārakāsuyamahitāpayanticai

sau tādisiṃ anubhavi dukkhavaidanaṃ| ()

71.

 khittaṃ kaliṅgaramivakāvidaivatā

ruddhānilubbhavakharavaidanāturaṃ,

vīraṃ vilaukiya patitaṃ tapauvanai

pabyākaruṃka varapurisau matau iti| ()

72. 

 kālaṃkarautya’yamiti kāci daivatā

naucāhukiṃ taditara daivatā vataṃ,

assai’va gautamasamaṇassa mārisā

āraucayuṃ viharaṇamīdisaṃiti| ()

73.

 yaṃnūna’haṃ paṭipadahaiyyamāyatiṃ

āhārayāpanaharaṇāya sabbasau,

aivaṃ sacintayi karuṇāya cauditā

tā daivatā tuvaṭumupaic‍ca gautamaṃ| ()

74.

 āraucayuṃ yadipana nic‍cabhaujanau-

pacchaidanaṃ samaṇatuvaṃ karissasi,

kāhāma tai mayamitilaumakupatau

dibbaujamaukiriya sariratappaṇaṃ| ()

75.

 ghāsassachaidanavīriyaṃ karaumi cai

yāpainti tā madhura sudhārasaina maṃ,

tainā’bhiyāpanavidhimicchatau satau

taṃkhautapaṃ nabhavati kiṃ musā mamaṃ| ()

76. 

 nālanti sau kuhanavasaina daivatā-

vimhāpanaiti’ha nijadaihatappaṇaṃ,

aivaṃ anussariya’nuvāsaraṃ vasī

āhāramāhari virasaṃ parittakaṃ| ()

77.

 svācailakau vicarikarāpalaikhaṇau

ācāramuttya’bhavi nacaihitiṭṭhikau,

uddissakaṃ abhihaṭakaṃ nimantanaṃ

nāsādayi piṭakakalaupikumbhikaṃ| ()

78. 

 sau daṇḍamuggaramusalai’ḷakantaraṃ

pāyantigabbhinipanitīhi cā’haṭaṃ,

sāmakkhikāvisaya muhin‍namaikikaṃ

saṅkittinaudanamapi nābhisādayī| ()

79.

 sauvīrakaṃ napivi suraṃ namairayaṃ

sukkhāmakaṃ yadapi tikauvisuddhikaṃ,

sau macchamaṃsakavikatiṃ paṭikkhipi

appaikadā tapasi nirāmagandhikau| ()

80. 

 sau sattatauppabhuti kamaina hāpayaṃ

yāvaikamāhari kabalaṃ balatthikau,

sau sattatauppabhuti kamaina hāpayaṃ

aikaṃ kulaṃ upagami yāva bhikkhituṃ| () (yamakabandhanaṃ)

81.

 aikāya dīhipi ticatūhi pañ‍cahi

din‍naṃ paṭiggahi chahi dattisattahi,

aikāhikappabhutikamaddhamāsikaṃ

mūlaṃ sayaṃ patitaphalaṃ pabhuñ‍ji sau| ()

82.

 sāmākataṇḍulamathasākamaddakaṃ

nīvārakuṇḍakahaṭadaddulādikaṃ,

piñ‍ñākagaumayatiṇa jhāmakaudanaṃ

vīrau mahāvikaṭamapānubhuñ‍ji sau| ()

83. 

 thaukaṃ pivi pakasatamitaṃ haraiṇuka-

yūsaṃ tathā canaka kulatthamuggajaṃ,

sau appabhaujanaparamau sajīvitaṃ

aikaina yāpayi tilataṇḍulaina’pi| ()

84.

 sāṇammasāṇa?jina’jinakkhipacchava-

dussaṃ tirīṭakakusavākacīrakaṃ,

sau kaisakambalamapivāḷakambala

mauḷukapakkhikaphalakānya’dhārayi| ()

85. 

 dubbaṇṇanattakamayamaggapuggalau

appaikadā paridahi paṃsukūlakaṃ,

attantapauvaraṇa parāyaṇau bhavi

sau massukuntalatanulaumalaucakau| ()

86.

 ubbhaṭṭhakau’bhavi parivaj‍jitāsanau

uk‍kaṭṭhamuk‍kuṭikavataṃ adhiṭṭhahī,

uddhaggakaṇṭakavīsamai apassayai

saiyyaṃ akā tadupariṭhānacaṅkamaṃ| ()

87.

 sau sāyatatiyakamudakāvarauhaṇa-

yuttau pavāhayitumaghaṃ samussa hī,

ātāpayaṃ iti paritāpayaṃ sakaṃ

daihaṃ ciraṃ parihari pāpabhīrukau| ()

88. 

 yau naikahāyanagaṇikau’tthi’tinduka-

rukkhassa khau papaṭikajātakhāṇukau,

aivaṃ tathā papaṭikajātamattanau

gattañ‍ca san‍nivitarajaumalaṃ bhavi| ()

89.

 sauvā parau natu parivaj‍jayī rajau-

jal‍lāni kaj‍jalamalināni pāṇinā,

daihaṃ subhaujanajahanaina jaj‍jaraṃ

tailaṃ vilaipiya rajasā’bhithūlayī| ()

90.

 sau dvai’kapassayikavataṃ papūrayī

āpānakau’ bhavi phalakai’pi thaṇḍilai,

saiyyaṃ akāka vihari vivaikakāmavā

ajjhaugahaṃ adutiyakau mahāvanaṃ| ()

91. 

 pāṇai imai visamagatai’tikhuddakai

nā’haṃ vadhissamiti paṭic‍ca’nuddayaṃ,

ussāvamaddanahirabhīrutāya sau

nāthau abhik‍kami ca satau paṭik‍kami| ()

92.

 nin‍natthalā vanagahanā vanāsayau

nin‍natthalaṃ vanagahanaṃ migau yathā,

hītau vipassiya vipinaupagai janai

tāsābhibhū papanati aivamaivakhau| ()

93.

 disvāna luddakavanakammikādayau

gaupālakai tiṇanaḷakaṭṭhahārakai,

mācaddasaṃ ahamapi taitya’yaṃjanau

mā maṃ vipassatu samadhiṭṭhahaṃ vataṃ| ()

94.

 aikauvasaṅgaṇikavihārabhītiyā

nin‍natthalā vanagahanā tapaunidhī,

nin‍natthalaṃ vanagahanaṃ papāta sau

tassāsi tādisi pavivittatā tadā| ()

95. 

 yasmiṃvanai caratamavītarāgīnaṃ

raumuggamau caraṇatalāni kamparai,

disvāna bhiṃsaṇakavanaṃ tathāvidhaṃ

ajjhaugahaṃ vasi pavivaikakāmavā| ()

96.

 ussāvapātatasamayai’ntaraṭṭhakai

haimantikai sisiratarāya rattiyā,

abbhāvakāsika mabhipūrayi vataṃ

kicchaṃ vasi vasi vanasaṇḍagau divā| ()

97.

 gimhautu pacchimadivasantarai divā

abbhāvakāsikadhutadhammapūrakau,

rattiṃ vanai vihari javaṭṭhikānyu’pa

nissāya sau asayi susānabhūmiyaṃ| ()

98. 

 buddhaṅkuraṃ upagamiyau’ṭhubhanti’pi

aumuttayantipi rajasau’kiranti’pi,

gaumaṇḍalā savaṇakhilaisu daṇḍakaṃ

datvā vadāpayitumupak‍kamanti’pi| ()

99.

 sauvādhivāsayi satimā upaikkhakau

taṃvaidanaṃ kaṭukaka manañ‍ñavaidiyaṃ,

dukkhai sukhai sumati tulāsarikkhakau

bālaisu taisva’pi navikaupayimanaṃ| ()

100.

 āhāratappaṇavidhinā visuddhi’ti

aikai vadanti’ha samaṇāñ‍ñatitthiyā,

kaulādibhaujanavikatiṃ tathāvidhaṃ

appicchatāya’nubhavi suddhikāmavā| ()

101. 

 appaujabhaujanavikatiṃ pabhuñ‍jatau

khattiṃsalakkhaṇasiriyā samuj‍jalaṃ,

kāyau suraddumarucirau’dhimattaka-

sīmānamaṭṭhikataca māpadhīmatau| ()

pabbānivā asitalanāsva’sitika-

val‍lisu un‍nata’vanatāni saiyyathā,

āsuṃ tathā karacaraṇādikāni’pi

tassu’n‍nataunata’vayavāti viggahai| ()

103.

 maukkhaisinau karabhapadaṃva nissiriṃ

nimmaṃsa mānisada mahū sirimatau|

tassu’ntatāvanatakapiṭṭhikaṇṭakau

āsi yathāvalayitavaṭṭanāvali| ()

104. 

 gaupāṇasi sithilitabandhanā jara-

sāḷāya haiṭṭhupariṭhitai’va dhīmatau,

nimmaṃsalauhitakakalaibarai pya’va-

bhaggā bhavuṃ pavisamaphāsukāvalī| ()

105.

 appaṃ kubhaujanavikatiṃ pabhuñ‍janau

tassa’kkhikūpagayugalakkhitārakā,

auk‍kāyikā abhavu magādhagā tadā

gambhīrakupagadakatārakāka viya| ()

106.

 vātātapaina’ bhiphusitau yathā’maka-

cchin‍nau’bhisamphuṭani alābu tittakau,

sīsacchavī sukhumachavissa bhaujanau-

pacchaidanaina’bhiphuṭitā ṭhitaṃ tathā| ()

107. 

 tassaudaracchavi pana piṭṭhikaṇṭakaṃ

al‍liyi sau muni malamuttamaucakau,

aukuj‍jitau paripati pūtimūlaka-

laumāni tabbapugalitāni bhūmiyaṃ| ()

108.

 sau piṭṭhikaṇṭakamavasaṅgapāṇinā

kucchicchaviṃ phusitumitau parāmasi,

sau piṭṭhikaṇṭakamavasaṅgapāṇinā

kucchicchaviṃ phusitumitau parāmasi| () (yamakabandhanaṃ)

109.

 kālaunukhau varapurisau’ti nau tathā

sāmaunukhau napi nanumaṅguracchavi,

āsuṃ tadā kavimatikathāparā narā

disvā malaggahitamasaubhanacchaviṃ| ()

110. 

 yai santī sampatī samaṇā’bhavuṃ purai

attantapā tapasi anāgatai siyuṃ,

tai vaidanaṃ kaṭukamitaudhikaṃ kimu

vaidainti vaidayu mabhivaidayissarai| ()

111.

 īhāya duk‍karakiriyāyi’māya’pi

naiva’jjhagā yamariyañāṇadassanaṃ,

attūpatāpanakasirassa kaivalaṃ

bhāgī bhavi anariyamaggagāmisau| ()

112.

 saṃsārai sātisārai barataradarathai saṃsaraṃ sac‍casandhau

khaidaivaidaisi daivāsiranarasaraṇauaisayaṃsattasantiṃ,

dhīrauvīrauvarauyaupabhavabhavabhayaupāpatāpabbipattau

āyaugaṃ yaugiyaugī parihari hirimāaivamaivacchavassaṃ| () (muttāhāra bandhanaṃ)

iti maidhānandābhidhānaina yatinā viracitai sakala kavijana hakayānandadānanidānai jinavaṃsadīpai avidurainidānai mahābaudhisattassa mahā padhānānuyaugappavattiparidīpau aikādasamausaggau|

1. 

 kāmaṃ kāmasukhal‍likā’nuyaugau

hīnau’natthakarau’tya’nai(’karūpaṃ),

cintaitvāna tapauvanaṃ vimānā

tvaṃ siddhatthu’pagamma kāhasai kiṃ| ()

2. 

 kaunāma’ntu’patāpanā’nuyuttau

pattau hauti sukhappadaṃ kadāci,

tasmā attu’patāpanā’nuyaugau

hīnau’natthakarau’ti cintayassu| ()

3.

 attānaṃ sayamaivamauvaditvā

piṇḍāyā’nugharaṃ caritva laddhaṃ,

bhattaṃ bhuttavatau sakamhi kāyai

āsuṃ pākatikāni lakkhaṇāni, ()

4.

 hīnantadvayavaj‍janaina jātu

ñāṇuk‍kaṃsagatamhi tamhi vīrai,

baudhāyū’pasamāya nibbidāya

uk‍kaṭṭhaṃ paṭipattimācarantai| ()

5. 

 chabbassānya’niduk‍karaṃ karitvā

baudhiṃ nājjhagatau subhaujanāni,

bhuñ‍jantau kimu kubbatai’ padhānā

vibbhantau iti pañ‍cavaggiyāyaṃ| ()

6.

 madaditvā sikataṃ sinaihaladdhā

kaivā’suṃ samaṇaṃ hi’maṃ upaic‍ca,

kau mūḷhau’dhigamādhigantumicchai

cintaitvā migadāyamautariṃsu| ()

7.

 sainānīnigamai tadāni saiṭṭhi-

dhītā sāmikulaṃ alaṅkatā’si,

bhārainā’vanataṅginī kucānaṃ

haṃsivā’lasagāminī sujātā| ()

8. 

 jātai patthitapatthanāsamiddhai

rukkhā’dhiggahitāya daivatāya,

kātuṃ sābalikammaka mānayitvā

dhainū laṭṭhivanaupagā sahassaṃ| ()

9.

 tāsaṃ pañ‍casatāni duddhakhīraṃ

pāyaitvā katapuna yāvatā’ḍhadhainū,

khīrānaṃ parivattanaṃ vidhāya

pac‍cūsamhi dudauha tā’ḍhadhainū| ()

10.

 missaitvā sayamaivava duddhakhīraṃ

pāyāsaṃ pacituṃ samārabhittha,

daivā tattha sudhārasaṃ khipitvā

ārakkhādimakaṃsu uddhanasmiṃ| ()

11.

 tassā’si himāvācalau’padhānaṃ

pal‍laṅkau pathavitalaṃ ahū cai,

hatthā pacchimapubbasāgaraisu

pādā dakkhiṇasāgarai bhaviṃsū| ()

12. 

 uggantvā tiṇajāti nāhirandhā

tassā’hac‍ca ṭhitā nabhaṃ asaisaṃ,

chādaisuṃ caraṇuṭṭhitā’ssa kaṇha-

sīsā’jānuyugā’pya’kaṇhakīṭā| ()

13.

 cattārau sakuṇā catuddisāhi

patvā tappadapañ‍jaraṃ vivaṇṇā,

saitā’suṃ puthumīḷhapabbatassa

sīsai caṅkami sau alimpamānau| ()

14.

 ic‍caivaṃ sumati’ṭṭhapākadāni

passitvā supināni pañ‍ca niṭṭhaṃ,

pattau aj‍ja bhavāmahanti buddhau

nigraudhaṃ samupaic‍ca san‍nisīdi| ()

15. 

 saudhaitaṃ sahitā tu puṇṇadāsī

pac‍cūsai vaṭamūlapubbasailai,

taṃ laukaikaraviṃ virājamānaṃ

disvā’vauca sujātamaitamatthaṃ| ()

16.

 lakkhagghaṃ haripātimāharitvā

sā āvaj‍jayi pak‍kabhājanaṃ sau,

pāyāsau vinivaṭṭitau ṭhitau’si

tāyaṃ paukkharapattatauva’tauyaṃ| ()

17.

 sā añ‍ñāya suvaṇṇapātiyā taṃ

chādaitvā muditā pasantacittā

gantvā maṇḍana maṇḍitā sasīsai

katvā pūjayi bhaujanaṃ sujātā| ()

18.

 kālaṃ aittakamaivabaudhisattaṃ

nātik‍kamma vidhātudin‍napattau,

sampattau’si adassanaṃ tatau taṃ

pātiṃ sauṇṇamayaṃ paṭiggahaitvā| ()

19. 

 haṃsālimalinīkatāravinda-

raiṇucchan‍nasunīlanīrapurā,

yā nairañ‍jaravissutā’si tāya

naj‍jātīramagañ‍ji sattasārau| ()

20.

 pāyāsāmisapuṇṇasauṇṇapātiṃ

kāsāvāni jinaṅkurau ṭhapaitvā,

tīrai tāya savantiyā nahātuṃ

titthaṃ gandhagajaurivau’tarittha| ()

21.

 raulambākulanīlanīrajaihi

saivālaihi nadījalaṃ sunilaṃ,

nikkhantaj‍jutisañ‍cayaihi daihā

autiṇṇassa jagāma piñ‍jarattaṃ| ()

22. 

 gaṅgākāmini kañ‍jaraiṇugandha-

cuṇṇaṃ tuṅgataraṅgabāhunā taṃ,

bhattāraṃ salilaina sītalaina

makkhaitvāsunahāpayantī’vā’si| ()

23.

 tulyaṃ tabbadanambujaina laddhuṃ

āyantaṃ raviraṃsisaṅgamaina,

haṃsassaiṇi saraujakausarāsiṃ

saṃdūsaisi āvāriyau hi pākau| ()

24.

 tīrai sārasacak‍kavākapakkhī

sausāya’ssavisāritaṃ’sapakkhā,

gambhīrambhasi mattamāhariṃsu

mañ‍ñai nik‍karuṇāya aittakantī| ()

25. 

 tuṇḍai maṇḍitapuṇḍarīkadaṇḍau

pakkhai kairavapaṇḍarai pasārī,

nāthassu’bbahi mattahaṃsarājā

saitacchattavibhutimuttamaṅgai| ()

26.

 vattamhaujapalaubhitālicak‍kaṃ

cakkhavāpāthagataṃ jinaṅkurassa,

saṃdassaisi padhānabhuṭhitassa

nilasmiṃ kasiṇamhi bhūtibhāraṃ| ()

27.

 vaiyyāvac‍cakarārivāpagāyaṃ

saivālādimalāpanaina mīnā,

pādañ‍candagamīnalakkhaṇassa

tassa’ggai vimalikariṃsu vāriṃ| ()

28. 

 uttiṇṇassa visālasāḷasākhī-

sākhāhatthapuṭaihi puñknāya,

gattaṃ mandasugandhagandhavāha-

vatthaṃ sāḷavanaṅganā adāsi| ()

29.

 laukindau parimaṇḍalaṃ nivatthau

chādaitvāna timaṇḍala’ntariyaṃ,

bandhitvaupari kāyabandhanampi

kāsāvaṃ paridhāyi paṃsukūlaṃ| ()

30.

 pāyāsassa nirūdakassa ūna-

pañ‍ñāsappamitai vidhāya piṇḍai,

pācīnābhimukhau nisaj‍ja naj‍jā

tīrai tāya akāsi bhattakic‍caṃ| ()

31. 

 pāyāsau madhurau’yamassa satta-

sattāhaṃ paṭividdhabaudhinau hi,

aujāsamapharaṇāya ṭhānamāsi

tasmā sau pavihāsi nibbihaisaṃ| ()

32.

 bujjhaiyyaṃ yadi baudhimaj‍ja sauhaṃ

uddhaṃsautamayaṃ suvaṇṇapāti,

gaṅgāyaṃ khipaki gacchatūti vatvā

dhīmā dakkhiṇahatthagaṃ tamagghaṃ| ()

33.

 sautaṃ bhindiya sā savantimajjhai

ṭhatvā pāti yatau asītihatthaṃ,

uddhaṃsautamupaic‍ca san‍nimuj‍ji

tasmā sau’pi nimuj‍ji pītinaj‍jaṃ| ()

34.

 nāgānaṃ bhavanaṃ upaic‍ca tiṇṇaṃ

buddhānaṃ panimamhi bhaddakappai,

sāpātiparibhuttasauṇṇapāti

ghaṭṭaitvāna ṭhitā katānurāvā| ()

35.

 taṃ dīghāyukakālanāgarājā

sutvā saddamathaj‍japai’kabuddhau,

uppan‍nauti jinaṃ abhitthavantau

aṭṭhāsi thutigītikāsataihi| ()

36.

 chāyābaddhavisālasāḷasālaṃ

patvāsāḷavanaṃ nadīsamīpai,

ājīvaṭṭhamasīlasaṃvaraina

ādauyaiva visuddhakāyavācau| ()

37.

 katvāṭṭhārasapiṭṭhikaṇṭakānaṃ

kauṭīnaṃpaṭipādanaṃ kamaina,

pal‍laṅkassanisaj‍jabandhanaina

kammaṭṭhānasatiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| ()

38. 

 ānāpānasatiṃpariggahaitvā

nibbattaisimalaggahītapubbai,

rūpārūpasamādhayau’ṭṭhapañ‍cā-

bhiñ‍ñāyau vasitāca sau vasīsau ()

39.

 jhānassādaratau divāvihāraṃ

katvā sāḷavanai surāsuraihi,

dhīrau maggamalaṅkataṃ karīva

gantuṃ autariyatrabaudhimūlaṃ| ()

40.

 lājādīkusumaihivippakiṇṇau

muttāpaṇḍaravāḷukātthatau sau,

maggau tuṅgataraṅga bhaṅgahāri

lakkhīvāsapayaudadhīri’vā’si| ()

41. 

 majjhā’raupitapaṅkajābhirāmaṃ

muttādāmasamākulaṃ samantā,

kaṇṇaulambasuvaṇṇaghaṇṭamassa

daiṭā dibbavitāna mukkhipiṃsu| ()

42.

 laukatthaṃ karaṇāya cauditasmiṃ

tasmiṃ laukadivākarai’kavīrai,

gacchantai sahajātabaudhimūlaṃ

ālaukau udapādi sabbalaukai| ()

43.

 āyantaṃ tiṇhārakau pathamhi

disvā sautthiyanāmabhūsurau taṃ,

pādāsi tiṇamuṭṭhiyau’ṭṭhamattā

nāthau tāni tiṇāni sampaṭicchi| ()

44. 

 vattattai varapāṭihāriyamhi

maggai gandhagajau’ca jamhamānau,

sampattau karuṇākalattabhattā

sambaudhādhigamāya baudhimūlaṃ| ()

45.

 tassausīdaṭhitaṃ’va cak‍kavāḷaṃ

haiṭṭhā dakkhiṇatau’ttarānanassa,

pañ‍ñāyu’ttaracak‍kavāḷamuddhaṃ

laṅghitvānaṭhitaṃ’va ābhavaggaṃ| ()

46.

 aivaṃ pacchimamuttaraṃ disampi

aṭṭhānanti padakkhiṇaṃ karauntau,

gantvā ṭhānavaraṃ puratthimasmiṃ

aṭṭhāsi vasi pacchimānanau sau| ()

47. 

 dhīmā dakkhiṇapāṇipal‍lavaina

aggai tāni tiṇāni sattharī sau,

tamhā cuddasahatthamuppatitvā

pal‍laṅkau samalaṅkarī dumindaṃ| ()

48.

 dakkhau kārupavīṇacittakārau

kātuṃ vā’likhituṃ yathānasak‍kā,

aṭṭhaṃsu haritāni santhatāni

aivaṃ tāni tiṇāni uppatitvā| ()

49.

 maṃsādī upasussarai nahārū

aṭṭhīcaipyavasissarai sarīrai|

muñ‍caiyyaṃ caturāsavaihi yāva

bhindissāmi natāvimaṃ ahanti| ()

50. 

 daḷhaṃ cintiya daḷhamānasau sau

pācīnābhimukhau dumindabandhaṃ|

katvā piṭṭhigataṃ nisīdi baudhi-

pal‍laṅkamhi yugandharai ravī’va| ()

51.

 laukaisau sasimaṇḍalāvabhāsaṃ

saitacchattamadhārayī tadañ‍ñai,

suddhāvāsatalaṭṭhadaivatā taṃ

pūjaisuṃ makuṭappitañ‍jalīhi| ()

52.

 yai rūpāvacarai vasanti daivā

tai ca’ñ‍ñatra asañ‍ñasattadaivai,

sampatvā vajirāsanai nisin‍naṃ

pūjaisuṃ kusumākulañ‍jalīhi ()

53. 

 aikac‍cai paranimmitādilaukā

patvā bhattibharā’marā mahiṃsu,

pūjābhaṇḍasamābhikiṇṇahatthā

mārāriṃ tahimāpa pāpimā kiṃ| ()

54.

 yai nimmāṇaratimhi nij‍jarā tai|

patvā gandhakaraṇḍamaṇḍalaihi,

sampūjaisumalaṅkataṅghipīṭhaṃ

naṃ saiṭṭhaṃ vijayāsanaupaviṭṭhaṃ| ()

55.

 aṭṭhāsi tusitālayā sasainau

patvā santusitavhadaivarājā,

vijaintau harimaura piñchapuñ‍ja-

saubhaṃ kañ‍canatālavaṇṭapantiṃ| ()

56.

 patvā yāmasurālayā sasainau

saṃvijaisi suyāma daivarājā,

dhīraṃ sauṇṇapaṇāḷikānipāta-

dhārāsan‍nibhacārucāmaraihi| ()

57. 

 daivindau vijayuttarākhyasaṅkhaṃ

vīsaṃ hatthasataṃ dhamītadañ‍ñai,

pūjaisuṃ tamupaic‍ca kauviḷāra-

pupphādīhi ca tāvatiṃsadaivā| ()

58.

 yakkhādīhi purakkhatā’pi daiva-

rājānau caturau catuddisāsu,

rakkhaṃ saṃvidahiṃsu daivalaukā

taṃ patvāna vinaṭṭhalaumahaṭṭhaṃ| ()

59.

 vādaintau saramaṇḍalaṃ vidhāya

vīṇaṃ pañ‍casikhau’pi baiḷuvākhyaṃ,

taṃ sampūjayi kālanāgarājā

thaumaintau thutigītikāsataihi| ()

60. 

 aivaṃ kāhaḷabhairisaṅkhavīṇā-

ghaṇṭāvījanichattacāmaraihi,

nac‍cādīhicalājapañ‍camaihi

dīpaddhupadhajaihi mānayuṃ taṃ| ()

61.

 siddhatthau paṭisiddhamāradhaiyyau

kattuṃ attavasai sadaivalaukaṃ,

sutvā vāyamatīti baudhimaṇḍai

mārau tatra samārabhitthagantuṃ| ()

62.

 tasmiṃ khau samayai bhayāvahāni

mārassau’taraṇāya kāraṇāni,

cakkhacāpāthagatāni dun‍nimitta-

rūpādīni tilaukalaucanassa| ()

63. 

 sukkha’mhaudhararāvabhairirāva-

vipphārābadhirīkatambarampi,

bhīmaṃ vij‍julatā’sighaṭṭaṇaihi

mārassā’havambḍalābhamāha| ()

64.

 mārassā’gamanañ‍jasai rajauva

vājīnaṃ khuraghaṭṭaṇaina jātau,

uk‍kāpātasataṃ janaisi tassa

cakkhavāniṭṭhaphakhalaṃ disāsu ḍāhau| ()

65.

 vaihāsai vicaruṃ kabandharūpā

kākaulā balipuṭṭhavāyasārī,

un‍nādiṃsu kharānilau pavāyī

abbhuṭṭhāsi rajau disāsu dhūmau| ()

66.

 ālaukau vigatau ghaṇandhakārau

autiṇṇau mahikāsamābhikiṇṇau,

ākāsau pathavi bhūsaṃ pakampi

maighacchan‍nadinaṃ dinaṃ babhūva| ()

67. 

 siddhatthañhi asiddhamatthamaitaṃ

kātuṃ assavamārakiṅkarāmai,

vatvai’thā’ti pajāpatī sasainau

tatthaiva’ntaradhāyi tāvadaiva| ()

68.

 sā saitā puratau pajāpatissa

āsī bārasayaujanaṃ vinaddhā,

aivaṃ dakkhiṇavāmanau ca lauka-

dhātvantāvadhīmāsi pacchatau’pi| ()

69.

 uddhaṃ sā navayaujanappamāṇā

saddau bhūmividāraṇauri’vā si,

sau’paḍḍhaṃ satayaujanaṃ babhūva

uc‍caṃ sau girimaikhalau gajindau| ()

70. 

 nāhaisuṃ parisāsu nimmitāsuṃ

dvaiyaudhā sadisāyudhādadhānā,

tabyāsaina alañhi laumahaṃsau

yassā’nussaraṇaina cai siyā mai| ()

71.

 māpaitvā sahasā sahassabāhuṃ

gaṇhitvā vividhāyudhāni taihi,

ārūḷhau girimaikhalaṃ sasainau

mārau pāturahausi baddhavairau| ()

72.

 daivaisau yasasā samaṃ sakaina

saitacchatta magañchi saṃharitvā,

daivaisau yasasā samaṃ sakaina

saṅakkhaṃ piṭṭhigataṃ vidhāya dhāvī| () (yamakabandhanaṃ)

73. 

 saṅkaucā’nanakāhalau jagāma

pātālaṃ khalu kālanāgarājā,

vīṇādauṇisakhau sakhānapaikhau

tamhā pañ‍casikhau kalahuṃ palāyi| ()

74.

 disvā mārabalaṃ samausarantaṃ

sampattā janatā palāyi bhītā,

sausīhau’va vihāsi sakyasīhau

aikau kamārakarindakumhabhaidī| ()

75.

 passitvā’dharakantibhāramassa

vattamhauruha mindirāvihāraṃ,

siddhatthaina samau nacatthi laukai

ic‍caivaṃ kalimā’ha mārasainaṃ| ()

76. 

 aitasasā’bhimukhā mayaṃ kadāci

nausak‍kauma’bhiyujjhitunti tātā,

vatvā uttarapassatau samārau

khandhāvāramabandhi baddhavairau|

77.

 disvā’jjhauttharamānamārasainaṃ

ārakkhāvaraṇaṃ thiraṃ vidhāya,

khandhāvāramabandhi saupi vīrau

jaituṃ taṃ dasapāramī bhaṭaihi| ()

78.

 mārau bhudharamairucak‍kavāḷai

rukkhādīni vicuṇṇituṃ samatthaṃ,

khaubhaitvā bhuvanattayaṃ disāsu

uṭṭhāpaisi samīraṇaṃ sughauraṃ| ()

79.

 vātau pāramidhāmavāritau sau

nittaijaṃ palayānilassamaupi,

pattau cāmaramandamārutauva

tandaihautuparissamaṃ jahāsi| ()

80. 

 dhārāvaigavihin‍nabhūmibhāgaṃ

gambhīrā’sanirāvanibbharā’ghaṃ,

mārau māpayi tuṅgavīcabhaṅgaṃ

vassaughaṃ paripātarukkhasailaṃ| ()

81.

 vīrau pāramipāḷibandhanaina

rakkhaṃ bandhi nijantabhāvakhaittai,

tainau’ghau vipathaṅgamau vipakkha-

saināyā’si pavāhaṇai nidānaṃ| ()

82.

 taijaukhaṇḍasamānamattanau sau

tattaṃ paj‍jalitaṃ sajautibhūtaṃ,

māpaitvau’palavassamappasayhaṃ

jhāpaituṃ tamupak‍kamittha mārau| ()

83. 

 mārassaiva patantamuttamaṅgai

ghauraṃ pāramivāyuvaigaruddhaṃ,

taṃvassaṃ vajirāsanūpacārai

pūjāpupphaguḷattanaṃ jagāma| ()

84.

 assaddhau visadiddhatiṇhadhāraṃ

ādittaṃ pihitambarau’daraṃ sau,

māpaisi asisattitaumarādi-

vassaṃ sabbadisānipātamānaṃ| ()

85.

 tasmiṃ pāramivammavammitasmiṃ

vissaṭṭhā’yudhavuṭṭhi kuṇṭhitaggā,

patvā sampati pupphavuṭṭhibhāvaṃ

tappādāsanamatthakai papāta| ()

86. 

 mārau vic‍ciṭacic‍ciṭāyamānaṃ

saṃvaṭṭānalakhaṇḍavibbhamaṃ sau,

vassaṅgāramayaṃ savipphuliṅgaṃ

uṭṭhāpaisi palāsapakupphavaṇṇaṃ| ()

87.

 khippaṃpāramimantajappanaina

aṅgārāninivāritānitāni,

taṃbuddhaṅkurapuṇṇacandabimbaṃ

saivantānivikiṇṇabhānivāsuṃ| ()

88.

 bhasmīkātumalantimāravairiṃ

dhūmākiṇṇamaniggataggijālaṃ,

māraubhairavarāvamussadābha-

mabbhuṭṭhāpayikhārabhasmavassaṃ| ()

89.

 saitāmuddhanivippakiṇṇabhasmiṃ

taṃvassaṃcitapāramībalaina,

patvācandanagandhacuṇṇabhāvaṃ

mārārissapapātapādamūlai| ()

90. 

 asmiṃ guvalayālavāḷagabbhai

sampātānaladaḍḍhavaṇṇudhāraṃ,

uttāsāvahamattanau’pi kaṇhau

vassāpaisi uḷāravaṇṇuvassaṃ| ()

91.

 disvā’ṅakghīnakharāliraṃsigaṅgā-

tīrussāritavaṇṇurāsi massa,

aṅgārau’va’dhikaudhapāvakaina

kaṇhau kaṇhatarau’si jhāpitattau| ()

92.

 dhūpāyantamavīcimac‍cimantaṃ

samphuṭṭhaṃ ghanaphaiṇaṇabubbulaihi,

vassaṃ paṅkamayaṃ bhusaṃ nimuggau

mārau māpayi pañ‍cakāmapaṅkai| ()

93. 

 tasmiṃpāramisattīsitibhūtai

paṅkai candanapaṅkabhāvayātai,

mārau passiya phul‍lapaṅkajāhaṃ

kaupā paṅkahatānanaurivāsi| ()

94.

 mārāriṃ iminā hanāmahantī

sau laukantariyandhakāraghauraṃ,

mārau sūvividāriyaṃ disāsu

uṭṭhāpaisighanandhakārakhandhaṃ| ()

95.

 sau’yaṃ pāramijātaraṃsijāla-

bhin‍nā’saisatamaujinaṅkuraiṇau,

pal‍laṅkaudayapabbatauditau’si

kāmaṃ māratusārasausanāya| ()

96. 

 aitaṃ gaṇhatha bandhathā’ti vatvā

niṭṭhaṃ kappamavaṇṇiyaṃ kavīhi,

saddhiṃ mārabalainu’pāgatau sau

kuddhau yuddhamakā pamattabandhu| ()

97.

 taṃ disvā’calanic‍calaṭṭhamaisa

pal‍laṅkau nacapāpuṇāti tuyhaṃ,

mayhaṃ hai’su’pakapakpataiva tasmā

asmā vuṭṭhahathā’vusautya’vauca| ()

98.

 aikā’pī samatiṃsapāramīnaṃ

pal‍laṅkatthamapūritā tayā’ti,

vuttai sau khipi nij‍jitau’racak‍kaṃ

cak‍kaṃ cak‍kavaraṅkitassa sīsai| ()

99.

 taṃ cak‍kāyudhamujjhitappabhāvaṃ

yuddhai laddhajayassa mārajissa,

ussisamhi varāsanūpacārai

saitacchantamivussitaṃ rarāja| ()

100. 

 tuyhaṃ sañ‍cinanamhi pāramīnaṃ

kau sakkhī’ti ahañ‍ca sakkhihaumi,

sakkhī’ganti pavattamārasainā-

ghausau bhumividāraṇauri’vāsi| ()

101.

 dāpaintaunijasakkhimuggataijau

bāhuṃtāvapasārayī pavirau,

sakkhīhantīvadaṃ’va mārasainaṃ

taj‍jaintau’va babhūva bhumicālau| ()

102.

 mātaṅgau girimaikhalau chitāriṃ

vandantau’vapapāta jan‍nukaihi,

mārau laddhaparājayau nivattha-

vatthassā’pi anissarau palāyi| ()

103. 

 ghauramārabalavāraṇādhipa-

mānadappanibhakumbhadāraṇau,

baudhimūlavajirāsanaupari

kaisarīva virarāja māraji| ()

iti maidhānandābhidhānaina yatinā viracitai sakala kavakijana hadayānandadānanidānai jinavaṃsadīpai avidūrai nidānai daivaputta mārabala viddhaṃsana pavattiparidīpau dvādasamau saggau|

1.

 nikhilamāratusāravisausinau

athajinaṅkuradīdhitimālinau,

ravi katāvasarau’va’parācalaṃ

(duta’vilambita) gāmimupāgamī| ()

2. 

 jaladhivārisinaihasupuritai

abhavi paṃsumahītalamal‍lakai,

paṇihitāparabhudharavattikā-

jalitadīpasikhai’va nabhaumaṇi| ()

3.

 udayapabbatagabbhasamubbhavaṃ

sakayasaupaṭibimbasamaṃsubhaṃ,

sapadi tappamukhai sasimaṇḍalaṃ

kasiṇamaṇḍalavibbhamamubbahi| ()

4.

 aruṇavaṇṇasudhākara bhākarā

divasasandhivilāsiniyā khaṇaṃ,

parihariṃsu’dayāparabhudhara-

savaṇagaṃ maṇimaṇḍana vibbhamaṃ| ()

5. 

 ravidhurā vidhurā sarasīvadhu

kamalakaumalakausapuṭañ‍jalī,

upavanai pavanai’ritabhūruhā

panamitānamitāva tapaudhanaṃ| () (yamakabandhanaṃ|)

6.

 aparasāgaramuddhani bhāsuraṃ

timirajālaparaṃ ravimaṇḍalaṃ,

mukulitamburuhassirimāhari

bhamaracak‍kabharaṃ sarasūpari| ()

7.

 lavaṇavāridhikācasarāvakai

aparabhūdhara kūṭa bhujappitā,

sūriyamaṇḍalapāti nimuj‍jiya

purimayāmamukhaṃnakimāhari| ()

8. 

 maṇipabhāruṇa bhākara maṇḍalaṃ

tamanubhuya mahambudhirāhunā,

mukhagataṃvamitaṃ viyaka lauhitaṃ

jaladarāji rarāja dinac‍cayai| ()

9.

 vitatamaighapabhāhi muhuṃ muhuṃ

kaḷita pāṭala pal‍lava sampadaṃ,

vanaghaṭaṃ viṭapantaragaṃ kamā

phūṭatamaupaṭalaṃpariṇāmayī ()

10.

 subhajanaibhajanainirapaikkhinī

vipatinī patinīva rajassalā,

sumadhupai madhupai parivaj‍jayuṃ

kamalinīmalinīkatanirajā| () (yamakabandhanaṃ|)

11. 

 madhumadālikulā makulāvalī

anilabhaṅga taraṅga bhujairitā,

paduminī ramaṇīhi sirimatau

sumahitāmaṇikiṅkiṇisaiṇiva| ()

12.

 rasikapak‍ka phalāphala sālisu

tarusiraisu samausaramānakā,

timirakhaṇḍanibhā badhirīkaruṃ

ravipathaṃ virutaihi vibhaṅgamā| ()

13.

 kumudinīpamadā’thasudhākara-

karasataihi parāmasanāparaṃ,

kusumahāsavilāsadharā bhusaṃ

bhuvanavandiragabbhamalaṅkari| ()

14. 

 himakarau hariṇañ‍janahārinā

nijakaraina nirākari taṅkhaṇai,

sakalalauka’vilaucana sambhavaṃ

ghanatamaupaṭalaṃhisajau yathā| () (silaisabandhanaṃ)

15.

 sapadipāramitāramitāsayau

navama’nussatiyāsatiyā paraṃ,

adhikatā’dhi samādhi samāhitau

purimajātibhavai tibhavai sari| () (yamakabandhanaṃ)

16.

 sumatipādaka jhāna samuṭṭhitau

purimakhandhasamūhamanuk‍kamaṃ,

asarisau’panisin‍najayāsana-

ppabhuti yāvasumaidhabhavāvadhiṃ| ()

17. 

 idhabhavai samanantarajātiyaṃ

tadiyakhandhapabandhamanussari,

ticatupañ‍cachasatta nava’ṭṭhapi

dasapi visatitiṃsati jātiyau| ()

18.

 lahumanussaritāḷisa jātiyau

pabhava khandhavasaina tahiṃtahiṃ,

bhavasataṃbhavupaḍḍhasataṃbhava-

dasasataṃbhavalakkhamathāparaṃ ()

19.

 aparimāṇa yugantagajātiyau

aparimāṇa vivaṭṭagajātiyau,

aparimāṇa yuganta vivaṭṭagā

aparimāṇa guṇau sarijātiyau| ()

20. 

 catusuyaunisusattamanaṭṭhiti-

tibhavapañ‍cagatīsuparibbhamiṃ,

kasirabhāravahau ahamañ‍jasai

sakaṭabhāravahau gavajauyathā| ()

21.

 itisamañ‍ña dharau’simamutra’haṃ

iti nihīnapasattha kulau bhaviṃ,

iti bhaviṃ abhīrūpavirūpimā

itipi bhatta phakhalāphala māhariṃ| ()

22. 

 anubhaviṃkusalākusalārahaṃ

vividhadukkhamadukkha madukkha khaṃ,

dasasatāyu satāyumitaubhaviṃ

itibhavaṃtibhavaṃsamanussari| () (yamakabandhanaṃ)

23.

 itiha yāvasumaidha bhavaṃ sudhī

sumariyā’tigatā’mitajātiyau,

asari saupaṭilaumavasā tatau-

ppabhuti yāva itau tatiyaṃ bhavaṃ| ()

24.

 punaramutra tataubhavatau cutau

samupapaj‍ji manantarajātiyaṃ,

tahimahaṃtusitai tidasālayai

bhavimatij‍juti santusitābhidhau| ()

25. 

 tusitadaivanikāyasamatvayau

paramarūpa vilāsadharau’bhaviṃ,

sumadhurāmatamāhari midisaṃ

anubhaviṃsukhamindriya gaucaraṃ| ()

26.

 samupajīvimamānusahāyana-

catusahassa mahaṃtusitālayai,

marugaṇamburuhāsanayācanaṃ

iha paṭic‍ca tatau bhavatau cutau| ()

27.

 jananirājiniyā maṇicaitiyai

sugatadhātumivā’samakucchiyaṃ,

ravikulai paṭisandhimahaṃ pitra-

narapatiṃ adhikic‍ca samappayiṃ| ()

28.

 itiha rūpamarūpamanādikaṃ

viparivattati vattati nāparaṃ,

visatiyā sati yāva dhiyā’sanaṃ

vihatamauhatamau’si bhavai sudhī| () (yamakabandhanaṃ|)

29. 

 cutupapattipabandhavasainahi

avasavakattanadhātuparamparā,

jalitadīpasikhai’va pavattati

nayidhapuggalavaidakakārakau| ()

30.

 purimakhandhapabandhamanaikadhā

itivavatthayatau hi kudiṭṭhiyau,

apagatā’ttani vīsativatthukā

tamihadiṭṭhivisuddhi’ti vuc‍cati| ()

31. 

 satimatau ravimaṇḍalasantibhā

sakaṭamagganibhā’yamanussati,

purimajātisu nābhivirajjhati

saravayai sarabhaṅgasarau yathā| ()

32.

 acutiyācutiyāmati māsanai

sutavatī’tavatī’hati bujjhituṃ,

samuditai’muditai kumudāni’ma

nakamalā kamalāni alaṅkari| () (yamakabandhanaṃ|)

33.

 ruciracandamaricivilaipinī

kumudasaṇḍavikāsavihāsinī,

rajanimajjhimayāmavilāsinī

tadadhisīladhanaṃ vijābhāsi kiṃ ()

34. 

 ghanasunīlavisālatapauvanaṃ

analabhāsurakīṭakulākulaṃ,

rajanirājiniyā kusumākulā-

viraḷakaisakalāpasiriṃ bhajī| ()

35.

 tadupahāraratāyi’va kaumudī-

bhujalatāya vibhāvaribhīruyā,

gahitalājakabhājanavibbhamaṃ

phuṭitakairavakānana māhari| ()

36.

 tibhuvanaikaraviṃ ravibhattari

aparadīpagatai sarasīvadhū,

rajaniyā vihitāvasarā’pi kiṃ

paricariṃsu patibbatamabbhutaṃ| ()

37.

 parilasiṃsu bhusaṃ bhuvanai’vu bhau

ravipathai vitatā, vitatārakā,

animisai hi mahāya mahimatau

jalitadīpasikhāca mahītalai| ()

38. 

 makaratainakaitanasantibhā

tuhinadīdhitidīdhiti majjhimai,

nisi dadāra sadārasarāginaṃ

hadayakairavakairavakānanaṃ| () (yamakabandhanaṃ)

39.

 atha bhavābhavadiṭṭhivibhaida naṃ

vimati mauha tamaupuṭapāṭa naṃ,

cutupapātapabhuti vijāna naṃ

kathamalattha sadibbavilauca naṃ| ()

40.

 kusalakammapabhāvasamubbhavaṃ

sukhumaduragatāni’pi gaucaraṃ,

animisāna pasādavilaucanaṃ

rudhirasaimbhamakalāpagataṃka yathā| ()

41. 

 tathariva’kkhisamaina sudhāsinaṃ

vimatidiṭṭhimisaudhanahaitunā,

hatamanaupakilaisamalaina sau

vigatamānusakainahi cakkhunā| ()

42.

 karatalamburuhauparicakkhumā

yathārivā’malakībadarīphalaṃ,

cutupapattigataipi tathāgatai

tibhuvanamhi yathicchita maddasa| ()

43.

 navupapātakhaṇaica cutikkhaṇai

visayabhāvamupainti tathāgatā,

tadupacāravasaini’hadassanaṃ

khamati aṭṭhakathācariyāsabhau| ()

44. 

 upadhihīna’dhihanīnatathāgatai

anapanītapaṇīta tathāgatai,

anabhirūpa’bhirūpatathāgatai

sugati duggati dugga mupāgatai| () (yamakabandhanaṃ|)

45.

 tiriyamuddhamadhaupatitīya sau

matipahaṃ abhipassi yathārahaṃ,

nicitakammapathaica tathāgatai

upari pādakajhānasamuṭṭhitau| ()

46.

 akusalāni kariṃsu imai tidhā

sucaritāni kariṃsu tidhā imai,

ariyamaggaphalaihika samaṅginau

nasamaṇā’tipi antimavatthunā| ()

47.

 guṇanirākaraṇaina asādhavau

upavadiṃsu nasanti guṇā’ti’mai,

apicasappurisā’riyapuggalai

tadanurūpaguṇaihi pasaṃsayuṃ| ()

48.

 vitathaladdhiparāmasanā imai

paramaladdhiparāmasanā imai,

gahitaladdhivasaina tahiṃtahiṃ

nicitakammapathā janatā ayaṃ| ()

49.

 caturapāyamapāyamapāyatiṃ

upagatā sugati sugatiṃiti,

yati samāhitavāhitavā’ ddasa

animisakkhisamakkhisamanvitau| () (yamakabandhanaṃ)

50. 

 sammāsammasatausatau satimatau kammādihaitubbhavaṃ

rūpārūpamanāgataṅni mahā mauhandhakārau dhiyā,

abbhatthaṅgami yāya sauḷasavidhā kaṅkhācataikālikā

sākaṅkhātaraṇabbisuddhu dutiyaiyāmai pavattā mati| ()

iti maidhānandābhidhānainayatinā viracitai sakalakavijana hadayānandadānanidānai jinavaṃsadīpai avidurai nidānai pubbainivāsañāṇadibbacakkhuñāṇādhigama pavattiparidīpau tairasamau saggau|

1. 

 sudhākarai vikasitakairavākarai

kamainu’ pāsati sati pacchimaṃ disaṃ,

vihāvarī sasirucirānanaṃ jinaṃ

bhajanti pacchimamathayāma mautari| ()

2.

 khaṇaṃ nisāpati virahāturaiva’yaṃ

nisāvadhū malinapayaudharambarā,

catuddisāyatanayanaihi sampati

mumauca sīkaranikarassubindavau| ()

3.

 kilaisanāsanapasutau samādhinā

puthuj‍janau tadanusayaṃ yathubbahai,

tathaṃ’sunā bhuvanakalaṅkasaudhanau

nahai kalānidhi sakalaṅka mubbahī| ()

4. 

 puratthimai nabhasi vikiṇṇa tārakā-

pabandhanibbharatimiraṃ viyākarī

nisāvadhu valayitahārabhāsura-

sunīlakaumalanavakuntalassiriṃ| ()

5.

 vipassanābalavimalīkatantarau

jinaṅkurau duritamalaṃva candimā,

maricisañ‍cayadhavalīkatambarau

tamaucayaṃ tamanucaraṃ nirākarī| ()

6.

 pavāyi sītalamalayānilau bhusaṃ

disaṅganā sisiratusārabindavau,

mumauca sā vicari nisā nisākara-

marīcimañ‍jariparicumbitai bhuvi| ()

7. 

 niraṅgaṇai nirupakilaisa nic‍calai

mudumhi kammaniyavisuddhibhāvagai,

samāhitai manasi vipassanāmanaṃ

athāsavakkhayamatiyā’ bhīnīhari| ()

8.

 sabārasaṅgikabhavacak‍ka majjhagā

anuk‍kamaina’pi paṭilaumatau sudhī

vavatthayaṃ yamariyañāṇadassanaṃ

visuddhiyā visaddhiyā taduc‍catai| ()

9.

 khaṇaina yau sarati sahassalaucanau

yathāvatau dasasatamattha massapi,

vidhātunau nijacaraṇaṅgulippabhā-

vibhusitā’khilabhuvanaudarassa’pi| ()

10. 

 asaisanīvaraṇatusārasausinau

samādhisambhava’ khilajhānalābhinau,

jagattayaṃ karabadaraṃ’va dassinau

nayassa kassaci visayatta māgataṃ| ()

11.

 adiṭṭha mappaṭi viditaṃ sayaṃpurā

anuttaraṃtamariyañāṇadassanaṃ,

imassa gautamasamaṇassa sijjhatai

garūpasaivanavirahassa abbhūtaṃ| ()

12.

 bhavaibhavai parivitadānapārami-

balaina’hū vijaṭitalaubhabandhanau,

samaittikhantyanugatasīlapāramī-

jalaina nibbutapaṭighādipāvakau| ()

13. 

 bhavaibhavai bhavi thirañāṇapāramī-

padhaṃsitā’khilaviparītadassanau,
