vināyakappabhuti garūpasaivana-

vasaina pucchiya hatamauhasaṃsayau| ()

14.

 bhavaibhavai budhajanapujanādinā

garūbhivādanabahumānanādinā,

janāpacāyanavidhinā vinaudayi

sadappa mun‍nati mabhimāna muddhaṭaṃ| ()

15.

 bhavaibhavai vibhavaratiṃ ratiṃbhavai

anaṅgasaṅgamarati maṅganāratiṃ,

gharā’bhinikkhamiya’nagāriyaṃratau

apānudī paṭilabhi jhāna mattanā| ()

16. 

 bhavaibhavai sa’vīriya sac‍capāramī-

parāyaṇau adhikusalaisu cāratiṃ,

jahaṃ vacīduritamalaṃ catubbidhaṃ

visaudhayī nijahadayañ‍ca paggahī| ()

17.

 bhavai bhavai iti samatiṃsapāramī-

visujjhitā’ kusalamanaumalimasau,

kamaina indriya paripākataṃ gatau

nacañ‍ñasampada mahipatthayī sudhī| ()

18.

 bhavai bhavai agaṇitamaiva baudhiyā

dayāya cauditahadayau’hi kapatthanaṃ,

akā catubbidhaphalasampadā tatau

padissarai niravasarā riva’ntani| ()

19. 

 bhavai bhavai sakapaṇidhānasattiyā

tidhā’hisaṅkhatakusalaṃ imassa bhau,

nasādhayai kimariyañāṇadassanaṃ

anañ‍ñapuggalavisayaṃ nasaṃsayau| ()

20.

 anic‍catau’dayavayatāya dukkhatau

sadukkhatāyapi avidhaiyatādinā,

anattatau varamati khandhapañ‍cakaṃ

punappunaṃ samupaparikkha mussahī| ()

21.

 kalāpasammasanamukhaina bārasa-

vidhai anādikabhavacak‍kanissitai,

kamaina sattasu anupassanāsu sau

jinaṅkurau tadavayavai’bhinīhari| ()

22. 

 anic‍cataubhusamanupassamuddhari

asaisasaṅkhatagatanic‍casañ‍ñitaṃ,

anattatau samanuvipassa dukkhatau

sasaṅkhataipajahi sukhattasañ‍ñitaṃ| ()

23.

 vinaudayī sumati virāganibbidā-

vasaina saṅkhatagatarāganandiyau,

niraudhanissajanavasā’nupassiya

tathaudayādiyanamasaisaṅghatai| ()

24.

 vidhāyadubbidhamanupassatau kasatau

asaisasaṅkhatamapi nāmarūpatau,

nidānatau punakhaṇatau’dayabbayaṃ

upaṭṭhahi dviguṇitapañ‍cavisadhā| ()

25. 

 sudhīmatau taruṇavipassanāyi’mai

vipassakassi’ti dasupak‍kilaisakā,

bhavuṃpabhāsatimatipītinicchayau

sukhī’hanāsamathanikantyupaikkhanā| ()

26. 

 pasan‍nalauhita paripuṇṇaviggahā

viniggatā niravadhilaukadhātusu,

vipassanābalapabhavā’bhipatthari

sudhantakañ‍canarasapiñ‍jarappabhā| ()

27.

 ayaṃpathau nabhavati tappabhādayau

visattikāpabhutikilaisavatthukā,

punaudayabbaya manupassatau tatau

pathau samubbhavi dasupak‍kilaisagaṃ| ()

28.

 pathāpathaṃ samupaparikkhatauiti

sudhīmatau taruṇavipassakassayā,

samuṭṭhitā nisitavipassanāmati

pathāpathikkhaṇakavisuddhi vuc‍catai| ()

29. 

 narāsabhau adhigatañātatīraṇa-

pariñ‍ñavā uparipahāṇasaṅkhayā,

pariñ‍ñayā ubhaya visuddhisiddhiyā

tisac‍cadassanapasutau samārabhī| ()

30.

 anic‍calakkhaṇamapaki dukkhalakkhaṇaṃ

anattalakkhaṇamatha sabbasaṅkhatai,

yathāvatau nasamanupassi santati-

riyāpathaihica pihitaṃghaṇainasau| ()

31.

 visaudhayaṃ matimudayabbayai tatau

lahuṃ tilakkhaṇavisadattagau bhusaṃ,

vipassanāpathapaṭipan‍na mattanā

alatthabhaṅgadhibhayañāṇamādikaṃ| ()

32. 

 sayambhunau uparinavānupassanā-

vibhāvanā navaguṇavaṇṇanāyidha,

vidhīyatai navavidhañṇabhāvanā

pavuc‍catai sapakaṭipadāvisuddhiti| ()

33.

 matīhitihipi catusac‍cachādaka-

tamauvidhaṃsana samanantaraṃ thiraṃ,

niraudhagaucaramalabhittha gautrabhu-

matiṃ sudhī anariyagauttabāhiraṃ| ()

34. 

 pasatthagautrabhumatidin‍nasañ‍ñakaṃ

samūlamuddhaṭakalusattayaṃ sudhī,

vibandhaduggativinipātanādikaṃ

athādimaṃpaṭilabhī ñāṇadassanaṃ| ()

35.

 yadaivanantaraphaladanti paṇḍitā

vadantitapphalamapi pac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ,

alattha sau puna dutiyāya bhumiyā

vipassanaṃ samadhigamāya bhāvayī| ()

36.

 bhusauvisausita bhavadukkhakaddamaṃ

akālikaṃ tanukatakibbisattayaṃ,

anuttaraṃ dutiyamalattha tapphalaṃ

sapac‍cavaikkhaṇamatha ñāṇadassanaṃ| ()

37. 

 vipassanaṃpunarapibhāvayaṃ sayaṃ

samuddhaṭālayapaṭighaṃ bhavāpahaṃ,

sapac‍cavaikkhaṇaphalañāṇamajjhagā

anuttaraṃ tatiyaka ñāṇadassanaṃ| ()

38.

 tilakkhaṇaṃ thiramatimā’bhipattiyā

subhāvayika upari catutthabhumiyā,

avāriyāsaniriva tālamatthakai

kilaisamuddhaniranihac‍cacārinaṃ| ()

39.

 nivāritākhila bhavacak‍kavibbhamaṃ

savāsanāparimita pakāpanāsanaṃ,

anañ‍ñagaucara varañāṇadassanaṃ

alattha tapphalamapipac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ| ()

40. 

 tadāsavakkhayamatigñāṇadassana-

visuddhivuc‍cati arahattapattiyā,

sahaivacuddasavidha buddhabuddhiyau

jinau catubbidha paṭisambhidālabhī| ()

41.

 asādhāraṇaṃ ñāṇachak‍kaṃbalāni

dasa’ṭṭhārasāvaiṇikā buddhadhammā,

catuddhāvisāraj‍jamiddhānubhāvā

samiddhāsahaicārahattaina tassa| ()

42.

 abhiñ‍ñaiyyadhammai abhiñ‍ñāya sāmaṃ

pariñ‍ñaiyya dhammai pariñ‍ñāya sāmaṃ,

pahātabba dhammai pahantvāna sāmaṃ

sanibbānamaggapphalaṃ sacchikatvā| ()

43. 

 siyāyāvatāñaiyya dhammappavatti

siyātāvatā tassa ñāṇappavatti,

abhiñ‍ñāya sabbañ‍ñutāñāṇa māsi

sahaivārahattaina sabbañ‍ñu buddhau| ()

44.

 mahāmauhaniddāpagaumaggañāṇa-

ppabuddhau’hisambaudhiyā saubhamānau,

munindau dinindaṃ’ susandauhahāsi-

’ndirāmandirindivarābhaṃahāsi| ()

45.

 subuddhābhisambaudhipubbācalambhā

sahaivāruṇau buddhasūraudayaina,

samuṭṭhāsi vainaiyya bandhūhisaddhiṃ

pabujjhiṃsu abbhuggatamhaujakausā| ()

46. 

 uḷārāvabhāsau tadā jātikhaittai

bhusaṃpātubhutau mahīsampavaidhi,

siḷāsāḷasaiḷāvataṃsā subhāni

nimattāni battiṃsajātātilaukai| ()

47.

 tamaujālaviddhaṃsanādīni laukai

karauntauva cattārikic‍cāni’māni,

samuṭṭhāsi tasmiṃkhaṇai raṃsimāli

rivādic‍cavaṃsubbhavau buddhasūrau| ()

48.

 mahīlājavuṭṭhīhi sañchan‍nabhutā

nabhaṃ kanibbharaṃ gandhadhupaddhajaihi,

chaṇhaukulā kaivalaṃ laukadhātu

mahāmaṅgalāvāsa kalīlāvalambiṃ ()

49. 

 tadātappadambhauja pūjāgatānaṃ

sirau bhattibhārañ‍jalīnaṃ surānaṃ,

nirālambamākāsa randhaṃ babhāsa

pabhāsāra cūḷāmaṇihakā’karāḷaṃ| ()

50.

 guṇaunāma sakkhandhasantāna suddhi

sakausuddhakhanvdhappabandhauhi buddhau,

namaubuddhabhutassa nipphantañāṇa-

ppahāṇānubhāvāhirūpassatassa| ()

iti maidhānandābhidhānainayatinā viracitai sakalakavijana hadayānandadānanidānai jinavaṃsadīpai avidurai nidānai mahā baudhisattassa ariyamaggañāṇābhisambaudhi samadhigama pavattiparidīpau cuddasamau saggau|

jinavaṃsadīpai avidurai nidānabhāgau dutiyau|

1. 

 assaiva pakal‍laṅkavarassakāraṇā

sīsakkhimaṃsāni ca dārasunavau,

datvā cirassaṃ akarinti duk‍karaṃ

sau (indavaṃsā’)bhidhajau vicintayī| ()

2.

 pal‍laṅkatau tāva navuṭṭhahaṃ tahiṃ

pal‍laṅkamādhāya nisaj‍ja sattahaṃ,

saṅkappapuṇṇau’dhisamādhayau muni

sammā samāpaj‍ji anaikakauṭiyau| ()

3.

 nā’yaṃ jahātai vijayāsanā’layaṃ

yaṃkiñ‍cikic‍caṃ karaṇiyamassahi,

saṃvij‍jatai dānipi kāci daivatā

saṅkābhighā’tābhivitak‍kayuṃ iti| ()

4. 

 tāsaṃ viditvāna vitak‍kamāsanā

abbhuṭṭhitau sau gaganaṅgaṇaṃ khaṇaṃ,

saṃdassayantau yamapāṭihāriyaṃ

nissaṃsayau saṃsayasal‍lamuddharī| ()

5.

 nissāya pal‍laṅkamimaṃ mahīruhaṃ

sampattasambaudhipadau’smi cintiya,

auruyha pakal‍laṅkaṭhitappadaisatau

pubbuttarānañhi disāna mantarā| ()

6.

 sattāha mindivaracārulaucana-

pañ‍cappabhāsāranisaicanaina sau,

samapūjayantau jayabaudhimāsanaṃ

aṭṭhāsi laukaikavilaucanau jinau| ()

7. 

 gantvā ṭhītaṭṭhānajayāsanantarā

pubbāparassaṃ disi sattavāsaraṃ,

nikkhittacak‍kaṅkitapādapaṅkajau

sau caṅkamī nimmitaratanacaṅkamai| ()

8.

 sau baudhitau uttarapacchimantarā

daivaihi nānāratanaihi nimmitai,

sattāhamattaṃ maṇimandirai muni

sammābhidhammaṃ pavihāsi sammasaṃ| ()

9.

 manthācalai’nambunidhiṃ’va kaisavau

viñ‍ñāṇakaṇḍādiṭasā catubbidhaṃ,

tasmiṃ nisin‍nau muni dhammasaṅgaṇi-

mālaulayī ñāṇabalaina duddasaṃ| ()

10. 

 vaibhaṅgiyaṃ khandhavibhaṅgamādikaṃ

gambhīramaṭṭhārasadhā sudubbudhaṃ,

sau mārabhaṅgau’tha vibhaṅgasāgaraṃ

saṃkhaubhayī ñāṇayugantavāyunā| ()

11.

 sau dhātusuñ‍ñattapajānanau jinau

nissattanij‍jivasabhāvadhātuyau,

vitthārayantau tadanantaraṃ varaṃ

nānānayaṃ dhātukathaṃ vavatthayī| ()

12.

 khandhādipañ‍ñattivasaina chabbidhā

pañ‍ñattiyau’ti suvibhattamātikaṃ,

pañ‍ñāya sabbañ‍ñujinau’tha puggala-

pañ‍ñatti mālaulayi aggapuggalau| ()

13. 

 vādībhasīhau sakavādamaṇḍalaṃ

auruyha sammā paravādamaṇḍalaṃ,

suttāni saṅgayha sahassamaṭṭhadhā

saṃkhitta mādau mukhavādayuttikaṃ| ()

14.

 ñāṇaina vitthāriyamāna mattanau

kiñ‍cāpya’nantāpariyanta muttamaṃ,

tak‍kīhi nak‍kāvacaraṃ nakaihici

nāthau kathāvatthu mathā’bhisammasi| ()

15.

 yaṃ mūlakhandhādivasā dasabbidhaṃ

uddaisaniddaisapadā’nurūpatau,

niṭṭhānatau saṃsayatau vibhāgiyaṃ

gambhīrañāṇaina’tha sāgarūpamaṃ| ()

16. 

 santaṃ paṇitaṃ nipuṇaṃ sududdasaṃ

guḷhaṃ atak‍kāvacaraṃ acintiyaṃ,

nānānayaṃ taṃ yamakaṃ susañ‍ñamau

dhammassarau sammasi nippadaisatau| ()

17.

 nānānayu’ttuṅgataraṅganibbharaṃ

naiyyatthanītatthamaṇihi maṇḍitaṃ,

dhammantarāvaṭṭasataṃ athāparaṃ

saddhammakhandhaudakakhandhapūritaṃ| ()

18.

 satthā catubbisa timattapac‍caya-

vailaṃ paricchaidavisālapaṭṭanaṃ,

gambhīrapaṭṭhāna mahaṇṇavaṃ kathaṃ

ālaulayaṃ sammasi ñāṇamairunā| ()

19. 

 nissaṅgañāṇau muni haitupac‍cayau

ic‍cādinā’raupitamātikārahaṃ,

niddiṭṭhanidaidasapadaṃ papañ‍cita-

ñaiyyaṃ catubbīsavidhaṃ sudubbudhaṃ| ()

20.

 nissāya bāvīsavidhai tikai tika-

paṭṭhāna mantaugadhanāmarūpikai

nissāya nissaisakai tathā duka-

paṭṭhāna mantaugadhanāmarūpikai ()

21.

 bāvisamattā tikamātikā dukai

pakkhippa paṭṭhānavidaṃ dukantikaṃ,

bāvīsamattāya tikai sataṃdukai

pakkhippa paṭṭhānamidaṃka tikaddukaṃ| ()

22. 

 katvā tikaisvai’va tikai duvīsati

paṭṭhāna mantaugatikai tikattikaṃ,

katvā dukaisvai’va tathā sataṃdukai

paṭṭhāna mantāgaitikai dukaddukaṃ| ()

23.

 ic‍cānulaumai janayāni pakkhipaṃ

cha ppac‍canīyai’pi nayāni pakkhipaṃ,

aivaṃ khipitvā anulaumapac‍cani-

yaicā’pi cha ppac‍caniyā’nulaumakai| ()

24.

 gambhīrapaṭṭhānamahaudadhiṃ iti

saṅkhaubhayaṃ sammasi ñāṇamairunā,

taṃ sammasantassa savatthukaṃ chavi-

vaṇṇau pasantau’si pasīdi lauhitaṃ| ()

25. 

 tasmiṃkhaṇai cattajavaṇṇadhātuyau

aṭṭhaṃsu ṭhānamhi asitihatthakai,

añ‍ñatra’dhiṭṭhānabalaṃ sarīratau

chabbaṇṇaraṃsī visarā’hiniccharuṃ| ()

26.

 saivālakālindinadi’ndirāpati-

nilambari’ndivaravaṇṇasantibhā,

kaisa’kkhimassūhika viniggatā bhusaṃ

nīlappabhā’saisadisā alaṅkaruṃ| ()

27.

 tāsaṃ vasā’ saisadisāvilāsinī

āsuṃ yathā pārutanīlakambalā,

tā cak‍kavāḷāti papūrayantiyau

dhāviṃsu nilaupalacuṇṇasan‍nibhā| ()

28. 

 nissaisalaukaṃ haritālakuṅkuma-

cuṇṇaihi sauvaṇṇarasaihi missakaṃ,

ālaipayantīviya pītaraṃsiyau

yā niggatā kañ‍canasan‍nibhattacā| ()

29.

 tāsaṃ vasainā’si siṇairupabbata-

rājā vilinau’va mahaṇṇavai jalaṃ,

saṃkappitā’va’ṭṭha disāgajā’bhavuṃ

niddhantacāmīkarakappanādinā| ()

30.

 lākhārasānaṃ parisaicanaṃ viya

nindūracuṇṇau’kiraṇaṃ’va yādisi,

sañjhāpabhārattasurattaraṃsiyau

nikkhamma dhāviṃsu samaṃsalauhitā| ()

31.

 tāsaṃ vasainā’khilabhūmikāminī

āsī nimuggāriva uttaraṇṇavai,

ambhaujarāgaihi sunimmitāni’va

sabbāni dabbānya’bhavuṃ jagattayai|()

32. 

 yākundasaugandhikacandacandikā-

kappūrakhīraudadhivīcipaṇḍarā,

aṭṭhīhi dāṭhāhī vitassaṭā bhusaṃ

audātaraṃsī dhavalīkaruṃ disā| ()

33.

 tāsaṃ vasainā’si yathā mahīvadhu

audātavatthaihi nivatthapārutā,

tā khīradhārāparisaikabandhurā

dhāviṃsu buddhassa yasaunibhāpabhā| ()

34.

 sabbādisāyau’khilalaukadhātuyau

mañ‍jiṭṭhapaṅkaihi vilaipayanti’ca,

nikkhamma mañ‍jiṭṭhapabhā tatautatau

dhāviṃsu sañ‍cuṇṇapavāḷasantibhā ()

35. 

 nīlādidhātussarasaihi pañ‍cahi

vaṇṇaihi pupphaihi maṇīhisattahi,

sampūrayantī’va pabhā pabhassarā

nikkhamma laukaṃ sakalaṃ alaṅkaruṃ| ()

36.

 tā raṃsiyau byāpiya maidiniṃ mahī-

sandhārakaṃ vāri mathau samīraṇaṃ,

haiṭṭhā’jaṭākāsatalaṃ tathūpari

gaṇhiṃsu laukaṃ tiriyaṃ nirāvadhiṃ| ()

37.

 daivaddumu’yyānavimānabhusaṇa-

canda’k‍katārānikarā’marā tatau,

saṇṭhānamattaihi vijāniyā’bhavuṃ

tā niggatā aj‍jatanā’pi dhāvarai| ()

38. 

 tamhā’bhīgantvā ghananīlasākhinau

nigraudhasākhissa’japālasañ‍ñinau,

mūlai nisaj‍jā’dhisukhaṃ vimuttijaṃ

satthā’nubhauntau pavihāsi sattahaṃ| ()

39.

 auruyha tasmiṃsamayai vimānatau

dānādayau pāramiyau bhavābhavaṃ,

dhāvaṃ asādhāraṇañāṇasiddhiyā

aisau’va nā’haṃ abhisaṅkhariṃ iti| ()

40.

 autārapaikhau navipassa aittakaṃ

kālaṃ kalaṅkaṃ akalaṅkarūpinau,

saukā’kulau acchi chamāya sauḷasa-

laikhā vilaikhaṃ kalimā avammukhau| ()

41. 

 kasmā napañ‍ñāyati’dāni naupitā

aulaukayanti k‍va gatau’ti dukkhitaṃ,

saukaina laikhā likhamāna mañ‍jasai

disvā nisin‍naṃ pitaraṃ sudummukhaṃ| ()

42.

 tatrau pagantvā vasavattidhītarau

pucchiṃsu taṇhā aratī ragā lahuṃ,

kiṃ tāta kiṃ tāta kimaittha jhāyasi

kau tai parau kaina parājitau tuvaṃ| ()

43.

 suddhaudanassā’vanipassa aurasau

patvā’hisambaudhipadaṃka mukhai masiṃ,

makkhaisi mai chinditamārabandhanau

tasmā’nusaucāmi kathaisi pāpimā| ()

44. 

 ānīya taṃ mattagajaṃ’va mārajiṃ

rāgādipāsaihi mayaṃ subandhiya,

dassāma vau passatha tāta nau balaṃ

māsauci mājhāyi’ti dhītarau’bravuṃ| ()

45.

 siṅgārasaṅgāmadharā’vatārinī

bhubhaṅgabāṇāsanamattadhārinī,

āraupitā’pāṅgasarā’pya’nissarā

kāmārimārārisaravyadāraṇai| ()

46.

 saivālanīlāmalakuntalākulā

bālindulaikhai’valalāṭamaṇḍalā

niluppalakkhī calahaimakuṇḍalā-

laṅkārakaṇṇā’likalāpabhā’lakā ()

47. 

 vāṇilatāvail‍litaphul‍lamālatī-

dantāvalī pal‍lavapāṭalādharā,

kandappakīḷālayahaimakāhaḷa-

saṅkāsanāsā kamalāmalānanā| ()

48.

 vij‍jul‍latā cārubhujā calācala-

līlāvalambatthanahaṃsamaṇḍalā,

cāmīkarāliṅgavilāsakandharā

lāvaṇṇa val‍lidalakaumalaṅgulī| ()

49.

 nimmaikhalālinavilaggabhāginī

kīḷānadīkulavisālasauṇinī,

kandappadappānaladhūmakaj‍jala-

raumāvalivail‍litanābhimaṇḍala| ()

50. 

 pīnaurujaṅghā kalikānakhāvalī

tā’naṅakgaraṅgaṅajahāriviggahā,

māraṅganā yatra niraṅgaṇau jinau

tatrā’gamuṃ rāgasurāmadā’turā| ()

51.

 aṅgīrasassā’nanasauṇṇadappaṇai

tā sundarī bimbitalaucanindirā,

kandappakīḷākalahaṃ vidhātavai

kālauyamic‍cāhu tuvaṃ yadicchasi| ()

52. 

 vyāpāritā tai paricārikā mayaṃ

aitthāgatā hauma manaubhunā’dhunā,

vattambujānaṃ paricumbanai ayaṃ

kālaunu bhaugautama kiṃnayicchasi| ()

53.

 bhau puṇṇakumbhai’va tavau’ramandirai

uddhaggalaumu’ssitanīlakaitanai,

kāmāhisaikussavamaṅgalāya nau

saj‍jaithi’mai pīnapayaudharai nakiṃ| ()

54.

 vattambujai nau adharaṃ’subandhurai

naittālimālā nahivumbarai tava,

amhaisuyaivā’bhipatanti bhaumuni

kandappabāṇā karuṇā kuhiṃ tavaṃ ()

55. 

 tvaṃ yaubbanau sāmi mayañ‍ca yaubbanī

kālau vasattau vipinaṃ manauramaṃ’

mandānilau vāyati kiṃ cirāyatai

tuyhaṃ anaṅgau’va niraṅgaṇau’si kiṃ| ()

56.

 dibbāni vatthābharaṇāni’māni’pi

laj‍jāya saddhiṃka sithilibhavanti nau’

amhaisvanaṅgaina samaṃ anaṅgaṇaṃ

daḷahatta māyāti manaṃ tava’bbhutaṃ| ()

57.

 ic‍cānigammaṃ hadayaṅgamaṃ giraṃ

vatvāna dibbaina saraina mañ‍junā,

kāmāturānanti pumānamāsayā

uc‍cāvacā cintiyamāradhītarau| ()

58. 

 kañ‍ñāvilāsādivasaina viggahai

nimmāya pac‍caikasataṃ padassiya,

pādai mayaṃ bhau paricārayāma tai

vatvā tamārādhayituṃ parak‍kamuṃ| ()

59.

 gāthā imā dhammapadai mahāmuni

saṅgāyi tāsaṃ tamanaṅgabhaṅgīnaṃ,

vatvā nasak‍kauma mayaṃ pakalaubhituṃ

tā rittahatthā pitaraṃ upāgamuṃ| ()

60.

 gantvā tatau sau mucalindasañ‍ñinau

rukkhassa mūlai mucalindabhauginau,

bhaugāvaligandhakuṭiṃka samappitau

sattāhamajjhāvasi jhānamuttamaṃ| ()

61. 

 mulamhi rājāyatanassa sākhinau

pal‍laṅka mādhāya nisaj‍ja sattahaṃ,

tamhābhi’gantvā bhavabandhanacchidau

satthā valañ‍jaisi vimuttijaṃ sukhaṃ| ()

62.

 satthā aivakaṃ vasantau parahitatiratau sattasattāhamattaṃ

yaṃkiñ‍cāhārakic‍caṃ dhuvaparihariyaṃ kic‍camuc‍cāvacampi|

nākāsi jhānamaggapphakhalasukhamakhilaṃ samphusantau vibhāsi

pādāsi dantapauṇaudaka magada’bhayaṃ tassa daivanamindau| ()

iti maidhānandābhidhānaina yatinā viracitai sakalakavijana hadayānandadānanidānai jinavaṃsadīpai santikainidānai bhagavatau sattasattāhavitik‍kamappavatti paridīpau paṇṇarasamau saggau|

1. 

 mūlai rājāyatanaviṭapacchāyāmanau (hārini)

vikkhālaitvā bhagavati mukhaṃ tasmiṃ nisin‍nai sati,

vāṇij‍jatthaṃ nija janapadā dvai bhātikāka vāṇijā

gacchantā majjhimajanapadaṃ taṃṭhāna majjhautaruṃ| ()

2.

 buddhaṃ disvā nirupamasiriṃ saddhāya sañ‍cauditā

sampādaitvā madhurivamadhuṃ manthaṃ madhūpiṇḍikaṃ,

bhantai tumhai anubhavatha nau bhikkhaṃ paṭiggaṇhiya

aivaṃ vatvātadubhayajanā aṭṭhaṃsu baddhañ‍jalī| ()

3.

 sabbai buddhā nahi karatalambhaujaisu gaṇhanti khau

saṅkappaittassi’ti bhagavataukismiṃ paṭicchāmahaṃ,

cittācāraṃ sumariya silāpattai pamāṇupagai

pādāsuṃ sampati catumahārājā kuvairādayau| ()

4. 

 aikaṃ katvā samuti caturau pattai adhiṭṭhāya tai

bhutvā’hāraṃ bhuvananayanau bhuttānumaudaṃ kari,

buddhaṃ dhammaṃ saraṇamagamuṃ tai tainuhau’vāṇijā

jātā laukai sakalapaṭhamaṃ dvaivācikau’ pāsakā| ()

5.

 taisaṃ dvin‍naṃ suparihariyaṃ daithā’ti saṃyācataṃ

jālaṅkā’laṅkatakaratalau rupindirā mandirai,

sīsaṃ nilaupalamaṇighaṭīlīlāvilāsaṃ phusaṃ

saivāli’ndīvaradalasiriṃ sau kaisamuṭṭhiṃ adā| ()

6. 

 patvā taisaṃ janapadamubhau pakkhippa tā dhātuyau

jīvantasmiṃ bhagavati mahāthūpaṃ patiṭṭhāpayuṃ,

vimhāpaintau vanasuragaṇaṃ yatrā’japālābhidhau

nigraudhau’si tahimupagamī muṭṭhāya tamhā jinau| ()

7.

 svā’yaṃ dhammau sayamadhigatau dubbaudhanaududdasau

ic‍caivaṃ sau samabhivivinaṃ dhammassa gambhīrataṃ,

appau’ssuk‍kau’bhavi bhagavatau cittaṃ viditvā mahā-

brahmā’gantvā sayamadhigataṃ daisaitumāyācī taṃ| ()

8.

 gaṇhitvā sau bhuvanasaraṇau ajjhausanaṃ brahmunau

daisaiyyaṃ khau paṭhamamasamaṃ dhammaṃ imaṃ duddasaṃ,

kassā’hanti sumariya vasī āḷārakaucuddakau

abbhañ‍ñātuṃ vata paṭibalātaisaṃ cutiṃ addasaṃ ()

9. 

 āvaj‍jaintau munikatavidū tai pañ‍cavaggai bhavā

bhikkhu dhammai kataparicayā kutrā’dhunā vij‍jarai,

bārānasyaṃ itisipatanai ñatvāna āsāḷhiyā

māsai pañ‍caddasiya masamau bārāṇasiṃ pāvisi| ()

10.

 āgacchantaṃ samuni upakaṃ ājīvakaṃ antarā-

maggai disvā gamanasamayai buddhattanaṃ attanau,

ācikkhitvā vimati matarī sañ‍ñāya taṃ dūratau

nibbinditvā katika makaruṃ vaggā samaggā vasī| ()

11. 

 ñatvā taisaṃ manasi vikatiṃ audissakaṃ samphari

maittaṃ cittaṃ narahari tatau phuṭṭhāsamānā vasī,

nāthaṃ natvā dasanakhasamaudhāna’jalīhā’gataṃ

pac‍cuggantvā tahimahiharuṃ papphauṭhayitvā’sanaṃ| ()

12.

 mākhau tumhai pariharathamaṃ aiyā’vusautyā’dinā

sañ‍ñāpaitvā samuni samaṇai sabbañ‍ñutaṃ attanau,

kauṭīha’ṭṭhārasahi navutau brahmaihi cā’laṅkari

bārāṇasyaṃ pani’sipatanai pañ‍ñattabuddhāsanaṃ| ()

13.

 saṃvattaitvā parahitakarau sau dhammacak‍kaṃ jinau

kauṇḍañ‍ñākhyassamaṇapamukhai kauṭīnamaṭṭhārasa,

brahmānau tai paṭhamakaphalai sammā patiṭṭhāpayi

kampitthā’yaṃka vasumativadhū suttāvasānai bhusaṃ| ()

14. 

 vappatthairau dutiyadivasai’ tyai’vaṃ dinānuk‍kamaṃ

sautāpan‍nā taditaravasī ātappamanvāya tai,

sabbaithairā bhaṇitasamayai vitthāritassuñ‍ñatai

suttattasmiṃ vigatadarathā āsuṃ pahiṇāsvā| ()

15.

 nibbinditvā nikhilavisayai nikkhanta māvāsatau

disvā satthā yasakulasutaṃ pak‍kausayitvāna taṃ,

tāyaṃ ratyaṃ paṭhamakaphalaṃ ratyāvasānai dinai

pabbājaisi uparimakhilaṃ maggapphalaṃ pāpayaṃ| ()

16. 

 pabbājaitvā yasakulasutassambhattamittai janai

sampāpaisi adhikacaturau pañ‍ñāsa maggapphalaṃ,

aivaṃ khīṇāsavavasigaṇai jātai’kasaṭṭhyā bhuvi

vassāna’ntai pahiṇi muni tai bhikkhu disācārikaṃ| ()

17.

 gacchantau sau haririva mahāvīrau’ruvailaṃ sayaṃ

disvā kappāsikavanaghaṭai tiṃsappamāṇai janai,

pabbājaisi katavinayanā tai bhaddavaggaiyyakā

bhikkhūmaggapphalarasamudā anvācaruṃ cārikaṃ| ()

18.

 naitā jaitā vijaṭitajaṭau pattau’ruvailaṃ tatau

saṃdassaitvā vimatiharaṇaṃ sau pāṭihīraṃ varaṃ,

pabbājaitvā jaṭilapavarai taibhātikai’naikadhā

chindāpaitvā vinayamakā antaubahiddhajaṭaṃ| ()

19. 

 pabbājaitvā pacurajaṭilai katvai’tarai nij‍jaṭai

saddhiṃ taihī dasasatamahākhīṇāsavaihā’samau,

nicchāraintau disidisi bhusaṃ chabbaṇṇaraṃsiṃka subhaṃ

rañ‍ñau vācaṃ sumariya puraṃ rājaggahaṃ pāvisi| ()

20.

 tatrā’santai vasati sugatai laṭṭhibbanuyyānagai

vuttantaṃ taṃ savaṇasubhagaṃ uyyānapālauditaṃ,

sutvā baddhañ‍jalisarasijai pādāsanai pūjayī

bhunāthau bārasahi nahutaihā’gamma sau māgadhau ()

21. 

 bhaitvā diṭṭhiṃ ciraparicitaṃ tai kassapādī vasī

disvā rājā bhagavati tadā dhammaṃcarantai tahiṃ,

nik‍kaṅkhau sau sumadhurataraṃ pitvāna dhammāmataṃ

saddhiṃ aikādasahi nahutaihā’dauphalaṃ pāpuṇi| ()

22.

 laddhassāsau darathavigamā hutvā mahaupāsakau

svaimaibhikkhaṃ kasugatapamukhau saṅghādhivāsaissatu,

āyācitvā namiya caraṇadvandāravindadvayaṃ

pac‍cuṭṭhāyā’gami saparisau sau bimbisārābhidhau| ()

23.

 pātau rājaggahanagaratau kauṭīnamaṭṭhārasa

daṭṭhuṃ buddhaṃ nirupamasiriṃ laṭṭhīvanai nāgarā,

rāsibhūtā dutiyadivasai aikassa bhikkhussa’pi

sambādhattā nahipavisanaukāsau’si dighañ‍jasai| ()

24. 

 āvaj‍jaitvākimanabhimataṃ sak‍kau nisin‍nāsanaṃ

uṇhākāraṃ janayi’ti tadā hutvā navau māṇavau,

maggau’tiṇṇau abhavi puratau gāthāhi vatthuttayaṃ

saṃvaṇṇaittau bhavatu bhagavā māchintabhattau iti| ()

25.

 dānaṃ datvā sugatapamukhassaṅghassa rājaggahaṃ

sampattassā’vanipatipuraṃ añ‍ñatravatthuttayā,

bhantai sauhaṃ kathamihavasai vailāya’vailāyapi

ākaṅkhaiyyaṃ tvadupagamituṃ ic‍caivamāraucayī| ()

26. 

 sandacchāyaṃ vijanapavanaṃ yaṃ vaiḷudāyavhayaṃ

uyyānaṃ mai jitasuravanaṃ taṃ nātidurantikai,

sampūjaintau jinakaratalai jālāvanaddhai hari-

bhiṅkārainā’hariyaka salilaṃ pātaisi taṃ patthivau| ()

27.

 laukālaukācalataṭakaṭī viñjhāṭavīlaucanā

gaṅakgāpāṅakgā danakasikharibāhā tikuṭatthanā,

ugghausaintī jaladhivasanā puñ‍ñānumaudantiva

sādhū’tyā’yaṃ vasumativadhū saṅkampi tasmiṃkhaṇai| ()

28.

 āramaṃ taṃ parama ruciraṃ satthā paṭiggaṇhiya

dhammaṃ vatvā’gami parivutau bhikkhūhi vuṭṭhā’sanā,

tasmiṃ kālai paramamamataṃ yai daḷhamittā ubhau

taṃ taṃ gāmaṃ nigamanagaraṃ anvaisamānā’caruṃ| ()

29. 

 āhiṇḍantaṃ tahimanugharaṃ piṇḍāya taisva’ssaji-

tthairaṃ disvā samitadamitaṃ vippau’patissā’bhidhau,

laddhau’kāsau padamanuvajaṃka sutvādvi gāthāpadaṃ

sautāpan‍nau’ bhavi vijaṭayaṃ saṃyaujanāhaṃ tayaṃ| ()

30.

 gāthaṃ sutvā amatamadhuraṃ taṃ sāriputtau’ditaṃ

mauggal‍lānau kaapanuditathāsaṃyaujanānaṃ tayaṃ,

pabbaj‍jitvā tadubhaya janā naitvā paribbājakai

patvārāmaṃ amataparamā satthāramārādhayuṃ| ()

31. 

 sattāhainai’va’dhigami mahābhutai pariggaṇhiya

mauggal‍lānau vasi taditaraṃ maggattayaṃ tapphalaṃ,

māsassa’ddhaṃ katavīriyavā suttaṃ parassau’ditaṃ

sutvā dhammaṃ adhigami vasī taṃ dhammasaināpatī| ()

32.

 dhammassāmī karahaci ubhau tai sāvakānaṃ mamaṃ

aggaṃ bhaddaṃ yugamiti imai pabyākarauttau munī,

aggaṭṭhānai purimacaritaṃ ñatvā patiṭṭhāpayī

sampinaintau sakalaparisaṃ candau’va kundāṭaviṃ| ()

33.

 sutvā suddhaudananarapati puttau mamaṃ sampati

buddhau hutvā padahiya ciraṃ nissāya rājaggahaṃ,

uttāraintau sakalajanataṃ saṃsārakantāratau

saṃvattaittau vasati sivadaṃ saddhammacak‍kaṃiti| ()

34. 

 jiṇṇauvuddhau pariṇatavayappattau’ hamasmyā dhunā

jīvantauyaivahi mamasutaṃ icchāmi daṭṭhuṃ bhaṇai,

aivaṃvatvā’dhikadasasataṃ aikaṃ amac‍caṃ tahiṃ

uyyaujaisi nayanavisayaṃka puttaṃ karauhīti mai| ()

35.

 gantvā’mac‍cau catuparisatiṃ dhammaṃ bhaṇantaṃjinaṃ

disvā baddhañ‍jali saparisau tatrai’kamantaṃ ṭhitau,

sutvādhammaṃ paramamadhuraṃ patvā’ggamaggapphalaṃ

pabbaj‍jitvā hadayakamalaṃ kasaṅkaucayī rājinau| ()

36.

 aṭṭhakkhattuṃ puna saparisai pāhaisi rājāparai

aṭṭhā’mac‍cai tathariva gatā’mac‍cā napac‍cā’gatā,

pabbaj‍jitvā adhigataphalā taicā’pi rañ‍ñaumanaṃ

nā’rādhaisuṃ sunisitadhiyā sañchintasaṃyaujanā| ()

37. 

 duj‍jānau mai maraṇasamayau jiṇṇau’smi tātā’dhunā

tasmā puttaṃ nayanavisayaṃ kātuṃ samatthau’sikiṃ,

aivaṃ vatvā kapuna saparisaṃ saukāphadāyiṃ tahiṃ

din‍naukāsaṃ pahiṇi sacivaṃ pabbaj‍jituṃ bhubhujau| ()

38.

 patvā’rāmaṃ parivutajanau sac‍caddasainau’ditaṃ

sutvā’mac‍cau thiramati catussac‍cā’nupubbikathaṃ,

pabbaj‍jitvā hatabhavabhayau hutvāna khīṇāsavau

aggaṭṭhānaṃ paṭilabhi kulappāsādikānaṃ idha| ()

39. 

 bārāṇasyaṃ kaisipatanatau pattassa rājaggahaṃ

sambuddhassā’dhikadinakatī pañ‍caivamāsā’ bhavuṃ,

haimantā’tusamayavigamā santai vasantai maṇi-

bhusākārā upavanavadhū cūtaṅkurā’ laṅkaruṃ| ()

40.

 kālaṃ ñatvā kapilanagaraṃ kālañ‍ñunau satthunau

gantuṃ kālau’yamiti adhunā sau kāludāyi vasī,

saṃvaṇṇaintau gamanasamayaṃ kātuṃ alaṃ saṅgahaṃ

ñātinantī sumadhurasarau gāthābhigāyī puthu| ()

41.

 mandaṃmandaṃ surabhipavanau sitau’dhunā vāyati

pupphākiṇṇā vipinaviṭapī mattālimālākulā,

gaṅgāvāpī vimalasalīlā samphul‍lakañ‍juppalā

sāyaṃka pātau ahani vivaṭā sabbādisā pākaṭā| ()

42. 

 bhantai maggai navadalasikhā jālau’j‍jalā mañ‍jarī-

bhasmacchan‍nā bhamaravisaraddhumandhakārā bhusaṃ,

jhāpain‍nā’pai’tarahi virahī samphul‍lacūtāṭavi-

dāvaggī tai lavamapi manautāpāya vattanti kiṃ| ()

43.

 kāmandhānaṃ bhadayamadhunā saucāpayattā bhusaṃ

sākhacchin‍nā vigalitadalā maggai asaukaddumā,

añ‍ñatrā’pī vanacaravadhū pādappahārā’turaṃ

tatvantai tai karakisalayassaubhaṃka virūḷhaṅkurā| ()

44. 

 pitvā cutaddūmaphalarasaṃ sammattapuṅkaukilā

saṃkujantai sarasamadhuraṃ vaitāḷikā’va’ñ‍jasai,

saiṇībhutā janapadavadhū tai pādapīṭhai muni

sampūjaituṃ navasarasijai hiyyau’vinantai dhunā| ()

45.

 āmulaggā dalitaviṭapi pupphañ‍jalihakā’dhunā

āgacchantaṃ tvamahimahituṃ saṃdissarai’vau’natā,

vātauddhutā bhamaramukharā kiñ‍jakkhapuñ‍jā’ñ‍jasai

ātatvantai tavaparimukhai sauvaṇṇasaṅkhassiriṃ| ()

46. 

 bhantai antaukalalasalilāvāsaina kālaṃ ciraṃ

ambhaujānaṃ mukulavikatī sitaiti’vā’kuñ‍citā,

aisantī’vai’tarahi saraṇaṃ tai pādabhaddāsanai

uggacchantai pajahiya manautāpaṃ vasantātapaṃ| ()

47.

 pāthaiyya’mbhauruhakuvalayā’laṅkāratuṇḍā kalaṃ

saṅkujantī pavanapadaviṃ uḍḍīyamānā’dhunā,

haṃsassaiṇika sirasi vajatau tai bhuyatai kiṅkiṇi-

ghausākiṇṇaṃ kusumavikaticchan‍naṃ vitānaṃ yathā| ()

48.

 sampūjainti ratanakanakālaṅkārabhārañ‍jalī

maggau’tiṇṇā vanasuravadhū tai lājavuṭṭhīhi’va,

kiñ‍jakkhaihi caraṇayugalaṃ kamandātilandaulitā

val‍lī bhiṅgāvalikisalayā’laṅkārasākhāvalī| ()

49. 

 sammārūḷhau pavanaturagaṃ kāmākarau mañ‍jari-

tuṇiraisū madhukarasarai sandhānayantau’dhunā,

campaiyyādīkusumakalikāsan‍nāhasambhāsurau

naṭṭhau laukau bahujanamanausaṅakgāma maugāhati|()

50.

 yasmā suddhaudananarapabhu ādic‍cavaṃsaddhajau

jiṇṇau vuddhau mamihapahiṇi tvaṃ daṭṭhukāmau pitā,

tasmā bhantai kapilanagaraṃ vainaiyyasattākaraṃ

kantvā rañ‍ñau hadayamakulaṃ baudhaitu saukākulaṃ| ()

51. 

 sādhu’dāyi savisayamahaṃ patvā narādhissaraṃ

uttāraiyyaṃ pitaramitarai bandhū’pi dukkhaṇṇavā,

aivaṃvatvā radanakira ṇālaṅkārabimbādharau

dhammassāmiparivutavasīrājaggahānikkhami| ()

52.

 patvā rañ‍ñau uparibhavanaṃ saukāludāyi’ddhiyā

bhuttā’hārau tadupagamanaṃ atvāha māraucayaṃ,

sambuddhatthaṃ pituru’pahaṭaṃ bhikkhaṃka pakaṭiggaṇhiya

assāsaintau vajati nabhasā saukākulaṃtaṃ kulaṃ| ()

53.

 taṃ bhuñ‍jantau divasadivasai sau yaujanaṃ yaujanaṃ

saṅkhaipaintau paramakaruṇārāmāya sañ‍cauditau,

naitvā khīṇāsavayativarai vīsaṃ sahassaṃ jinau

lakkhīvāsaṃ kapilanagaraṃ māsaihidvīhau’tari| ()

54. 

 nānuppattai bhagavati puraṃ nau ñātisaiṭṭhaṃ kuhiṃ

passissāmā’tya’jahitamanaukautuhaḷā sākiyā,

ārāmauyanaṃ vijanapavanau nigraudhasak‍kassa taṃ-

sāruppauti tahimabhinavai saināsanai māpayuṃ| ()

55.

 pac‍cuggantvā surabhikusumākiṇṇañ‍jaliha’ñ‍jasai

āgacchantaṃ sumahakiya jinaṃ rājiddhiyā’laṅkatai,

kaituggāhai daharadaharai katvā kumārai purai

rājā’mac‍cā paramaruciraṃ ārāma mautārayuṃ| ()

56. 

 pal‍laṅkainau’dayagirisirai candau’va tārāvutau

nānākhiṇāsavaparivutau pañ‍ñattabuddhāsanai,

āsinau’yaṃ manakumuduniṃ sakyānamun‍niddayaṃ

nissaukau sau muni parihari saukandhakāraṃ pitu| ()

57.

 siddhatthau’yaṃ paramadaharau amhaihi vuddhā mayaṃ

jāmātā’yaṃbhavati tanujau nattānujau nau iti,

mānatthaddhā daharadaharai sakyā kumārai’bravuṃ

tumhaigantvā paṇamatha jinaṃ vau piṭṭhitāhaum nau| ()

58.

 āvaj‍jaitvā sakalaparisaṃ ñatvā tadajjhāsayaṃ

mānummattā vibhavamadirāmattā imai khattiyā,

muddhābaddhañ‍jalikisalayā yasmā navandanti maṃ

vandāpaituṃ alamiti tatau jhānaṃ samāpaj‍jiya| ()

59. 

 pattā’bhiñ‍ñau nijapadarajauraṃsihi sañ‍cumbitai

taisaṃ cūḷāmaṇigirisirai sambuddhasurau lasaṃ,

saṃdassaintau yamakamasamaṃ mānandhakāraṃ haraṃ

baudhāpaisi vadanakamalai gaṇḍambamūlai yathā| ()

60.

 disvā suddhaudananaravarau taṃ pāṭihīraṃ varaṃ

pādambhaujai paṇami sirasā ānandabhāraunatau,

cak‍kaṅkālaṅkatapadarajau samphuṭṭhamuddhāñ‍jali

rājañ‍ñānaṃ kamalakalikāsaṇḍassiriṃ vyākaruṃ| ()

61. 

 sañjhāmaighāvaliparivutau surauriva’tthācalaṃ

khamhā bhaddāsanamavatarī sauvaṇṇavaṇṇau jinau,

subabhujiñchai nayanabarihī kaiḷāyanaṃ paukkhara-

vassaṃ vassi nijanakharuciṃ taisaṃ samājai sati| ()

62.

 sutvā vuttaṃ purimacaritaṃ vaissantarākhyaṃ tatau

pak‍kantānaṃ phusiya sirasā tappādacūḷāmaṇiṃ,

bhantai bhikkhaṃ sugatapamukhau saṅghaudhivāsaitu nau

ic‍cai’kaupi paṭhamadivasai nākāsi ajjhaisanaṃ| ()

63.

 nānākhīṇāsavaparivutau laukānukampāparau

laukādhisau dutiyadivasai āciṇṇakappārahaṃ,

sambuddhānaṃ kapilanagarai pātau’va lakhyākarai

hīnuk‍kaṭṭhaṃ kulamavijahaṃ piṇḍāya sampāvisi| ()

64. 

 āhiṇḍattaṃ tahimanugharaṃ piṇḍāya santindriyaṃ

satthāraṃ taṃ nirupamasiriṃ chabbaṇṇaraṃsuj‍jalaṃ,

pāsādaṭṭhā’nimisanayanambhaujaihi sampūjayuṃ

ugghāṭaitvā harimaṇimayaṃ jālāvaliṃ nāgarā| ()

65.

 auhāraitvā kusumasurabhīsaṅkhārasambhāvitai

kaisai massuṃ rajanamalinaṃ kāsāvavatthaṃ kharaṃ,

acchādaitvā kapaṇapurisau’va’yyau gahaitvā sīḷā

pattaṃ pattau kapilanagaraṃ pakiṇḍāya āhiṇḍati| ()

66. 

 vuttantaṃ taṃ savaṇakaṭukaṃ sutvāna bimbādharā

bimbādaivī marakatasiḷājālantarā vithiyaṃ,

āhiṇḍantaṃ parivutagaṇaṃ mattaibhagāmiṃ jitaṃ

aulaukaintī nayanamaṇikai assūhi sampūrayi| ()

67.

 cumbanti sātanujaratanaṃ tandassanabyāvaṭā’-

sityā’nubyañ‍janavilasitaṃ byāmappabhālaṅkataṃ,

rūpaṃ rūpassiri nirupamaṃ saṅgāyi gāthaṭṭhakaṃ

saṃvaṇṇaitvā caraṇatalatau yāva’ssa uṇhisatau| ()

68.

 īsaṃ kālaṃ alasagamanaṃ sā kālahaṃsaupakari

auraupainti abhinavakucandā’tibhārāturā,

gantvā sīghaṃ khaḷitavacatā puttau mahārāja tai

piṇḍāya’smiṃ carati nagarai rājānamic‍cabruvi| ()

69. 

 rājā saināparivutasamau taijaunubhāvādinā

taṃ sutvāṃ’sai sukhumavasanaṃ katvā navaṃsāṭakaṃ,

acchādaitvā nihitamakuṭau nikkhittakhaggau bhusaṃ

laj‍jāpan‍nau tuvaṭatuvaṭaṃ gantvā tadaggai ṭhitau| ()

70.

 kauṭṭhagārānya’pi pitukulai rittāni kimmañ‍ñasi

kasmā laj‍jāpayasi pitaraṃ tvaṃ bhānuvaṃsubbhavau,

bhantai tuyhaṃ pakarivutavasīsaṅghassi’tau bhaujanaṃ

mā kapiṇḍāyā’cari anudinaṃ daj‍jaiyya mic‍cabruvi| ()

71. 

 tuyhaṃ vaṃsau anariyapadau ādic‍cavaṃsau siyā

mayhaṃ vaṃsau sadariyapadau sambuddhavaṃsau siyā,

asmiṃvaṃsai anuvicaraṇaṃ piṇḍattha manvālayaṃ

cārittaṃ bhaupurimasugatā’ciṇṇanti kavatvā jinau| ()

72.

 uttiṭṭhādiṃ avadi kasugamaṃ gāthaṃ ṭhitau vīthiyaṃ

sautāpantau’vanipati bhavī sautāvadhānaina sau,

gāthādhammaṃ suṇiya madhuraṃ dhammaṃcarai’tyā’dikaṃ

pattau maggaṃ dutiyamavīraṃ dhammānudhammaṃ caraṃ| ()

73.

 sutvā rājā cariyamaparaṃ yau dhammapālavhayaṃ

pattau maggaṃ tatiyamakhilaṃ kāmālayaṃ cālayaṃ,

saitacchattu’l‍lasitasayanai’nuṭṭhānasaiyyu’pagau

saṅkhārānaṃ visadamatiyā yau lakkhaṇaṃ sammasi| ()

74. 

 viddhaṃsaitvā namuviparisaṃ saṃkailasamārādikaṃ

surau ramhāvanamiva’sinā sau aggamaggāsinā,

tuṭṭhau maggapphalasukhasudhāpānaina vairisamai

pañ‍cakkhandhai vijaya malbhī nibbānaraj‍jassiriṃ| ()

75.

 āraupaitvā uparibhavanaṃ pattaṃ gahaitvā tatau

rājā saṅghaṃ sugatapamukhaṃ khaj‍jaina bhauj‍jaina ca,

santappaitvā puna saparisau nīcai nisaj‍jāsanai

sārānīyaṃ kathayamavasi sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ| ()

76.

 itthāgāraṃ hadayasarasimajjhai nimuggatthana-

haṃsaṃ dinānanasarasijaṃ saukai’ṇatāpainiva,

buddhaṃ baddhañ‍jaliharasiraukumbhaihi sampūjayī

taṃ vātabbhāhataharilatālīlaṃ jagāmau’nataṃ| ()

77. 

 antaugabbhai nayanasalalaṃ samapuñchamānā jinaṃ

bimbādaivī saparijanatāvyāpāritā vandituṃ,

appatvā mai yadiguṇadhanaṃ attha’yyaputtau sayaṃ

taṃ kamaṃ daṭṭhuṃ nanupavisatī’tvai’vaṃ vadantī ṭhitā| ()

78.

 rañ‍ñā saddhiṃ purisanisahau tāyindirāmandiraṃ

antaugabbhaṃ maṇigaṇapahābhin‍nandhakāraṃsadā,

ādāya’ggaṃ yatipatiyugaṃ patvā’cchi bhaddāsanai

pañ‍ñattai sau’dayagirisirai bālaṃsumālī yathā| ()

79. 

 disvā pīnatthanabharanatā sā rājadhītā jinaṃ

patvā mālā kanakaratanālaṅkārahīnā lahuṃ,

haṃsimañ‍ñai sarasijavanaṃ pādai yathājjhāsayaṃ

sañ‍cumbantī paṇami sirasā ādāyagaupphadvayaṃ| ()

80.

 pāsāda’ntauvarakasarasi dhammil‍lasaivālakai

aumuj‍jantī nijabhujalatālīlātaraṅgākulai,

nāthassa’ṅghītalanakhasikhākantippabandhāmbhasi

laddhassāsā ciravirahajaṃ tāpaṃ vinaudaisi sā| ()

81.

 sutvā naisā kanakaratanā’saṃdhāraṇaṃ dhāraṇaṃ

kāsāvānaṃ tavahiridhanā’vissaj‍janaṃ saj‍janaṃ,

nā’jjhācārai anabhiramaṇaṃ uc‍cāsanai cā’sanai

rājā’vauca tvamanukurutai snaihaudayā’haudayā| ()

82. 

 sutvā tassāniravadhiguṇādhārāya’nūnaṃ guṇaṃ

āvīkatvā’gami bhavapaṭicchan‍nāpadānaṃ jinau,

naitvā gaihappavisanakaraggāhā’bhisaikussavai

saṃvattantai dutiyadivasai nandākhyarājatrajaṃ| ()

83.

 gacchantau’pī saha bhagavatā sau pañ‍cakalyāṇiyā

sīghaṃ jālaṃ vivariya thiyā vīthiṃ vilaukaintiyā,

bhaṅgāpāṅgāyatabhujalatāsaṅkaḍḍhitabbhantarau

pattaṃ bhantai haratha vacanaṃ bhatyā na taṃ vyākari| ()

84. 

 pabbājaitvā visayamadirāmattāya tassā gīraṃ

sutvā nandāpahanahadayaṃ nandaṃ narindatrajaṃ,

icchāpaitvā kakuṭacaraṇidibbaccharāliṅganai

ñāyainā’nuttarasukhamahāraj‍jai patiṭṭhāpayī| ()

85.

 bimbādaivi sukhaparibhataṃ kīḷāparaṃ rāhulaṃ

āliṅgitvā tanujaratanaṃ sā sattamai vāsarai,

ugghaṭaitvā ratanakhacitaṃ jālaṃ vimānaudarai

āgacchantaṃ purisatisabhaṃ nijjhāyamānā ṭhitā| ()

86.

 nānākūṭācalavalayitau daivindacāpākulau

āgacchantau kanakasikharīrājāka yathā jaṅgamau,

tatā khīṇāsavaparivutau jabbaṇṇabhānuj‍jalau

aisau tuyhaṃ naraharika pitā ic‍cākaha pakassāhi naṃ| ()

87. 

 aitassā’suṃ vividhanidhayau puñ‍ñānubhāvuṭṭhitā

nāhaṃja kapakassāma’bhigamanatau paṭṭhāya taikhaunidhī,

bhūsāpaitvā tanujaratanaṃ sā sattavassāyukaṃ

yācassū’ti pahiṇi pitunau ñattaṃ dhanaṃ paittikaṃ| ()

88.

 uppādaitvā pitari balavaṃ paimaṃ jalaivu’ppalaṃ

puttautyā’haṃ tvamasijanakau chāyā’pi taimai sukhā,

aḍhāsai’vaṃ lapitavacanau vuṭṭhāya bhaddāsanā

bhuttāhārauka parivutavasi gantuṃ jinaucā’rabhī| ()

89.

 dāyaj‍jaṃ mai samaṇa dadataṃ atthaudhanainā’ti mai

yācaṃ yācaṃ jinamanuvajaṃ sāraṅgarājak‍kamaṃ,

sīhacchāpauriva bhagavatau daḷhaṃ surattaṅgulī-

mālāyālaṅkari bhujalataṃ bhaugindabhaugāyataṃ| ()

90. 

 saṃyācan‍naṃ vibhava kamanugaṃka vaṭṭānugaṃ rāhulaṃ

pabbājaitvā’riyadhananidhiṃdaimīti cintāparau,

patvā’rāmaṃ ajahitasutau saddhammarājā imaṃ

pabbājaihi’tya’vadi sumukhaṃ tvaṃ dhammasaināpati| ()

91.

 chaitvā nīluppaladalamuduṃ cūḷākalāpaṃ mahā-

mauggal‍lānau kavasi abhinavaṃ kāsāvamacchādayī,

tassau’vādaṃ akari dhutavā thairau mahākassapau

pabbājaisi tanujaratanaṃ taṃ sāriputtau vasi| ()

92. 

 sikkhākāmau aparasamayai thairau mahārāhulau-

vādaṃ sutvā’dhikataraguṇaṃ sampāpuṇī rāhulau,

sutvā kasuttaṃ puna taditaraṃ sikkhāgarūnaṃ garu-

ṭṭhānaṃ pattau tibhavamatari patvā’ggamaggapphalaṃ|ka ()

93.

 tasmiṃ suddhaudananaravarau pabbaj‍jitai nattari

ajjhaugāḷhau ravikuladhajaunissīmasaukaṇṇavai,

din‍nau’kāsaṃ kamapitanayaṃ mātāpitūhā’yatiṃ

pabbājaiyyuṃ alamitivaraṃ saṃyāci vausāvakā| ()

94.

 rañ‍ñau datvā varamati varaṃ bhuttāsanau āsanā-

vuṭṭhāya’ntaubhavanavanatau nikkhamma mandānilaṃ,

rukkhacchāyāviraḷasarasītīraṃ vivaikakkhamaṃ

sītaṃ sitabbanamavasarī chaddantadantī’vasau| ()

95. 

 tasmiṃkālai gahapatikulai jātau mahāsaiṭṭhipi

pattau rājaggahapuravaraṃ saddhau sudattābhidhau,

buddhauka hutvā yamadhivasatai’tya’ssausi suddhaudanī

pac‍cūsasmiṃ amaravivaṭadvāraina tatrā’gamā| ()

96.

 appaitvā’ṅakghīratanaphalakai khittañ‍jalīmañ‍jariṃ

bhatyācūḷārajatakalasaṃ cittappasādāvahanaṃ,

dhammaṃ sutvā paṭhamadivasai laddhādimaggapphalau

dānaṃ datvā sugatapamukhai saṅghai sudattaudhanī| ()

97. 

 bhantai lakkhīkamakalamalakā saṅkāsamatthāya nau

iddhaṃ phakhītaṃ sujanabhajitaṃ sāvatthisaṅkhaṃ puraṃ,

dhammassāmi vajatu karuṇāchāyāsakahāyau lahuṃ

laddhassāsau savisayamagāaivaṃ katajjhaisanau| ()

98.

 buddhatthaṃ sau gahapati mahāmaggai samaggai divā-

rattiṭṭhānappabhutisubhagai pac‍caikalakkhaṃ dhanaṃ,

vissaj‍jaitvā pacuravibhavau daṭṭhabbasāraipurai

kāraupaisi amarabhavanākāraika vihārai varai| ()

99.

 kauṭīha’ṭṭhārasahī asamaṃ bhūmiṃ kiṇitvā samaṃ

kauṭīha’ṭṭhārasahi pacuraṃ māpaitva saināsanaṃ,

kauṭīha’ṭṭhārasahi paramaṃ ārāmapūjāmahaṃ

saj‍jaitvā sau gahapati navaṃkammaṃ suniṭṭhāpayi| ()

100. 

 aivaṃ jaitavanaṃ vihārapavaraṃ kārāpayitvā mahā-

vīrassā’gamanāya dūtapurisai paisaisi saiṭṭhissarau,

taisaṃ sīsaharañ‍jalihi mahitau sutvāna taṃ sāsanaṃ

sambuddhau jalitiddhimā saparisau rājaggahā nikkhami| ()

iti maidhānandābhidhānaina yatinā viracitai sakala kavijana hadayānandadāna nidānai jinavaṃsadīpai santikaika nidānai bhagavatau pakiṇṇaka caritappavatti paridīpau sauḷasamau saggau|

1. 

 satthu jaitavananāmamindirā-

rāma mautaraṇamaṅgalussavai,

rāmalakkhaṇabhagī (rathauddhatā)

saiṭṭhiputtapamukhā sumaṇḍitā| ()

2.

 pañ‍camattasatamāṇavā navā

pañ‍cavaṇṇadhajabhāsura’ñ‍jalī,

pañ‍cakhāṇasiriyu’j‍jalā’bhavuṃ

pañ‍camārajinarājinau purai| ()

3.

 haimakumbhakucakumbhavibbhamā

saiṭhidhītupamukhā kumārikā,

puṇṇakumbhasatanibbhara’ñ‍jalī

tassa satthu puratau tatau bhavuṃ| ()

4. 

 saiṭṭhipādaparicārikāsakhī

mantharā’vajitahaṃsadhainuyau,

pañ‍camattasatanāriyau’bhavuṃ

pacchatau gahitapuṇṇapātiyau| ()

5.

 saitavatthasunivatthapārutau

pañ‍casaiṭṭhisatamattasaivitau,

laukanāyaka manāthapiṇḍikau

saiṭṭhi pītiairitau tamanvagā| ()

6.

 nīlapītapabhūtīhi mārajī

daiharaṃsivisaraihi jautayaṃ,

añ‍jasaṃ sahacarācaraṃ bhuvi

jaṅgamau kanakabhūdharauriva| ()

7.

 maggadaisakajanassa piṭṭhitau

bhikkhusaṅghaparivāritau yahiṃ,

jaitanāmavanamautarī tahiṃ

laukalaucananipīyamānakau| ()

8. 

 pucchitassa paṭipaj‍janak‍kamaṃ

tāya jaitavanapujanāya sau,

daihi buddhapamukhai tapauvanaṃ

bhikkhusaṅghavisayai’ti pabruvi| ()

9.

 dammi buddhapamukhai tapauvanaṃ

bhikkhusaṅghavisayai’ti bhindiya’

byappathaṃ thiramanāthapiṇḍikau

saiṭṭhi dhautakarapaṅkajauka sadā| ()

10.

 jālalakkhaṇavicitrakaumaḷa-

pāṇipal‍lavatalaisu satthutau,

dakkhiṇaudakamadāsi kañ‍cana-

kuṇḍikāya surabhippavāsitaṃ| ()

11. 

 sitamuṇhamanilātapaṃ paṭi-

hanti ḍaṃsamakasai siriṃsapai,

svā’numaudanamakā jinau paṭi-

ggayha jaitavanamaivamādinā| ()

12.

 sau sudattavisutau samāpayi

vatrabhūpamapabhūvibhūsaṇau,

saiṭṭhi jaitavanapūjanāmahaṃ

cattakauṭidhanasañ‍cayau sadā| ()

13.

 indirāya suramandiraupamā

candanāgarusugandhabandhurā,

tatra gandhakuṭi bhāti maidinī-

sundarisirasi saikharau yathā| ()

14.

 tāya gandhakuṭiyā’dhirauhiṇī

dhammarājacaraṇindirā’dharā,

saggamaukkhabhavanappavaisani-

ssaiṇipaddhatirivā’ti naumati| ()

15. 

 tabbihāraparitau sudhāmaṇi-

baddhamāvaraṇacak‍ka māharai,

satthu kittisirikhīrasāgaru-

tatuṅgavīcivalayassiriṃ sadā| ()

16.

 tabbihāraparivaiṇamausadhi-

tārakādhavalavāḷukākulaṃ,

vyākarauti jinakundabandhunau

saṅgamaina saradambarassiriṃ| ()

17.

 tattharattamaṇikaitusaṃhatī-

raṃsibhin‍natimirambarai na kiṃ,

kauvidaihi ravicanda tārakā

jautiriṅgananibhā’ti vuc‍carai| ()

18. 

 bhāti phul‍lavanarājilakkhiyā

rattakambalamivā’hisanthataṃ,

cac‍caraṃ caraṇasampaṭicchanai

satthunau kusumaraiṇu nibbharaṃ| ()

19.

 bhiṅgapantimaṇitantusibbitaṃ

mandamārutatharussitaṃ tahiṃ,

puppharaiṇupaṭalabbitāna mā-

bhātisatthu’pari vāritātapaṃ| ()

20.

 rājarukkhakaṇikārasākhinau

phul‍litā parisamantatau tahiṃ,

satthu dhammasavaṇaina dissarai

cīvarāni’va nivatthapārutā| ()

21. 

 uggatā’likuladhūmakaj‍jalā

nibbikāsakalikāsikhāvalī,

campakadadumapadīpasākhinau

uj‍jalanti satavaṇṭavattikā| ()

22.

 jhāyataṃ kamadhuradhammabhārati-

nijjharaihi sikharidari tahiṃ,

sammavaigaparisausitā da’pi

kiṃ navūpasamayanti sādhavau| ()

23.

 kujitālikulakaukilā tahiṃ

phul‍litaggasahakārasākhinau,

tibbarāgacaritai’pi mūlagai

bhāvanāsu naramāpayanti kiṃ| ()

24.

 lājapañ‍camakapupphasanthataṃ

tantapauvanapavaisanañ‍jasaṃ,

vītarāgacaraṇaṅkasaj‍jitaṃ

saggamaggamapahāsatai sadā| ()

25. 

 nārivāmacaraṇāturā’pi yai

saṅgamaina vigataṅgaṇaṅginaṃ

laumahaṃsajanitai’va pītiyā|

tatra’saukatarurāji rājatai, ()

26.

 kiṃsukādikusumaihi bhāsuraṃ

taṃ tapauvana manālayālayaṃ,

taisa muggatapataijasā bhusaṃ

aggipaj‍jalitamaiva dissatai| ()

27.

 uddhavaṇṭagaḷitaihi phul‍lasai-

phālikākuḍumalaihi sālinī,

mālakā rajatavaidikā kaviya

vidadumaihi khavitā virājatai| ()

28. 

 pīta cuta makaranda bindavau

tatra kīrakaravikasārikā,

kiṃka haranti madhuraṃ ravantipi

mañ‍jubhāṇīmunibhāratissiriṃ| ()

29.

 haimakūṭamakuṭaihi nijjhara-

bhārabhāsurataṭau’rapīvarā,

bhuribhuridharabhubhujā tahiṃ

cumbarai jinasuta’ṅghipaṅkajai| ()

30.

 cārucañ‍cupuṭatuṅgacucukā

cak‍kavākakucamaṇḍalā tahiṃ,

nīlikākacakalāpasālinī

nīlanīrajavilaulalaucanā| ()

31. 

 saiṇibaddhakalahaṃsamaikhalā-

dāmabhārataṭapīnasauṇinī,

bhiṅgacak‍karatanaṅgadāvalī

bhaṅgavīcikaṇahārabhāsurā| ()

32.

 kaṇṇikāgaḷitakañ‍jakaisara-

piñ‍jarambuvimalambarā subhā,

gandhavāhasukhaphassadā siri-

mandirā kumudamandahāsinī| ()

33.

 kaisarāliradanā saraujinī-

kāminī vikacapaṅkajānanā,

vītasabbadarathaihi saivitā

dibbapaukkharaṇiyau najainatikiṃ| ()

34. 

 muddikāpabhutival‍livail‍lita-

jiṇṇacīvarakuṭīhi jhāyataṃ,

piñchāsāritasikhaṇḍimaṇḍalā-

khaṇḍataṇḍavasumaṇḍitaṃ vanaṃ| ()

35.

 satthu kasāvakasataihi bhāvanā-

sattibhin‍natimisāti katthaci,

dissarai niracakāsatau tahiṃ

gabbharau’darasamausarāni’va| ()

36.

 kālakā dhutapisaṅgavāladhī

māḷakaisu kalaviṅkasāḷikā,

bhattasitthamanubhūya nibbhayā

dhammarāvamanukūjarai tahiṃ| ()

37. 

 vitamac‍cubhayabhantalaucanaṃ

ālavālajalapānadauhaḷaṃ,

satthu mañ‍jusarapāsanic‍calaṃ

dissatai hariṇamaṇḍalaṃ tahiṃ| ()

38.

 hatthavail‍litalatāhi vāraṇā

vānarāca maṇivijanīhi’va,

vijayanti bhavatāpabhīrukai

rukkhamūlagatajhāyinau tahiṃ| ()

39.

 maighavaṇṇavanarājirājinī

kandamūlaphalabhaujanaihi sā,

dānapāramiratai’va pīṇayai

bhikkhūsaṅghasahitaṃ tathāgataṃ| ()

40. 

 dhammamaṇḍapavitānamuddhati

lambamānamaṇibubbulaudarai,

nic‍capaj‍jalitavij‍jurājiyau

bhanti nij‍jitaravindutārakā| ()

41.

 rukkhakauṭarakulāvakaudarai

kujitaihi sakuṇaihi taṃvanaṃ,

jaiti saṅkhaghaṇavaṃsaval‍lakī-

rāvasārasuraraṅgabhusiriṃ| ()

42.

 indanīlamaṇitauraṇippabhā-

bhinditabbatimiraupamaṃ tahiṃ,

candacaṇḍakaramaṇḍaladvayaṃ

vindataiva asurindavibbhamaṃ| ()

43. 

 khīrasāgarataraṅgapaṇḍarā

naikacaṅkamanamālakā tahiṃ,

phuṭṭhacārucaraṇindirā bhusaṃ

bhanti jhānapasutāna massamai| ()

44.

 bhāvanāya pavanāni pāvanā

daisanāya rasanā vibhūsanā,

saivakā danavakā sasāvakā

mānayantī kavikanayaṃ sukhānayaṃ| () (yamakabandhanaṃ|)

45.

 kīcakā tyanilakūja kīcakā

vācakā rivagaṇassa vā cakā,

maucakā navaphalassa maucakā

maicakā camaṇithambha maicakā| () (yamakabandhanaṃ|)

46. 

 kūjitā’li bhajitā’parākajitā

rājitā’lakajitā hi pūjitā,

gāravā’kararavāyakairavā

kairavākararavā sagāravā| () (yamakabandhanaṃ|)

47.

 kaitakī kusumahantacātakī

ambarai ṇukaṇikā’valambarai,

vuñ‍citā utuniyāmayañ‍jitā

rāmabhumi paramābhirāmabhū| () (yamakabandhanaṃ|)

48.

 tāsadā havisadāna māsadā

yau sadātiya sadā kama taṃ sadā,

sau tamau dahatamau hi tattamau

vītamau muhatamau hī gautamau| () (yamakabandhanaṃ|)

49. 

 sālakā nanavilāsapā latā

mālakā valisubhāsamā lakā,

māḷakā valisubhāsamā lakā

sāḷakā nanavilāsapā lakā| () (yamakabandhanaṃ)

50.

 vānaiva jātau vijitau vanaiva

jinauva’naijau kavanajānanau nau,

naitā vinaitā vijanānu vātai

vanī janaṃ jaitavanai vinaintau| () (caturakkharika citta yamakaṃ|)

iti maidhānandābhidhānanayatinā viracitai sakalakavijana hadayānandadānanidānai jinavaṃsadīpai santikai nidānai jaitavana vihārālaṅkāra paridīpau sattarasamau saggau|

1. 

 bhuvika yassa jinassa tādinau

vidurānaṃ mukharaṅgamandirai,

arahādi guṇāti sundarī)

lasatai kitti vilāsa sundarī| ()

migadāyatapauvanai isi-patanai gautama gautta kaitusau,

narasārathi vassa mādimaṃ

vasi bārāṇasirājadhāniyaṃ| ()

3.

 puna vaiḷuvanai vināyakau

nagarai rājagahai giribbajai,

dutiyaṃ tatiyaṃ catutthakaṃ

avasi vassamanuddayāparau| ()

4. 

 muni pañ‍camavassa mindirā-

layavaisāli puraṃ mahāvanai,

pavihāsi puraṅgapīvaraṃ

upanissāya yathaiva kaisari| ()

5.

 timirāpaharau’sadhīlatā-

jalitai sītala nijjharākulai,

munichaṭṭhamanālayau sukhaṃ

vasi vassaṃ puthumaṅkulācalai| ()

6.

 tidasālayagau sudubbudhaṃ

abhidhammaṃ kathayaṃ silāsanai,

sunisaj‍ja savītināmayī

sugatau sattamavassa manvahaṃ| ()

7. 

 haricandanagandhapāvana-

pavanai bhaisakalābhidhai vanai,

sanarāmara laukanāyakau

munināgau vasi vassamaṭṭhamaṃ| ()

8.

 madhurassarabhāṇi ghausita-

visutai ghausita saiṭṭhikāritai,

navamaṃ vasi vassamassamai

varakausambi purai munissarau| ()

9.

 munikaisari pārilaiyyaka-

karinājīvitadānatau bhatau,

dasamaṃ vasi pārilaiyyakai

vanasaṇḍai tarusaṇḍamaṇḍitai| ()

10.

 varadhammasudhārasaina sa-

j‍jana maikādasamaṃ samaṃ jinau,

dvijagāmavarai’bhipīṇayaṃ

vasi nāḷāviditai nirālayau| ()

11. 

 dharaṇīsuragāma māsadā

punavairañ‍ja maniñ‍janau jinau,

pucimandadumindamūlagau

asamau bārasamaṃ samaṃ vasī| ()

12.

 vikacappalacārulaucanau

munirājā vajirācalācalau,

sikharākulacāliyācalai

avasī taiḷasamaṃ guṇālayau| ()

13.

 salilāsayasītalai mudu-

pavanai jaitavanai tapauvanai,

vasi cuddasamaṃ samaṃ mahā-

samaṇau assamaṇā’pasādanau| ()

14. 

 kapilavhayarājadhāniyā

avidurai nararāhasaiyyakai,

pavihāsi tipañ‍camaṃ samaṃ

muni nigraudhatapauvanai subhai| ()

15.

 khara mālavakañ‍ca rakkhasaṃ

damayaṃ bhūdhara pīvaraudaraṃ,

rucirālavirājadhāniyaṃ

viharī sauḷasamaṃ samaṃ jinau| ()

16.

 puna vaiḷuvanaṃ tapauvanaṃ

upanissāya giribbajaṃ puraṃ,

dasa sattama vassa māvasī

munisīhau hatamāravāraṇau| ()

17.

 bhavadukkharujāhi maucayaṃ

janataṃ dhammakathā’gadaina sau,

vasi cāliyapabbatālayai

jinavaij‍jācariyau dasaṭṭhamaṃ| ()

18. 

 tadanantaravassa muggadhi

vipulai cāliyapakabbatai’va sau,

vasi vīsatimaṃ giribbajai

nagarai vaiḷuvanai tapauvanai| ()

19.

 anibaddhavihāratau iti

viharantau bhagavā tahiṃtahiṃ

maṇijautirasau’va kāmadau

saradai vīsati vītināmayī| ()

20.

 muni jaitavanai tapauvanai

bhavanai cā’pi migāramātuyā,

mahitai vasi pañ‍cavīsati-

mitavassāni tibaddhavāsagau| ()

21. 

 anibaddhanibaddhavāsatau

vasatau tassa satau tahiṃ tahiṃ,

nanu vij‍jati kic‍capañ‍cakaṃ

katakic‍cassa kathananuvāsaraṃ| ()

22.

 aruṇuggamanai samuṭṭhitau

tadupaṭṭhākajanassa’nuggahaṃ,

munirānanapādadhauvanaṃ

pavidhāyā’khilakic‍ca mattanau| ()

23.

 sunisaj‍ja susaj‍jitā’sanai

sapadānācaraṇāya yāvatā,

samayau samayañ‍ñu vindati

naciraṃ jhānasukhaṃ rahaugatau| ()

24. 

 paribandhiya tāyabandhanaṃ

sunivatthantaravāsakau’pari,

arahaddhajachāditaṅgimā

maṇivaṇṇaupalapatta mubbahaṃ| ()

25.

 abhisaṅkhatapuñ‍ñasattiyā

vivaṭadvāravihāragabbhatau,

girigabbharatau’va kaisarī

bahinikkhamma kadāci aikakau, ()

26.

 valayikatatārakāvalī

navacandauriva vāridaudarā,

yatisaṅghapurakkhatau tatau

bahi nikkhamma kadāci sau muni| ()

27.

 pakatīgatiyā’pi bhikkhituṃ

gatiyā sappaṭihāriyā’yapi,

yugamattadasau samācarai

nigamaggāmapurīsu katthaci| ()

28. 

 caratau varapāṭihāriyaṃ

samadhiṭṭhāya kadāci bhikkhituṃ,

vimalīkurutai mahītalaṃ

puratau mandasugandhamārutau| ()

29.

 puratau’pasamainti dhūliyau

caratau cārutarañ‍jasai sirai,

vilasanti vitāna vibbhamā

navamaighā phusitāni muñ‍carai| ()

30.

 kusumāni samīraṇā’parai

vipinainā’hariyau’kirantipi,

nijapādatalaṃ’va bhūtalaṃ

samataṃ yāti pathai padappitai| ()

31. 

 mudukā sukhaphassadā mahī-

vanitā tappadasaṅgamai’kadā,

kamalānipi cumbarai’kadā

pathaviṃ bhaij‍ja tadaṅghīpaṅkajai| ()

32.

 caraṇak‍kamitā’ravindaja-

makarandā’ti sugandhabandhurā,

jinagandhagajinda māsiraṃ

parivāsainti samaukirantipi| ()

33.

 makarandapabandhavibbhamaṃ

juti sindhūravicuṇṇabandhurā,

abhibhūya supiñ‍jarāyatai

caratau laukamimaṃ carācaraṃ| ()

34.

 kalahaṃsa mayūrasārikā

karavīkā’pi sakaṃsakaṃ ravaṃ,

dvipadā’pi catuppadā’parai

vajatau tassa napūjayanti kiṃ| ()

35. 

 turiyāni vibhūsaṇāni’pi

sayamaivā’bhiravanti taṅkhaṇai,

tamudikkhiya pāṭihāriyaṃ

sugatai kauka hi nasampasīdati| ()

36.

 vividhabbhutapāṭihāriya-

katasañ‍ñāya mahājanau jinau,

janayaṃ janatāya’nuddayaṃ

idhapiṇḍatthamupāgatau iti| ()

37.

 kusumādiyamākulañ‍jalī

sadanaiha’ntaravithi mautarai,

jinaraṃsi pabandha kambala-

satasañchan‍na vivaṇṇaviggahā| ()

38. 

 janatā nakharālidīdhiti-

nikarākāsanadīnimuj‍jitā,

abhivandati vandanārahaṃ

muninau pādayugaṃ pamauditā| ()

39.

 dasavisativā mahājanau

jinapāmaukkhayatī satampivā,

abhiyācati daitha nauiti

bhagavantaṃ vibhavānurūpatau| ()

40.

 adhivāsanamassa jāniya

janatā’dāya jinassa hatthatau,

tamadhiṭṭhitapatta mindirā-

sadanaṃ dānagharaṃ pavaisayai| ()

41. 

 catujātikagandhabhāvitai

bhuvi pañ‍ñattavarāsanaupari,

ahatāhatavatthajātitai

sunisin‍naṃ sugataṃ sasāvakaṃ| ()

42.

 paṭiyattapaṇītabhaujana-

vikatīhai’va sahatthapaṅkajā,

abhitappayatai mahājanau

patimānaiti ca cīvarādinā| ()

43.

 saraṇāgamanai’pi pañ‍cayu

adhisīlaisu patiṭṭhahanti yai,

catumaggaphalaisu katthavi

tadabhiñ‍ñā’nusayāsayāditau| ()

44.

 bhagavā katabhattakic‍cavā

anurūpāya kathāya dhammiyā,

ravibandhu vinaiyya bandhunaṃ

hadayambhaujavanaṃ pabaudhayai| ()

45. 

 harimairugiri’va jaṅgamau

parinaddhindasarāsanāvalī,

visatai satapuñ‍ñalakkhaṇau

muniru’ṭṭhāya vihāramāsanā| ()

46.

 varamaṇḍalamāḷakai tahiṃ

muni pañ‍ñattamahārahāsanai,

khaṇamāgamayaṃ nisidati

yaminaṃ bhaujanakic‍casādhanaṃ| ()

47.

 maṇivammasuvammitā viya

karinau pārutapaṃsukūlikā,

yatayau yatirājayūthapaṃ

parivārainti upaic‍ca taṅkhaṇai| ()

48. 

 samayaṃ samayañ‍ñunau tatau

tadupaṭṭhākavarau nivaidayai,

jinagandhagajau suvāsitaṃ

visatai gandhakuṭiṃ sugandhinā| () (puraibhattakic‍caṃ)

49.

 athagandhakuṭīmukhai jinau

virajau pādarajau nacatthipi,

paridhautapadāni nikkhipaṃ

maṇisaupāṇatalai khaṇaṃ ṭhitau| ()

50.

 udayau’pi jinassa dul‍labhau

khaṇasampattisamiddhi dul‍labhā,

manujaisu’papatti dul‍labhā

jinadhammassavaṇampi dul‍labhaṃ| ()

51.

 samaṇatta mapai’ttha dul‍labhaṃ

tividhaṃ sāsana mappamādatau,

yatayau’vadatā’nusāsati

abhisampādayathā’ti bhikkhavai| ()

52. 

 abhivandiya kaici bhikkhavau

bhagavantaṃtaka paṭipattipūrakā,

atha sampaṭipādanak‍kamaṃ

paṭipucchantivipassanādisu| ()

53.

 padadāti vipassanādisu

muni taisaṃ cariyānurūpikaṃ,

paṭigaṇhiya satthusāsanaṃ

maṇidāmaṃ viya makaṇḍanatthikau| ()

54.

 pavidhāya jinaṃ padakkhiṇaṃ

atha tai bhattisamappitañ‍jalī,

pavisanti yatī sakaṃsakaṃ

vasatiṃ santanivātavuttinau| ()

55. 

 vatapabbatapādakandara-

pabhūtīsva’ñ‍ñataraṃka padhānikā,

pavisanti surāsurauraga-

garuḷānaṃ bhavanaisucā’parai| ()

56.

 atha gandhakuṭiṃ yadicchati

pavisitvā pavivaikakāmavā,

muni dakkhiṇapassatau satau

sayanaṃ kappayatī’sakaṃ divā| ()

57.

 vupasantasarirajassamau

muniru’ṭṭhāya anaikakauṭiyau,

anubhūyaka samādhayau khaṇaṃ

bhuvanaṃ passati buddhacakkhunā| ()

58. 

 samathamhi vipassanāyavā

dhuranikkhaipakatai tathāgatai,

tahimiddhibalainu’paṭṭhitau

puna vuṭṭhāpayatai dayānidhi| ()

59.

 iti pañ‍casatampi sāvakai

atikhippaṃ kabhagavā’nusāsiya,

padumāniva tai pabaudhayaṃ

nabhāsā yāti vihāra mattanau| ()

60.

 jinasindhavapādavik‍kamaṃ

jinachaddantagajindakuñ‍canaṃ,

jinakaisarasīhagaj‍janaṃ

abhipassāma suṇauma nau iti| ()

61.

 viharaiyya yahiṃ jinau tahiṃ

aparaṇhai kusumākulañ‍jalī,

sunivatthasupārutā bhusaṃ

muditā san‍nipatantikhau janā| ()

62. 

 atha dassitapāṭihāriyau

pavisatvā varadhammamaṇḍapaṃ,

sūriyauva yugandharau’pari

sunisaj‍jā’sanamatthakai jinau| ()

63.

 karavikavirāvahārinā

madhurau’dārasaraina sautunaṃ,

caturā’riyasac‍camīrayai

anupubbāya kathāya nissitaṃ| ()

64.

 paṭigaṇhiya dhammamādarā

nijavauhāra’nurūpagaucaraṃ,

abhiyāti padakkhiṇaina sā

parisātaṃ sirasā’bhivandiya| () (pacchābhattakic‍caṃ)

66. 

 varavāraṇakumbhadāraṇau

migarājāva kudiṭṭhabhañ‍janau,

atha niṭṭhitadhammagaj‍janau

muniru’ṭṭhāyu’pavaisanā’sanā| ()

65.

 kamalaṃ’va kalaivaraṃ varaṃ

vimalaṃ vitarajaumalaṃ jinau,

avasiñ‍citukāmavā sacai

pavisitvāna nahānakauṭṭhakaṃ| ()

67.

 tadūpaṭṭhitikaina bhikkhunā

paṭiyattainudakaina viggahai,

samitautuparissamau muhuṃ

punarāgamma nivatthacīvarau| ()

68. 

 sunisaj‍ja sina’ssamai samai

parivaiṇai ṭhapitāsanaupari,

anubhauti muhutta mattanā

suvimuttau’pi vimuttijaṃ sukhaṃ| ()

69.

 tadupaṭṭhitipac‍cupaṭṭhitā

abhinikkhammatatautatauyatī,

mahitañ‍jalipupphamañ‍jarī

parivāraintitilaukanāyakaṃ| ()

70.

 paṭipattipapūraṇak‍kamaṃ

paṭipucchanti vipucchanāni’pi,

yatayau hi visuṃvisuṃ jinaṃ

savaṇaṃ dhammakathāya yācarai| ()

71.

 bhagavā karuṇāya cauditau

tadadhippāya mavaic‍ca buddhiyā,

abhisādhaya matthamuttamaṃ

purimaṃ yāmamatik‍kamai iti| () (purimayāmakic‍caṃ)

72. 

 bhagavanta manantadassinaṃ

sirasā taisugataisu bhikkhusu,

abhivandiya jātikhaittatau

labhamānā’vasaraṃ surāsurā| ()

73.

 upagamma tapauvanaṅgaṇaṃ

kurumānā chavivaṇṇapiñ‍jaraṃ,

maṇimauḷimaricisañ‍caya-

paricumbīkaḷitaṃ sirimatau| ()

74.

 nakhakaisaramaṅgulīdalaṃ

caraṇambhaujayugaṃka pavandiya,

caturakkharikampi puccharai

varapañha’ntamasau’hisaṅkhataṃ| ()

75. 

 sivadau vadataṃanuttarau

muni vissaj‍jati tabbipucchanaṃ,

atha vitakathaṅkathi tada-

bbhanumaudanti abhitthavantipi| ()

76.

 nijadhammapadīpataijasā

janasammauha tamauvidhaṃsanau,

iti majjhimayāma manvahaṃ

katakic‍cau muni vītināmayai| () (majjhimayāmakic‍caṃ)

77.

 abhibhutasarīrajassamau

katakic‍caihi’riyāpathaihica,

atha caṅkamaṇaina pacchimai

paṭhamaṃ bhāga matik‍kamai muni| ()

78. 

 paṭivāta’nuvāta vāyita-

guṇagandhaihi sugandhitaṅgimā,

maṇidīpapabhāsamuj‍jalaṃ

sugatau gandhakuṭiṃka upāgatau| ()

79.

 sayanaupari sampasārayaṃ

paṭimārūpasarūpaviggahaṃ,

sayanaṃ kurutai’va kaisarī

dutiyasmiṃ satisampajañ‍ñavā| ()

80.

 asamiddhakilaisamiddhavā

bhagavā bhaṅgabhavaṅgasattati,

sunisaj‍ja pabujjhitau mahā-

karuṇājhāna mupaiticā’sanai| ()

81. 

 purimaisu bhavaisu pāṇinau

yadi vij‍janti katādhikārinau,

raviraṃsivikāsanūpaga-

padumānī’va pabaudhanārahā| ()

82.

 karuṇāya samuṭṭhitau tatau

karuṇāsītalamānasau muni,

abhipassati buddhacakkhunā

bhuvi tai maggaphalaupanissayai| ()

83.

 iti pacchimayāma manvahaṃ

tatiyaṃ bhāga matik‍kamai jinau,

purimauditakic‍cakārinau

katakic‍cassa acintiyāguṇā| () (pacchimayāmakic‍caṃ)

84. 

 pasidanti rūpappa māṇapi buddhai

pasidanti ghausappamāṇapi buddhai

pasidanti ḷukhappa māṇapi buddhai

pasidanti dhammappamāṇapi buddhai ()

iti maidhānandābhidhānainayatinā viracitai sakalakavijana hadayānandadānanidānai jinavaṃsadīpai santikai nidānai pañ‍javidhabuddhakic‍ca paridīpau aṭṭhārasamauka saggau|

1. 

 niravadhibhuvanālavālagabbhai

sucaritamūlavirūḷhakittival‍lī,

navaguṇaniyamautu saṅgamaina

bhagavatamāsiyathaiva (pupphitaggā)| ()

2.

 suvisadamatimā savāsanaihi

sakala kilaisa malaihi cārakāyau,

itipi bhagavatau budhābhigītau

bhuvivisutau arahanti kittighausau| ()

3.

 gahita nisita maggañāṇa khaggau

varamati duc‍caritārayau achindi,

itipi bhagavatau hatārinauyau

bhuvivisutau arahanti kittighausau| ()

4.

 tibhavarathasamappitaṃ avij‍jā-

bhavatasiṇāmayanābhika māsavakkhaṃ,

vicitupacitakamma sañ‍cāyā’raṃ

jaṭitajarāmaraṇairunaimi vaṭṭiṃ ()

5. 

 avidita pariyanta kālasīmaṃ

paribhamitaṃ bhavacak‍kamatthi tassa,

thiravīriyapadaihi baudhimaṇḍai

suvimalasīlamahītalaiṭhitau yau| ()

6.

 hanivihani arai visuddhasaddhā-

karakamalaina samādhisāṇapiṭṭhai,

sunisita masamaṃ nihanti kamma-

kkhayakarañāṇakuṭhārimādadhānau| ()

7. 

 hatabhavaratha vibbhamassa magga-

rathamabhiruyha sivaṃpuraṃ gatassa,

itipi bhagavatau hatārakassa

bhuvivisutau arahanti kittighausau| ()

8.

 avidita pariyanta dukkha vaṭṭaṃ

athabhavacak‍kaka mituc‍catai avij‍jā,

tahimupahitanābhī mūlakattā

bhavatijarāmaraṇaṃ tadantanaimi| ()

9.

 ghaṭita tadubhayantarā arāssu

dasa abhisaṅkharaṇādisaisa dhammā,

kasirasamudayai niraudha maggai

bhavati pajānamajānanaṃ avij‍jā| ()

10 

 bhavati tividhabhumikā avij‍jā

catuvidha sac‍casabhāva chādakā’yaṃ,

abhivicinana caitanāna maddhā

vividhanayaina bhavattayai nidānaṃ| ()

11.

 sakasaka paṭisandhicittahaitu

dvaya mabhisaṅkhata kammamaiva kāmai,

cita kusala marūpa rūpa gāmiṃ

tadubhayabhupaṭisandhihaitu hauti| ()

12.

 kusalamakusalaṃ tidhā vibhattaṃ

purimabhavainicitaṃ yathānurūpaṃ,

nabhavati paṭisandhitauparaṃ kiṃ

tibhava pavatti vipāka citta haitu| ()

13. 

 samudayamaya kāmarūpapākā

sati paṭisandhi pavattiyaṃ bhavantī,

saka saka bhavanāma rūpahaitu

vividha nidānavasaina cānurūpaṃ, ()

14.

 tathariva caturau arūpapākā

sakasakabhumikanāmapac‍cayāva,

samupacita manaṃ asañ‍ñasattai

nabhavatikiṃka bhuvi rūpamattahaitu| ()

15. 

 tadubhaya mavipāka cittataupi

pabhavati tīsubhavaisu cā’nu rūpaṃ,

sugati dugatiyaṃ padaṃhi kāmai

bhavati saḷāyatanassa nāma rūpaṃ| ()

16.

 bhavati padaka masañ‍ñi vaj‍jarūpa-

bhuvi tividhāyatanassa nāma rūpaṃ,

havati samudayau bhavai arūpai

sukhuma manāyatanassa nāma mattaṃ| ()

17.

 iti tividhabhavai bhavanti taṃ taṃ-

bhava pabhavāyatanāti phassahaitu,

tathariva kamatau bhavānu rūpaṃ

tividhabhavaisu chaphassavaidanaijā| ()

18. 

 vividha bhavagatiṭṭhitīsu taṃ taṃ

chaṭita jaṭā gahaṇassa haitu hauti,

punarubhaya bhavassa daḷhagāhau,

bhavati bhavautibhavamhi haitujātyā| ()

19.

 tividha bhavu’papattijātiraisā

bhavati jarāmaraṇādi dukkha haitu,

sakalakasirupaddavā’savānaṃ

samudayahaitutatausiyā avij‍jā| ()

20.

 thiragahaṇavasaina yauhi kauci

sucarita duc‍caritaṃcaraiyya tassa,

sugati dugati gāmi kammamaittha

kathayati kammabhavau’ti kammavādī| ()

21. 

 vicitupacita kammasattijātā

vadatupapatti bhavau’ti pañ‍cakhandhā,

tadabhijanana māhajāti taisaṃ

cuticavanaṃ paripākatājarā’ti| ()

22.

 pahavaphalapabandhatau ṭhītānaṃ

sarasagabhīrapaṭic‍ca sambhavānaṃ,

kayirati visadāya yāya dhamma–

ṭhitimatināmadhiyā pariggahaṃsā| ()

23.

 idhapana caturausiyuṃsamāsā

purimabhavau’daya mauha kammamaikau,

bhavatihanabhavacittanāma rūpā-

yatana cha phassa cha vaidanāti caikau| ()

24. 

 api bhavati bhavau nikanti gāhau

janana jarāmaraṇaṃ anāgatai’kau,

pabhava phalavasaina sambhavānaṃ

iti catu saṅkhipanaṃ siyātiyaddhaṃ| ()

25.

 idha yathariva’tītahaitupañ‍ca

abhiratigāhabhavaihi kammamauhā,

tathariva saha mauhakammunāpi

abhiratigāhabhavā idāni haitū| ()

26.

 nabhavati phalapañ‍cakaṃ kimaita-

rahipaṭisandhika manādipañ‍ca dhammā,

bhavati phakhalamanāgatai tathaiva

janana jarāmaraṇādi pañ‍ca dhammā| ()

27. 

 bhavati bhavupapattiya’ntarai’kau

abhirati vaidayitāna mantarai’kau,

tathariva citacaitanāmanānaṃ

iti bhavacak‍katisandhayau bhavanti| ()

28.

 suvisadamativīsatā’ katāraṃ

sa’tiparivaṭṭa tisandhikaṃ tiyaddhaṃ,

tadavagamiyatāya dhātu dhamma-

ṭṭhitimatiyā catusaṅgahaṃ dvimūlaṃ| ()

29.

 hanivibhani jagattayai bhavanta-

bhavaratha cak‍kasamappitākhilārai,

itipi bhagavatau budhāhigītau

bhuvi visutau arahanti kittighausau| ()

30. 

 bhagavati uditai mahānubhāvau

tamahimahaiyya bhusaṃ sadaivalaukau,

tadahimahi kadāvi nairu matta-

maṇiratanāvaliyā sahampatīpi| ()

31.

 pacurasuranarā balānurūpaṃ

yamabhimahiṃsu anātha piṇḍikaupi,

gahapati tamupāsikāvisākhā

saparisa kausalabimbisārabhūpā| ()

32.

 bhagavati parinibbutai asauka-

vahayadharaṇīpati dīpacak‍kavatti,

dasabalamasamaṃ paric‍cajitvā

abhimahi chan‍navutippamāṇakauṭī| ()

33. 

 agaṇita vibhavaṃ paric‍cachitvā

iharatanāvali caitiyaṃ vidhāya,

suranarasaraṇassa dhātudaihaṃ

narapatimānayi duṭṭhagāminī’pi| ()

34.

 dasabalamabhipūjayiṃsu pūjā-

vidhibahumānana bhājanaṃ tadañ‍ñai,

itijana mahanīya cīvarādī-

catuvidhapac‍caya pūjanāka visaisaṃ| ()

35.

 guṇajaladhi yadagga dakkhiṇaiyyau

arahati cāhuṇa pāhuṇā rahassa,

itipi bhagavatau kavippa satthau

bhuvi visutau arahanti kittighausau| ()

36. 

 idhaparamanipac‍cakāra giddhā

samaṇaka bhusurakā vibhāvimānī,

rahasi akusalaṃ silaukakāmā

na kimasilaukabhayaina sañ‍cinanti| ()

37.

 nacakarahaci kiñ‍cidaiva pāpaṃ

kayirati raisarahaupipāpabhīrū,

itipi bhagavatau rahāpagassa

bhuvivisutau arahanti kittighausau| ()

38.

 anupasamita rāgadausa mauhā

thiramanabhāvita kāyacitta pañ‍ñā,

ariyapaṭipadāya yai vipan‍nā

anariya dhammacarā narādhamātai| ()

39. 

 sugahita sugatārahaddhajantā

jinamanubandhiya santikaika varāpi,

itipi bhagavatau bhavanti durai

bhuvivisutau arahanti kittighausau ()

40.

 tathariva suvidura bhāvamāpa

munirapitaihinihīna puggalaihi,

itipi bhagavatau satampa satthau

bhuvivisutau arahanti kittighausau|ka ()

41.

 vihata sakalasaṃkilaisa dhammā

satata subhāvita kāya cittapañ‍ñā,

ariyapaṭipadaṃ papūrakārī

anariyadhammapathārakāka sudhīrā| ()

42. 

 satadasasata yaujanaihi dūrai

yadiviharanti jinassa ārakātai,

itipi bhagavatau na tāva dūrai

bhuvi visutau arahanti kittighausau| ()

43.

 tathariva avidūra bhāvamāpa

munirapi sappurisāna mīdisānaṃ,

itipi bhagavatau bhavantagassa

bhuvivisutau arahanti kittighausau| ()

44. 

 budha jana rahitabba pāpadhammā

pavūramanatthakarāka rahāvadanti,

itipi bhagavatau rahā na yassa

bhuvivisutau arahanti kitti ghausau| ()

45.

 garahiya rahitabbatā’riyaihi

paramaputhuj‍jana puggalaihi yasmā,

itipi bhagavatau naca’tthi’massa

bhuvivisutau arahanti kittighausau| ()

46.

 apica bhagavatā nataikadāci

vigarahiyā rahitabbakā bhavanti,

itipi bhagavatau rahānayassa

bhuvivisutau arahanti kittighausau| ()

47. 

 gamana miharahauti vuc‍catai taṃ

tibhavaparibbhamaṇaṃ rahau na yassa,

itipi bhagavatau gatassa pāraṃ

bhuvi visutau arahanti kittighausau| ()

48.

 niratisaya’dhisīlacittapañ‍ñau

parama vimutti vimutti ñāṇalābhī,

asandisa guṇa bhājanauka anaijau

asamasamau asamau anuttarau’ti| ()

49. 

 kusalabala samiddharūpavāti

vividhaguṇaihi siyā pasaṃ siyau yau,

itipi bhagavatau pasaṃsiyassa

bhuvivisutau arahanti kittighausau| ()

50.

 iminā imināpi kāraṇaina

bhagavā gautama gautta kaitubhūtau,

arahaṃ arahanti kittirāvau

dakatailaṃvatatāna sattalaukai| ()

iti maidhānandābhidhānainayatinā viracitai sakalakavijana hadayānandadānanidānai jinavaṃsadīpai santikai nidānai bhagavatau arahantināmapañ‍ñattiyāabhidhaiya paridīpau aikūnavīsatimauka saggau|

1. 

 sammā sāmaṃ sabbadhammāna maddhā

buddhattā pañ‍ñānubhāvaina tassa,

sammā sambuddhauti abbhuggatāya

āsikityā(sālinī) lauka dhātu| ()

2.

 yau cā bhiñ‍ñaiyyai pariñ‍ñaiyya dhammai

bhāvaitabbai sacchikātabba dhammai,

sammāsāmaṃ bujjhi tasmāsa buddhau

sammā sambuddhauti vikhyāsi laukai| ()

3.

 tatrā’bhiñ‍ñaiyyā catussac‍ca maiva

dukkhaṃ sac‍caṃ khau pariñ‍ñaiyya dhammā,

bhāvaitabbā magga sac‍caṃ niraudha-

sac‍caṃ tacchaṃ sacchikā tabba dhammā| ()

4. 

 taṇhāpakkhai sambhavaṃ dhamma jātaṃ

maggānaṃvajjhaṃka pahātabba dhammā,

saddhiṃ jātyādīhi dukaikhahi pañ‍cu-

pādānakkhandhā siyā dukkha sac‍caṃ| ()

5.

 yāyaṃ taṇhā kāma taṇhādibhaidā

dukkhānaṃ sāhaitu sac‍caṃ dvitiyaṃ,

bandhānaṃ yatrāpya’bhāvau niraudha-

sac‍caṃ yañ‍cā gamma taṇhāya cāgau| ()

6. 

 sammādiṭṭhādyaṭṭhamaggaṅga dhammā

nibbānaṃ sampāpakā magga sac‍caṃ,

taisaṃ dhammānampi sambujjhinattā

sammā sambuddhauti vikhyāsi laukai| ()

7.

 cakkhaiva’daṃ dukkhaṃ taduppāda haitu

taṇhā naisānaṃ abhāvau niraudhau,

maggau baudhūpāya pañ‍ñāti tassa

aivaṃ pac‍caikaṃ padaṃ cauddharitvā| ()

8.

 āraupaitvā sac‍ca dhammaisu sac‍ca-

sandhātā yau sac‍cadassī sa buddhau,

sammā sāmaṃ tikkhapañ‍ñāya bujjhi

sammā sambuddhauti vikhyāsi laukai| ()

9. 

 chan‍naṃ dvārānañ‍ca chārammaṇānaṃ,

chan‍naṃ cittānañ‍ca chabbaida nānaṃ,

chan‍naṃ sañ‍ñānaṃ cha sañ‍caitanānaṃ

chan‍naṃ phassā naṃ vitak‍kādi kānaṃ| ()

10.

 aivaṃ chan‍naṃ rūpa taṇhādikānaṃ

taṇhākāyānaṃ samāraupaṇaina,

sammā sāmaṃ bujjhi sac‍caisu tasmā

sammā sambuddhauti vikhyāsi laukai| ()

11.

 pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhāna maṭṭhārasan‍naṃ

dhātūnaṃ cakkhādinaṃ bārasan‍naṃ,

sammā sāmaṃ bujjhitattā sayambhu

sammā sambuddhauti vikhyāsi laukai| ()

12. 

 rūpajjhānānaṃ catun‍naṃ arūpa-

jjhānānañ‍cānussatīnaṃ dasan‍naṃ,

khatti sākāra’ppamañ‍ñāsubhānaṃ

kammaṭṭhānānaṃ navan‍naṃ bhavānaṃ| ()

13.

 buddhattā saṃsāra cak‍kai avij‍jā-

dyaṅgānaṃ sac‍caisucāraupaṇaina,

sammāsāmaṃ aisa nissaṅga ñāṇai

sammā sambuddhau vikhyāsi laukai| ()

14.

 pac‍cuppan‍nānāgatātīta dhammai

nibbānaṃ nissaisa paṇṇatti dhammai,

sāmaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsya’nañ‍ñaupa daisau

sammā sambuddhauti vikhyāsi laukai| ()

15.

 sabbaṃñaiyyaṃ tassa ñāṇanti kaṃhi

ñāṇampaivaṃ ñaiyya dhammantikaṃhi,

ñaiyyantaṭṭhānaumañāṇassalābhā

sammāsambuddhauti vikhyāsi laukai| ()

16.

 k‍laisānaṃ yāvāsanāsatatītāya

saddhiṃyauka sammauhaniddāya sammā,

sāmaṃbuddhau magga ñāṇaina tasmā

sammā sambuddhauti vikhyāsi laukai| ()

17. 

 sattādhīsau pāramīcauditattau

pal‍laṅkaināsaj‍jayau baudhimulai,

ambhaujaṃka bhānuppahāka saṅgamaina

saubhaggappattaṃ pabuddhaṃ’va sāmaṃ| ()

18.

 sāmaṃ sammā’nañ‍ña sādhāraṇagga-

maggaubhāsainappabuddhau samānau,

sampattau sabbañ‍ñutāñāṇa saubhaṃ

sammā sambuddhauti vikhyāsi tasmā| ()

19.

 aivaṃ sabbaisaṃ dhammānaṃ

sammā sāmaṃ buddhattā sau,

sammā sambuddhau buddhauti

saddau laukai abbhuggañ‍ji| ()

iti maidhānandābhidhānainayatinā viracitai sakalakavijana hadayānandadānanidānai jinavaṃsadīpai santikaika nidānai bhagavatau kasammāsambuddhauti nāmapañ‍ñattiyā abhidhaiya paridīpau vīsatimau saggau|

taṃ  khau pana bhavantaṃ

gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇai kittisaddau abbhuggatau

itipi sau bhagavā vij‍jācaraṇa sampan‍nauti|

1.

 sampan‍navij‍jācaraṇai avij‍jā-

ghaṇandhakāraṃ bhidurau pavīrau,

rāja vij‍jācaraṇubbhavāya

uḷārakittissiriyā kathaṃ taṃ| ()

2.

 asaṅkhakappaipi nivutthakhandhai

paiyyālapāḷiṃ viya ñāṇagatyā,

kaṇṭhiravassu’ppatanaṃ yathaiva

saṅkippa khippaṃ visayāvalambaṃ| (1)

3. 

 sarau’tisūrau sarabhaṅgaka satthu-

khittau saravyamhi virajjhatai kiṃ

aivaṃ atitaisu bhavantaraisu

asaj‍jamānaṃ avirajjhamānaṃ| ()

4.

 yathicchitaṭṭhāna matitakhandha-

saṅkhāta māhac‍ca pavattamānaṃ,

pubbainivāsānugatañhi ñāṇaṃ

anañ‍ñasādhāraṇamāsi yassa| ()

5.

 pahīṇa’vij‍jānusayau jinau sau

vij‍jāyu’paitau paṭhamāya tāya,

ic‍cassa daṇḍāhatakaṃsapāti-

saddauva sampatthari kittisaddau| ()

6. 

 hanīnappaṇītādi pabhaida vattai

uppaj‍jamānai ca nirujjhamānai,

sattai yathākammu’pagai gatīsu

pasādacakkhā’visayai ca rūpai| ()

7.

 anañ‍ña sādhāraṇa dibbacakkhu-

saṅkhātañāṇaina pahassaraina,

dibbaina cā’lauka pariggahaina

yainā’hijānāti jinau anaijau| ()

8.

 cutupapātabbisayāya satthā

vij‍jāyu’paitau dutiyāya tāya,

ic‍cassa sampatthari haima ghaṇṭā-

ṭaṅkāraghausauriva kittighausau| ()

9.

 dukkhañ‍ca dukkhappabhavau niraudhau

maggau ca dukkhassa niraudhakau’ti,

cattāri sac‍cāni yathāsabhāvaṃ

pavaidi ñāṇaina sayambhu yaina| ()

10. 

 yaicā’savā āsavasambhavau yau

taisaṃ khayau yavā’savanāsupāyau,

taṃ sabbamañ‍ñāsi sayambhu ñāṇa-

balaina yainā’sava vippamuttau| ()

11.

 khīṇa’ti jātī vusita’nti saiṭṭha-

cariyaṃ kataṃ’tī karaṇiya maddhā,

nacā’parantyai’va manantañāṇau

ñāṇaina’bhiñ‍ñāya vihāsi yaina| ()

12. 

 sayambhu sabbāsava saṅkhayāya

vij‍jāyupaitau tatiyāya tāya,

ic‍cassa vipphāragahīratairi-

rāvau’va sampatthari kittirāvau| ()

13.

 vij‍jāhihaiṭṭhā gaditāhi tīhi

samaṅakgi bhutassa tathāgatassa,

tadubbhavaṃ kitti sarīra bimbaṃ

sataṃmanaudappaṇagaṃ vibhāti| ()

14.

 cātummahābhūtikarūpikau yaṃ

mātāpitun‍naṃ karajamhi jātau,

yau bhattakummāsahataupi kāyau

anic‍caviddhaṃsanabhaida dhammau| ()

15.

 parittakāmāvacaramhi bhutau-

pādāya bhaidamhi tadattabhāvai,

yaṃ nissitaṃ vaidayitatta sañ‍ñā-

saṅkhāra viñ‍ñāṇa pabhaidanāmaṃ| ()

16. 

 yau vippasan‍nau maṇivaṃsa vaṇṇau

tatrāvutaṃ suttamivakkhimā taṃ,

ñāṇakkhinārūpa mavaikkhi yaina

sacakkhumā tatrasitañ‍ca nāmaṃ| ()

17.

 sītādinā ruppaṇalakkhaṇanti

rūpañ‍ca nāmaṃ natilakkhaṇanti,

taduttariṃ vaidakakārakau vā

attāttabhāvī paramatthatau na| ()

18.

 añ‍ñauñ‍ñasambandhavasaina yantī

nāpaṅgulandhā puthagaiva yanti,

tathā’ñ‍ñamañ‍ñau’panidhāya nāma-

rūpāni vattanti’ha nauvisunti| ()

19. 

 vavatthayantassi’ti nāmarūpaṃ

nissattanij‍jivasabhāva massa,

yā diṭṭhi duddiṭṭhivisaudhanaina

samuṭṭhitā diṭṭhi visuddhisaṅkhā| ()

20.

 avij‍ju’pādānanikantikamma-

haitubbhavaṃ rūpamarūpamādau,

pakavattiyaṃ haitucatūhi rūpaṃ

vatthādihaituppabhava’nti nāmaṃ| ()

21.

 sabbattha sabbaisu sadā samau na

nā’haitukaṃ taina nanic‍cahaitu,

aivaṃ taduppādaka pac‍cayānaṃ

pariggahaṃ yāya dhiyā akāsi| ()

22. 

 ahaṃ nu khau’siṃ nanukhau ahausiṃ

ic‍cā’dya’tītādipabhaida bhutā,

kaṅkhā’ssa kaṅkhātaraṇabbisuddhi-

saṅkhātapañ‍ñāya vigañchi yāya| ()

23.

 khandhā atītādi pabhaidavantau

parikkhayaṭṭhaina anic‍ca dhammā,

bhayāvahaṭṭhaina dukhā anattā

asārakaṭṭhaini’ti sammasantau| ()

24.

 tāḷisadhā lakkhaṇapāṭavatthaṃ

khandhāna maisaṃ navadhā’tha nāthau,

tikkhindriyau sau bhaya sattakānaṃ

vasaina sammadditanāmarūpau| ()

25. 

 pañ‍ñāsadhā bandhudayabbayānaṃ

pariggahaṃ yāyadhiyā akāsi,

yadā’ssa tāruñ‍ñavipassanāya

upak‍kilaisā dasa pātubhūtā| ()

26.

 ñāṇakkhiṇā yaina tilakkhaṇaṃ sau

addakkhi dhammaisu tadā’pi taisu,

jahāsu’pakailasapaduṭṭhamaggaṃ

vipassanā saudhitamaggagāmi| ()

27. 

 vavatthapaitvāna pathāpathai’vaṃ

vipassanāvīthi manauk‍kamitvā,

yā magga’maggikkha visuddhi nāma

samubbhavā tīraṇatikkha buddhi| ()

28.

 anantaraṃ tīraṇuṇapāraṃ

pattau pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayāya,

nipphattiyā yau navañāṇupaitaṃ

visuddhi mākaṅkhi visuddhikāmau|

29.

 pabandhatau cai’riyatau ghanaina

chan‍naisu dhammaisva’nupaṭṭhahan‍nai,

tilakkhaṇai yainu’dayabbayaina

punāpi sau sammasi nāmarūpaṃ|

30. 

 uppādabhaṅgaṭṭhititau yadā’ssa

vivaṭṭayitvāna vipassatau yaṃ,

saṅkhārabhaṅgai’va pavatta maṭṭha-

vidhānisaṃsaṃbhavi bhaṅgañāṇaṃ| ()

31.

 vipassatau bhaṅgamahiṇhamassa

hutvā bhayaṃ vāḷamigādayau’va,

upaṭṭhitā’tītabhavādibhaida-

bhavattayaṃ yaṃ bhayañāṇamāsi| ()

32.

 atha’ssa khandhāyatanādhi dhammā

ukkhittakhaggā madhakādayau’va,

upaddavādīnavatau vibhutā

patvā yadādīnavañāṇa māsuṃ| ()

33. 

 suvaṇṇahaṃsādi’va pañ‍jaraisu

bhavaisu diṭṭhādinavaisu tīsu,

nibbinditattau bhuvanaikanaittau

yaṃ nibbidāñāṇa malattha tibbaṃ| ()

34.

 pāsāditau pāsagatai’va sattā

vimuttikāmassa bhavaihi tīhi,

nissaisasaṅkhāra vimaukkha kāmaṃ

babhuva yaṃ muñ‍citukāma ñāṇaṃ| ()

35.

 anic‍cadukkhā’subhatau ca khandhai

anattatau bhāvayatau abhiṇhaṃ,

tassā’si saṅkhāravimaukkhūpāya-

sampādakaṃ yaṃ paṭisaṅkhañāṇaṃ| ()

36. 

 attaina vā attaniyaina suñ‍ñau

dvidhātya’yaṃ saṅkhata dhammapuñ‍jau,

aivaṃ catuddhā bahudhā chadhā’pi

vipassatau buddhimatau abhiṇhaṃ| ()

37.

 yā khau sikhāppattavipassanākhyā

vuṭṭhānagāmīnica sānulaumā,

sāmuddakākīriva kūpayaṭṭhiṃ

tilakkhaṇalambanikā babhūva| ()

38.

 nahārudaddūl‍la mivaggipattaṃ

saṅkhāradhammaṃ paṭiliyamānaṃ,

vissaṭṭhadāraṃ’va upaikkhakassa

saṅkhārupaikkhā’si mahaisinauyā| ()

39. 

 āgrautrabhuñāṇa masaisakhandhai

tilakkhaṇa’raupaṇa nin‍napauṇaṃ,

vipassanāñāṇa manaikabhaidaṃ

yadai’ttha saṅkhaipanayaina vuttaṃ| ()

40.

 vij‍jāya sau māraji tāya tāya

vipassanāñāṇagatāyu’paitau,

ic‍cassa saṃvaḍḍhita kittival‍li

laukālavālamhi vikāsa māpa| ()

41.

 muñ‍jā isikaṃ asikausiyā’siṃ

yathā karaṇḍā phaṇi muddharaiyya,

sabbaṅga pac‍caṅgika mindriyaggaṃ|

manaumayaṃ rūpimitau sarīrā| ()

42. 

 añ‍ñaṃ sarīraṃ abhinimmiṇitvā

mahiddhimā iddhimatānu rūpaṃ,

caitauvasipakpattavasippadhānau

yvākāsi vainaiyyajanānamatthaṃ| ()

43.

 mahiddhikau tāyamanaumayiddhi-

saṅkhāta vij‍jāya samanvitau sau,

ic‍cassa abbhuggatakittirāvau

nissaisalaukaṃ badhirīkarittha| ()

44.

 aikaupihutvā bahudhāca hauti

yau hauticai’kau bahudhāpi hutvā,

karai tiraubhāva mathāvibhāvaṃ

mahiddhikau iddhimataṃ cariṭṭhau| ()

45. 

 yathā nirālambanabhautalamhi

yau iddhimā viṇṇavasi vasindau,

vajai tiraupabbata gaihabhitti-

pākāra macchidda masaj‍jamānau| ()

46.

 karauti ummuj‍janimuj‍janiddhiṃ

yau vāripiṭṭhairiva bhumipiṭṭhai,

abhaij‍jamānau salilai salīlaṃ

padappitau yāti yathā pathavyā| ()

47.

 pakkhī’va yau saṅkamatai nabhamhi

pal‍laṅka mābhuj‍ja mahānubhāvaṃ,

mahiddhimantaṃ ravicandabimbaṃ

sapāṇiphuṭṭhau parimaj‍jatai yau| ()

48. 

 ābrahmalaukāpi kalaibaraina

vasaṃ pavattaiti mahiddhimā yau,

suvaṇṇakārauviya yaṃyadaiva

icchānurupābharaṇabbisaisaṃ| ()

49.

 yathicchitaṃ pac‍canubhauti jātu

nānāvidhaṃ iddhividhaṃ jinau yau,

sau tāya vij‍jāyapi saṅgatau’ti

abbhuggatau tassa yasaupabandhau| ()

50.

 sautappasādabbisayaṃ yathaiva

addhānamaggaṃ paṭipan‍napausau,

visuṃvisuṃ kāhaḷasaṅkhabhairi-

vīṇādisaddaṃ vividhaṃ suṇaiyya| ()

51.

 dūrantikai mānusakai ca dibbai

ubhaupisaddai sukhumai uḷārai,

visuddhanimmānusakaita yaina

sau dibbasautaina suṇāti nāthau| ()

52. 

 samaṅgibhūtauti sadibbasauta-

saṅkhātavij‍jāya jitāri tāya,

abbhuggatau tassa kavībhigīta-

silaukasaddau’va silaukasaddau| ()

53.

 sarāgacittampi virāgacittaṃ

sadausacittampi adausacittaṃ,

samauhacittampi vimauha cittaṃ

saṃkhittavikkhittagatampi cittaṃ| ()

54. 

 mahaggatampī amahaggatampī

sauttaraṃ citta manuttarampi,

samāhitampī asamāhitampi

vimuttacittampya’vimuttacittaṃ| ()

55.

 sakaṃ mukhaṅkaṃviya dappaṇamhi

acchaudakai maṇḍanajātikau yau,

paric‍ca caitau parapuggalānaṃ

yainā’bhijanāti vimuttacaitau| ()

56.

 sau tāya caitaupariyābhidhāna-

vij‍jāyu’paitauti dayānidhānau,

tilaukagabbhai’ka vitānasaubhā

tatāna tassu’bbhavasaitakitti| ()

57. 

 vij‍jāttayaina’ṭṭhavidhāhi’māhi-

vij‍jāhu’paita’ssa tathāgatassa,

vainaiyya kundākaracandikābhaṃ

vibhāti yāvaj‍ja yasausarīraṃ| ()

58.

 sumaṇḍitau saṃvutapātimaukkha-

saṅkhātasīlābharaṇaina yaina,

irīyatai yau karuṇa nidhānau

tapaudhanau sīlavataṃ padhānau ()

59.

 vaiḷupakpadānadivasaina cāṭu-

kamyaina dutaiyyapahainakaina,

sau pāribhaṭyainapi muggasūpya-

samaina sac‍cālikabhāsaṇaina| ()

60. 

 agaucaraṭṭhāna mupāsanaina

vikaupayai kimpana pātimaukkhaṃ,

hitvā anācāramagaucaraṃ taṃ

carai sadācārasugaucaraṃ sau| ()

61.

 anuppamāṇaisupi sabbadassi

sāvaj‍jadhammaisu bhayānupassi,

laddhaggamaggapaphalasiddhasīla-

sikkhāya sikkhāgaru sikkhatai sau| ()

62. 

 khaimaṃ disaṃ sañ‍caratī’ti pāti-

maukkhādhisikkhācaraṇaina taina,

abbhuggatau tac‍caraṇanu bandhau

ādic‍cabandhussa yasaupabandhau| ()

63.

 kantampirūpāyatanādi chak‍kaṃ

cakkhādinā sau visayīkaritvā,

nimitta’nubyañ‍janagāhi nāthau

nahauti yaini’ndriyasaṃvaraina| ()

64.

 cakkhādichadvāra masaṃvaritvā

rāgādidhammā viharanta mainaṃ,

atvāssa vaiyyuṃ satisaṃvaraina

tassaṃvaratthaṃ paṭipaj‍ji yaina| ()

65. 

 khaimaṃdisaṃ sau caraṇaina taina

jitindriyau indriyasaṃvaraina,

abbhuggatau saṃcaratīti tassa

tilaukanāthassa silauka saddau| ()

66.

 yai lābhasak‍kārasilaukakāmā

pāpicchakai’cchāpakatāsamānā,

kaivi’dhalaukai catupac‍cayānaṃ

paṭikkhipitvā paṭisaivanaina| ()

67.

 sāmantajappāya catubbidhassa

iriyāpathassā’ṭhapanādināca,

kuhāyanainā’lapanādināca

sac‍caṃ hiyā’nuppiya bhāsanaina| ()

68. 

 attā’vacaṭṭhānu’paraupaṇaina

muggassa sūpyainava pāribhaṭyā,

naimitta kattādivasaina micchā-

jīvaina duj‍jīvika mācarantī| ()

69.

 yathaiva tai nau bhagavā kadāci

kauhañ‍ñavutyā’lapanādināca,

naimitta nippaisikatāya kiñ‍ci

lāhaina lābhaṃka nijigiṃsa naina| ()

70.

 nimittasatthā’dipakāsanaina

ājīvasīlaṃ avikaupayitvā,

namaṇḍanatthaṃ na vibhusaṇatthaṃ

davāya vā naiva madāya naiva| ()

71. 

 anuppabandhaṭṭhitiyā imassa

kāyassa cā’bādha nisaidhanatthaṃ,

pavattiyā paggahanāya saiṭṭha-

cariyassa paurāṇa khudāpanaituṃ| ()

72.

 nūppādanatthañ‍ca navaṃ jighacchaṃ

yātrāya kāyassa’navaj‍jatāya,

sukhaṃ vihārāya ca bhaujanamhi

mattañ‍ñukau bhuñ‍jati piṇḍapātaṃ| ()

73. 

 tilaukanāthau caraṇaina taina

mattañ‍ñubhāvaina hi bhaujanamhi,

khaimaṃ disaṃ sañ‍caratīti laukai

abbhuggatau tassa silauka saddau| ()

74.

 divā nisaj‍jāya ca caṅka maina

tathā rajanyā’varaṇīya dhammā,

suddhantarau dvīhi’riyāpathaihi

sapacchimaivā paṭhamamhi yāmai| ()

75.

 vuṭṭhānasañ‍ñau satisampajañ‍ñau

sa’majjhimasmiṃ muni dakkhiṇaina,

passaina kappaiti ca sīha saiyyaṃ

pādai padaṃ thaukaka matibbidhāya| ()

76. 

 aṅgīrasau jāgariyānuyauga-

dhammaina sammācaraṇaina taina,

khaimaṃdisaṃ sañ‍caratīti tassa

abbhuggatau abbhutakitti ghausau| ()

77.

 sambaudhiyā saddahanā samiddha-

visuddhasaddhācaraṇaina taina,

khaimaṃdisaṃ sañ‍caratīti tassa

abbhuggatau abbhutakitti ghausau| ()

78.

 guthaṃyathā pāpa jigucchanaina

ariyaina laj‍jācaraṇaina taina,

khaimaṃdisaṃ sañ‍caratīti laukai

abbhuggatau tassasilaukasaddau| ()

79. 

 pāpāsamuttāsanalakkhaṇaina

auttappasaṅkhācaraṇaina taina,

khaimaṃ disaṃ sañ‍caratīti laukai

abbhuggatau tassa silauka saddau| ()

80.

 anañ‍ña sādhāraṇa bāhu sac‍ca-

dhammaina dhīmā’caraṇaina taina,

khaimaṃdisaṃ sañ‍caratīti tassa

samubbhavau’dāta yasausadhīsau| ()

81.

 thāmaina daḷhaina parak‍kamaina

vīriyaina vīrau caraṇaina taina,

khaimaṃdisaṃ sañ‍caratīti tassa

samubbhavau’ dāta yasausadhī sau| ()

82. 

 cirakriyānussaraṇai’tisūra-

tarāya satyā’caraṇaina satthā,

khaimaṃdisaṃ sañ‍caratīti tassa

yasaupabandhau visaribabhuva| ()

83.

 anañ‍ñasāmañ‍ñagabhīrañāṇau

ariyaina pañ‍ñācaraṇaina taina,

khaimaṃdisaṃ sañ‍caratīti tassa

yasaupabandhau visarībabhuva| ()

84.

 yau diṭṭha dhammamhi sukhāvahassa

vinissaṭassā’caraṇaihi yassa,

catuk‍kajhānassa nikāmalābhī

akicchalābhī bhagavā’si buddhau| ()

85.

 nikāmalābhaihi catūhi rūpa-

jjhānaihi nāthau caraṇaihi taihi,

khaimaṃdisaṃ sañ‍caratīti laukai

abbhuggatau tassa yasau pabandhau| ()

86. 

 tīha’ṭṭhahi vij‍jāhi

tipañ‍cacaraṇaihi’maihi sampantassa,

vij‍jācaraṇa visuddhaṃ

yasausarīraṃ virājatai yāvaj‍ja ()

iti maidhānandābhidhānainayatinā viracitai sakalakavijana hadayānandadānanidānai jinavaṃsadīpai santikai nidānai bhagavatau vij‍jācaraṇa sampan‍nauti nāmapañ‍ñattiyāabhidhaiya paridīpau aikavīsatimau saggau|

taṃkhau pana  bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau itipi sau bhagavā sugatauti|

saubhanagamanattā sugatauti|

1.

 gamana māhu gatanti susaubhanaṃ

ariyamaggagataina sivaṃdisaṃ,

(dutavilambita)tā’pagatau gatau

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

2.

 gamana mācariyā ya manuttara-

vibhavadaṃ pavadanti’ha saubhanaṃ,

tadariyaina gataina gatau yatau

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

3.

 muni timaṇḍalachādanatapparau

suparimaṇḍala mantaravāsakaṃ,

kanakakattariyā sunivāsayai

navadalaṃ kamalaṃ’va vikantayaṃ| ()

4. 

 kanakadāmavaraina parikkhipaṃ

paduma hattha mivau’pari bandhati,

samuni thāvaravij‍julatāsiri-

musitacārikalaibarabandhanaṃ| ()

5.

 sirighaṇau ghanakañ‍canacaitiyai

ratanakambalavattha mivā’hataṃ,

taruṇabhānupabhāruṇacīvaraṃ

sirisarīravarai paṭisaivati| ()

6.

 samuni jālavinaddhamanauhara-

karatalaihi sunīlamaṇippabhaṃ,

upalapatta malaṅkurutai yathā

bhamaramamburuhaihi sarauvarau| ()

7. 

 vajati saubhana mindasarāsana-

jaṭitajaṅgamamairuriva’ñ‍jasai,

samaṇamaṇḍanamaṇḍitaviggahau

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

8.

 suragajau’riva nandanakānanā

maṇiguhāya harī’va yugandharā,

navaravinduri’vā’maravāpitau

samadahaṃsavarau’va’hinikkhamaṃ| ()

9.

 vanaguhāditapauvanatau subhaṃ

vajati nikkhamiyā’samarūpimā,

nirupamassiriyā bhusa mul‍lasaṃ

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

10. 

 visaravipphuritā’mitaraṃsinā

suparisaikasuvaṇṇarasaini’va,

vajati piñ‍jaritau vasudhambaraṃ

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

11.

 karivarau’va karīhi purakkhatau

sakalapāpamalā’pagatau sayaṃ,

vajati vitamalaihi nisaivitau

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

12.

 asamabuddhavilāsalavaina yau

abhibhavaṃ sanarāmaravibbhamaṃ,

paṭipathaṃ paṭipaj‍jati saubhanaṃ

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

13. 

 purimapacchimadakkhiṇavāmatau

pabhavadaihapabhāhi pahāsayaṃ,

ratana’sītimitaṃ vajatai bhuvi

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

14.

 nigamagāmapurisu ca cārikaṃ

carati yau karuṇāparicārikau,

amitasattamanauratha māvahaṃ

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

15.

 kumudapaṅkaja campakamālati-

kusumavuṭṭhisuphassitaviggahau,

vajati cārutaraṃ jalitiddhimā

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

16. 

 tagarakuṅkumalauhita candana-

surabhicuṇṇavikiṇṇamahāpathai,

vajati gandhagajau viya saubhanaṃ

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

17.

 turiyarāva satānugatatthuti-

padasataihi abhitthutasagguṇau,

vajati haṃsavilāsitagāmiyau

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

18.

 suranarādivilaucanabhājana-

pivitarūpavilāsasudhārasau,

vajati sīhavijambhitavik‍kamau

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

19. 

 caraṇatāmarasassiribhārataṃ

anadhivāsini’vā’vanikāminī,

vajati tamhi pavaidhati kampati

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

20.

 sukhumakunthakipakilalikamakkhikā-

makasakīṭapaṭaṅga manuddayau,

vajati yau avihaiṭhaya mañ‍jasai

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

21.

 ṭhapitacak‍kavaraṅkitadakkhiṇa-

caraṇa paṅkaja piñ‍jaritañ‍jasau,

vajati yau paṭhamaṃ yadi nikkhipaṃ

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

22. 

 anupalitta malaihi samaṃ phusaṃ

kamalakaumalapādatalaihi yau,

vajati dhūtamalaṃ vasudhātalaṃ

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

23.

 bhavati bhairitalaṃ’va pakasārita-

caraṇatāmarasaihi suduggamaṃ,

avanatun‍nataṭhāna mapāvanī

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

24.

 pathavitu’bbhavapaṅkajamuddhani

ṭhapitakaumalapādatalambujau,

vajati raiṇupisaṅgasubhaṅgimā

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

25. 

 vajati antamasau’palasakkharā

sakalikākaṭhailā’pi sakaṇṭakā,

apavajanti pathā dipaduttamai

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

26.

 nijapadaṃ atidura manuddharaṃ

atisamīpa manikkhipa mañ‍jasai,

vajati gaupphakajānu maghaṭṭayaṃ
