itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

27. 

 atilahuṃ sanikaṃ samitindriyau

na caratai caratai jutiyu’j‍jalaṃ|

bhuvi samai visamai asamau samaṃ

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

28.

 anavalaukiya uddhamadhaudisaṃ

anudisañ‍ca catuddisa mañ‍jasai,

vajati yau yugamatta mapaikkhakau

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

29.

 timadabandhurasindhurakaisarī-

gativilāsaviḍambanavik‍kamau,

vajati pādatalaṅka madassayaṃ

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

30. 

 nirupamaj‍jutiyā purisāsabhau

vasabharājaparājitavik‍kamau,

vajati sañ‍janayaṃ janasammadaṃ

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

31.

 sūriyaraṃsi samaiti pavāyati

kusumagandhasugandhasamīraṇai,

vajati tabbimalañ‍jasamajjhagau

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

32.

 jaladharā puratau jalabindavau

naramarū kusumāti kirantipi,

tadupasattarajamhi pathai vajai

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

33. 

 ruciracāmarachattadharāmarā-

suranaraihi’pi gacchati sak‍katau,

garukatau mahitau patimānitau

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

34.

 yadi migindagajindaturaṅgama-

migavihaṅgamanādasupūjitau,

vajati pupphavitānadharau sirai

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

35.

 nayanatauraṇacārutara’ñ‍jasai

parilasanti gatai jinakuñ‍jarai,

sakasakā’bharaṇāni’pi pāṇinaṃ

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

36. 

 bhavati acchariyabbhutamaṅgala-

chaṇamahussavakaiḷinirantaraṃ,

tibhuvanaṃ sugatai sugatai pathai

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

37.

 siva masaṅkhatadhātu manuttaraṃ

paramasundaraṭhāna manāsavaṃ,

vigatajātijarāmaraṇaṃ gatau

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

38.

 murajadundubhichidda mivau’pari

nabhasi yāvabhavagga masaṃvuṭaṃ,

vivaṭa kamaiti yadubbhavapaṅkaja-

pamitiyā jinasaṅkha mapādisai| ()

39. 

 yadapi maṇḍanabhumi subaudhiyā

acalaṭhāna manañ‍ñavalañ‍jiyaṃ,

lalitapiñ‍jakalāpanibhau yahiṃ

vijayabaudhi idāni’pi rājatai| ()

40.

 paṭhama mubbhava mantapabhaṅguraṃ

vasumatīyuvatihadayaupamaṃ,

tada’pi kasundaraṭhāna mupāgatau

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

41.

 yadapi baudhipadanti pavuc‍cati

ariyañāṇamihagga manuttaraṃ,

yada’pi ñāṇa manāvaraṇaṃ tathā

nikhilañaiyyapathā’nativattanaṃ| ()

42. 

 purimajātisu pūritapārami-

balavapac‍cayasantiparāyaṇau,

tada’pi sundaraṭhāna mupāgatau

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

43.

 ariyamaggacatuk‍kapahīṇakaṃ

napunarai’ti kilaisagataṃ sataṃ,

apunarāgamanaṃ sugatau yatau

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

44. 

 sumati suṭhugatau paṇidhānatau

ppabhuti yāva jayāsanupāsanaṃ,

tidasapāramiyau paripūrayaṃ

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

45.

 tadubhayantabhavābhavadiṭṭhiyau

anupagamma gatau hitamāvahaṃ,

paṭipadāya hi suṭṭhutarāya yau

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

46.

 rucirabhāratibhattutibhaucatu-

parisamajjhagatau viyakaisari,

gadati vītabhayau giramāsabhiṃ

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

47. 

 surabhinā mukhatāmarasai vacī-

sucaritappabhavaina subhāsitaṃ,

gadati dhammasabhaṃ parivāsayaṃ

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

48.

 ratikaraṃ karavīkavirāvatau

paṭutaraṃ sutaraṃ sarasaṃ giraṃ,

gadati sautarasaṃ parisattarai

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

49.

 gadati sabbavacīduritaihi yau

paviratau abhisandhiya bhindiya,

avitathaina tathañ‍ca kathaṃkathā

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

50. 

 thirakathaṃ nakadāvi visaṃvadau

gadati pac‍cayikaṃ acalācalaṃ,

parisagau catusac‍cadasau sadā

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

51.

 sahitabhin‍najanaisudayāparau

anupadāniyamaiva’bhisandhiyaṃ,

gadatiyauvacanaṃpaṭigaṇhiyaṃ

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

52.

 piyakaraṃ sukumārataraṃ giraṃ

sutisukhaṃ sugamaṃ hadayaṅgamaṃ,

gadati naila manailagalaṃ yatau

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

53. 

 vihitavāṇivilāsinisaṅgamau

sumati sāmayikaṃ samayaṃ vidū,

gadati bhuta pavatti manañ‍ñathā

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

54.

 gadati ñaiyyapadatthavidau sadā

janahitattha manatthapanūdanaṃ,

gadita matthagataṃ ubhayatthadaṃ

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

55. 

 sakalasaṅkhatadhammavimuttiyā

gadati damma masaṅkhadhātuyā,

ariyamaggaphalaihi’pi nissitaṃ

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

56.

 vinayavādi vinaiyyajanai yatau

vinayanattha manattanayatvitaṃ,

vinayanissitakaṃ gadatai kathaṃ

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

57.

 hadayakausanidhānavatiṃ sadā

sadupamaṃ pariyantavatiṃ kathaṃ,

gadati mañ‍jugadau vadanaṃ varau

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

58. 

 muni rasaṅkuvitānanapaṅkajau

purimamaiva giraṃ parisantarai,

gadati aṭṭhavidhaṅgikaka māsabhiṃ

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

59.

 avitathaṃ vitathampi niratthaka-

mapi kathaṃ suṇataṃ piya mappiyaṃ,

nahivadanti kadāci tathāgatā

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

60. 

 avitthaṃ suṇataṃ pakiya mappiyaṃ

abhivadantī sadatthasitaṃ kathaṃ,

ya’dahivauharaṇai samayañ‍ñunau

itipi sau sugatau sugatau siyā| ()

61.

 tāya tāya’bhisāvayaṃ janataṃ sakāya niruttiyā

aihisāgatavādi gautamagauttakaitu tathāgatau,

mūlamāgadhibhāsayā gadatai sabhaṃ kaparitausayaṃ

taina sau bhuvanattayai sugatau siyāti suvissutau| ()

62. 

 laukaṃ laukappabhavaṃ

laukaniraudhañ‍ca laukamaukkhūpāyaṃ,

catubhī abhisamayaihi

nāthau sammā gatau tatau sau sugatau| ()

iti maidhānandābhidhānaina yatināna viracitai sakalakavijana hadayānandadānanidānai jinavaṃsadīpai santikaika nidānai bhagavatau sutagauti pañ‍ñattiyā abhidhaiya paridīpau| bāvīsatimau saggau|

taṃkhau pana  bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau| itipisau bhagavā laukavidūti|

1.

 tassa sa (daudhaka) lakkhaṇacāru-

cak‍kavara’ṅkitapādatalassa,

laukavidū’tipi yāva bhavaggā

aikasilaukaravau udapādi| ()

2.

 lakkhaṇamūlaniraudhaniraudhau-

pāyavasaina paki laukakamasaisaṃ,

yau paṭivijjhi tilaukahitau kaghau

laukavidūti pavuc‍cati tasmā| ()

3.

 laukamidhaiva kalaibaramattai

laukanidānaniraudhamavaidi,

laukaniraudhakaraṃka paṭipattiṃ

laukavidūti pavuc‍cati tasmā| ()

4. 

 laukamahambudhipāragu satta-

saṅkhatabhājanalaukapabhaidaṃ,

sau bhagavā’navasaisamavaidi

laukavidūti pavuc‍cati tasmā| ()

5.

 sau hi bhavābhavadaṭṭhisabhāva-

ñāṇanulaumikakhantipabhaidaṃ,

āsayadhamma mabujjhi pajānaṃ

laukavidūti pavuc‍cati tasmā| ()

6. 

 pātubhavaṃ sati kāraṇalābhai

sattavidhānusayampi janānaṃ,

sau paṭivijjhi vicaṭṭitalaukau

laukavidūti pavuc‍cati tasmā| ()

7.

 raj‍janadussanamuyhanasaddhā-

buddhivitak‍kavimissavasaina,

sau caritaṃ paṭivijjhi pajānaṃ

laukavidūti pavuc‍cati tasmā, ()

8.

 hīnapaṇīta’dhimuttivasaina

dubbidhamaiva’dhimutti mavaidi,

laukaniruttividau janatāya

laukavidūti pavuc‍cati tasmā| ()

9. 

 apparajakkha manussadapāpaṃ

ussadapāpa muḷārarajakkhaṃ,

dubbidhalaukamabujjhi yatausau

laukavidūti pavuc‍cati tasmā| ()

10.

 indriyapubbaparaupariyatti-

ñāṇapabhau tikhiṇindriyalaukaṃ,

sau paṭivijjhika mudindriyalaukaṃ

laukavidūti pavuc‍cati tasmā| ()

11.

 vaṭṭavivaṭṭapatiṭṭha masādhu-

sādhusabhāvagataṃ bhagavā sau,

dvāktikai’taralauka mavaidi

laukavidūti pavuc‍cati tasmā| ()

12. 

 sādhupasatthasadattaniyāmaṃ

ñāpayituṃ sukarāsukarampi,

sattanikāyamavaidi yatau sau

laukavidūti pavuc‍cati tasmā| ()

13.

 kammakilaisavipākavibandha-

muttyavimuttigatai paṭivijjhi,

bhabbajanaiya mabhabbajanai sau

laukavidūti pavuc‍cati tasmā| ()

14.

 nipphalatāya navuttamananta-

sattapamāṇa manāvaraṇaina,

ñāṇabalaina sayaṃ viditaṃhi

laukavidūti pavuc‍cati tasmā| ()

15. 

 vuttanayaini’ha sau muni satta-

laukamanaikavidhaṃ paṭivijjhi

sattanikāyasaraujavanai’ṇau

laukavidūti pavuc‍cati tasmā| ()

16.

 pac‍cayasaṇṭhitikaṃ paṭivijjhi

saṅkhatalaukamasaṅkhatadassi,

aikavidhampyavaraupitalaukau

laukavidūti pavuc‍cati tasmā| ()

17.

 ruppaṇalakkhaṇatau’khilarūpaṃ

nāmasalakkhaṇatau catunāmaṃ,

dubbidhalauka mavaidi munindau

laukavidūti pavuc‍cati tasmā| ()

18. 

 laukahitau sukhadukkhamupaikkhā-

vaidayitattikatau suvibhattaṃ,

sau bhagavā kapaṭivijjhi tilaukaṃ

laukavidūti pavuc‍cati tasmā| ()

19.

 pañ‍cavidhaṃka muni bandhavasainā-

hāravasaina catubbidhalaukaṃ,

laukapadīpanibhau paṭivijjhi

laukavidūti pavuc‍cati tasmā| ()

20.

 advayavādi saḷāyatanākhya-

chabbidhalaukamavaidi jinau sau,

sattavidhampi manaṭṭhitilaukaṃ

laukavidūti pavuc‍cati tasmā| ()

21. 

 lābhapabhutika maṭṭhavidhampi

laukasabhāvamavaidi yatau sau,

sakyamunī navasantanivāsai

laukavidūti pavuc‍cati tasmā| ()

22.

 sau dasabārasadhā’yatanānaṃ

bhaidavasaina tilaukapadīpau,

laukamavaidi tilakkhaṇavaidī

laukavidūti pavuc‍cati tasmā| ()

23.

 dhātuvasaina yatau suvibhattaṃ

lauka matha’ṭṭhadasapparimāṇaṃ,

saṅkhatalaukabhidau paṭivijjhi

laukavidūti pavuc‍cati tasmā| ()

24. 

 sau maṇikañ‍canarūpiyamuttā-

saṅkhapavālasilākaṭhalādiṃ,

laukamavaidi atindriyabaddhaṃ

laukavidūti pavuc‍cati tasmā| ()

25.

 rukkhalatāphalapal‍lavapatta-

pupphapakarāgapabhaidavasaina,

sau sukhumantaralaukamavaidi

laukavidūti pavuc‍cati tasmā| ()

26.

 yattakamaivu’tujaṭṭhakalāpa-

rapagataṃ ihabhājanalaukai,

vij‍jati tampaṭivijjhi asaisaṃ

laukavidūti pavuc‍cati tasmā| ()

27. 

 sau bhagavā himavatta pamāṇaṃ

aṭṭhamahānirayādi pamāṇaṃ,

nāgasupaṇṇavimāna pamāṇaṃ

brahmasurāsuralauka pamāṇaṃ| ()

28.

 paṃsujalānilabhumi pamāṇaṃ

dīpasavantisamudda pamāṇaṃ,

mairumahidharakūṭa pamāṇaṃ

kappatarūravicanda pamāṇaṃ| ()

29.

 pac‍cayasaṅkhatadhammasamuhaṃ

bhājanalaukagataṃ sakalampi,

uddhamadhautiriyaṃpaṭivijjhi

laukavidūti pavuc‍catitasmā| ()

30. 

 laukālaukakarau tilaukatilakausau sattalaukaṃ imaṃ

bujjhitthā’nusayāsayādividhinā saṅkhāralaukaṃ tathā,

āhārādipamāṇatādividhinā aukāsalaukaṃ yatau

tasmā laukavidūti vuc‍cati jinau saṅkhāralauka’ntagau| ()

itimaidhānandābhidhānaina yatinā viracitai sakalakavijana hadayānandadānanidānai jinavaṃsadīpai santikainidānai bhagavataulaukavidūti nāmapañ‍ñattiyā abhidhaiyaparidīpau taivīsatimausaggau|

taṃkhau pana  bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau itipi sau bhagavā anuttarau purisadammasārathīti|

1.

 abhāvatau paramatarassa kassaci

janassa sagguṇavisaraihi attanā,

samāsanibbacananayaina sau muni

anuttarau sasi (rucirā)’nanambujau| ()

2.

 tathāhi sau narahari sīlasampadā-

guṇaini’maṃ abhibhavatai sadaivakaṃ,

samādhinā varamatiyā vimuttiyā

vimuttidassanaguṇasampadāyapi| ()

3.

 yatau navij‍jati adhisīlasampadā-

samādhidhipabhutiguṇaihi tassamau,

kutaunu vij‍jati’ha taduttarītarau

siyā tataupya’ya masamau mahāmuti| ()

4. 

 nirūpamau asamamunīhi saumuni

yatau samau asamasamau siyā tatau,

tathāgatassi’ha dutiyassa kassaci

abhāvatau adutiyakau tathāgatau| ()

5.

 yatau navij‍jati paṭimāpi tassamā

samau tadā’samatanusampadāyapi,

sahāyakau nahi paṭividdhabaudhiyā

tatau yamappaṭima’sahāyakau muni| ()

6.

 kalaibarainapi abhirūpahārinā

guṇaihi tappaṭisamapuggalau nahi,

nacatthi pāvacanavibhāgakapakpanai

sayaṃ vinā bhuvi paṭibhāgapuggalau| ()

7. 

 anañ‍ñagaucaravarabaudhisiddhiyā

sa’haṃ sayambhuti paṭipuggalaunahi,

paṭiñ‍ñamappayitu malaṃ sayaṃ vinā,

anuttarau’tipi naradammasārathi| ()

8.

 sudantapuggaladamitabbapuggalai

damaiti sārayati adantapuggalai,

yatau jinau vinayanupāyakauvidau

anuttarau’tipi naradammasārathi| ()

9. 

 yathā hayai mudukaguṇaikhana sārathi

tathāgatau sugatikathāya dhammiyā,

damaiti sārayati tathā tathāgatai

anuttarau’tipi naradammasārathi| ()

10.

 yathā hayai pharusaguṇaina sārathi

apāyataj‍janavidhinā tathāgatau,

damaiti sārayati tathā tathāgatai

anuttarau’tipi naradammasārathi| ()

11.

 adammiyai mudupharusaina sārathi

yathā’bhimārayati tathā tathāgatau,

jahātya’nauvadiya nacānusāsiya

anuttarau’tipi naradammasārathi| ()

12. 

 karī’bhidhāvati damakaina sāritau

puratthimādisu disamaiva kaivalaṃ,

anuttaraina hi naradammasārathi-

jinaina sāritapurisānatādisā| ()

13.

 nisaj‍ja katthaci sayanāsanamhi tai

disāsu aṭṭhasu atisaṅgacārinau,

vidhāvarai turitamanuttaraṃ disaṃ

anuttarau’tipi naradammasārathi| ()

14.

 patiṭṭhitai muni’radhisīlasikkhayā

vasī’nusāsiya adhicittasikkhayā,

yathārahaṃ damayati bhabbapuggalai

anuttarau’tipi naradammasārathi| ()

15. 

 samāhitai muni radhicittasikkhayā

vipassanāya’pi samaṇai’nusāsiya,

yathārahaṃ damayati baudhanārahai

anuttarau’tipi naradammasārathi| ()

16.

 tathuparūpari paṭivaidhapattiyā

yathāk‍kamaṃ anariyasaikkhapuggalai,

damaiti sau vinayati laukanāyakau

anuttarau’tipi naradammasārathi ()

17. 

 vinaiyyabandhavamanakundacandimā

vinaisi kausalamagadhādhipādayau,

anaikakhattiyapurisai vināyakau

anuttarau’tipi naradammasārathi| ()

18.

 kudiṭṭhikuñ‍jarahari kūṭadantabhu

surādibhūsurapurisai vibhāvinau

jināsabhau vinayi yatau’nusāsiya

anuttarau’tipi naradammasārathi| ()

19.

 upālināmikapamukhai durāsadai

vināyakau gahapatipaṇḍitai puthu

vinaisi sau upanayanakkhamai yatau

anuttarau’tipi naradammasārathi| ()

20. 

 asac‍cadiṭṭhikamapi sac‍cakavhayaṃ

anañ‍ñavainayikanigaṇṭhanāyakaṃ

vinaisi tappabhutidigambarai jinau

anuttarau’tipi naradammasārathi| ()

21.

 jināsabhau sabhiyasubhaddasañ‍ñinau

tappassinau timisabhidau sadhammiyā

kathāyi’taubahi samaṇaipi sikkhayi

anuttarau’tipi naradammasārathi| ()

22.

 damaisi saumuni uruvailakassapa-

gayādikassapajaṭilādikai yatau,

jaṭādharai vijaṭitajālinījaṭau

anuttarau’tipi naradammasārathi| ()

23. 

 pahāṇasaṃvaravinayuttarau muni

anaikakhattiyasamaṇaipi sāsanai,

vinaisi sārathiriva uttaruttariṃ

anuttarau’tipi naradammasārathi| ()

24.

 manussasauṇitapisitāsanaihi sau

vinaisi pīvarajaṭharaṃ nisācaraṃ,

sughauramānavaka manaikarakkhasai

anuttarau’tipi naradammasārathi, ()

25.

 vināyakau suvinayi rāhunāmikaṃ

mahattabhāvika masurādhipaṃ yatau,

surādhipappabhutisurai tathā’surai

anuttarau’tipi naradammasārathi| ()

26. 

 pajāpatiṃ nikhilapajānukampakau

bakābhidhānikampi tucchaladdhikaṃ,

vinaisi sau naditaranīrajāsanai

anuttarau’tipi naradammasārathi| ()

27.

 kasaṅkusaihi’pi avinaiyyakai yatau

tiracchajātikapurisai narāsabhau,

vinaisi sau tisaraṇasilasaṃvarai

anuttarau’tipi naradammasārathi| ()

28.

 kapaulasaicanamadakaṇṇacāmaraṃ

hutāsanāsanirivabhiṃsanaṃ yatau,

damaisi māraji dhanapālakuñ‍jaraṃ

anuttarau’tipi naradammasārathi| ()

29. 

 vinaisi saumuni himavantavāsinaṃ

patāpapaj‍jala mapalālabhauginaṃ,

kharaṃ bhayaṅkara maravālabhauginaṃ

anuttarau’tipi naradammasārathi| ()

30.

 nandau’panandu’ragapatiṃ mahaudara-

cūlaudarauragapamukhai ca nibbisai,

dhumassikhā’nalasikhabhaugīnau akā

tainā’pyanuttaranaradammasārathi| ()

31.

 damanupāyakauvidau hi baudhanaiyyabandhavai

ariyamaggavīthibhāsuraṃ varaṃ sivamapakuraṃ,

paṭipadārathaina sārayi yathaiva sārathi

purisadammasārathiti vuc‍catai anuttarau| ()

itimaidhānandābhidhānaina yatinā varacitai sakalakavijana hadayānanda dānanidānai jinavaṃsadīpai santikainidānai bhagavatau anuttarau purisadammasārathīti nāma pañ‍ñattiyā abhidhaiya paridīpau catubbīsatimausaggau|

taṃkhau pana  bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇaikitti saddau abbhuggatau itipi saubhagavā satthā daivamanussānaṃti|

1.

 kantāraṃ kharatak‍karaṃ nirudakaṃ kattāramautārimaṃ

kantāraṃ migarājakuñ‍jaramahā (saddulavik‍kīḷitaṃ),

kantāraṃ avatārabhurijanataṃ yau satthavāhau sudhi

tāraitvā nayatai dayāparavasau khaimantabhumiṃ yathā| ()

2.

 ic‍caivaṃkaruṇānidhānahadayau saṃsāradukkhāturai

sattai jātijarāvikāramaraṇassaukādikantāratau,

tāraitvā dasasaṃkilaisagahanā pāpaisi khaimaṃpuraṃ

tasmā satthupasatthakittivisarau satthā’ti sampattharī| ()

3. 

 atthā’natthavicāraṇā’ticaturau laukuttaratthaina’pi

yasmā sāsati laukiyaina ubhayaina’tthainalaukaṃ imaṃ,

sabrahmaṃ sanarāmaraṃ sasamaṇaṃ sabrāhmaṇaṃ yauhi sau

satthā’tvaiva pasatthakittinikarau satthāramabbhuggatau| ()

4.

 bhītiṃ jātijarārujādikasiraṃ nissāya jātaṃhi yau

satthā satthadharaurivā’rivisaraṃ nikkhittasatthau sadā,

sattānaṃ tasasatai vihiṃsati dhiyā siddhatthasārau tatau

sau satthā’ti yasausarīrasurabhī laukattayaṃ vyāpayī| ()

5.

 laukatthābhiratau anatthaviratau jātyādikantāratau

uttāraitica satthavāhasadisau yau atthadhammainavā,

sattai sāsati hiṃsatī’ti janatāsantānajātaṃ bhayaṃ

vuttā’nvatthavasaina sauhibhagavā satthāti vaṇṇīyatai| ()

taṃkhau panabhavantaṃ  gautamaṃ aivaṃkalyāṇai kittisaddau abbhuggatau itipisau bhagavā buddhauti|

6.

 yau saṅkhāravikāralakkhaṇaparausaṅkhārapañ‍ñattisu

ñaiyyatthaisva’nanussutaisu purimaṃ cattāri sac‍cāni’pi,

bujjhitvā’cariyaupadaisarahitau tatthaiva sabbañ‍ñutaṃ

pattau ñāṇabalaisu pāpuṇi vasībhāvaṃ sayamabhuka sayaṃ| ()

7. 

 baudhaitā’ti pajāya nibbacanatau sac‍cāni sau bujjhitā

sac‍cānīti’pi sac‍cavādi bhagavāka nissaisañaiyyassapi,

matyā bujjhanasattiyā mahatiyā yasmā samaṅakgī tatau

buddhau nāmasiyāti kittivisarau tambuddhamabbhuggatau| ()

8.

 yaisaṃja baudhanavasattiyā sumatiyā cā’nañ‍ñanaiyyau sayaṃ

buddhattā ca yathāvikāsapadumaṃ sau bujjhanaṭṭhainapi,

nānābuddhaguṇassa vissavanatau buddhauti suddhaudanī

abbhuggañchi tibuddhakhaittabhavanai taṃkittigītassarau| ()

9. 

 rāgassādhigataggamaggamatiyā dausassa mauhassapi

chin‍nattā ca samulaghātamakhilakailasārivaggassapi,

sau khīṇāsavatāya caupadhiparic‍cāgaina buddhautyayaṃ

uc‍cāriyati cārikittiracanā viñ‍ñūhi yāvaj‍japi| ()

10.

 dhammassāmi yathā pabuddhapurisau auk‍kantaniddakkhayā

nājjhau’tiṇṇakilaisamiddhavidhamā baudhāpitau kainaci,

buddhambhaujatibhānanau hī bhagavā sāmaṃ pabuddhau yatau

buddhaunāmasiyāti tabbhavayasaughausau vibhusāyatai| ()

11.

 gatyatthāvagamatthadhātusamatāsabbhāvatau vā gatau

yainaikāyanamaggamuggamatimā aikau, hīsambujjhīsau,

sambaudhiṃ jayabaudhimūlamupagau sattuttarau’nuttaraṃ

buddhautī’dha jagattayai nijayasau yāvaj‍ja vij‍jumbhatai| ()

12. 

 khīṇattā paramāya maggamatiyā dubbuddhiyā buddhiyā

laddhattāpi kaanuttaruttaraguṇālaṅak‍kārasāmaggiyā,

sau sambaudhiparāyaṇau sirighaṇau buddhauti suddhaudanī

laukambhaudhimalaṅkari nijayasaukal‍laulamālāhi’maṃ| ()

13.

 sambuddhau’ti’mināpadaina muninau sac‍cāvabaudhāvahaṃ

ñāṇaṃ tappaṭivaidhañāṇa managhaṃ nā’ñ‍ñaihisādhāraṇaṃ,

buddhau’tī’dha padaina satthu karuṇāpubbaṅgamaṃ daisanā-

ñāṇaṃ ñaiyyapadatthabaudhanakaraṃ ñāṇañ‍ca dassiyatai| ()

14. 

 taṃ sabbañ‍ñutañāṇathaumanavasā sammādisambuddhi’ti-

saddassā’riyamaggakittanavasā buddhautisaddassaca,

yaugau’pai’tthakatau’tya’bhāsi vibudhau sau dhamma pālābhidhau

buddhānussativaṇṇanāvivaraṇai viñ‍ñātasatthāgamau| ()

itimaidhānandābhidhānaina yatinā viracitai sakalakavijana hadayānandadānanidānai jinavaṃsadīpai santikainidānai satthādaivamanussānaṃ buddhauti nāmapañ‍ñattīnaṃ abhidhaiya paridīpau pañ‍cavīsatimau saggau|

taṃkhau pana  bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇai kittisaddau abbhuggatau itipi saubhagavā bhagavāti|

1.

 kavibhāratipaddhatichandasi ta-

gguṇathaumana (tauṭaka) vutya’bhavi,

bhagavā’ti vibhattapadatthavatī

madhurā suṇataṃ surataṃ madhurā| ()

2.

 adhisīlasamādhimatippabhuti-

guṇarāsivisiṭṭhatarassa tatau,

bhagavā’ti sadaivamanussapajā-

pavarassa sagāravanāma’midaṃ| ()

3.

 bhagavāvacanaina pavuc‍cati yau

saniruttinayau vacanatthavarau,

sa’hi gāravasaiṭṭhavisiṭṭhatarau

bhagavāti nimittakanāmamidaṃ| ()

4. 

 paripācitasañ‍citapāramitā-

mitabhāgya manuttariyu’ttariyaṃ,

yadi vij‍jati’massa anañ‍ñasamaṃ

bhagavā’ti pavuc‍cati sau bhagavā| ()

5.

 yadi mārabalaṃ pabalaṃ sakalaṃ

kadalī dviradauriva tālavanaṃ,

asanī’va kilaisamabhañ‍ji tatau

bhagavā’ti pavuc‍cati sau bhagavā| ()

6.

 yadi bhaggamakā’khilalaubhamapā-

khiladausamapā’khilamauhamapi,

viparītamanaukaṇañ‍ca tatau

bhagavā’ti pavuc‍cati sau bhagavā| ()

7. 

 yadi kaudhu’panāha musuyanama-

cchariyaṃ ahirik‍kanirauttapanaṃ,

api makkhapalāsa mabhañ‍ji bhavā-

bhavadiṭṭhi manaj‍java’maddavataṃ| ()

8.

 kharaphārusatā karaṇuttariyaṃ

yadi māna’bhimāna’pamādamadaṃ,

saṭhaphārusatā karaṇuttariyaṃ

saṭhamāyamabhañ‍ji’ti mauhajaṭaṃ

bhagavā’ti pavuc‍cati sau bhagavā| ()

9. 

 tividhā’kusalaṃ tividhabbisamaṃ

tivitak‍katimūlatisañ‍ñamapi,

timalaṃ tipapañ‍ca mabhañ‍ji tatau

bhagavā’ti pavuc‍cati sau bhagavā| ()

10. 

 caturaugha catubbidhayauga catu-

bbidhagantha catubbidhagāha mapi,

caturāsavadhamma mabhañ‍jitatau

bhagavā’ti pavuc‍cati sau bhagavā| ()

11.

 vinibaddha manaukhīla nīvaraṇā-

nya’bhīnandanamac‍cariyāni tatau,

yadi pañ‍cavidhāni’pi bhaggamakā

bhagavā’ti pavuc‍cati sau bhagavā| ()

12. 

 chavivādapadāni’pi sattavidhā-

nusayaihi kusitakavatthu’matau,

ya mabhañ‍ji’tarāti’pi aṭṭhavidhaṃ

bhagavā’ti pavuc‍cati sau bhagavā| ()

13. 

 navadhā’layamula mabhañ‍ji tathā

dasadhā’kusalaṃ dasakammapathaṃ,

sakalāni kudiṭṭhigatāni tatau

bhagavā’ti pavuc‍cati sau bhagavā| ()

14. 

 pariḷāhadaraṃ vividha’ddhasataṃ

bhavakanaittivicāra mahañ‍ji tatau,

satamattasahassakilaisagataṃ

bhagavā’ti pavuc‍cati sau bhagavā| ()

15.

 aṇimā laghimā mahimā vasitā-

pabhuti’ssariya’ṭṭhabhagaihi yatau,

subhagaihi samaṅakgībbhūva tatau

bhagavā’ti pavuc‍cati sau bhagavā| ()

16.

 aṇunau nanunau’ nanunaukaraṇaṃ

karaṇaṃ lahunau’lahunau aṇimā,

laghimā mahimā mahimākaraṇaṃ

karaṇaṃ vasitā vasitāya tahiṃ| ()

17. 

 saya micchitaṭhāna mupāgamanaṃ

lahu vicchitakāriyasādhanatā,

abhipatti pakamya masaisavasī-

karaṇai’sikatā paramissaratā| ()

18.

 nabhasā padasā gamanādivasā

vajatau pariniṭṭhitakāriyatā,

nijakāma’vasāyikatātiyahiṃ-

paramissariyākhyabhagā’ṭṭhavidhā| ()

19.

 catumagga catupphalasantipadā-

riyadhammasamuhabhagaihi yutau,

vinalīkatapāpamalaihi tatau

bhagavā’ti pavuc‍cati sau bhagavā| ()

20. 

 caraṇadiguṇa’tisayādhigatā-

samakittisarīrabhagaita yutau,

bhuvanattayavipphuritaina tatau

bhagavāti pavuc‍cati sau bhagavā| ()

21.

 janalaucananīharaṇāya nirū-

pama rūpasarīragatāya tatau,

nikhilāvayavassiriyā sabitau

bhagavā’ti pavuc‍cati sau bhagavā| ()

22.

 abhipatthita micchita mattahitaṃ

parasattahitampi samijjhati yaṃ,

iti tādisakāmabhagaina yutau

bhagavā’ti pavuc‍cati sau bhagavā| ()

23. 

 yadanuttariyaina ca pāramitā-

vīriyaina payattabhagaina yutau,

garubhāvapadappabhavaina tatau

bhagavā’ti pavuc‍cati sau bhagavā|

24.

 paramissariyāyamadhammayasau-

sirikāmapayattabhāgā chayimai,

yadi yassa jinassa bhavanti tatau

bhagavā’ti pavuc‍cati sau bhagavā| ()

25.

 subhagaina anañ‍ñasamaina nirū-

pamarūpavilāsabhagaina yutau,

satapuñ‍ñasamuj‍jalitaina tatau

bhagavāti pavuc‍cati sau bhagavā| ()

26. 

 nijadhammasarīravibhūti yathā

nijarūpasariravibhūti tathā,

iha vuc‍cati bhaggasubhāgyamiti

api taihi samaṅgi jinau bhagavā| ()

27.

 kusalādipadaihi vibhattamakā’-

yatanādivasaina ca bandhavasā,

vata dhammasamuhasabhāva matau

bhagavā’ti pavuc‍cati sau bhagavā| ()

28.

 catudhā catudhā catudhā catudhā

catusac‍cadasau’riyasac‍campi,

vibhajī vibhajī vibhajī vibhajī

bhagavā’ti pavuc‍cati sau bhagavā| (yamakabandhanaṃ)

29. 

 yadi dibbavihāra masaivi bhaji

surajaiṭṭhavihāra manañ‍ñasamaṃ,

ariyañ‍cavihāra manañ‍ñasamaṃ,

ariyañ‍ca vihāra masaivi tatau

bhagavā’ti pavuc‍cati sau bhagavā| ()

30. 

 yadi kāyavivaikasukhaṃ abhajī

bhaji cittavivaikasamādhisukhaṃ,

upadhīhi vivaikaka masaivi tatau

bhagavā’ti pavuc‍cati sau bhagavā| ()

31.

 bhaji vaṭṭagatañ‍ca vivaṭṭagataṃ

saya muttarimānusadhamma mapi,

tividhañahi vimaukkha masaivi tatau

bhagavā’ti pavuc‍cati sau bhagavā| ()

32. 

 punarāgamanāvaraṇaina bhavai

bhavanaittisamañ‍ña midaṃ gamanaṃ,

yadi vantamakā’riyamaggamukhau

bhagavā’ti pavuc‍cati sau bhagavā| ()

33.

 bhagavā’ti visiṭṭha’bhidhānamimaṃ

na’ca mātupituppabhutihi kataṃ,

sahabaudhipadādhigamaina gatā

tathasammuti tassajinassa’bhavi| ()

athamahāniddaisāgatanayau vuc‍catai|

34.

 laukuttarāya matiyā

rāgaṃ bhaggaṃ akāsi dausaṃ mauhaṃ,

yasmā kaṇṭakamānaṃ

kilaisamāraṃ tataupi buddhau bagavā| ()

35. 

 yasmā vibhaj‍javādi

bhaji vibhaji pavibhajī sadhammakkhandhaṃ,

laukuttarañ‍ca katavā

bhavānamattaṃ tataupi buddhau bhagavā| ()

36.

 yasmā bhāvitakāyau

bhāvitasilau sadā subhāvitacittau,

bhāvitapañ‍ñau sabbhi

subhāvanīyau tataupi buddhau bhagavā| ()

37.

 bhagavā kavanapatthāni

paṭisal‍lānabbihārasāruppāni,

janavātāpagatāni

vanāni saināsanāni yau pantāni| ()

38. 

 bhudharakandaralaiṇaṃ

guruhamūlaṃ pakalāla mabbhaukāsaṃ,

sivathikaṃ bhaji yasmā

tiṇasatthāraṃ tataupi buddhau bhagavā| ()

39.

 catubbidhānaṃ saddhā-

daiyyānaṃ cīvarādisambhārānaṃ,

subharau yasmā bhāgī

paramappicchau tataupi buddhau bhagavā| ()

40.

 attharasassa subhāgī

dhammarasassa ca yatau vimuttirasassa,

adhisīlassa’dhicitta-

ssa’dhipañ‍ñāyaca tataupi buddhau bhagavā| ()

41.

 rūpārūpāvacara-

jjhānāna catun‍na mapakpamañ‍ñānampi,

viddhaṃsitīvaraṇau

yasmā bhāgī tataupi buddhau bhagavā| ()

42. 

 aṭṭhan‍nañ‍caṭṭhan‍naṃ

vimaukkhadhammāna mābhibhāyatanānaṃ,

anupubbavihārānaṃ bhāginavan‍naṃ tataupi buddhau bhagavā| ()

43.

 dasakasiṇasamāpatti

dasasañ‍ñābhāvanāna mapi bhāgīvā,

asubhasamāpatyā’nā-

pānassatiyā tataupi buddhau bhagavā| ()

44. 

 sammappadhāna pabhuti-

satipaṭṭhāni’ddhipādadhammānampi,

catudhā suvibhattānaṃ

bhāgī yasmā tataupi buddhau bhagavā| ()

45.

 pañ‍can‍nampi balānaṃ

yasmā pañ‍can‍na mindriyānaṃ bhāgī,

tasmā dasabaladhārī

jitindrisau yau tataupi buddhau bhagavā| ()

46.

 yasmā baujjhaṅgānaṃ

ariyassa’ṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassāpi,

tathāgatabalānaṃ yau

bhāgi dasan‍naṃ tataupi buddhau bhagavā| ()

47.

 catuvaisāraj‍jānaṃ

yadi catupaṭisambhidāna maddhabhāgī,

chabuddhadhammānampi

chaḷabhiñ‍ñānaṃ tataupibuddhaubhagavā| ()

48. 

 bhagavā’tyai’taṃ nāmaṃ

nakataṃ mātāpitūhi bhātubhaginīhi,

sakamittāmac‍caihi

na ñātisālauhitaihivā pañ‍ñattaṃ| ()

49.

 samaṇaihi bhusuraihi

na daivatāhi ca nana yaina kainaci racitaṃ,

uṭṭhaṭakibbisamūlai

subaudhimūlai subuddhasambaudhīnaṃ| ()

50.

 paṭilābhahaitu taisaṃ

bhagavantānaṃ anāvaraṇañāṇassa,

pavimaukkhantikamaitaṃ

yadidaṃ bhagavāti sacchikāpañ‍ñatti| ()

athaṭīkāgatanayauvuc‍catai|

51. 

 niratisayāsīlādi-

sagguṇabhāgā anañ‍ñasāmañ‍ñā yai

yassu’palabbhanti tatau

bhagavā’tya’bhidhīyatai sabuddhau bhagavā| ()

52.

 tathāhi sīlaṃ samādhi

pañ‍ñā vimutti vimuttidassanañāṇaṃ,

hiri auttappaṃ saddhaṃ

vīriyaṃ sati sampajañ‍ña maitai dhammā| ()

53.

 sīlavisuddhi ca diṭṭhi-

visuddhi kusalāni tīṇi tammūlāni,

tayau vitak‍kā sammā

tissau dhātvānavaj‍jasañ‍ñā tissau| ()

54. 

 catusatipaṭṭhāni’ddhi-

ppādā sammappadhānadhammā caturau,

paṭisambhidā catassau

caturau maggā phalānikhau cattāri| ()

55.

 cattārau, riyavaṃsā

yauniparicchaidakāni catuñāṇāni,

catuvaisāraj‍jāni

padhāniyaṅgāni pañ‍ca parimāṇāni| ()

56.

 pañ‍caṅgikau’pi sammā

samādhi pañ‍cindriyāni pañ‍cabalāni,

nissāraṇīyadhātu

pañ‍cavimuttiparipācaniyā dhammā| ()

57. 

 pañ‍ca vimuttāyatana-

ñāṇāni chagāravā chabahulavihārā,

chā’nussatiṭhānāni

nissāraṇiyā chadhātu chalabhiñ‍ñāyau| ()

58. 

 chabbidha’nuttariyāni

jabbidhanibbaidhabhāgiyā sañ‍ñāyau,

chaasādhāraṇañānā-

nya’riyadhanānya’parihāniyā dhammā| ()

59.

 sappurisāriyadhammā

baujjhaṅgā satta sattasañ‍ñā satta,

khīṇāsavabalakathanā

sattavivadhā dakkhiṇarahānañ‍ca kathā| ()

60. 

 aṭṭhan‍naṃ pañ‍ñānaṃ

paṭilābha nidānadaisanā sammattā|

laukasabhāva’c‍cagamā

aṭṭha’kkhaṇadaisanā ca aṭṭhavimaukkhā| ()

61.

 vatthunyā’ramhāni

mahāpurisatak‍kanā’bhibhāyatanutti,

aṭṭhavidhā navu’pāyā

manasikaraṇamūlakā padhānyaṅgāni| ()

62. 

 nava sattāvāsakathā

āghātapaṭivinayā ca nava nānattā,

navā’nupubbavihārā

navasañ‍ñā dasavidhā kusalakammapathā| ()

63.

 dasa kasiṇāyatanāni

dasa sammattāni nāthakaraṇadhammā,

balāni cā’riyavāsā

maittāyai’kādasānisaṃsā dhammā| ()

64. 

 bārasadhammā cak‍kā-

kārā tairasadhutaṅgadhammā cai’pi,

cuddasamattā buddhi

pañ‍cadasavimuttipācanīyā dhammā| ()

65.

 ānāpānassatiyau

sauḷasa sauḷasavidhā’ parantapatīyā,

aṭṭharasa buddhaguṇā

aikūṇavīsati pac‍cavaikkhaṇabuddhi| ()

66.

 catucattāḷisavidhā

pañ‍ñāvatthū’dayabbayaiñāṇāni,

pañ‍ñāsa kusaladhammā

sattādhikasattatippabhāvatthūni| ()

67. 

 catuvīsati kauṭilakkha-

ppamita samāpattiyañ‍caravajirañāṇaṃ,

samantapaṭṭhānapac‍ca-

vaikkhaṇañāṇāni daisanāñāṇāti| ()

68.

 sattāna manattānaṃ

vibhagañāṇānicā’sayānusayānaṃ,

vuttavibhāgā santī

guṇabhāgā bhagavatau tatau bhagavā sau| ()

69.

 manussattabhāvādikai aṭṭhadhammai

samaudhānayitvā’hisambaudhiyā yai,

samiddhā’dhikāraihi sattuttamaihi

mahābaudhisattaihi sampādanīyā| ()

70. 

 adhiṭṭhānadhammādayau pañ‍cu’ḷāra-

paric‍cāgadhammā catussaṅgahā ca,

cariyattayaṃ pāramīdhammarāsi

bhavattyā’bhisambaudhisambhārabhūtā| ()

71.

 pabhutyā’bhinīhāratau yāvabaudhi

asaṅkhaiyyakappāni cattāri’massa,

salakkhāni tai baudhisambhāradhammā

bhavā vuddhipakkhai bhatā sambhatā’ti| ()

72.

 bhajīyanti yā puñ‍ñavantaihi laukai

payaugaṃ samāgamma sampattiyau tā,

bhagānāma vaṭṭabbivaṭṭānugā’ti

pavuc‍canti taisaṃ ubhin‍naṃ bhagānaṃ| ()

73. 

 purai baudhitau baudhisattau samānau

bhusaṃ baudhisambhāradhammai vinantau,

patiṭṭhāsi yasmiṃ bhagai tai vanīti

manussaisu daivaisu uk‍kaṃsabhutai| ()

74.

 tathā’nañ‍ñasāmañ‍ñasāhiñ‍ñajhāna-

ssamāpattibhaidaggamaggapphalādī,

bhagai baudhimūlai vivaṭṭānugai’pi

sayaṃ buddhabhutau samānau vanī’ti| ()

75.

 catubbisa yai kauṭilakkhappamāṇa-

samāpattibhāgā kamahābhāgadhaiyyau,

paraisaṃ nahitāya?ttanau diṭṭhadhamma-

sukhatthāya tai nic‍cakappaṃ vanīti| ()

76. 

 abhiñ‍ñaiyyadhammaisu yai bhāvitabba-

pahātabbabhāgā pariñ‍ñaiyyabhāgā,

siyuṃ sacchikātabbabhāgā vanī’ti

jinau bhāvanāgaucarāsaivanau tai| ()

77.

 asādhāraṇai saisasādhāraṇa yai

imai dhammabhāgā’dhisīlādibhaidā,

phalaṃ yāvatā baudhanaiyyaisu satthā

vanī patthayī suppatiṭṭhānukhauti| ()

78. 

 avaic‍cappasantā imassa’tthi daiva-

manussā bahū bhattiyuttā tathāhi,

asādhāraṇā’naupamānattañāṇa-

ppabhāvāditau sabbasattuttamau sau| ()

79.

 anatthāpahārādipubbaṅgamāya

hitatthā’bhinipphādanai tapparāya,

payaugābhisampattiyā baudhanaiyya-

pajāyau’pakārāvahāyā’mitāya| ()

80.

 viyāmappabhā kaitumālākulāya

bhusaṃ lakkhaṇā’sityanubyañ‍janaihi,

vicittāya rūpindirāmandirāya

samiddhattabhāvā’bhisampattiyāpi| ()

81. 

 yathābhuc‍casīlādidhammubbhavaina

uḷāraina laukattayabyāpināpi,

saman‍nāgatattā kavisuddhaina kitti-

ssarīraina khīraudadhīpaṇḍaraina| ()

82.

 ṭhitattā visiṭṭhāsu uk‍kaṃsakauṭiṃ

paviṭṭhāsu santuṭṭhitā’ppicchatāsu,

catun‍naṃ visāraj‍jadhammāna maddhā

dasan‍naṃ balānañ‍ca sabbhāvataupi| ()

83.

 samantāpasādāvahattā’pirūpa-

ppamāṇadikai jīvalaukai surānaṃ,

narāna’ñ‍jalīvandanāmānapūjā-

vidhānārahattāpi sambhattiṭhānaṃ| ()

84.

 avaic‍cappasādainu’paitā’nusiṭṭhi-

paṭiggāhakā yaijanā kainacāpi,

manussaina daivaina vā brahmunā vā

asaṃhāriyā bhatti taisaṃ kadāci| ()

85. 

 paric‍caj‍ja tai sāvakā jivitampi

jinaṃ dhammapūjāya pūjainti daḷhaṃ|

tathāhi’ssa pañ‍ñattasikkhāpadāni

navītik‍kamantai samuddau’va vailaṃ| ()

86.

 pavuc‍canti bhāgāti dhammassabhāva-

vibhāgā hi tai khandhadhātvādinā’pi,

atītādirūpādibhaidaihi taipi

anaikappabhaidā vibhattā bhavantī| ()

87. 

 papañ‍cattayaṃ sabbasaṃyaujanāni

jinau ganthayaugā’savau’ghau’padhīca,

samucchij‍ja maggaina nibbānadhātvā-

mataṃ sau pibantau vamī tai ca bhāgai| ()

88.

 chacakkhādivatthuni jā’rammaṇāni

chacittāni chabbaidanā phassachak‍kaṃ,

chasañ‍ñā chataṇhā chasañ‍caitanā cha-

bbitak‍kai vicārai cha bhāgai vamīti| ()

89.

 yamā’nanda cattañ‍ca vantaṃ vimuttaṃ

pahīṇaṃ vinissaṭṭha maṅgīrasassa,

na taṃ jātu pac‍caissatītyā’bha satthā

yathāvuttabhāgai vamītvaivamaiva| ()

90. 

 jinau kaṇhasuk‍kaica vaj‍jānavaj‍jai

nihīnappaṇitai adhammai ca dhammai,

asādhāraṇaina’ ggamaggā’nanaina

apac‍cāgamaṃ pāpayī uggirīti| ()

91.

 paraisañ‍ca saṃsāranirākaramhā

samul‍lumpanatthāya kul‍lūpamaṃ sau,

yathājjhāsayaṃ daisayitvāna dhammaṃ

pamāpaisi taihā’pi bhāgai’ti sabbai| ()

92.

 purai pūrayaṃ pāramīdhammajātaṃ

mahābaudhisattau samānau bhagābyaṃ,

siriṃ issarattaṃ yasauhatthasāraṃ

vamī chaḍḍhanīyaṃ yathākhaiḷapiṇḍaṃ| ()

93. 

 tathāhi’ssa laddhaṃ purai saumanassa-

vhayau taimiyau’yaugharau hatthipālau,

kumārausamānau’bhinikkhamma gaihā

siriṃ daivaraj‍jassiriṃ uggiri sau, ()

94.

 anaikāsu jātīsu sampan‍nabhaugau

bhāgai laddhabhaugai’vamaivu’ggiritvā,

sakaṃ hatthagaṃ pacchimai attabhāvai

anaumassiriṃ cak‍kavattissirimpi| ()

95.

 catuddīpikaṃ daivaraj‍jā’dhipac‍ca-

samānādhipac‍caṃ yathābhuc‍ca muc‍caṃ,

yasañ‍cā’pi tatnissayaṃ pañ‍cakāmai

alaggau tiṇaggāya’pā mañ‍ñamānau| ()

96. 

 pahāyā’ bhigantvābhisambaudhiraj‍jai

patiṭṭhāya saddhammarājā babhuva,

asārai tusārai’va saṃsārasārai

suvuttappakārai bhagai sau vamīti| ()

97.

 pavattanti nakkhattarūpaihi bhaihi

samaṃ cak‍kavāḷāvakāsaisu yātā,

tikuṭaddi kuṭaddi canda’k‍ka nairu-

vimānādisaubhā bhagā nāma haunti| ()

98.

 jinau tassamaṅgī janaukāsalaukai

havai chandarāgappahāṇaina yaina,

mahābaudhimaṇḍai nisin‍nausamānau

vibhūtāvibhūtai bhāgai tai vamīti| ()

99. 

 saubhāgavā’ti bhatavā’ti bhagaivanī’ti

bhāgaivanī’ti abhipatthayi bhattavā’ti,

bhāgaivamī’ti tibhavaisu bhagaivamī’ti

anvatthatau hi bhagavā bhagavā samañ‍ñau| ()

100.

 ic‍caiva’massa arahādiguṇappabandha-

pubbācalu’bbhavayasauvisarausadhīsau,

paj‍jañ‍ca saj‍janamanaukumudāni’vai’daṃ

cittāni baudhayati kiṃ purisādhamānaṃ| ()

itimaidhānandābhidhānaina yatinā viracitai sakalakavijana hadayānanda dānanidānai jinavaṃsadīpai santikainidānai bhagavā’tināmapañ‍ñattiyā abhidhaiyaparidīpau chabbīsatimau saggau|

1. 

 aittha’ttahisampattiparahitapaṭipattitau

nissīmāpi dvidhā buddhaguṇā saṅgahitā kathaṃ| ()

2.

 tāsva’ttahitasampattisaddhammacak‍kavattinau|

pahāṇasampadāñāṇasampadābhaidatau dvidhā| ()

3.

 rūpakāyā’nubhāvāsuṃ tatthai’va’ntaugadhā dvidhā,

paratthapaṭipattī’pi payaugāsayabhaidatau| ()

4.

 payaugau lābhasak‍kārasilaukanirapaikkhinau,

dukkhū’pasamaṇatthāya nīyyānikau’padaisanā| ()

5.

 āsayau daivadattādipac‍cāmittajanaisupi,

hitajjhāsayatā nic‍caṃ maittākantāya bhattunau| ()

6. 

 indriyā’paripak‍kānaṃbaudhanaiyyāna manvahaṃ,

pañ‍ñindriyādisampākasamayā’vagamāditau| ()

7.

 daiyyadhammapaṭiggāhappabhutīhā’ nukampiya,

parahitapaṭipatyā’si paraisaṃ hitasādhanaṃ| ()

8.

 taisaṃ guṇavisaiyānaṃ vibhāvanavasainapi,

pāḷiyaṃ arahantyādipadānaṃ gahaṇaṃ kathaṃ| ()

9.

 tatthā’rahanti iminā padaina paridīpitā,

pahāṇasampadānāma attanau hitasampadā, ()

10.

 padaihi sammāsambuddhau laukavidūti attanau,

ñāṇasampattisaṅkhātā nahitasampatti dīpitā| ()

11.

 vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau’ti’minā dassitā’ttanau,

vij‍jācaraṇappabhuti sabbā’pi hitasampadā| ()

12. 

 sugatau’ti’minā vuttā paṭṭhāyapaṇidhānatau,

attanauhitasampatti paratthapaṭipattica| ()

13.

 satthā daivamanussānaṃ purisadammasārathī,

paratthapaṭipatyai’va padañ‍cayaihi dīpitā| ()

14.

 padañ‍cayaina buddhauti bhagavāti vibhāvitā,

yāva’ttahitasampatti parahitapaṭipatti ca| ()

15.

 tidhā buddhaguṇā haituphakhalasattau’pakāratau,

saṃkhittā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau’ti padaihica| ()

16.

 vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau laukavidū’ti’maihi ca,

catūhi phakhalasampattisaṅkhātā kittitā guṇā| ()

17. 

 purisadammasārathi satthā dvīhipadaihi tu,

sattaupakārasampattivasaina gaditā guṇā| ()

18.

 phalasamapattisattaupakārasamapattibhaidatau,

ubhau buddhaguṇā buddhau’ti’minā paridīpitā| ()

19.

 sugatau bhagavā dvīhi padaihā’dic‍cabandhunau,

vibhāvitā haitu phalasattau’pakārasampadā| ()

20.

 thīrasāratarau’dāruttuṅga sagguṇamairunā,

girirājā’pi nīcattaṃ jagāma jinarājinau| ()

21.

 tassā’nupubbagambhīrasampuṇṇaguṇasāgarai,

sāgarau’yaṃ paricchin‍nau bindumattaṃ’va khāyati| ()

22.

 thāvarā’calapatthiṇṇapatiṭṭhāguṇabhumiyā,

naupaiti paṃsupathavī kalabhāgampi satthunau| ()

23. 

 cak‍kavāḷasahassāni sambādhikaḷitāni’va,

guṇalaisānubhāvaina dissantairavibandhunau| ()

24.

 anantāpariyantaina guṇākāyaina satthunau,

ākāsau’manantau’pi antabhutau’va gamyatai| ()

aivaṃ buddhaguṇānantāpariyantā acintiyā,

avāciyā’naupamaiyyā ahauacchariyabbhutā| ()

itimaidhānandābhidhānaina yatinā viracitai sakalakavijana hadayānandadānanidānai jinavaṃsadīpai santikainidātai navan‍namarahādiguṇānaṃ saṅkhaipanayaparidīpau

sattavīsatimausaggau|

1. 

 dhutanijjharacāmarānilaina

sisirai kūṭabhujaihi gijjhakūṭai,

haribhudharahāridaihadhāri

viharantau karuṇākarau kadāci| ()

2.

 vasamāna masaisabhikkhusaṅghaṃ

iha rājaggahanāmarājadhānyā,

varamaṇḍalamāla mānayassu

munirānandayanindamic‍cavauca| ()

3.

 yati sampati san‍nipātayitvā

yatisaṅghaṃ yatirājamabruvī sau,

rucirañ‍jalipūjitaṅghikañ‍jau

samayaṃ mañ‍ñatha yassadāni bhantai| ()

4. 

 atha khau sugatau tatau’higantvā

navasañjhāghanaraṃsivippakiṇṇau,

varamaṇḍalamāḷa mautarittha

ravi mandāramivaudayā’calamhā| ()

5.

 tahi māsanamatthakai nisin‍nau

migarājāriva kañ‍canācalaggai,

parisāsu visāradau abhāsi

munirājā’parihāniyaica dhammai| ()

6.

 abhinkhamiya’mbalaṭṭhikāyaṃ

viharantau bhagavā tatau puramhā,

navapal‍lavamaṇḍitambasākhī

riva’nubyañ‍janacārurūpakāsau| ()

7. 

 itisīlapabhāvitau samādhi

saphalau cittapabhāvitā ca pañ‍ñā,

saphalā’ti pavattadhammacak‍kau

atha nālandamupāgamī sasaṅghau| ()

8.

 tahi mambavanai yathābhirantaṃ

viharantaṃ tamupaic‍ca thairanāgau,

makahītañ‍jalimañ‍jarika siraina

caraṇacanda mavandi sāriputtau| ()

9.

 sunisaj‍ja asaj‍jamānañāṇaṃ

bhagavantaṃ pacuraṃ abhitthavantau,

nadi sihanibhau abhītavācaṃ

bhagavā ca’bbhanumaudi bhāsitaṃ taṃ| ()

10.

 kathayaṃ adhisīlacattapañ‍ñā-

paṭisañ‍ñuttakathaṃ tahiṃ vasitvā,

yatisaṅghapurakkhatau tatau sau

agamā pāṭaligāmamuggadhīmā| ()

11. 

 muni pāṭaligāmupāsakānaṃ

anukampāya sumāpitai nivāsai,

nivasaṃ savaṇañ‍jalīhi paiyyaṃ

vadhuraṃ dhammasudhārasaṃ adāsi| ()

12.

 acirāpagataisu’pāsakaisu

bhagavā pāṭaligāmikaisu taisu,

janasuñ‍ñanikaitanaṃ anañ‍ñau

pavisitvāna akāsi sīhasaiyyaṃ| ()

13.

 magadhādhipatissa bhupatissa

nagaraṃ tatra sunidhavassakārā,

sacivā tidasaihi mantayitvā

viya tasmiṃsamayai sumāpayanti| ()

14. 

 abhipassiya dibbacakkhunā taṃ

bhagavā’nanda mavauca haissatai’daṃ,

ariyā’yatanaṃ vaṇippathau’ti

nagaraṃ pāṭaliputtanāma maggaṃ| ()

15.

 mithubhaidavasaina aggitauvā

dakatau pāṭaliputtasañ‍ñinau khau,

nagarassa kadāci antarāyā

muni vaidaihamuniṃ tayau’tya’vauca| ()

16.

 tadahai’vupasaṅkamiṃsu yaina

bhagavā taina sunīdhavassakārā,

jinapādakirīṭaphuṭṭhasīsā

abhisittai’va khaṇaṃ lasiṃsu’ hau tai| ()

17.

 thirasāraguṇaina dhammarañ‍ñau

dhanudaṇḍaiva ṭhitā nataṅakgayaṭṭhi,

tadubhau savivā nimantayiṃsu

sugataṃ aj‍jatanāya bhaujanaina| ()

18. 

 adhivāsana massa tai vidatvā

paṭiyattaihi paṇītabhaujanaihi,

bhagavanta matappayuṃ sasaṅgaṃ

kamalāvāsanivāsagaṃ sahatthā| ()

19.

 bhagavā’tha sunīdhavassakārai

paribhuttau apanītapattapāṇī,

anumaudi nipīya dhammapānaṃ

pacuraṃ pītiphuṭantarā’bhavuṃ tai| ()

20.

 anuyantajanaihi dhammarañ‍ñau

vajatau bhikkhupūrakkhatassa tamhā,

puthulauratalaina yaṃ visālaṃ

nagaradvāra manantaribabhuva| ()

21. 

 iti gautamabuddhapādaphuṭṭhaṃ

tadidaṃ dvāra mahausī gautamākhyaṃ,

tahi mautari yattha kākakapaiyyā

muni gaṅgākhyasavanti tuṅgavīci| ()

22.

 bahaḷā’nilabhaṅgavīcimālā-

lulitāyā’ti gabhīranin‍nagāya,

ya manaṅgapabhaṅgurau tarittha

tayidaṃ gautamatitthanāma māsi| ()

23.

 sugatau paratīragau’ghatiṇṇau

janataṃ passiya sāvakaihi saddhiṃ,

taraṇattha mulumpakul‍lanāvā

pariyaisanta mudānagātha māha| ()

24. 

 narasārathi yaina bhumikantā-

makuṭākārakuṭīhi nāvakāsau,

upasaṅkami taina kauṭigāmau

uditambhauru hu’pāhanappitaṅghī| ()

25.

 ahamasmi pabuddhasac‍cadhammau

punaruppatti nacatthi mai’ti vatvā|

tahi mauvadi vāsagau tisikkhā-

paṭisaṃyuttakathāya bhikkhusaṅghaṃ| ()

26.

 muni nātikanāmagāmayātau

kathitānandayatindapuṭṭhapañhau,

paridīpayi dhammadappaṇākhyaṃ

pariyāyaṃ gatipac‍cavaikkhaṇāya| ()

27. 

 arahādiguṇakarauka mahaisi

viharaṃ tatrapi giñ‍jakānivāsai,

piṭakattayasaṅgahaṃ vasinaṃ

adhisīlādikathaṃ kathaisi bhīyyau| ()

28.

 sugatau pagatau sabhikkhusaṅghau

atha vaisālipuriṃ purīnamaggaṃ,

tahi mambavanai vasaṃ vasinaṃ

satipañ‍ñāparamaṃ abhāsi dhammaṃ| ()

29.

 jinagandhagajau mama’mbapāli-

gaṇikā ambavanai’ni’dāni sutvā,

abhiruyha payāsi bhaddayānaṃ

kucabhārātisamiddhabhattibhārā| ()

30.

 gaṇikā’tha katañ‍jalinisin‍nā

ghanapīnatthanabhārarumbhītaiva,

karavikavirāvamañ‍jughausau

madhuraṃ dhamma mabhāsi tāya satthā| ()

31. 

 katabhattanimantanā pasādaṃ

rasanādāmasaraihi vāharanti,

pavidhāya padakkhiṇaṃ munindaṃ

agamā haṃsavadhuva mandiraṃ sā| ()

32.

 ahatāhatanīlapītaratta-

sitamañ‍jiṭṭhavirāgasāṭakaihi,

sunivatthasupārutā’bhirūḷhā

suraputtāriva bhaddabhaddayānaṃ| ()

33.

 atha licchavirājarājaputtā

upasaṅkamma paṇamma dhammarañ‍ñau,

nakharaṃsipabandhasindhutīrai

samayuṃ maggaparissamaṃ nisin‍nā| ()

34. 

 vilasiṃsu kiriṭabhiṅgamālā-

viraḷā licchavikañ‍jakausarāsi,

ravibandhavadhammabhākaraina

phuṭitā’dhaṭṭhitasilagandhasāli| ()

35.

 saphalīkatadul‍labhantabhāvā

viphalībhutanimantānā janā tai,

virajaṅghirajaupisaṅgamauḷī

pura mārūḷharathā tatau payāsuṃ| ()

36.

 janalaucanatauraṇākarāḷaṃ

avatiṇṇau vimalañ‍jasaṃ sasaṅghau,

gaṇikāya gharaṃ mahaisi pātau

caraṇak‍kantathalambujau jagāma| ()

37. 

 katabhaujanasaṅgabhāvasānai

gaṇikā pañ‍jalikā nisaj‍ja dhammaṃ,

sunisamma sasāvakassa’dāsi

sugatassa’mbavanaṃ samiddhasaddhā| ()

38.

 muni rambavanaṃ paṭiggahaitvā

viharitvātahimaita daiva dhammaṃ,

kathayaṃ adhisīlacittapañ‍ñā-

paramaṃ baiḷuvagāmakaṃ jagāma| ()

39.

 ahamaittha vasāmi bhikkhavai’kau

samaṇhai’ttasahāyakaihi tumhai,

upagacchatha vassa massamaisu

muni vaisālisamanatatautya’bhāsi| ()

40. 

 jitamārabalasasa baiḷuvasmiṃ

atha vassupagatassa ghauraraugau,

udapādi ca māraṇantikā’suṃ

kaṭukā kāyikavaidanā’tibāḷhā| ()

41.

 adhivāsanakhantipāragau sau

sukhadukkhaisu tulāsamau tadāni,

bhagavā avihañ‍ñamānarūpau

adhivāsaisi satau ca sampajānau| ()

42.

 anapaikkhiya tāva bhikkhusaṅghaṃ

idhu’paṭṭhākanivaidanaṃ akatvā,

analanti mamā’nupādisaisa-

parinibbānapadaṃ sacai labhaiyyaṃ| ()

43. 

 vīriyaina paṭippaṇāmayitvā

balavā’bādha malabbhayāpanīyaṃ,

paṭisaṅkharaṇārahaṃ visaisaṃ

samadhiṭṭhāya sajīvitindriyassa| ()

44.

 bhagavā’tha samādhi mappayitvā

paṭipassambhiya dukkhavaidanaṃ sau,

pavihāsi mahāvipassanāya

nahi vikkhamhita vaidanā punāsuṃ| ()

45.

 ravibandhu vihāratau’higantvā

bahichāyāairaṇaṅgaṇappadaisai,

sunisaj‍ji susajajitā sanamhi

pariyuṭṭhāya lahuṃ gilānabhāvā| ()

46.

 jitajātijarārujau nisīdi

yahimānandatapaudhanau’ pagamma,

tahi mañ‍jalikau mayā sudiṭṭhaṃ

khamanīyaṃ tava sāta mic‍cavauca| ()

47. 

 tava bāḷhagilānatāya bhantai

mama patthaṅghanau viya’ttabhāvau,

sakalāpi disā’nupaṭṭhahanti

napi dhammā paṭibhanti manti vatvā| ()

48.

 apicā’si mamai’sa sāvakānaṃ

hadayassā’salavau nakiñ‍civatvā,

bhagavā napanā’nupādisaisa-

parinibbāna padaṃ bhajai’ti bhantai| ()

49.

 yamanantarabāhiraṃ karitvā

nanu cā’nandapakāsitau hi dhammau,

gurumuṭṭhi tathāgataisu natthi

vada kiṃ patthayatai mamai’sa saṅghau| ()

50. 

 adhunā’ha masīti vassikausmi

parijiṇṇausmi tathāgatassa kāyau,

sakaṭaṃviya jaj‍jaraṃ jarāya

bhidurau vattati vaikhamissakaina| ()

51.

 sanimittakavaidanāniraudhā

upasampaj‍ja vimuttijaṃ samādhiṃ,

viharaiyya yadā tadāttabhāvau

vayadhammaupi atīvaphāsuhauti| ()

52.

 adhunāga miva’ttadhammadīpā

bhavathā’nañ‍ñaparāyaṇāttha tumhai,

bhagavāvadi tai’va sattamā’ti

samaṇā bhāvitakāya cittapañ‍ñā| ()

53. 

 punarāgami tattha vutthavassau

bhagavā jaitavanaṃ mahāvihāraṃ,

upagamma tadāni dhammasainā-

pati satthāra mavandi sāriputtau| ()

54.

 vividhiddhivikubbaṇaṃ vidhāya

yatināgau muninā katāvakāsau,

tava pacchimadassananti vatvā

nivutau pañ‍casataihi satthukappau| ()

55.

 abhinamma padakkhiṇaṃ karitvā

bhagavantaṃ samupaic‍ca mātugaihaṃ,

janitau’varakai nipaj‍jaka mañ‍cai

parinibbāyi tadā’si bhūmicālau| ()

56. 

 atha kaulitanāmathairanāgau

parinibbāyi tathā katāvakāsau,

puna dhātusarīra mappayitvā

munikārāpayi caitiyāni taisaṃ| ()

57.

 janalaucanapīyamānarūpau

muni vaisālipuraṃ kamaina patvā,

sunivatthasupārutau kulaisu

cari piṇḍāya karī’va sairicārī| ()

58.

 bhagavā paribhuttapātarāsau

bhavatā’nandadivāvihārakāmau,

atha gaṇha nisīdananti vatvā

gami cāpālasamañ‍ñacaitiyaṃhi| ()

59. 

 atha khau bhagavā nisidi yaina

tadupaṭṭhākavarau’pagamma taina,

katapañ‍jalikau nisidi vatvā

ramaṇīyaṃti udainacaitiyampi| ()

60.

 sugatassa panī’ddhipādadhammā

caturau bhikkhu subhāvitā suciṇṇā,

bahulīkaḷitā’ti cāha bhīyyau

muni tiṭṭhaiyya sacai khamaiyya kappaṃ| ()

61.

 karuṇāparibhāvitāsayaina

jitamāraina tivāra mattamaivaṃ,

ujukaṃ muninā karīyamānai

vipulaubhāsanimittajappanamhi| ()

62.

 pariyuṭṭhitamānasau riva’ñ‍ñau

kharamāraina pamuṭṭhamānasau sau,

na ca taṃ paṭivijjhi naiva yāci

bhagavā tiṭṭhatu yāvatā’yukappaṃ| ()

63. 

 vaja kaṅkhasi yassadānikālaṃ

pahitā’nandatapaudhanau’tivatvā,

vasavattivasikatau muhuttaṃ

avidūramhi nisīdi rukkhamūlai| ()

64.

 upagañchiya baudhanaiyya bandhu

bhagavā yaina pamatta bandhu taina,

bhujagauriva bhuttanaṅgalaina

abhimānaina anaunataṅgayaṭṭhi| ()

65.

 ajapālasamañ‍ñinau kadāci

uruvailāya vaṭaddumassa mūlai,

katakic‍ca? tayā katā paṭiñ‍ñā

likhitā vattati cittapautthakai mai| ()

66. 

 samaṇā tava sāsanā’va tiṇṇā

adhunā dhammadharā’nudhammacārī,

paṭipattiratā bahussutā ca

suviyattā suvisāradā vinītā| ()

67.

 paṭisiddhaparappavādivādā

sahadhammaina sapāṭihāriyaṃ tai,

kathayanti kathāpayanti dhammaṃ

parinibbātu tatau bhavantya vauca, ()

68.

 pariniṭṭhitasabbabuddhakic‍cau

muniraivaṃ samudiritai tivāraṃ,

analanti nirālayau bhavaisu

tadū’(pacchandasikaṃ) nisaidhanāya| ()

69. 

 appaussuk‍kau samānau viharatu kalimā hau timāsac‍cayaina

sac‍cālaukappakāsau durita tamahidau pañ‍catāḷisavassaṃ|

sammā khīṇassinaihau tibhuvanabhavanai dhammarājappadīpau

nibbāyissatya’bhāsi tadahani vijahañ‍cā’yu saṅkhāravaigaṃ ()

70.

 cāpālai caitiyai’vaṃ vijahati satiyā sampajañ‍ñainavāyu-

saṅkārai bhūmicālau bhavi paṭupaṭahārāva gambhiraghausau,

gajjiṃsu vij‍jurājibhujasatapahaṭā sukkhajimūtabhairi

laukau saukandhakārai paripati janitau bhiṃsanau laumahaṃsau| ()

itimaidhānandābhidhānaina yatinā viracitai sakalakavijana hadayānandadānanidānai jinavaṃsadīpai santikai nidānai bhagavatau āyusaṅkhāraussaj‍jana pavatti paridīpau aṭṭhavīsatimau saggau|

1. 

 yainā’nandau vasati bhagavā taina gantvā nisin‍nau

pādambhaujai sumahiya suhaibaddhamuddhāñ‍jalihi,

bhantai sukkhāsati ca ailitā hiṃsanau laumahaṃsau

jātau kasmā vasumativadhū sampavaidhītya’pucchi| ()

2.

 haiṭṭhā’kāsai balavapavanai vāyamānai kadāci

vātaṭṭhā yā salilapathavī taṭṭhitā paṃsubhumi,

saṅkampantai yatharivatari laula kal‍laulamāli-

majjhautiṇṇā pathavicalanaṭṭhāna mānanda cai’taṃ| ()

3.

 appaikac‍cai samaṇa samaṇabrāhmaṇā appamāṇā

āpausañ‍ñā sukhumapathavi bhāvitā santi yaisaṃ,

pattābhiñ‍ñā paricitavasī tai samāpattilābhī

kampaintīmaṃ tadapi bhavatai bhūmicālassa ṭhānaṃ| ()

4. 

 gabbhauk‍kantau bhacati ca yadā sampajānau satauva

gabbhasmā nikkhamati carimai attabhāvai tadāpi,

sambaudhiṃ vā purisanisahau bujjhatai kampatai’yā

aitai dhammā samaṇa mahatau bhumicālassa haitu| ()

5.

 buddhau hutvā bhuvananayanau dhammacak‍kaṃ pajānaṃ

saṃvattaitī vijahati yadā cā’yusaṅkhāravaigaṃ,

kampatyai’sāpathavi phusatai khandhanibbānadhātuṃ

ānandai’tai mahatipathavikampanatthāya haitu| ()

6. 

 tabyāsainabbigatahadayā’nandamānandathairaṃ

assāsaitvā uparupari sau daisanaṃ vaḍḍhayitvā,

ānandā’haṃ karahacivasiṃ yassa nairañ‍jarāya

naj‍jā tirai jitajalamucassā’japālassa mūlai| ()

7.

 tatrā’gantvā phusatu bhagavā khandha nibbānadhātuṃ

issāmāyāmalinahadayau pāpimā ic‍cavauca,

laddhaukāsau punarapi kamamaṃ aivamaivā’bhiyāci

aj‍jā’sīnaṃ paramarucirai pya’tra cāpālacaityai| ()

8.

 appausasukaikā tvamiha kalimā hauhi māsaihi tīhi

khandhānaṃ nibbuti bhagavatau haissatī’c‍caitamatthaṃ,

āraucaintaina hi kasatimatā samapajañ‍ñaina bhikkhu

aussaṭṭhau mai jitanamucinā cā’yusaṅkhāradhammau| ()

9. 

 aivaṃ vuttai caraṇakamalacanda mānandathairau

natvā bhantai bahujanahitatthāya tiṭṭhā’yukappaṃ,

vatatikkhattuṃ paramakaruṇācauditau yācidāni

nā’yaṃ kālau bhavati sugataṃ yāvanāyi’c‍ca’vauca| ()

10.

 sambaudhiṃ tvaṃ yadi bhagavatau saddahantau’si kasmā

nippīḷaisī dasabala manul‍laṅghanīyā’bhilāpaṃ,

tasmiṃ tasmiṃ sati bhagavatā ka yamānai nimittai

tumhaivai’taṃ viya kalimatā duk‍katañ‍cā’paraddhaṃ| ()

11.

 yācaiyyāsi yadi dasabalaṃ cai paṭikkhippa vācā

satthā’dattai tava tatiyakaṃ vippayaugau piyaihi,

naṇvā’kkhātau samaṇa paṭigac‍caiva mai saṅkhataṃ yaṃ

jātaṃ bhūtaṃ avipariṇataṃ taṃ kutau’ pai’ttha labbhā| ()

12. 

 aikaṃsainā’vitathavacasā sac‍casandhaina cāyu-

saṅkhārau’ hīyati bhagavatā vyākatā’nanda bhikkhu,

yāsā vācā yathariva chiyāmuttakhāṇau tathā taṃ

pac‍cāgacchai napunavacanaṃ jīvitārakkhahaitu| ()

13.

 aivaṃ vatvā sapadi sugatau gandhanāgindagāmī

yainārañ‍ñaṃ vipulamalakāsāravaisāliyaṃ sau,

kūṭāgāraṃ tadavasariyā’nandathairaina saddhiṃ

ic‍cābhāsī samaṇaparisaṃ san‍nipātaihi sīghaṃ| ()

14. 

 aivaṃ bhantai lapitavacanau saukasal‍laina viddhau

sauhā’yasmā vasigaṇa mupaṭṭhānasāḷāya māsuṃ,

rāsikatvā mahitacaraṇau’pāhanau tassa kālaṃ

āraucaisi gamiya bhagavā pīṭhikāyaṃ nisaj‍ja| ()

15.

 āmantaitvā samaṇaparisaṃ baudhipakakkhai bhavā mai

yaitai dhammā sayamadhigatā daisitā sādhukaṃ vau,

uggaṇhitvā yathariva siyā sāsanañ‍caddhanīyaṃ

bhāvaitabbā suparihariyā saivitabbā’ti vatvā| ()

16.

 nibbāyissatya’vaca bhagavā ac‍cayainā’ciraina

taimāsānaṃ bhuvanabhavanu’j‍jautapaj‍jautarūpau|

tumhai sampādayatha samaṇā appamādaina sabbai

saṅkhārā yaṃ samudayamayā lakkhaṇabbhāhatā’ti| ()

17. 

 pubbaṇhai sau karakisalayā’dhāna’viṭṭhānapattau

pattau satthā pacuracaraṇau cīvaracchan‍nagantau,

gattaubhāsāruṇitaparikhāvīthipākāracak‍kaṃ

cak‍kaṅkaiha’ṅkitapadatalassālivaisālināmaṃ| () (yamakabandhanaṃ)

18.

 āhiṇḍitvā tahi manugharaṃ piṇḍa manvaisamānau

pacchābhattaṃ bhuvananayanau laucanindīvaraihi,

taṃ vaisāliṃ dviradagatimā’nanda nāgāpalaukaṃ

aulaukaitvā ida mavaca mai pacchimaṃ dassana’nti| ()

19. 

 tambhāṭhānā nayanasubhagaṃ saivitau sāvakaihi

bhaṇḍaggāmāṭavi mavasaṭau diṭṭhivādībhasīhau,

nicchāraitvā sarasamadhuraṃ dhammagambhīraghausaṃ

taṇhākhīṇā mamapunabhavau bhikkhavai natthya’bhāsi| ()

20.

 tissau sikkhā pariharatha vau sādhukaṃ bhikkhavai’ti

aivaṃ vatvā matibhagavatibhattubhūtau sayambhu,

tamhāgāmā punarupagamī hatthigāma’mbagāmaṃ

jambuggāmaṃ vamitagamanau hatthivik‍kantigantā| ()

21. 

 patvā bhaugāyatana manasau bhauganāmaṃ subhikkhaṃ

nibbhaugau sau nagaramaparaṃ bhāratibhatturūpau,

cittābhaugaṃ kurutha samaṇā sādhukaṃtaṃ suṇātha

daisissāmī’tya’vadi caturau vau uḷārāpadaisai| ()

22.

 aisau dhammau bhavati vinayau sāsanaṃ satthu caidaṃ

abbhañ‍ñātaṃ vata bhagavatau sammukhā mai sutanti,

sakkhīkatvā yadi vadati maṃ bhikkhavai kauci bhikkhu

nādatabbaṃ tadadhivacanaṃ nappaṭik‍kausitabbaṃ| ()

23.

 pakkhittānaṃ mama tipiṭakai tappadabyañ‍janānaṃ

yaṃyaṃṭhānaṃ avatarati saṃdissatai niddhamaittha,

gantabbaṃ vau sugahitamidaṃ bhāsitaṃ bhikkhunauti

 

 chaḍḍaitabbaṃ kavacanamitaraṃ duggahītanti nau cai|()

24. 

 āvāsai yau viharati mahābhikkhusaṅgau amutra

thairā bhikkhū tipiṭakadharā thairavaṃsaddhajā yai,

yvābhiñ‍ñātau paṭibalatarau bhikkhu vā sammukhā mai

taisaṃ taisaṃ idamavagataṃ suggahītatti vuttai| ()

25.

 autāraitvā tadapi vinayai satthu suttābhidhammai

saṃsandantaṃ yadipana paṭiggaṇhitabbaṃkata na naucai,

cattārau mai itivibhajitai nippadaisāpadaisai

dhāraiyyātha’bruvi muni ranādhānagāhī sadā vau| ()

26. 

 patvā pāvāpuravara mathau’raupitakkhandhabhārau

ambārañ‍ñai viharati mamaṃ dhammarājāti sutvā,

tibbacchandau javanamatinau dassanassādanamhi

cundau gantvā caraṇakamalaṃ vandi kammāraputtau| ()

27.

 sammādhammassavaṇapasutau aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau

sautāpan‍nau paṭhamadivasai dassanainaivasatthu,

buddhaṃ pañ‍ñābhagavatipatiṃ svatanāyā’bhiyācaṃ

candau pubbācalamiva gharaṃ pāvisi cundanāmau| ()

28.

 sampādaitvāgahapati bahuṃ tāyaratyā’vasānai

khaj‍jaṃ bhauj‍jaṃ sumadhutaraṃ sūkaraṃ maddavampi,

pakkhittaujaṃ pacuravibhavau ñāpayī dhammarañ‍ñau

kālau bhantai’tarahi bhagavā niṭṭhitaṃ bhaujananti| ()

29. 

 sālakkhandhāyatabhujayugau muggavaṇṇaṃ gahaitvā

pattaṃ pattatthavikapihitaṃ pak‍kanigraudhavaṇṇaṃ,

acchādaitvā parivutavasi cīvaraṃ paṃsukūlaṃ

pāsādabbhantara mabhiruhī tassa sauvaṇṇavaṇṇau| ()

30.

 bālādic‍cauri’va dasabalau tāva pubbācalaggai

pañ‍ñattasmiṃ ratanakhavitai bhaddapīṭhai nisaj‍ja,

āmantaitvā jitadhanapatiṃ cundamādic‍cabandhu

satthāraṃ tvaṃ parivisiminā maddavainātya’bhāsi| ()

31. 

 santappaitvā sugatapamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ sahatthā

maṃsaṃ saubbhai nikhaṇiya tatau satthubhuttāvasaisaṃ,

bhatyā dhammassavaṇanirataṃ baudhayitvāpayāsi

pūrau paṅkairuhamiva jinau cundakammāraputtaṃ| ()

32.

 bāḷhābādhau balavakaṭukā vaidanā tassa bhattaṃ

bhuttāvissā’bhavi bhagavatau rattapakkhandikā’si,

vikkhambhaitvā tamapi satimā sampajānau’vidūrai

maggautiṇṇau muni rupagamīrukkhamūlaṃ kilantau| ()

33.

 pañ‍ñāpaitvā catuguṇamupaṭṭhākathairau adāsi

yaṃ saṅghāṭiṃ narahari tahiṃ vissamattau nisaj‍ja,

gantvā’nandā’hara sarabhasaṃ tvaṃ pipāsāturassa

pānīyyaṃ mai nikhiladarathā nibbutassaitya’bhāsī| ()

34. 

 yasmā bhattai sakaṭasatasañ‍cārasambhin‍namaggā

gaurūpānaṃ vigaḷitaṭī siṅgasaṅghaṭṭaṇaina,

cak‍kacchin‍nā kalalakalusībhutasantattavāri

nālaṃ pātuṃ salilamadhunā kun‍nadi sandatai’dha| ()

35.

 ac‍cāsan‍nai kakudhaviṭapīmūlasaṃsaṭṭhakulā

vātakkhittā’malajalakaṇā sātasitaudapuṇṇā,

sak‍kā bhantai savati kakudhāsindhu gattāni sīkiṃ

kātuṃ pātuṃ dharaṇiramaṇi baddhahārābhirāmā| ()

36. 

 aivaṃ vuttai puna bhagavatā cauditau pattahatthau

patvā’nandau kalalavisamaṃ kun‍nadītitthamāsuṃ,

naitvā sitaudaka malulitaṃ nimmalaṃ sandamānaṃ

ñatau bhantai pivatu bhagavā’tyā’ha buddhānubhāvau| ()

37.

 tasmiṃkālai samitatasiṇaṃ rukkhamūlai nisin‍naṃ

naṃ disvāna’ṅkusanisitadhī puk‍kusau kamal‍laputtau,

pabyākāsika paṭutarasamāpattiyākittanaina

āḷārassā’dhikavupasamai attanau’bhippasādaṃ| ()

38.

 gaj‍jantisvā’sanisu paritau niccharantīsu jātu

vij‍jummālāsu ca galagalāyantiyā vuṭṭhiyāhaṃ,

sañ‍ñibhutau nanu khaphasamāpattiyā santavittau

nā’ssausiṃ bhau sutikaṭuravaṃ nāddasaṃ rūpavāha| ()

39. 

 vuttaṃ sutvā’matarasahīraṃ uddharitvāna dhīmā

saddhābījaṃ panihita kamathā’ḷārakālāmakhaittai,

yaibhuyyainā’samacupasamai siṅgīvaṇṇai pasan‍nau

datvā buddhaṃ saraṇa magamā sāṭakaṃ siṅgīvaṇṇaṃ| ()

40.

 tattaṅgāraudaramiva tamaṅgīrasaṅgaupanītaṃ

vatthaṃ vītac‍cika mabhinavaṃ siṅgivaṇṇaṃ rarāja,

pacchā pac‍cuttariya kakudhāsindhu majjhaugahaitvā

ambārañ‍ñaṃ tahi mavatarī sakyasihau sasaṅghau| ()

41.

 saṅghāṭiṃ patthariya sahasā cundathairaina mañ‍cai

pañ‍ñattasmiṃ sapadi samadhiṭṭhāya vuṭṭhānasañ‍ñaṃ,

ac‍cadhāyā’dhikakilamathau sau satau sampajañ‍ñau

pādai pādaṃ bhavabhayabhidau sīhasaiyyaṃ akāsi| ()

42. 

 āmantaitvā niravadhidayau thaira mānandanāmaṃ

dvai mai laddhā samasamaphalā piṇḍapātā visiṭṭhā,

sandaihau yau karabhavi siyā cundakammāraputta-

ssai’vaṃ vatvā pariharatu tañ‍cāha mai ac‍cayaina| ()

43.

 tamhā khīṇāsavaparivutau bhuripañ‍ñau hirañ‍ña-

vatyā naj‍jā vijanapavanaṃ pārimai tirabhāgai,

phul‍laṃ sālabbana mavasarī kausiṇārāna maggaṃ

mal‍lānaṃ sau suravanasiriṃ rājadhānyā’vidūrai| ()

44. 

 ānandainā’ nadhivaravacau cauditainau’ pacārai

pañ‍ñantasmiṃ tathaṇayamakassālarukkhantarāḷai

mañ‍cai pañ‍ñāsatiparimukhau uttarādhānasīsai

katvā pādauparipada manuṭṭhānasaiyyaṃakāsau ()

45.

 sitacchāyā vigaḷitarajaudhūsarā sabbaphāli-

phul‍lā bhantī jaṭitaviṭapakkhandhamūlā’ ñ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ,

saṅkiṇṇālī sapadi yamakassālasālā visālā

dissantai’vaṃ vakulatilakā’saukacampaiyyasākhī| ()

46.

 nac‍caṃ gītaṃ vividhaturiyaṃ vattatai’dāni dibbaṃ

dibbaṃ cuṇṇaṃ malayajamayaṃ dibbamandāravāni,

passā’nandabbikacayamakassālapupphānya’kālai

samūjāyai’vahi bhagavatau antalikkhā patanti| ()

47. 

 aitai brahmāmaranaraphaṇī cāmaracchattahatthā

mālāmālāguḷaparimalaṇḍupadīpaddhajaihi,

chan‍naṃ tāḷāvacarabhajitaṃ maṅgalāgārabhutaṃ

jātikkhaittaṃ nanu bhagavatau kaivalaṃ pūjanāya| ()

48.

 ānandai’vaṃ satipi bhagavā tāvatā sak‍katauvā

sammā taisaṃ nacagarukatau namānitau pūjitauvā,

yau khau dhammaṃ carati samaṇau’ pāsakau vā’nudhammaṃ

bhatyā sau maṃ paramavidhānā kamānayai pūjayaiti| ()

49. 

 amhai tasmātiha paṭipadaṃ suṭṭhu dhammānudhammaṃ

sampādaimā’tya’vaca muni vau sikkhitabbañhi aivaṃ,

dhammāssāmiṃ sapadi puratau vījamānau samānau

hatthicchāpau yathariva ṭhītau thairanāgau’pavānau| ()

50.

 mal‍lānaṃ khau nagaravaratau yāvatā sāladāyaṃ

rāsibhūtā’surasuravarabrahmarājūhi yasmā,

daṭṭhuṃ buddhaṃ dasabaladharaṃ khittavālaggakauṭi-

mattaṭṭhānai dasadasahi vā natthya’phuṭṭhappadaisau| ()

51.

 kandantīnaṃ pakiriya sakai kaisapāsai ca bāhā

paggaṇahitvā sirasi pathavisañ‍ñinīdaivatānaṃ,

jhāyantīnaṃ bhuviparipatantīna mujjhāyinīnaṃ

daintau’kāsaṃ apanayi parañ‍caiḷhakainau’pavānaṃ| ()

52. 

 saṅkhārānaṃ khayavaya manāgāminau vītarāgā

daivabrahmā sumariya yathaivi’ndakhīlācalaṭṭhā,

nāmhai bhantai’tarahī viya vau ac‍cayainātya’vaucuṃ

passissāmā’yati miga manaubhāvanīyaipi bhikkhū| ()

53.

 jātaṭṭhānappabhutika midhānandaṭhānaṃ catuk‍kaṃ

puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ bhuvi bhagavatau sabbhisaṃvaijaniyaṃ,

addhā saddhāvisadahadayā sādhavau cārikāyaṃ

āhiṇḍantā pavurakusalaṃ tatrapatvā viṇanti| ()

9 54. 

 puṭṭhassaivaṃ kathamapi mayaṃ mātugāmaisu bhantai

vattissāmā’tyamitamatimā’ nandathairassa’bhāsi,

tan‍nijjhānaṃ tadabhilapanaṃ mākarauthāti tumhai

aivaṃsantai satiparimukhā hautha chadvārarakkhā| ()

55.

 puṭṭhassaivaṃ maya mutusamuṭṭhānarūpāvasiṭṭhai

vattaiyyāmhai tavanirupamai rūpakāyai kathan‍nu,

mākhau tumhai bhavatha muninau daihapūjāvidhānai

sakhyāpārā upari ghaṭathā’hā’savānaṃ khayaiti| ()

56.

 saṃvij‍jantai bhagavati idhānanda bhīyyaupasan‍nā

rūpībrahmāmarapabhutayau khattiyabrāhmaṇā yai,

sak‍kac‍caṃ tai yathariva janā cak‍kavattissarīrai

sabyāpārā naraharisariraupahārai siyunti| ()

57. 

 cattārau mai bahujanahitā buddhapac‍caikabuddhā

yasmā maggapphalasukhamudā sāvakā cak‍kavattī,

rājā pūjāvidhisumahiyā haunti thūpārahai’va

tasmā thūpau mamapi bhavatā’nandasiṅghāṭakamhi| ()

58.

 aivaṃ vuttai sariya tamurautaumaribhūtasaukau

thairānandau pavisiya nirālambadhammau vihāraṃ,

ālambitvā vilapiya bahuṃ aggalatthambhasīsai

satthā saikhai kaḷitakaruṇāpāṅgabhaṅgau paraudi| ()

59. 

 āmantaitvā tamanadhivarau puñchamakānassudhāraṃ

thairaṃ mākhauvilapi ala mānanda māsauci haivaṃ,

saṅkhārānaṃ kathamiha labhai nic‍cataṃ nibbikāraṃ

akkhātaṃ mai nanu piyajanabbippayaugau siyāti| ()

60.

 maittāpubbaina hi cirataraṃ kāyakammaina vācā-

kammainā’yaṃ guṇamaṇimanaukammunā bhikkhavai maṃ|

sak‍kac‍caṃ san‍nicitakusalaupac‍cupaṭṭhāsi tasmā

ātāpī sau tvamasi nipapakau haisi khīṇāsavauti| ()

61. 

 chāyāmañ‍ñai cira manucaraṃ saiyyathā’nandabhikkhū-

paṭṭhākau mai bhavati sutavā nāgatātitakānaṃ,

sambuddhānaṃ bhagavata mupaṭṭhāyakā cai’tadaggā

ic‍cā’hai’tapparamasamaṇāyaiva haissantya’haisuṃ| ()

62.

 saṃvaṇṇaisi niravadhiguṇau ukkhipantau’va mairuṃ

saṅkhaubhaintau viya jalanidhiṃ pattharantau’va bhumiṃ,

vitthāraintau viya ravipathaṃ saṅghamajjhai ṭhitassa

ānandassu’ttaritaraguṇaṃ abbhutacchairabhūtaṃ| ()

63.

 hitvā sākhānagara manalaṃjaṅgalaṃ issarānaṃ

vāsaṭṭhānaṃ taditarapuraṃ sabbasampattisāraṃ,

patvā rājaggahapabhutikaṃ nātha nibbāyatūti

aivaṃ vuttai muni ravaca māhaivamānandavauca| ()

64. 

 pubbai dibbaupamasukha vidhā’nanda’haṃ cak‍kavatti-

rājā hutvā cira manubhaviṃ dhammikau dhammarājā,

mal‍lānaṃ khau tadahani kusāvatya’yaṃ rājadhāni

āsi lakkhivasati ralakārājadhānī’va phītā| ()

65.

 gāmakkhaittai jitaripuraṇau kausiṇārāna masmiṃ

sālārañ‍ñai jinakarivarau mārakaṇṭhiravaina,

yāmai aj‍jāhani rajaniyā pacchimai hañ‍ñatai’ti

vāsaiṭṭhānaṃ pahiṇi yatimāraucanatthaṃ tamatthaṃ| ()

66. 

 santhāgārai mahatiparisā kiñ‍cikammaṃ paṭic‍ca

rāsibhūtā yativaravacaucauditā mucchitāsi,

uyyānaṃ tai pavisiya tadā mal‍laputtā ca mal‍lā

vyāpaj‍jiṃsū kasiranikarāvārapārai nimuggā| ()

67.

 yaṃnūnāhaṃ narapatikulaṃ aikamaikaṃ gahaitvā

vandāpaiyyaṃ caraṇakamaladvanda maṅgīrasassa,

ic‍cānandau yati saparisau puttadāraihisaddhiṃ

itthan‍nāmau paṇamati jinaṃ mal‍larājāti vatvā| ()

68.

 mal‍lānañ‍cāhariya paṭhamaiyaiva yāmai rajanyā

vandāpaisi sakalaparisaṃ tainupāyaina buddhaṃ,

sutvā vidvā bhagavata manuṭṭhānasaiyyappavattiṃ

sāḷārañ‍ñaṃ avasari paribbājakau yau subhaddau| ()

69. 

 aukāsaṃ mai dadatha samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ pucchanāya

kaṅkhādhammaṃ pajahitu midhānandapattau hamasmi,

vuttai satthā kilamati alaṃhāvusau mā vihañ‍ñi

vatvā’nandau bhagavati dayācauditau vārayī taṃ| ()

70.

 laddhaukāsau dasabaladayājālabaddhau subhaddau

añ‍ñāpaikhau pavisiya tahiṃ tabbihaisānapaikkhau,

pañhaṃ pucchi tadupanayanai kauvidau’bhāsidhammaṃ

vitthāraintau ariyavinayai puggalai suppatiṭṭhai| ()

71. 

 pabbaj‍jitvā bhagavati sayaṃ bhikkhubhāvābhisittau

saṅkhārānaṃ khayavaya mathau bhāvayitvā subhaddau,

viddhaṃsaitvā sakalakalusaṃ satthupac‍cakkhabhutau

un‍nādaitvā parisamarahaṃ pacchimausāvakausi| ()

72.

 pañ‍ñattau yau bhavati vinayau daisitau yauca dhammau

sau vau satthā paramasaraṇau’tyā’ha mai ac‍cayaina,

thairai bhikkhū taditaravasī gāravaṃ vauharantu

bhantai vatvā pariharatha vau sādarāsappatissā| ()

73.

 pac‍cakkhau nau bhavi dasabalau pucchituṃ sammukhā taṃ

nāsakkhimhā mayamidamitī māhuvatthānutāpā,

muttai tuṇhībhavi vasigaṇaukiñ‍ci buddhaicadhammai

saṅghai maggai vimati yadivau bhikkhavai pucchathāti| ()

74. 

 yasmā khandhai pajahati jinau bhikkhavai bhandadāni

āmantaimī niyatabhadurā sabbasaṅkhāradhammā,

sampādaithā’tya’mitamatimā appamādaina tumhai

pabyākāsi bhagavata mayaṃ pacchimāhautivācā| ()

75.

 rūpārūpāvacarakiriyajjhānasañ‍ñāniraudha-

saṅkhātā yā navavidhasamāpattiyau tā’nulaumaṃ,

nissīmamhaunidhinibhaguṇau mārasaṅgāmasūrau

buddhau nārāyanabaladharau sau samāpaj‍ji tāva| ()

76. 

 taṃ taṃ jhānaṃ muni paṭisamāpaj‍jamānau niraudhā

sammā pac‍cuṭṭhahiya paṭhamajjhānavausāna māpa,

taṃ taṃ jhānaṃ punaranusamāpaj‍jamānau catuttha-

rūpajjhānu’ṭṭhahiya vigatāsaisasaṅkhāradhammau| ()

77.

 snaihaparikkhayajātau

paj‍jautau viya tilaukamaṇipaj‍jautau,

buddhaunirupadhisaisa-

parinibbutiyā sayambhu parinibbāyi| ()

78. 

 sampati daivamanussa-

pajāya saddhiṃ paraudamānāya bhusaṃ,

saukaini’va saṅkampi

sahassarāvaina’yaṃ mahāpathavī| ()

79.

 vipphuritavij‍jurāji-

daṇḍāhatamaighadundubhīphalitā’suṃ,

kadalivanaṃ viya nāgau

nippīḷaisi asaisalaukaṃ saukau| ()

80.

 jātikkhaittaṃ khaittaṃ

viya sambādhaṃ chaṇaihi taisaṃtaisaṃ,

vijanaukāsā’ kāsā

laukai maṅgalanimittajātaṃ jātaṃ| () (yamakabandhanaṃ)

itimaidhānandābhidhānaina yatinā viracitai sakalakavijana hadayānandadānanidānai jinavaṃsadīpai santikai nidānai tathāgata parinibbānappavatti paridīpau aikūnatiṃsatimau saggau|

parinibbutai  bhagavati sahaparinibbānā brahmāsahampati imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi|

sabbaivanikkhipissanti bhūtā laukai samussayaṃ

yathā aitādisau satthā laukai appavipuggalau,

tathāgatau balappattau sambuddhau parinibbutauti|

parinibbutai bhagavati sahaparinibbānāsak‍kau daivānamindau imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi|

anic‍cā vatasaṅkārā uppāda vayadhamminau,

uppaj‍jitvā nirujjhanti taisaṃvūpasamau sukhauti|

parinibbutai bhagavati sahaparinibbānā āyasmā anuruddhau imā gāthāyau abhāsi|

nāhuassāsapassāsau ṭhita cittassa tāditau

anaijāsanti mārabbha saukālamakarī muni,

asal‍līnaina cittaina vaidanaṃ ajjhavāsayi

paj‍jautassaiva nibbānaṃ vimaukkhau caitasau ahūhi|

parinibbutai bhagavati sahaparinibbānā āyasmā ānandau imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi|

tadāsiyaṃ hiṃsanakaṃ tadāsilaumahaṃsanaṃ,

sabbākāravarūpaitai sambuddhai parinibbutaiti|

1.

 laukattayaikanayanaumunikhippamaiva

paggayhamuddhanibhujai parinibbutauti,

kandiṃsu tāva vilapiṃsu vivaṭṭayiṃsu

āvaṭṭayiṃsu papatiṃsu mavītarāgī| ()

2. 

 assāsayaṃ vasigaṇaṃ samalaṃ sasaukaṃ

ratyāvasaisa matha dhammakathāya thairau,

taṃ vītināmayi niruddhatamau’nuruddhau

ānandathairasuhadau hadayaṅgamāya| ()

3.

 mal‍lāna magganagaraṃ kusiṇāra middhaṃ

āraucanāya parinibbutabhāva massa,

ānandathaira maniruddhayasau sarīrau

pāhaisi attadutiyaṃ anuruddhathairau| ()

4.

 mal‍lā tadatthapasutā samayaina taina

yatrā’bhisan‍nipatitā patimattayanti,

thairaupana’ttadutiyau tamupaic‍ca santhā-

gāraṃ tamattha mabhivaidayi saukadīnau| ()

5. 

 thairassa tassa suniyamakma samal‍laputtā

mal‍lā samal‍lasuṇisā bhuvika mal‍lachāyā,

kandiṃsu tāva papatiṃsu pariddaviṃsu

dhammil‍lavail‍litabhujā’hatasaukasal‍lā| ()

6.

 nānāvidhāni turiyāni sugandhamālaṃ

ādāya pañ‍casatadussayugāni mal‍lā,

yainā’si tassasugatassu’tujaṃ sarīraṃ

taṃ sāḷadāya mupavattana mausariṃsu| ()

7.

 tai gandhadhūpakusumaihi ca nac‍cagīta-

vaj‍jaihi chattamaṇivijanicāmaraihi,

cailabbitāna navamaṇḍalamāḷakādiṃ

katvāna chāhamakariṃsu sarīrapūjaṃ| ()

8. 

 pamaukkhamal‍lapurisā’ṭṭhanahātasīsā

gandhaudakaina sunivatthasupārutattā,

muttāmaṇīhi khacitāya tathāgatassa

gattaṃ suvaṇṇasivikāya niraupitaṃ taṃ| ()

9.

 cālaitu mappamapi sattamavāsaramhi

pucchiṃsu thaira manuruddha masayha kinti,

sau dibbacakkhumatiyā’mitadaivatānaṃ

pabyākari parivitak‍ka mavaic‍ca thairau| ()

10.

 nissaisamānusikadibbamahāmahaihi

taṃviggahaṃ bhagavatau’bhimahīyamānaṃ,

ukkhippa tāsamanuvattakamakal‍labhūpā

gantvāna uttaradisāya purassu’dīciṃ| ()

11. 

 majjhainamajjha kamahinībharu muttaraina

dvāraina tassanagarassa pavaisayitvā,

sutvāna bandhulapajāpatimal‍likā taṃ

dvārai ṭhitā’thanavakauvidhānabbayāya| ()

12.

 dhautāya gandhasalilaina mahālatābya-

bhusāya sattaratanaihi samuj‍jalāya,

aṅgīrasassa satapuñ‍ñavīlāsacittaṃ

chādaisi gatta matulaṃ kaḷitāvakāsā| ()

13.

 mal‍lāna magganagarassa puratthimāyaṃ

saṃvij‍jatai makuṭabandhanacaitiyaṃ yaṃ,

daihaṃ tilaukasaraṇassa puratthimaina

dvāraina nikkhamiya tatra samappayiṃsu| ()

14. 

 sā yāvasandhisamalā nagarī tadāni

mandāravaihi pihitā’bhavi chan‍numattaṃ,

pucchiṃsu satthu paṭipaj‍jana mattabhāvai

ānandathaira matha bhumibhujā kathanti| ()

15.

 daihai yathaiva paṭipaj‍jati cak‍kavatti-

rañ‍ñau tathaiva paṭipaj‍jatha buddhadaihai,

ic‍cāha sau bhagavatau’tujarūpakāyaṃ

tai vaiṭhayuṃ ahatakāsikasāṭakaihi| ()

16.

 kappāsapaṭṭavihataihipi vaiṭhayiṃsu

katvāna pañ‍casatadussayugaihi aivaṃ,

pakkhippa tailaparipuṇṇasuvaṇṇadauṇyā

añ‍ñā’yasāya paṭikuj‍jiya dauṇiyā tai| ()

17. 

 gattaṃ jinassa citakaṃ katacittakammaṃ

mālālatāvilasitaṃ satahatthamuc‍caṃ,

vīsādhikaṃ agarucandanadāruka puṇṇaṃ

āraupayiṃsu siriyā jitavaijayantaṃ| ()

18.

 pāvāya mal‍lanagaraṃ kusiṇāranāmaṃ

gantā mahāpabhutikassapathairanāgau,

bhikkhūhi pañ‍casatikaihi pathauk‍kamitvā

rukkhassa mūla mupagamma khaṇaṃ nisīdi| ()

19.

 addhānamagga mathakhau paṭipaj‍ji tamhā

mandāravaṃ kusuma mañ‍ñatarau gahaitvā,

ājīvakau ta mahipassiya kassapākhyau

jānāsi gautama manantajinantyapucchi| ()

20. 

 āmāvusau bhagavatau parinibbutassa

hauntya’j‍ja santadivasāti tataumayaidaṃ,

mandāravaṃ kusuma māharitanti vuttai

kandiṃsu kaici vilapiṃsu avītarāgā| ()

21.

 tāya’cchi bhikkhuparisāya subhaddabuḍḍha-

pabbaj‍jitau sapadi satthari baddhavairau,

yau dubbacau yati mahāsamaṇaina taina

muttā mayaṃhi yadupaddutakāyavācā| ()

22.

 icchāma yaṃja maya midānikarauma taṃyaṃ

naicchāma’dāni nakarauma mayaṃ tataumā,

sauvittha mācavilapittha’ lamāvusauti

vatvā pahāra madadī jinadhammacak‍kai| ()

23. 

 mal‍lā narindamakuṭaṅak‍kitapādapīṭhā’-

limpaitu massa caturau cita mappasayha,

pucchiṃsu thaira manuruddha mi’tabruvi sau

pattai’dha paj‍jalati kassapathairasīhai| ()

24.

 dhammāgadaina hatasaukahadaihi yaina

bhikkhūhi satthucitakau vasikassapavhau,

tainau’pasaṅkami subhaddakathaṃ sasīsai

sukkhāsanī’vu’rasi satti’va mañ‍ñamānau| ()

25.

 katvā padakkhiṇa madhiṭṭhahi sau catuttha-

jjhānuṭṭhitau bhagavatau citakaṃ tivāraṃ,

baddhañ‍jalī sirasi thairavarassa bhaitvā

taṃ supakpatiṭṭhitapadāni patiṭṭhahiṃsu| ()

26. 

 thairāsahassa karatāmarasaihi satthu

pādaṃsumāli vihitāhinipac‍cakārau,

aumuj‍ji gandhavitakāparasāgaramhi

laukauca tappabhava saukaghanandhakārai| ()

27.

 thairainacāhinamitai citakau samantā

sampaj‍jalittha sayamaivu’tujattabhāvai,

daḍḍhai jinassa masivā napichārikāsi

sārīrikaṭṭhīvisarau’bhayathāvasiṭṭhau| ()

28.

 sattaiva nābhivakiriṃsu lalāṭagīvā

dhātvakkhakaṭṭhi muninau catudantadhātū,

siddhatthakhaṇḍakatataṇḍulamuggamattā

saisā pabhāyapi tidhā pakiriṃsu dhātu| ()

29. 

 nibbāpayiṃsu citakānala mantalikkhā

nikkhamma cāmaramanauhara nīradhārā,

sāḷaddumaihi salilāni tathā samantā

mal‍lā sugandhaparivāsitavārināpi| ()

30.

 aulambamānamaṇidāmasuvaṇṇadāmaṃ

cittabbitānamathabandhiyamal‍labhūpā

katvā sugandhaparibhaṇḍa muḷārasanthā-

gārai susanthariya kaujavakambalāni| ()

31.

 taṭṭhānatau kamakuṭabandhananāmasāḷaṃ

yāvañ‍jasaṃ rajatakañ‍canatauraṇaihi,

lājādinā kadalipuṇṇaghaṭaihi dīpa-

dhūpaddhajaihi paritau patimaṇḍayitvā| ()

32. 

 taṃ satthudhātuparipuṇṇa suvaṇṇadauṇiṃ

mal‍lāna maggapura māhariyu’ssavaihi,

saitātapattalasitai sarabhāsanamhi

muttāmaṇīhi khacitai tahi mappayitvā| ()

33.

 kumbhaina kumbha mupahac‍ca gaḷaṃ gaḷaina

cak‍kaina cak‍ka mupahac‍ca bhujaṃ bhujaina,

rakkhaṃ vidhāya caturaṅginiyā samantā

saināya haimakavacaihi gavacchikaṃva| ()

34.

 katvaivamaiva dhanurāvaraṇañ‍ca satti-

hatthaihi pañ‍jara manantajinassadhātū,

sammānayiṃsu sumahiṃsu susādhukīḷaṃ

kīḷiṃsu sattadivasaṃ sahanāgaraihi| ()

35. 

 sutvāna bhupati tamattha majātasattu

satthāhi khattiyakulappabhavau ahampi,

sārīrabhāga marahāma mayampi tasmā

pāhaisi dūta matha mal‍lanarādhipānaṃ| ()

36.

 vaisālikā pacuralicchavibhubhujā ca

sakyādhipā kapilavatthupurādhivāsī,

bhūpā’l‍lakappavijitai bulayāca rāma-

gāmamhi kauliyamahīpatayau tathaiva| ()

37.

 yau vaiṭhadīpanagarai dharaṇīsurau sau

pāvaiyyakā ca mahipā pahiṇiṃsu taisaṃ|

pac‍caikadūtapurisai caturaṅginīhi

saināhi taipi nivutā’bhimukhībhaviṃsu| ()

38. 

 āgamma yaina bhagavā sayamaiva gāma-

kkhaittau’pavattanavanai parinibbutau nau,

dassāma nau’ti jinadhātulavampi mal‍lā

pāhaisu mun‍natimanā paṭisāsanāni| ()

39.

 sā rājadhāni nijarājapurakkhatāhi

saṅgāmasūracaturaṅginivāhinīhi,

yuddhāya baddhakalahāhi khaṇaṃjagāma

vailātivattapalayambudhinibbisaisaṃ| ()

40. 

 yau dauṇabhusurasudhī bhavi jambudīpai

rājūhi pūjitapadau sunisammakārī,

sau dauṇagaj‍janamakā kaparisaṃ vinaintau

dvaibhāṇavāramita māhavakaiḷisaj‍jaṃ| ()

41.

 sutvā’nusāsana mathācariyassa tassa

sabbai samagganiratā vasudhādhināthā,

atthāya nau vibhajathā’ti samaina tumhai

dhātūna maṭṭhapaṭiviṃsaka mabruviṃsu| ()

42.

 khīṇāsavau hi sarabhū citakaina gīvā-

dhātuṃ samāhariya laukahitāya yattha,

laṅkāya bhāti mahiyaṅgaṇathūparājā

tasmiṃ samappayi subhai maṇithūpagabbhai| ()

43. 

 khaimavhayau citakatau vasi vāmadāṭhā-

dhātuṃ samaggahiya’ dāsi kaliṅgarañ‍ñau,

dauṇaudvijau bhariya dhātuvibhāgasaj‍jau

vaiṭhantarai nidahi dakkhiṇadantadhātuṃ| ()

44.

 dāṭhaṃ jaṭārajatacaitiyagaṃ dvijassa

taṃ dakkhiṇakkhaka mapāhari daivarājā,

dauṇau suvaṇṇamayadauṇimavāpuritvā

taisaṃ vibhaj‍ja samabhāgamadāsidhātu| ()

45.

 tai bhubhujā sakasakaivisayai vidhāya

sampūjayiṃsu jinadhātunidhānathūpai

aṅgāra māhariya mauriyakhattiyā’tha

dauṇaupi haimamayakumbha makaṃsu thupai| ()

46. 

 disvāna tassapamahāvasi tatthatattha

dhātvantarāya matha gaṇhiya rāmagāmai,

añ‍ñatra dauṇa mitadhātu majātasattu-

rañ‍ñau thiraṃ nidahituṃ padadāsi dhātu| ()

47.

 aṭṭha’ṭha vaḍḍhitakaraṇḍakathūpagabbhai

pakkhippabhumipati dhātumahānidhānaṃ,

sau thairanāgasaraṇau pavidhāsi vāḷa-

saṅghāṭayanta miha yaujayi daivarājā| ()

48.

 rājā asaukaviditau sirijambudīpai

tamhā samāhariya satthu sarīradhātu,

kārāpayī caturasitisahassathūpai

dhīmā mahindavasi dīpamimaṃ tadā’pa| ()

49. 

 daivānamādipiyatissanarādhipaina

thūpai vidhāya jinadhātacihapattamattā,

vitthāritā paṇihitā jayabaudhisākhā-

vāmaitarakkhakasilāmayapattadhātu| ()

50.

 nattā pana’ssa abhayau jalituggataijau

yau duṭṭhagāmini raṇambudhipāragāmī,

rājā’nurādhanagarai nagarādhirājai

katvāna laṅkamakalaṅkamakāsiraj‍jaṃ| ()

51.

 laṅkaṅganāya kucamaṇḍalanibbisaisaṃ

sauvaṇṇamāli matulaṃ ratanuj‍jalantaṃ,

sau thuparāja makari thira mappayitvā

dhātuppabandha mabhinimmitabuddharūpaṃ| ()

52. 

 sārīrikaihi’tarathupavaraihi laṅkā-

bhumitthi mauḷimaṇi kañ‍canamālināma

saggāpavaggasukhadau vara thūparājā

sau yāva dhātuparinibbutiyā vibhātu| ()

53.

 iddhānubhāva madhikic‍ca jinassa dhātu-

kāyau’pahārarahitau garukāraṭhānaṃ,

patvā tahiṃtahi mathantaradhānakālai

yau saṅkamissati’ha kañ‍canamālithupaṃ| ()

54.

 sau nāgadīpamupagamma tatauvidhātu-

laukā ca nāgabhavanā tidasālayamhā,

nikkhamma nimmita nirūpamabuddharūpau

rāsi bhavissati sumaṇḍita baudhimaṇḍai| ()

55. 

 buddhānubhāvapabhavā’bhinavaggijālā-

mālāhi paj‍jalitadhātumayattabhāvai,

tāvānu’mattamapi dissati nāvasaisaṃ

katvāna dhātuparinibbuti haissataiva| ()

56.

 buddhāpadāna marahādiguṇavadātaṃ

bhatyā punappuna mimaṃ sarataṃ sataṃ bhau,

cittaṃ kilaisapariyuṭṭhita muj‍jubhūtaṃ

addhā sudhantakanakaṃva visuddhimaiti| ()

57.

 cittaujutāyapi vitak‍kavicāradhammā

vattanti satthuguṇasañ‍cayagaucarā’tha,

saṃjāyatai pavurapīti ca kāyacitta-

passaddhi niddarathatāya sukhaṃ samādhi| ()

58. 

 gambhīratāya tadadhīnaguṇaṇṇavassa

niddhutanīvaraṇatau pavivaikabhūtaṃ,

chāyaitha jhānamupacāra mathā’dhigacchai

tappādakaṃ ariyamga phakhalañ‍ca yaugī| ()

59.

 tassā’ticārucaritassaraṇānuyuttau

hautaivava satthari sagāravasapakpatissau,

saddhindriyādi pariṇāmagatau’dhigacchai

pītippamaudabahulau kusalau’bhisandaṃ| ()

60.

 satthussa caitiyagharaṃva tadattabhāvau

pūjārahau ca bhayabhairavadukkhasayhau,

laj‍ji ca bhīru labhatai sahavāsasañ‍ñaṃ

saggāpavaggavibhavassa bhavaiyya bhāgī| ()

61. 

 laṅkāya lakkhapatigāmavaramhi khaittā-

rāmādhipaina guṇabhusaṇabhusitaina,

vikhyātanimmalayasauvisaraina val‍li-

gāmubbhavaina parisāvacarakkhamaina| ()

62.

 thairainu’pāyacaturaina bhadattasaṅghā-

nandābhidhaina garunā garubhāvagaina,

sissaurasau’panayanaina nijaṃva naittaṃ

rakkhaṃ vidhāya mabhivuddhiya mappitau yau| ()

63.

 yau jīvitampi nirapaikkhiya tambapaṇṇī-

dīpaṃ tivāra mavatiṇṇa mimaṃ vasīhi,

muttāmaṇīhi khacitaina namahagghasiddhagī-

caṅgauṭakaina mahituṃ jinadantadhātuṃ| ()

64. 

 sikkhāgaruṃ vajiranāma vihārasāmiṃ

rājādhirājagurulañchadharaṃ yatindaṃ,

katvānu’pajjhamupasampādamāpa dhamma-

majjhaitu mautariya rammamarammaraṭṭhaṃ| ()

65.

 saṃvaḍḍhitaṃ pitupadādhigataina maiṇḍuna

rañ‍ñā pasīdiya kusaggadhiyā sakāya,

sissaṃ asaisapariyattidharassa ñaiyya-

dhammābhivaṃsaviditassa’pi saṅgharañ‍ñau| ()

66.

 saṃjayuttabhāṇakakumārabhivaṃsanāmaṃ

rājādhirājagarulañchadharaṃ sudhīraṃ,

thairāsabhaṃ supaṭipattigaruṃ garuṃ yau

nissāya dhammavinayai paṭutaṃ jagāma| ()

67. 

 lakkhīsarai mauraṭunāmapurai surammai

jātaina issarajanāyananamhi jātyā,

vassaṭṭhatiṃsaparimāṇavayauguṇaina

pāsāṇadūrapuragaucaragāmikaina| ()

68.

 tainā’bhayādikaruṇāratanābhidhānā-

rāmādhipaina gaṇvācakābhāvagaina,

saṃsuddhabuddhaguṇādīpanatapparaina

saṃsārasāgarasamuttaraṇāsayaina| ()

69.

 pītippamaudajananaṃ samaṇaina maidhā-

nandābhidhaina kavinā kavikuñ‍jarānaṃ,

ādic‍cabandhujinarājaguṇappabandha-

suddhāpadānaparidīpakathāsarīraṃ| ()

70. 

 saddānusāsani’tihāsanighaṇṭuchandau-

laṅkārasāra mabhidhammakathāgabhīraṃ,

saggāpavaggasukhadaṃ samatiṃsasaggaṃ

vyākhyāsamaita manurūpa padappayaugaṃ| ()

71.

 dvisahassabandhasamākulaṃ jinavaṃsadīpamanākulaṃ

racitaṃ samāpa samāpanaṃ paramaṃpabandhasiraumaṇiṃ,

subhamāghamāsikavāsarai nirupaddavaina tathāgatai

parinibbutai dvisahassasaṭṭhicatussataudayahāyatai| ()

iti maidhānandābhidhānaina yatinā viracitai sakalakavijana hadayānandadāna nidānai jinavaṃsadīpai santikainidānai bhagavatau dhātuparinibbānappavattiparidīpau tiṃsatimausaggau|

sātiraikau santikainidānabhāgau tatiyau|
